# Awesome DeepSeek Harness (DSH) Plugin [![Awesome](https://awesome.re/badge.svg)](https://awesome.re) ![awesome · DSH plugin](https://awesome-dsh-plugin.com/badge.svg) ![plugin count](https://img.shields.io/endpoint?url=https%3A%2F%2Fawesome-dsh-plugin.com%2Fcount.json)

[![Awesome DSH Plugin](https://awesome-dsh-plugin.com/banner-en.png)](https://awesome-dsh-plugin.com)

English | [中文](README.zh.md)

> A curated list of plugins for [DeepSeek Harness](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness) (`dsh`).

DeepSeek Harness is DeepSeek's open-source agent harness — a runnable coding agent (Web and headless), built on a framework where everything is a plugin: models, tools, sandboxes, session storage, UI, even the agent loop itself. Plugins can extend the official coding agent, swap out its core parts, or assemble something entirely different.

This list collects community plugins that are installable via `dsh plugin add` (each declares a `dsh.bundle` manifest). [PRs welcome](#contributing).

> 📡 **This list is also a plugin market index.** On dsh 0.2.1+, open **Plugins → Discover → Index sources** and add `https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/dsh-plugin-catalog/index.json`. That is this list as a format-1 index, served from npm so it loads quickly everywhere: dsh gives a source 15 seconds, and the GitHub Pages copies of the same document — `https://awesome-dsh-plugin.com/index.json` and its mirror `https://dshmarket.com/index.json` — can exceed that from some networks. The Discover tab and the `plugin_market` agent tool then search the same entries. The index covers plugins published on npm with a known version; GitHub-only entries still install with the command beside them.

> 🛒 **Recommended: [dsh-market](https://github.com/dsh-market/dsh-market#readme)** (optional) — the plugin market inside DeepSeek Harness, with every plugin on this list. Simple, friendly UI: one-click plugin install and upgrade, one-click theme switching:

```sh
dsh plugin --profile web add dshmarket
```

<a href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/dsh-market/dsh-market/main/assets/demo-en.png"><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/dsh-market/dsh-market/main/assets/demo-en.png" width="320" alt="dsh-market plugin browser inside DeepSeek Harness Settings: searchable plugin cards with one-click Install, category filters and a Themes tab"></a>

<sub><i>The plugin market inside Settings — click to enlarge.</i></sub>

> 💡 Prefer chat? [dsh-find-plugin](https://github.com/awesome-dsh-plugin/dsh-find-plugin#readme) lets your agent find plugins for you (`dsh plugin --profile web add dsh-find-plugin`).


> ℹ️ **On desktop clients.** This list is client-agnostic. A plugin is listed because it follows the official protocol — it declares a `dsh.bundle` manifest and installs with `dsh plugin add` — not because it adapts to any particular client.
>
> Clients worth a look: [dsh-desktop](https://github.com/dataelement/dsh-desktop), [deepseek-harness-desktop](https://github.com/hairyf/deepseek-harness-desktop), and [deepseek-harness-desktop](https://github.com/anywhere-labs/deepseek-harness-desktop) by anywhere-labs — all ship dsh-market built in, so everything on this list is one click away. Any other good third-party client works too.

> [!WARNING]
> Installing a plugin runs third-party code on your machine with your own permissions — it can read your files, use your credentials, and reach the network. Tool approvals don't sandbox plugin code. Being on this list is not a security review: check the source before you install, and try unfamiliar plugins somewhere that doesn't hold your keys. See the full disclaimer at the bottom of this page.

<details>
<summary><b>What it takes to be listed here</b></summary>

An entry is added when the plugin installs with `dsh plugin add`, does what its one-line description says, sits in the right category, and is maintained. Every submission is checked against its own source before merging — if a description claims "46 tools", someone counts them.

That is the whole bar. **This list doesn't rank plugins or judge their quality, and we don't want to.** Plenty of good software will never appear here, and a slot here proves nothing beyond meeting those rules. They exist so that whatever you pick installs and behaves the way the line said it would.

A listing isn't permanent either: entries whose repos go away, stop being maintained, or turn out to be broken get removed. Full criteria and the review checklist: [how submissions are reviewed](contributing.md#how-submissions-are-reviewed--收录如何评审).

</details>

## Plugins

<!-- BEGIN PLUGINS -->
### AGI Architecture Exploration

- [Anmulzhao/DSH-AGINT](https://github.com/Anmulzhao/DSH-AGINT) - Self-evolving agent framework on DeepSeek Harness (dsh): 40 Cordis plugins and 4 presets covering memory, reflection, gates, metrics and an auditable evolution loop. Not a one-command install. Clone the repo and run install/install.sh --profile=web (needs bash, Node 20+, python3, DSH 0.1.7-rc.1 or newer with an initialized profile), then restart.
- [AnonyJcy/dsh-j-space](https://github.com/AnonyJcy/dsh-j-space) - J-Space Cognition Suite SV1 native agent preset and standalone Cordis plugin: 13 modules, persistent controller and decoupled workspace.
- [anticbv/dsh-preset-deep](https://github.com/anticbv/dsh-preset-deep) - A fifth DeepSeek Harness work mode that requires a falsification design (at least two pieces of evidence that could disprove the plan) in every plan and tags each factual claim with its source (\[文件]/\[检索]/\[记忆]) or \[未知]; evidence grading follows the project's AGENTS.md.
- [Asiu-x/dsh-harness2](https://github.com/Asiu-x/dsh-harness2) - Harness² recursive self-improvement for DSH agents: Propose–Probe–Compose, isolated probes, anonymous selection, and a per-domain experience library.
- [benrong2048-boop/dsh-prompt-polish](https://github.com/benrong2048-boop/dsh-prompt-polish) - Turns a one-line DSH draft into a complete task brief (role / goal / constraints / output format). The engine is a byte-verified port of linshenkx/prompt-optimizer (three modes, 10 templates identical to upstream); history is consent-gated with version chains and structural comparison; every run reports its token usage. Nothing is written back or sent without a click.
- [buaaflyaway/dsh-progress-nudge](https://github.com/buaaflyaway/dsh-progress-nudge) - Counts the assistant rounds that call tools without writing anything to the user, then inserts a short progress reminder as a visible user message at the next model-request boundary, so a long silent tool loop reports what it has done before continuing. A Settings page adjusts the silence threshold (default 10), the reminder wording, the language (en / zh-CN / zh-TW), whether the reminder repeats every further threshold of silent rounds, whether subagent sessions are counted, and whether Agent Teams nudges the lead only or every member.
- [burning-frappuccino/dsh-turbo](https://github.com/burning-frappuccino/dsh-turbo) - Per-session execution gears for the DSH composer: an OVERDRIVE toggle turns Turbo on for the current session only, the Fast and Max gears swap the agent's planning and verification policy and may lower the request's reasoning effort, and the Host keeps the authoritative mode plus per-turn latency, step count, tool calls and whether a verification command ran.
- [dsh-answer-reviewer](https://github.com/bycall/dsh-answer-reviewer) - Agentic answer reviewer for DeepSeek Harness: each final assistant turn is re-reviewed by a separate LLM graded 1-100; below-threshold scores steer the agent back with concrete feedback. The score is shown as a chip on the answer's action row (green pass / red needs-work, with retry count and reason on hover). Live gate config from the Reviewer 配置 tab in the conversation view ring above the composer (对话 / 轨迹 / 记忆系统 / Reviewer 配置), from a 127.0.0.1 HTTP server, or from an optional right-sidebar tab via dsh-better-sidebar.
- [CAI-MH/dsh-quality-review](https://github.com/CAI-MH/dsh-quality-review) - Audit each finished assistant turn with an independent reviewer model and steer the agent to fix failed output, with at most 2 review rounds per turn and SOP-folder standards injected as an extra review dimension.
- [CanaryJing/dsh-big-fat-whale-maid-adaptive](https://github.com/CanaryJing/dsh-big-fat-whale-maid-adaptive) - Agent preset with adaptive tool presentation and environment-aware shell routing. Ports modules from yjh051108/dsh-routing-suite and xiaobright/dsh-anchored-standard (the former is listed here), so do not enable it alongside those presets.
- [ccll/dsh-advisor-flow](https://github.com/ccll/dsh-advisor-flow) - Executor/advisor workflow ported from pi-advisor-flow: the executor calls ask_advisor for a second opinion at key decisions, after repeated failures and before completion; equivalent repeated tool calls are intercepted before execution for an advisor review with a proceed/revise/blocked verdict, and hard mode force-reviews every turn before it closes. Per-call token and cost ledger with /advisor status readout, privacy tiers for repo context and file contents, six-shape secret redaction, and a web settings card; advisor failures never stall the main loop.
- [Clearailhc/clearai-dsh](https://github.com/Clearailhc/clearai-dsh) - An agent preset for evidence-checked research: every step advances only through system-verified observations, L3+ claims go to an independent evaluator, and the session folds into a replayable ledger that grows into a browsable domain ontology. Requires DSH >=0.1.7-alpha.1.
- [DDL095/dsh-moa-native](https://github.com/DDL095/dsh-moa-native) - Native generation-time Mixture-of-Agents: every turn, reference models run the same session context in parallel as pre-run drafts and one aggregator executes with full tooling; config-as-model recipes, F mirror / R trimmed views, enforced subagent gating (dual-shape session indexing), derived per-recipe vision (vision refs relay image reads to text-only aggregators), tiered cadence with separate steering and patrol rosters plus frozen reuse between beats, degeneration guard dropping degenerate reference output, anonymized model face (failure notices carry no provider identity), and cache-friendly guidance placement (the rehearsal record is pinned where it was produced instead of appended to every request, so the cached prefix covers it and each step pays only for what was added since - on by default across every cadence).
- [DoreiHime/dsh-help-me-to-ask](https://github.com/DoreiHime/dsh-help-me-to-ask) - On the first real work message of each chat window, asks for the missing goal, deliverable, constraints and acceptance criteria, then turns the answers into a short task sheet and injects it into the chat. Pure greetings are ignored.
- [FelixJiashengFu/dsh-force-collab](https://github.com/FelixJiashengFu/dsh-force-collab) - Forced collaboration: a composer picker sets 0–6 sub-agents, and while it is on every hands-on task must be split across exactly that many child agents through a collab_fanout tool that has no count parameter, then merged by the main agent.
- [FelixJiashengFu/dsh-muse-code](https://github.com/FelixJiashengFu/dsh-muse-code) - Meta's Muse Code CLI inside DSH: a composer switch starts muse serve, a right-sidebar panel and five chat tools (muse_open, muse_prompt, muse_read, muse_status, muse_interrupt) share one Muse session, approvals are decided only in the panel, and a local spend guard hard-stops at $10 by default. Windows only; needs the Muse Code CLI installed and signed in. The $10 cap is a local estimate for the muse serve this plugin starts.
- [ff66ccff/dsh-ATN](https://github.com/ff66ccff/dsh-ATN) - Adaptive topology multi-agent preset for DeepSeek Harness with peer messaging, task handoff and live network visualization.
- [Funny1Potato/dsh-outsourcing-expert](https://github.com/Funny1Potato/dsh-outsourcing-expert) - Agent presets whose leader does no work itself: every non-management tool call is denied at the harness boundary so the task has to go to a subagent, after a one-time lookup of the available subagent models and a per-task model choice; a second preset deliberately picks the model least suited to the task.
- [FuRongJun-1999/dsh-memory](https://github.com/FuRongJun-1999/dsh-memory) - White-box AGI architecture exploration: metacognition (self-cognition loop), continual learning (knowledge flywheel), world model (condition space, spatiotemporal memory graph), self-improvement (bootstrap discipline), zero-LLM white-box pipeline, and auditable trust guardrails.
- [highho/dsh-game-studio-presets](https://github.com/highho/dsh-game-studio-presets) - Eleven game-development agent presets cut by workflow stage — concept, design, build, art and audio, QA, release, narrative, level, engine — plus one orchestrator that delegates across them, carrying 37 bundled skills. Regroups the 48 agent prompts of Claude Code Game Studios (MIT) by stage so each preset loads only the skills its stage needs. Installs with dsh plugin add; run node install.mjs once afterwards to deploy the skills the presets reference.
- [HYClub/deepseek-harness-game-studios](https://github.com/HYClub/deepseek-harness-game-studios) - DeepSeek Harness port of Claude Code Game Studios (CCGS v1.1.2, MIT): 76 skills, 49 agents, hook bridge.
- [icyaaaww/dsh-soul](https://github.com/icyaaaww/dsh-soul) - Records recurring task corrections and tool failures locally, then compiles them into changes to eight harness components through explicitly started evolution, paired baseline/candidate checks, trial activation and rollback. Includes a budget and audit ledger; requires Node.js 24+ and a compatible DSH 0.2.1-rc.2 or later 0.2.x host.
- [JiewiW/dsh-cot-anchor](https://github.com/JiewiW/dsh-cot-anchor) - Injects conclusions already established in the previous reasoning block after each tool result, so the model continues from them instead of re-deriving the same conclusions.
- [jiuaiwo/dsh-expert](https://github.com/jiuaiwo/dsh-expert) - Expert roster for DeepSeek Harness: 636 experts across 22 divisions with full Chinese names and bios, summoned as subagents from the @ menu or by the model through list_t_experts / summon_t_expert / summon_t_experts, plus custom experts and a skill on/off switch.
- [dsh-proactive](https://github.com/john-walks-slow/dsh-proactive/tree/main/packages/dsh-proactive) - Host-level proactive wake-ups for DeepSeek Harness: the model schedules its own alarms (once, recurring interval, or cron, with optional random jitter), cold sessions are woken on time by session resume, model-initiated follow-ups are gated by quiet hours and a daily delivery budget, silent wake turns end with proactive_reclaim and fold out of model context as tombstones, declared schedule files glob-sync JSON schedules into host alarms idempotently, min_idle_seconds defers wakes until the destination session quiets down, and a web panel (settings section plus per-session tab, SSE live refresh) manages alarms and wake history — the heartbeat engine for AI companions and roleplay characters with their own daily routines and world evolution.
- [john-walks-slow/dsh-simulated-life](https://github.com/john-walks-slow/dsh-simulated-life) - Simulated life context for DeepSeek Harness: every turn injects the past 24h of simulated life events from the workspace .life/ directory (sliding-window filter, session-level deduplication with delta updates, world summary) as a collapsed context notice, and the life_react tool lets the agent record feelings, thoughts and actions back into its life log for the next round of world evolution — turning a DSH agent into a roleplay character with a persistent daily life.
- [Jonah-Wu23/dsh-gungnir#dsh-plugin](https://github.com/Jonah-Wu23/dsh-gungnir/tree/main/packages/dsh-plugin) - Evidence-driven goal verification plugin for DeepSeek Harness. Locks goals via /ultragoal and verifies completion against command exit codes and generated artifacts, preventing the model from falsely reporting task completion.
- [Kanadego/dsh-heartbeat](https://github.com/Kanadego/dsh-heartbeat) - An agent that wakes up on its own schedule: by default every 60 minutes, ±20% (48–72). It browses the interests you set, drops what it finds into a local pool of notes, and keeps a profile of what you like. When it has enough worth sharing it hands the session you linked a short package of notes — whether to speak, and which note to use, is that session's agent's call in the moment. Quiet hours, busy windows, a daily limit and a cooldown are decided in code; when the answer is no, the decision and expression phases after the gate call no model at all — browsing runs before the gate and is counted separately. Windows, Linux and macOS (Linux and macOS are minimal support).
- [kenz1117/dsh-engram](https://github.com/kenz1117/dsh-engram) - Cross-session long-term memory as a local SQLite store: dual scopes (user + per-git-origin project), hybrid FTS5 + local-vector retrieval fused with RRF, an evidence gate that forces an explicit sufficiency judgement, provenance audit chains, SM-2 spaced-repetition review, consolidation distillation and decay forgetting, and a nine-view "Memory Palace" sidebar panel. 22 narrow-parameter tools, no external service, no API key, no Docker.
- [leeyoung1/dsh-advisor-plugin](https://github.com/leeyoung1/dsh-advisor-plugin) - Pairs the running executor with a stronger reviewer model through zero-parameter advisor() calls and step-based patrol checks, returning plan, correction, or stop guidance.
- [liangzz1998/dsh-thinking-language](https://github.com/liangzz1998/dsh-thinking-language) - Injects one system-prompt section that makes the agent reason and answer in a chosen language (Simplified Chinese by default), with separate thinking and answer settings, a custom extra line, and support for standard, PTC and custom presets; the minimal preset is not affected.
- [lifangjin/dsh-maoying](https://github.com/lifangjin/dsh-maoying) - Web-novel writing assistant preset for DeepSeek Harness: the main agent acts as editor-in-chief and delegates outlining, drafting, review, lore-keeping and research to five helpers across a seven-phase workflow, with the whole novel kept as a structured markdown/YAML novel/ directory (character cards, a foreshadowing state machine, timeline, narrative memory), plus a sidebar creator panel and six zero-dependency CLI commands.
- [LittleAlety/dsh-team-conductor](https://github.com/LittleAlety/dsh-team-conductor) - Control plane for the harness's own Agent Teams: members are ordinary independent sessions created through the same seam the GUI uses, each with its own model, so they open and continue like any other conversation while a sidecar roster and task board coordinate them. Task entries carry a definition of done, a member's completion is submitted and waits for the Lead's review, and availability tells idle apart from awaiting-review or a failed last turn. With delegationEngine=auto it uses independent sessions, continuable subagents, or the kernel roster depending on what the composition provides.
- [mazov2love/dsh-remote-control](https://github.com/mazov2love/dsh-remote-control) - Let Agents directly control independent local DSH instances with zero intrusion—no plugins or components required on the target. Isolated environments enable autonomous configuration and testing, while Agents with different models and personalities can chat, role-play, or form nested control chains. Supports conversational setup, user-like operations, incremental reading, saved references, and remote session/workspace search.
- [MichengAI/dsh-pua](https://github.com/MichengAI/dsh-pua) - Adds /pua to steer the agent to try another approach and verify results before claiming completion, with 15 company styles, persona modes, global and per-conversation settings, and an optional loop that continues until a verification command passes, the iteration limit is reached, or the user cancels.
- [mrzturn/dsh-switchman](https://github.com/mrzturn/dsh-switchman) - Old conversation is folded into short summaries by a side model before the window fills (on-disk ledger survives restarts and re-anchors after host compaction), so long sessions stop getting more expensive every turn — one real full-day session: 6 folds replaced 209 old messages and the live context stayed in the tens of thousands of tokens. The session model picks the model per subtask across six pools (economy/mechanical/main/hard/vision/review); or pick Auto and a small intent classifier routes each message itself, switching only past a confidence threshold to keep prompt caches warm. A three-line watermark ends in an automatic four-step handover (fork backup, handover doc, compact, wake) without losing in-flight subagents; /ctx-pause, /ctx-resume and /ctx-handover keep it manual when wanted. Optional Agent Teams mode adds teammates and a shared task board; /vision gives text-only models eyes via the vision pool; tiered verification sends bigger diffs to a tester plus an independent reviewer. Settings UI, READMEs and language preferences cover the same 11 languages. Bundles three skills: read-only MySQL/Redis verification, commit-message generation, and a requirements/design document convention.
- [NEINC1/muche-dsh-plugin](https://github.com/NEINC1/muche-dsh-plugin) - Chat panel for the MuChe companion with per-user backend settings, plus an outbound bridge that runs tasks on the user's local dsh.
- [nlflora/dsh-empty-turn-guard](https://github.com/nlflora/dsh-empty-turn-guard) - Recovers a DeepSeek Harness turn that ended with reasoning only, with no text and no tool call, by steering the same turn one more step. Structure-only detection, so it costs no extra model call.
- [panando/dsh-pua](https://github.com/panando/dsh-pua) - Fork of @michengai/dsh-pua that removes the upstream global kill-switch so the composer entry is always visible while the feature stays off by default and can be toggled per session; the entry is a dropdown (Turn on / Turn off / Options) with a more compact options panel.
- [penghao73551/dsh-prompt-balance](https://github.com/penghao73551/dsh-prompt-balance) - Measures what your assembled system prompt is actually made of - rules, identity, workspace policy, tool descriptions - in bytes and percentages, then moves the operating protocol ahead of the rule block so the model reads who it works for before it reads the prohibitions. Config-driven via ~/.dsh/prompt-balance.json; two hooks, no build step, no dependencies.
- [pf-sjtu/dsh-early-stop-guard](https://github.com/pf-sjtu/dsh-early-stop-guard) - Keeps a DSH agent turn open when the model ends it by narrating work it has not done: on agent/turn-stopping the guard replays the turn log and classifies the trailing message with regexes — in the default mode wait / ask / done / gray end the turn with no model call, and only a promise of pending work reaches the judge — then has a judge chain of four dialect kinds (System One three-question noul, OpenAI-compatible, OpenAI Decisions, or the session LLM) rule on whether the turn is really over, and steers the same turn with agent.steer when it is not.
- [pk14742952-AD/dsh-host-rsi](https://github.com/pk14742952-AD/dsh-host-rsi) - Reflective self-evolution plugin for local Qwen3.8 27B/3.8 Flash and online APIs: a coding-loop convergence steerer curbs Qwen3.8 27B/Flash repeated-verification deep-thinking to converge faster and cut thinking time; a five-state delivery guard detects and corrects stuck turns and infinite loops; model-agnostic host logic, mostly compatible with online APIs; per-use continual self-improvement with counter-reason self-checks, critic escalation, inverted-index memory retrieval, coding-loop convergence steering, and a five-state delivery guard.
- [qian-dsh/dsh-as-mcp-server](https://github.com/qian-dsh/dsh-as-mcp-server) - Exposes DSH as an MCP server so another agent can watch its thinking, tool calls and file writes in real time, share intent, take a floor lock that denies DSH's write/edit/git tools, inject mid-turn alerts, and delegate reviews.
- [qian-dsh/dsh-outer-brain](https://github.com/qian-dsh/dsh-outer-brain) - A philosopher's way of thinking made executable: dialectics and practice-testing to govern hallucination and long-session drift, so done is a verifiable fact rather than a claim.
- [Qulierm/orbital-agents](https://github.com/Qulierm/orbital-agents) - Persistent Endeavour and Challenger peer sessions for DeepSeek Harness that plan, execute sequential tasks, and verify reported results.
- [rootsbymenda/dsh-second-angle](https://github.com/rootsbymenda/dsh-second-angle) - Checks each claim in a finished answer against the tool output that same turn read: a contradiction whose quote is found in that output steers one correction in the same turn, and claims the output does not back are flagged in the session log and on stderr. A free scan for absolute words and unmatched numbers runs first, and a turn that ran no tool never reaches the model.
- [samirliu/dsh-goalloop-agentteams](https://github.com/samirliu/dsh-goalloop-agentteams) - Deterministic goal-completion gate for DSH: a contract-first AC grammar with named failable check commands, a gate that re-runs every check itself (zero model trust), verdicts bound to a tree digest so file changes void passing verdicts, and a hard deny on false completion at update_goal via tools/pre-execute with a false-complete counter that blocks after two catches; scope-aware task-level claim handling (strict or goal-only) keeps multi-task Agent Teams loops from deadlocking.
- [shiliuyu316/dsh-optimization-2027](https://github.com/shiliuyu316/dsh-optimization-2027) - Five-tier reasoning protocol injected into the system prompt — off / light / standard / deep / multi-agent, 0 / 3 / 9 / 17 / 19 rules — with the tokens each tier adds per request shown beside it. Alongside it: a claim ledger that marks a load-bearing claim with no evidence or check as unsupported, read-only analyst briefs, JSON-declared custom tools that work in the same turn, one persistent PowerShell session, and re-injection of host context after a configurable message gap. Tier 0 injects nothing and mounts no tools. A derivative of husongan/dsh-deepseek-2027 (MIT); do not install both — each injects its own reasoning-protocol section into the system prompt.
- [sososilencing/dsh-network-engineer](https://github.com/sososilencing/dsh-network-engineer) - Standalone Network Engineer Assistant agent preset plus a bundled 32-doc command knowledge base skill for DPtech, H3C, Huawei and Ruijie switches and routers: KB-first retrieval, cross-vendor command translation, topology and CLI-output screenshot reading, online manual lookup with cited sources, and mandatory risk annotations for dangerous commands; answers networking topics only.
- [STARDUSTLC666/dsh-dream](https://github.com/STARDUSTLC666/dsh-dream) - Experience memory and reflection for DSH agents: automatically extracts candidate lessons after tasks and recalls user-reviewed lessons in relevant later tasks. Supports source provenance, project-scoped retrieval, conflict handling, usage feedback and expiry review; capture and recall have separate controls, and writing lessons to AGENTS.md requires preview and explicit application. A step-by-step first-run guide (4 steps) now opens inside its settings panel.
- [starkingcn/dsh-story#story-mode](https://github.com/starkingcn/dsh-story/tree/main/story-mode) - Story Mode preset for DeepSeek Harness: role-play GM persona, three-way choices, .md character cards and world book, context distillation and prompt wrapping.
- [StarsHarryZhu/dsh-assistant](https://github.com/StarsHarryZhu/dsh-assistant) - General-purpose chat agent on a parent agent + worker sub-agent split, for DSH Desktop only: a sidebar row opens each assistant's long-running conversation, where the parent chats, records schedule, lists, memos, logs, profile, persona and projects as plain files in that assistant's workspace folder, and hands other work to background workers through the bundled complete-subagent, picking each worker's model from the ones you enable and approving their write access. A heartbeat wakes it for reminders, a daily brief and wake-ups it sets itself, and holds back during quiet hours. Through the bundled im-robot it chats over WeChat: the model decides per reply with im_route whether the reply also goes to WeChat, and images and files sent from WeChat appear in the conversation and stay in the workspace's IM/WeChat folder for a set number of days. Its tools never appear in normal sessions.
- [StellaryToucher/dsh-muse-code](https://github.com/StellaryToucher/dsh-muse-code) - Adds a `muse` subagent provider and the `subagent_muse` tool to DeepSeek Harness, so the main agent can hand one self-contained task to the Meta Muse Code CLI and get back only its final report. A patched fork of `@pascapone/dsh-muse-code` that loads on DSH 0.2.1: it carries local equivalents of the removed `settleRun` export and `subagents.start` method, and documents an optional proxy for the gateway stream-phase bug. Needs the `muse` CLI installed. Install either this fork or the upstream @pascapone/dsh-muse-code, not both: both register the same subagent-muse loader row.
- [sxwpskd/dsh-milieu](https://github.com/sxwpskd/dsh-milieu) - Prepares a workspace management protocol for an agent — three libraries (cognition, tools, external information), an optional preloaded session that has already read the workspace, and a re-read card when the workspace has changed enough.
- [syyr1987/dsh-linghun](https://github.com/syyr1987/dsh-linghun) - A judgment core for DeepSeek Harness: a cognition loop that gives every decision a source and attribution and drives planning to a close, hippocampus memory that condenses running experience into knowledge (journal ledger, episodic archive, low-confidence decay), judgment identity responsibilities in the architecture (consistent rubric, judgment bound to its source), and an A2A memory-management team via dsh-linghun-assembler. Requires DSH ^0.1.0-rc.7 (rc.2 supported).
- [syyr1987/dsh-linghun-assembler](https://github.com/syyr1987/dsh-linghun-assembler) - Memory-assembly team for DeepSeek Harness: every message triggers a five-step cognition loop (judge, retrieve, assemble, feedback, calibrate) carried by role-playing subagents (judge, retriever, archivist, advocate, editor, scribe), using BM25 retrieval, time-ordered material, and LLM assembly to build useful material packs injected into linghun. Works with dsh-linghun.
- [Tests4gptplay/deepseek-servant](https://github.com/Tests4gptplay/deepseek-servant) - Autonomous AI assistant and multi-agent collaboration for DSH: discuss goals, plan work in the background, coordinate multiple Workers to execute tasks and review results, pause and resume work, recover after interruptions, keep task history and deliverables, and build working memory and skills; offers a standard local mode and optional experimental private-Git collaboration.
- [Tisitan/dsh-my-go](https://github.com/Tisitan/dsh-my-go) - Multi-agent orchestration preset: a lead agent dispatches subagents from a built-in role roster over separate read and write lane pools with per-role model binding, and collects each subagent report on a shared board; adds go_work/continue/need_help/forward tools, a tree-view panel and a settings page.
- [tyr1105/dsh-buddy#dsh-buddy](https://github.com/tyr1105/dsh-buddy/tree/main/packages/plugin) - Persistent named AI teammates that watch your sources, wake only when there is a reason, decide their own next check, and bring you questions, approvals and alerts in chat threads.
- [WDahah/portable-dsh-multi-agent-plugin](https://github.com/WDahah/portable-dsh-multi-agent-plugin) - Route each task to one model and its review to a different one, with qualification evidence, failover and a durable journal.
- [ws1234567891011/dsh-interim-narration](https://github.com/ws1234567891011/dsh-interim-narration) - Makes the agent narrate its process: every turn opens with how it reads the message and what it will do first, then short interim notes between tool-call batches, with in-app settings for style, sentence cap and toggles.
- [wuanthony397-hash/dsh-codex-peer](https://github.com/wuanthony397-hash/dsh-codex-peer) - Peer collaboration between a DeepSeek Harness agent and the local Codex CLI: split a goal into a shared work list, hand tasks over, review what comes back, and keep every prompt, event stream, and answer on disk.
- [xfgil/dsh-second-opinion](https://github.com/xfgil/dsh-second-opinion) - Expose a second-opinion tool: when the main agent is stuck it hands the problem to an independent session, receives a plan back, and continues — without waking or impersonating the user.
- [xingyanghaifu/dsh-reqtrace](https://github.com/xingyanghaifu/dsh-reqtrace) - Requirement-to-evidence audit for DSH sessions: maps each requirement to tool calls, files and commands, records per-requirement verification, and sends missing items back to the agent.
- [yunxiyang/dsh-loop-continue](https://github.com/yunxiyang/dsh-loop-continue) - Resumes an agent turn that ended after narrating its next action without calling a tool: the guard replays the turn log on agent/turn-stopping, asks a judge model one true/false question, and steers the same turn to run one more step.
- [ZekaiShi/Orivii](https://github.com/ZekaiShi/Orivii) - Animated personal assistant with independent chats, shared conversation memory, natural-language reminders and background tasks.
- [zhoulvyuan/dsh-plugin#dsh-claude-code-web](https://github.com/zhoulvyuan/dsh-plugin/tree/master/dsh-claude-code-web) - Claude Code-style workbench in DSH: workspace/session sidebar, streaming document-style transcript with unified tool cards and thinking, Markdown and Mermaid rendering, dark theme, and a draggable overlay panel backed by an in-host HTTP/WebSocket service.

### UI Enhancements

- [01men/DeptAI-UI-DSH](https://github.com/01men/DeptAI-UI-DSH) - A collaboration front-office for AI agents and humans — department boards, tickets, approvals and industry calculations run inside the DSH conversation and panel, so agents do the work in chat while humans stay accountable in the panel. It calls the 01men/ybkkasi governance base over existing contracts (asset ledger, IAM, OIDC SSO, RBAC and scene-level authorization) instead of re-implementing governance. Ships as a Cordis bundle and mounts at /gate01; the selftest suite is green on every push. Do not install this and 01men/ybkkasi as dsh bundles in the same profile: both register the ops-platform-core / ops-iam / ops-authn rows. Requires DSH >=0.1.0-rc.7 <0.2.0.
- [01Virex/dsh-status-rotator](https://github.com/01Virex/dsh-status-rotator) - Rotates the dsh turn-status label through 1059 bilingual phrases with typewriter output, an animated rainbow gradient, danmaku, 12 toggleable theme packs, live placeholders and tab-title rotation. Two star packs ship disabled by default; the stargazer pack carries one phrase per stargazer, refreshed weekly by a workflow.
- [0imzero/dsh-workspace-menu](https://github.com/0imzero/dsh-workspace-menu) - Workspace/chat context menu for the DSH home page: pin, rename, open in file explorer, archive, fork, copy, and open in a new window.
- [0x7A7A6572/dsh-forge-studio#plugin-notes](https://github.com/0x7A7A6572/dsh-forge-studio/tree/main/packages/plugin-notes) - Sticky-notes board for DeepSeek Harness: a sidebar entry opens a Markdown note panel with auto-save, colors, image paste and task-list rendering.
- [0xsline/dsh-spotlight](https://github.com/0xsline/dsh-spotlight) - Keyboard-first command palette for the DSH Web UI.
- [1010n111/dsh-about](https://github.com/1010n111/dsh-about) - Adds an "About" settings page to the DeepSeek Harness web interface, showing the running DSH version and recent release notes.
- [1070296335-create/dph-taskboard](https://github.com/1070296335-create/dph-taskboard) - Session-based task board in the sidebar: drag sessions into todo/doing/review/done columns, create sessions with model and reasoning-effort selection, trash with restore, notes, export/import.
- [1186258278/dsh-zh-ui](https://github.com/1186258278/dsh-zh-ui) - Chinese frontend UI design spec for dsh: injects the zh-ui-design skill and prompt rules (section-name headings, no filler copy, one signature element), plus a 10-rule UI copy/style linter.
- [1207291706/dsh-restart-foot](https://github.com/1207291706/dsh-restart-foot) - A restart button at the sidebar foot, just above the account entry, for the DSH desktop app on Windows: two clicks stop the app and bring it back, with no console window.
- [1250589385/dsh-quick-send](https://github.com/1250589385/dsh-quick-send) - Shortcut buttons in the composer row that send preset text into the current conversation in one click. Button label, size, opacity, shape and payload are editable in place, and the whole group moves left or right of the context meter by right-click dragging, persisted in localStorage. The group takes only the width it needs, so the context meter and the usage area stay clickable.
- [13071301808/dsh-composer-expand](https://github.com/13071301808/dsh-composer-expand) - Composer expand/collapse toggle for the Web UI: a ⬆/⬇ button in the composer tool row grows the input to a tall 70vh writing view for long drafts.
- [1314521gjy/dsh-wujie-canvas](https://github.com/1314521gjy/dsh-wujie-canvas) - Adds a 无界画布 page to the DSH left sidebar with a bundled front-end, local workbench status/start, and DSH Agent tools that read and edit the open canvas; the canvas has no built-in Agent. Requires a local 无界工作台 installation with ComfyUI; the plugin bundles neither ComfyUI, CUDA, models nor API keys.
- [1321928757/dsh-prompt-polish](https://github.com/1321928757/dsh-prompt-polish) - Input-bar prompt optimizer: rewrites drafts with 6 strategies × 3 languages plus custom instructions, optional goal/todo/compaction-summary/tool-result and chat context, a confirm dialog, last-5 history and three-tier settings persistence.
- [13520rygvv/dsh-restart-button](https://github.com/13520rygvv/dsh-restart-button) - Restarts the DeepSeek Harness desktop app from three places: a Settings row, the account menu above Sign out, and a configurable keyboard shortcut. Asks before interrupting running tasks. If a newer update is already downloaded and waiting, restarting installs it first. Windows desktop app only.
- [1420079678-ctrl/agent-body#dsh-anatomy-panel](https://github.com/1420079678-ctrl/agent-body/tree/main/workspace/plugins/dsh-anatomy-panel) - Anatomy panel for the Agent-Body organs: serves a live vitals dashboard at /anatomy inside the harness itself - organs, heartbeat, healing ledger, self-training counters and the real pulse stream - read straight from the runtime files rather than retyped. Host-only, no client bundle and no build step.
- [1497105876/dsh-text-inject](https://github.com/1497105876/dsh-text-inject) - Adds a "Text Injection" section to the DSH Settings sidebar: user-maintained blocks in a standalone Markdown file are injected into the system prompt every turn or into session context with keyword gating, with hot reload and automatic history backups.
- [1700563146/dsh-plugin-rightbar-divider](https://github.com/1700563146/dsh-plugin-rightbar-divider) - Restores the DSH right sidebar divider drag after whole-interface zoom shifts the host handle off it, and widens the hit area to 12px with a hover affordance.
- [1700563146/dsh-plugin-ui-zoom](https://github.com/1700563146/dsh-plugin-ui-zoom) - Whole-interface zoom for the DSH Web UI from 70% to 160%, driven by Ctrl+wheel, DSH's shortcut center or an inline pill, with content-width compensation so zooming out fits more text instead of narrowing the column.
- [1834395550/dsh-session-tabs](https://github.com/1834395550/dsh-session-tabs) - Browser-style Session tab bar for the DSH Web GUI - one tab per Session in a full-width strip at the top of the window, Sessions grouped per Workspace in collapsible boxes, tabs created only for the Sessions you actually open.
- [18569663yz-web/dsh-plugin-crypto-ticker](https://github.com/18569663yz-web/dsh-plugin-crypto-ticker) - A live crypto ticker card in the DeepSeek Harness sidebar footer, above the balance and Settings, showing BTC, ETH, SOL and JUP prices, 24-hour change and 24-point sparklines, with a pulse on each price tick, zero dependencies and no API key.
- [18840024879/dsh-float](https://github.com/18840024879/dsh-float) - Windows desktop floating assistant for DeepSeek Harness: an always-on-top chat panel with streaming answers, model switching, and region-screenshot questions.
- [1985899182/dsh-harness-chat-control](https://github.com/1985899182/dsh-harness-chat-control) - Adds ChatGPT-style stop, edit-and-resend, native quote annotations, and an independent sidebar follow-up chat with model and image input for DeepSeek Harness Desktop.
- [1DAdidi/dsh-memory-brain-plugin](https://github.com/1DAdidi/dsh-memory-brain-plugin) - A 3D neural memory map panel for the meow-memory store inside DeepSeek Harness. Sidebar entry + desktop embed, with 8-region memory classification and retrieval. Requires the meow-memory plugin (it reads .dsh-meow/memory.db) and Node 22.5+ (node:sqlite).
- [1jincheng/dsh-first-paint](https://github.com/1jincheng/dsh-first-paint) - First-paint splash for DSH: pushes a branded splash page into the boot HTML as an index-injection row, so the desktop window opens already showing the splash instead of a white/loading flash. Fades out and hands over once the startup-animation plugin starts (checked via `window.__DSH_STARTUP_RUNNING__`), with a 6-second fallback (configurable via `maxMs`) so it can never trap the app behind the splash. A normal dsh.bundle plugin: no host changes, no app.asar patching.
- [2002XiaoYu/dsh-session-diff](https://github.com/2002XiaoYu/dsh-session-diff) - Session-scoped diff view for the DSH Web right sidebar: opening a file the current conversation changed renders it in place with git-style added/removed line backgrounds, +/− gutter markers and dual old/new line numbers under syntax colouring, a header with +added −removed counts, a Changes only / Whole file toggle and a Re-read button, plus a companion tab that lists every file the session changed and opens each one in the same view. Files the conversation did not touch keep the shipped text viewer.
- [2026heshao/dsh-draft-attachments](https://github.com/2026heshao/dsh-draft-attachments) - Persists unsent composer attachments per session, so images and files pasted into the input box survive page switches, conversation switches and app restarts: the bytes are mirrored in IndexedDB and handed back through the official createDrafts/addAttachments channel, and are dropped as soon as the message is sent or you remove them.
- [2370998716-jpg/dsh-turn-eta](https://github.com/2370998716-jpg/dsh-turn-eta) - Shows elapsed time, tokens, tokens per second and a shrinking countdown estimate in the chat header for the running turn, and estimates output tokens, context size, cost and duration above the composer before you send.
- [2384418321/dsh-integration-settings](https://github.com/2384418321/dsh-integration-settings) - Adds two visual pages to DSH Settings. MCP servers are kept as a JSON source of truth that rewrites the profile patch managed block, with YAML validation and timestamped backups, plus import of a pasted mcpServers JSON that first lists what will be added or overwritten. Thinking unlock probes each OpenAI-compatible gateway for the reasoning efforts it really declares and writes the per-level config back while leaving other hand-written YAML byte ranges untouched; the available model list syncs from a separate button that previews additions and removals, then writes levels and models in one atomic pass, and can also run on the page schedule.
- [2404376192/dsh-dian-du](https://github.com/2404376192/dsh-dian-du) - Marks paragraphs or a single code line in an answer and asks the model about it in a docked right-hand column, leaving the conversation itself unmodified.
- [2768651338/dsh-effort-slider](https://github.com/2768651338/dsh-effort-slider) - A Claude Code-style reasoning-effort slider for the DSH Web UI: stepless drag, snap-on-release, a WebGL fire trail, and real, working thinking-effort control for any custom third-party model or provider.
- [2nd1st/dsh-plugin-open-app](https://github.com/2nd1st/dsh-plugin-open-app) - Runs open-mcp-apps inside DSH: every MCP app gets its own sidebar container with a separate workspace, session, and App mode, an agent status strip under the app, and inline app rendering in ordinary chats.
- [3361805598-gif/dsh-workspace-archive](https://github.com/3361805598-gif/dsh-workspace-archive) - Batch-archive or restore all conversations of a workspace from a sidebar panel or its row menu, hide a workspace from the sidebar once every conversation in it is archived, and pin workspaces to the top. Hiding never deletes logs, moves directories, or changes membership.
- [3450892121/dsh-task-monitor#dsh-task-monitor](https://github.com/3450892121/dsh-task-monitor/tree/main/dsh-task-monitor) - Right-side overlay panel for DSH sessions showing git status, background jobs, skills and MCP usage, artifacts and visited pages, with a commit/push dialog and one-click GitHub publish.
- [379385796-sys/dsh-chat-comfort](https://github.com/379385796-sys/dsh-chat-comfort) - Adjust chat bubbles and message spacing with comfortable or compact density on verified Windows Desktop RC2/RC3/RC5/0.2.2-rc.1 layouts, retaining native styles for unknown host fingerprints.
- [3Tiga3/dsh-plugin-namecards](https://github.com/3Tiga3/dsh-plugin-namecards) - Icon, Chinese display name and a one-line note for every DSH plugin, organized with folders, favorites, drag or pinyin ordering, list and grid views. Icons are auto-assigned to all installed plugins and can be overridden; import and export cover icons, names, notes, folders and order. Display-only: plugin files and cordis config are never modified; enable switches call the official plugin manager.
- [439436269-ctrl/dsh-blend-preview](https://github.com/439436269-ctrl/dsh-blend-preview) - Opens .blend files in the right-Sidebar document preview: Blender headless exports a cached GLB with scene statistics, rendered by a dependency-free WebGL viewport. Requires Blender 3.6+ on PATH (or set blenderPath).
- [439436269-ctrl/dsh-file-explorer](https://github.com/439436269-ctrl/dsh-file-explorer) - Sidebar file explorer and file previewer for DeepSeek Harness: a lazy workspace tree with in-panel preview for text, Markdown (tables included) and images, an action that opens the file in the conversation's right-hand document pane, and one that hands it to the system default app. Read-only, confined to configured roots.
- [439436269-ctrl/dsh-pinned-sessions](https://github.com/439436269-ctrl/dsh-pinned-sessions) - A pinned-sessions group in the sidebar, above the workspace list: every pinned session across all workspaces in one collapsible, searchable list, with click-to-open and a hover-revealed unpin button.
- [439436269-ctrl/dsh-preview-blender](https://github.com/439436269-ctrl/dsh-preview-blender) - Open .blend files in the right-Sidebar document preview: cached Blender headless GLB export plus a dependency-free WebGL viewport and scene statistics. Requires Blender 3.6 or later installed locally.
- [439436269-ctrl/dsh-preview-media](https://github.com/439436269-ctrl/dsh-preview-media) - Play video and audio in the right-Sidebar document preview, streamed over an authenticated HTTP Range route with desktop seek.
- [439436269-ctrl/dsh-preview-stl](https://github.com/439436269-ctrl/dsh-preview-stl) - Open STL meshes in the right-Sidebar document preview: browser-side binary/ASCII parsing, a WebGL viewport, and size/volume/surface-area/watertightness statistics.
- [439436269-ctrl/dsh-sticky-notes](https://github.com/439436269-ctrl/dsh-sticky-notes) - A tiled sticky-notes board for the DSH sidebar (a notes plugin): per-row density, colours, pins, search and colour filters, a trash with restore, Markdown export, and note_* tools that read and write the same notes.
- [439436269-ctrl/dsh-stl-preview](https://github.com/439436269-ctrl/dsh-stl-preview) - Opens STL meshes in the right-Sidebar document preview: binary/ASCII parsing in the browser, a WebGL viewport, and size, volume, surface-area and watertightness statistics.
- [456erddgr/dsh-lag-guard](https://github.com/456erddgr/dsh-lag-guard) - Clamps the session history page request to 120 messages and replaces request/header tool schemas larger than 200k characters with an empty array before React renders them, so long sessions open without freezing the transcript.
- [5101good/amoji#amoji](https://github.com/5101good/amoji/tree/main/adapters/dsh) - AI-native expressions for two-way chat: people see images, while text-only models read fixed meanings; includes a picker, custom expressions, import/export, and two visual styles.
- [514006234/dsh-weekly-check#dsh-weekly-panel](https://github.com/514006234/dsh-weekly-check/tree/main/packages/dsh-weekly-panel) - Sidebar panel for a weekly, human-curated Chinese ranking of the DSH ecosystem: 31 tracked projects (17 installable plugins plus tools/skills) ranked by live GitHub stars with week-over-week deltas, and a real-call availability survey of the "free" models. Sponsor slots are labelled and sit outside the ranking.
- [534119219/chicheng-peak](https://github.com/534119219/chicheng-peak) - Edge breathing border showing DeepSeek peak/valley hours, with flowing light and scheduled push reminders.
- [534119219/chicheng-quickinput](https://github.com/534119219/chicheng-quickinput) - Vault button beside the DSH composer: save keys, servers, phone numbers, addresses and URLs, auto-collected from conversations, password-protected with WebDAV backup; click to insert, Ctrl+click to send.
- [535408248-oss/dsh-douyin-webview](https://github.com/535408248-oss/dsh-douyin-webview) - An embedded Douyin window docked in the DSH sidebar: a real Electron webview guest, so douyin.com's X-Frame-Options: DENY no longer applies.
- [64511982lin-hash/dsh-swap-enter](https://github.com/64511982lin-hash/dsh-swap-enter) - Swap Enter and Shift+Enter in the chat composer: Enter inserts a line break and Shift+Enter sends. Works with no configuration; menu-open, IME composing, read-only and modifier-key presses are left to the app.
- [654970/dsh-typing-studio](https://github.com/654970/dsh-typing-studio) - Bilingual Chinese and English typing practice with timed passages, IME-aware scoring, per-character feedback, and locally stored records.
- [66-zhimeng/dsh-chart-lab](https://github.com/66-zhimeng/dsh-chart-lab) - Interactive chart cards in the conversation: the host loads a workspace dataset, and the browser card switches chart type, dimension, aggregation, filtering, Top-N and colour scheme, then exports CSV or PNG — all locally, with no model round-trips.
- [6pofx/dsh-tool-explorer](https://github.com/6pofx/dsh-tool-explorer) - Skills and MCP management console in the settings panel: merged skill catalog across every provider (hierarchical browse by source, online create/edit, independent model and user invocation switches, recoverable trash), GitHub install with multi-select batch install and update checks, MCP server add/edit/remove and enable/disable through the profile patch layer, cross-agent import from Claude Code, Cursor, Codex, Cline, Roo Code, Continue and Windsurf, and an independent SDK connection probe.
- [6shenhonghong9/dsh-startup-screen](https://github.com/6shenhonghong9/dsh-startup-screen) - Rhine Lab-style boot animation for DeepSeek Harness: six acts from a black title plate through irradiated white-out to a warm dossier panel, five access-authorization steps (the first two can wait for a click or Enter, and Esc skips), real-voice recordings for the first five lines, and a settings page that switches between warm-light and dark themes and live-edits 33 playback, identity, voice and visual options.
- [764475881/dsh-chat-width](https://github.com/764475881/dsh-chat-width) - Drag-to-resize the chat content width in the DeepSeek Harness web UI: a handle on the column right edge overrides the built-in 748px width (default 1040px, 520-2400 range, persisted per browser, double-click resets).
- [778672151/dsh-session-turn-eta](https://github.com/778672151/dsh-session-turn-eta) - Live progress bar under the composer with a percentage and an estimated time left for the current DeepSeek Harness turn; the estimate re-anchors as the turn runs and never reads 100% until the turn ends, and the bar turns red when the turn fails or is interrupted.
- [7AAAAAH/dsh-restart-app](https://github.com/7AAAAAH/dsh-restart-app) - A restart button in the window title strip, right of the sidebar toggle, that stops every process the desktop app owns (Electron shell, Host, renderers and tool subprocesses) and starts the executable again, then reloads the page once the new instance answers. Windows desktop app only, and it hides itself on any other Host.
- [dsh-my-plugins](https://github.com/7gzgn/dsh-my-plugins) - See what you actually installed: a chip shows how many plugins your profile really loads, and a searchable panel lists every one with its version, its author-written one-liner, and whether it is built-in or third-party. Read-only, zero config.
- [822376583-hub/dsh-works-gallery](https://github.com/822376583-hub/dsh-works-gallery) - A right-sidebar gallery of every image and video in your workspace, newest first, with thumbnails, type filters, search, and a lightbox.
- [9426Haruka/dsh-markdown-live](https://github.com/9426Haruka/dsh-markdown-live) - Live Markdown rendering inside the DSH composer plus Markdown-rendered user messages; the submitted text stays literal Markdown.
- [978064659/dsh-restart-button](https://github.com/978064659/dsh-restart-button) - Adds a restart button to the Windows title bar, left of the minimize control, that stops and relaunches the desktop shell together with its Harness Host so a newly installed plugin takes effect.
- [A-f-ish/dsh-emotion-feedback](https://github.com/A-f-ish/dsh-emotion-feedback) - Judges the working model's own emotion each turn and silently re-injects it before the next turn, closing a self-observation → state carry-over → behavior shift loop. Ships a host plugin plus a composer-dock panel, a read-only emotion_status tool and an optional read-only HTTP route.
- [a1073097082/dsh-model-search](https://github.com/a1073097082/dsh-model-search) - Adds searchable filtering to the model selector by provider, model name, and model ID; spaces act as wildcards.
- [a179-sanae/dsh-auto-collapse](https://github.com/a179-sanae/dsh-auto-collapse) - Codex-style workflow auto-collapse: finished turns fold into a single "processed in Xs" row leaving only the final answer; tool calls and think blocks collapse into live-summary chips that follow streaming; expand level by level, fully reversible on uninstall.
- [A2chitect/dsh-openviking-enhance](https://github.com/A2chitect/dsh-openviking-enhance) - OpenViking in the DSH web GUI: a Studio panel, a per-session memory-commit status pill with its commit timeline, and a right-sidebar tab showing what the current session retrieves.
- [a735624258/dsh-skill-picker](https://github.com/a735624258/dsh-skill-picker) - WorkBuddy-style skill picker: a button beside the composer opens a searchable list of installed skills; picking one inserts the official /skill-name gesture, so the skill loads with your message.
- [a792883583/dsh-chat-toc](https://github.com/a792883583/dsh-chat-toc) - Interactive chat table of contents with bookmarks, turn pinning, global shortcuts, and heading extraction.
- [a903067276-rgb/dsh-file-mentions](https://github.com/a903067276-rgb/dsh-file-mentions) - Clickable file paths in DSH replies: Codex-style inline open, reveal in file manager, and a mentioned-files chip list at the turn tail.
- [a903067276-rgb/dsh-hud](https://github.com/a903067276-rgb/dsh-hud) - HUD status panel: Git status, MCP servers, skills, model and token usage in a floating side panel.
- [a903067276-rgb/dsh-plan-switch](https://github.com/a903067276-rgb/dsh-plan-switch) - One-click enter/exit Plan mode for the DSH web input bar, a quick-click shortcut for /plan.
- [aa2246740/dsh-better-display](https://github.com/aa2246740/dsh-better-display) - A calmer reading view for DeepSeek Harness: native process details, live reasoning, and clean final answers.
- [aa2246740/dsh-dragndrop-attachments](https://github.com/aa2246740/dsh-dragndrop-attachments) - Adds drag-and-drop attachments for local files, folders, ZIP archives and Office documents.
- [aa2246740/dsh-watcher](https://github.com/aa2246740/dsh-watcher) - Read-only session work-path observer: fold turns into stage, run, duration and tok/s summaries, then expand parallel branches, tool results and vendor-visible reasoning.
- [aadmin61-design/dsh-selection-chat](https://github.com/aadmin61-design/dsh-selection-chat) - Adds text-selection quote cards above the composer and temporary native sidebar chats, with a Save button to keep a chat in its source workspace.
- [Aafff623/dsh-callout](https://github.com/Aafff623/dsh-callout) - GitHub-style alert cards for DSH assistant Markdown: a qualifying `> [!NOTE]`, TIP, IMPORTANT, WARNING, or CAUTION blockquote becomes a theme-aware card. Stored session content is left unchanged.
- [Aafff623/dsh-keyboard-manager](https://github.com/Aafff623/dsh-keyboard-manager) - Keyboard shortcuts for the DSH Web UI: Ctrl+Q toggles the left sidebar, Ctrl+E toggles the right panel, and Ctrl+S opens Settings. Physical key codes can be overridden in the profile patch.
- [AaronOhO/dsh-plugin-selection-comments](https://github.com/AaronOhO/dsh-plugin-selection-comments) - Selection comments on assistant output plus an independent side-chat window in the DSH Web GUI: annotate model output with floating comments, send those comments with the next message, and continue a question in a separate forked conversation that stays out of the sidebar.
- [AaronOhO/dsh-plugin-wenkai-font](https://github.com/AaronOhO/dsh-plugin-wenkai-font) - Bundled LXGW WenKai (霞鹜文楷) typeface plugin: switches the Web GUI interface and conversation text to the kai-style font with no configuration, using a system-installed copy when present and otherwise serving the packaged offline web font.
- [abbccdd/dsh-panel-tint](https://github.com/abbccdd/dsh-panel-tint) - Add independent opacity controls for the sidebar, message panels and composer in dsh web.
- [AbelKeithsun/dsh-question-nav](https://github.com/AbelKeithsun/dsh-question-nav) - In-session question navigator for the DSH Web UI: a dot minimap on the conversation edge (left or right, configurable in settings), one dot per user question — hover shows the full question text, click jumps to it.
- [acdsh4869/dsh-deepseek-web-relay](https://github.com/acdsh4869/dsh-deepseek-web-relay) - Adds a native sidebar menu item in DSH that opens chat.deepseek.com in a standalone browser window with persistent login, and supports bidirectional text selection transfer between DSH and the web page.
- [ACGNnsj/dsh-conversation-find](https://github.com/ACGNnsj/dsh-conversation-find) - Adds a Ctrl+F-style find bar to the DSH interface: highlights every match in the conversation or on the current page, and steps between them.
- [ACGsu/dsh-sidebar-panel-fold](https://github.com/ACGsu/dsh-sidebar-panel-fold) - Folds the DeepSeek Harness sidebar's global panel list into a single row, with a popover to switch panels.
- [AcidGr/dsh-web-mobile-fix](https://github.com/AcidGr/dsh-web-mobile-fix) - Mobile layout fixes for the Web UI on narrow screens: full-screen settings panel, one-row plugin nav, full-screen sidebar, centered popups, icon-only session-log button.
- [adcx222/dsh-enhance-prompt](https://github.com/adcx222/dsh-enhance-prompt) - Adds a sparkle button to the DSH composer that rewrites the current draft into a clearer, more specific prompt with a single tool-free LLM call, then writes the result back into the input.
- [addie-ace/dsh-livebench-rankings](https://github.com/addie-ace/dsh-livebench-rankings) - LiveBench leaderboard for DSH — discovers the release the official site currently publishes, then reads its CSV and category JSON, and renders model rankings in an isolated same-origin iframe opened from a button beside the composer. Search, organization / open-weight / reasoning filters, per-category sorting, task-level detail, up to four models side by side, CSV export; refreshes every 60 s by default (configurable 30–3600) and caches only to the plugin's own .cache/rankings.json.
- [AdilAlmahmoud/dsh-rtl-chat](https://github.com/AdilAlmahmoud/dsh-rtl-chat) - Right-to-left reading mode for the conversation answer area, with a button to switch back to left-to-right.
- [adithyanraj03/dsh-verbose](https://github.com/adithyanraj03/dsh-verbose) - A session-header 3-dots menu: download the session log, switch each chat's transcript between Normal and Verbose, and define what Verbose expands (thinking blocks, tool calls, terminal output).
- [Admilkk/dsh-sidechat](https://github.com/Admilkk/dsh-sidechat) - Temporary side conversations seeded from the main context, with quoted questions, main-task status updates, and replies returned to the main draft.
- [adrian-atlas/dsh-avatar-balance](https://github.com/adrian-atlas/dsh-avatar-balance) - DeepSeek account balance in the sidebar foot, directly above the account avatar. The row shows the top-up balance plus any bonus, turns amber with a warning mark below an adjustable threshold and green above it, and refreshes when a task finishes.
- [adrianleb/dsh-tmux-cc](https://github.com/adrianleb/dsh-tmux-cc) - Persistent tmux control-mode cockpit for DSH Web that mirrors native panes in a dock.
- [advance-lion/dsh-brand](https://github.com/advance-lion/dsh-brand) - One-click brand replacement for the DSH web GUI — logo, product name, version badge, hero headline, tab title and favicon.
- [Aealen/dsh-coding-workspace](https://github.com/Aealen/dsh-coding-workspace) - Coding workbench for DSH: docked workspace panels, a grouped project sidebar, split editor tab groups with hibernate/resume, file preview and diff views — built on parallel git worktree development with cross-session collaboration primitives.
- [Aeroscis/dsh-workspace-manager](https://github.com/Aeroscis/dsh-workspace-manager) - Sorts the DSH sidebar workspace list: the workspace you last sent a prompt in rises above the first non-running one, with pin, drag-to-reorder, name filtering and one-click tidy; also removes empty workspaces and relocates a workspace folder to a new path while carrying its conversations over.
- [afawfawffaf/dsh-plugin-lx-music](https://github.com/afawfawffaf/dsh-plugin-lx-music) - Shows the lyrics LX Music is playing in a floating panel on the right of the DSH conversation page, with previous / play-pause / next controls. Scroll the lyric window to read ahead, double-click a line to start the song there, and if the player is not running, pressing play starts it in the system tray. Windows only, and LX Music has to be installed with its local Open API enabled.
- [afoxsss/dsh-conversation-map](https://github.com/afoxsss/dsh-conversation-map) - Conversation minimap docked on the right edge of the dsh Web chat: colour-block and to-scale thumbnail modes, drag-to-resize (10–320px), hover loupe preview, click-to-jump and drag-to-scroll with a live viewport indicator, and collapse to a 4px strip.
- [ags-neto/dsh-status-telemetry](https://github.com/ags-neto/dsh-status-telemetry) - Adds a session status pill to the composer dock that expands into a panel of turn, token, performance and context readings.
- [AhaCrazy/dsh-nav-history](https://github.com/AhaCrazy/dsh-nav-history) - Global back/forward navigation for the DeepSeek Harness Web UI: remembers every session and whole-page panel you visit, shows a configurable 1-7 entry history strip you can click, and supports browser-style shortcuts plus mouse side buttons.
- [AhaCrazy/dsh-stock-quotes](https://github.com/AhaCrazy/dsh-stock-quotes) - Watchlist panel for A-share, Hong Kong and US quotes plus Eastmoney boards, with intraday sparklines and an agent stock_quote tool.
- [Ai-design-001/-DSH_mod-](https://github.com/Ai-design-001/-DSH_mod-) - Adds a delete entry to every session row in the dsh sidebar, including the blank New Session row, plus a settings section for font sizes and per-region colors. The entry archives the session (recoverable from Archive) rather than erasing it.
- [ai-eks/dsh-docking-layout](https://github.com/ai-eks/dsh-docking-layout) - Dockable DSH Web UI layout that organizes conversations as tabs across draggable split groups.
- [Aicksion/dsh-clean-desktop-shell-pro](https://github.com/Aicksion/dsh-clean-desktop-shell-pro) - Enhanced desktop shell for DSH: auto-starts the local backend on launch (no browser tab), asks before closing the backend on quit, and starts the backend in parallel with Electron for faster cold start. Fork of dsh-clean-desktop-shell.
- [Aik358/dsh-anchored-monitor](https://github.com/Aik358/dsh-anchored-monitor) - A whip for DeepSeek V4 Pro: it watches the reasoning fingerprint of every thinking block and pulls the model back when it slips from focused "We will / I will" mode into scattered "let me" mode.
- [AikawaNaya/dsh-agents-md-editor](https://github.com/AikawaNaya/dsh-agents-md-editor) - Left-sidebar panel that views, edits and saves ~/.dsh/AGENTS.md live, with version-guarded saves and atomic writes.
- [AikenFra/dsh-alive](https://github.com/AikenFra/dsh-alive) - Zero-token online status indicator for the DeepSeek Harness web UI: an always-visible online/offline dot in the conversation header, auto-refreshed every 15 seconds with no LLM calls.
- [AIMentalModel/prompt-manager](https://github.com/AIMentalModel/prompt-manager) - Prompt manager for DSH: imports prompts from JSON or Markdown sources and DSH's own system sections, then controls what is injected into the system prompt — per prompt, or per agent preset — with title editing, drag ordering, LLM translation and the option to inject either the original text or its translation.
- [AirPineApplea/feiyu-chat-companion](https://github.com/AirPineApplea/feiyu-chat-companion) - Desktop companion for DeepSeek Harness: a Win32 layered window that keeps a whale-girl above other applications, speaks through a bubble, comments on its own when you stay on one window for ten seconds, and answers a single question typed into a right-click input box.
- [Aisland-SJL/dsh-worktable#01_content](https://github.com/Aisland-SJL/dsh-worktable/tree/main/01_content) - Workbench plugin for the DeepSeek Harness web UI: collects your own projects and mounted plugin entries with rename, icon, sort, show/hide, project-to-session binding and a per-project file cabinet, adds a split-pane workspace engine with draggable panes, tabbed windows and built-in Explorer, terminal, browser, animation and custom panes, and auto-mounts an agent output into the matching window when it writes widget-result.json.
- [aiyacharley/dsh-at-sider](https://github.com/aiyacharley/dsh-at-sider) - Enhances the native right-sidebar Files tab rather than replacing it: each row gets an @ reference button that inserts the canonical path mention into the composer, plus a size column, a sortable modification-time column, and a status dot for untracked, unstaged and staged files. Below the tree a git bar shows the branch with ahead/behind counts and expands upward into the 20 most recent commits, with a repository selector when a workspace has independent repositories in subdirectories. A header search box walks the workspace recursively and can open a result or expand the tree at it, preview tabs offer reveal-in-tree and one-click @, the tree is keyboard navigable with ARIA tree semantics, and a right-click entry on the Files tab switches back to the native tree at runtime.
- [AK-blank/dsh-plugin-offpeak-badge](https://github.com/AK-blank/dsh-plugin-offpeak-badge) - Shows whether the DeepSeek API is currently billing at the off-peak or peak rate in the sidebar brand row, with the Chinese statutory-holiday calendar built in and zh/en copy.
- [akilonewbility/dsh-workspace-optimize](https://github.com/akilonewbility/dsh-workspace-optimize) - Redraw the left-hand workspace sidebar to add color highlighting, sorting, status indicators, and system notifications. You can also switch back to the original sidebar, which likewise implements some of these features.
- [AkiNoYo-desu/dsh-effort-slider-universal](https://github.com/AkiNoYo-desu/dsh-effort-slider-universal) - Reasoning-effort slider in the model menu for DeepSeek Harness that also appears for providers and models with no declared effort metadata. Based on Microqian2th/dsh-codex-effort-slider — uninstall that upstream plugin before installing this one.
- [AKIRACOD/dsh-drag-and-drop](https://github.com/AKIRACOD/dsh-drag-and-drop) - File-drag fork: drop documents as removable chips above the composer, send without typing.
- [aklnaaw/dsh-xiaoke-widget](https://github.com/aklnaaw/dsh-xiaoke-widget) - A Claude-persona desktop pet pinned to the bottom-right corner of the DSH web GUI — shows balance, today's usage, peak/off-peak pricing and per-turn cost, with editable bubbles (text, balance, usage, pricing, images, random lines and weighted choices), per-line styles and fonts, a floating quick editor, sound effects, and custom character/audio. Ships animated roles that breathe and blink, plus a balance-alert variant that switches to a crying face when the balance falls below the alert line; 256/384 animation tiers and an optional 512 HD pack installed from the resource manager. 63 Claude-flavored lines. A derivative of MeteorNOX/DeepSeek-Balance-Whale-Widget (MIT).
- [AKS1st/dock](https://github.com/AKS1st/dock) - VSCode-style workbench shell for the DSH Web UI: activity bar, side bar, editor area, panel and status bar, with a registry service (ctx.workbench) for mounting panels, views and commands.
- [AKS1st/dock-editor](https://github.com/AKS1st/dock-editor) - Text viewer and editor for the dock workbench: undo/redo, Ctrl+S save, unsaved-change confirmation and binary detection, registered as the default file viewer.
- [AKS1st/dock-files](https://github.com/AKS1st/dock-files) - File explorer for the dock workbench: tree browsing of the session workspace with create/rename/copy-paste/delete, drag-and-drop import and paste of local files and clipboard images.
- [AKS1st/dock-git](https://github.com/AKS1st/dock-git) - Git history visualization for the dock workbench: swimlane commit graph, branch and tag management, stage/commit/push and remote operations.
- [AKS1st/dock-images](https://github.com/AKS1st/dock-images) - Image viewer for the dock workbench: PNG, JPEG, GIF, WebP, BMP, SVG, ICO and AVIF, with SVG rendered as image data only.
- [AKS1st/dock-markdown](https://github.com/AKS1st/dock-markdown) - Markdown viewer for the dock workbench: GFM rendering with DOMPurify sanitization, document outline and relative-path image/link resolution, with one-click switch to editing.
- [AKS1st/dock-spreadsheet](https://github.com/AKS1st/dock-spreadsheet) - Read-only XLSX, XLS, ODS, CSV and TSV viewer for dock-files with worksheet tabs, sorting, filters, search and cell links.
- [AKS1st/dsh-sysmon](https://github.com/AKS1st/dsh-sysmon) - System status floating widget: live CPU/memory/disk usage in the bottom-right corner of the DSH Web UI with threshold colour warnings.
- [AL-Yichen/dsh-fullscreen-input](https://github.com/AL-Yichen/dsh-fullscreen-input) - Adds a full-screen panel to the DSH composer for writing multi-line text, movable and resizable, sharing one draft with the composer; Enter inserts a newline and only Ctrl+Enter sends, so an IME cannot turn Shift+Enter into a send.
- [AL1ghtm3ter/dsh-right-click-menu](https://github.com/AL1ghtm3ter/dsh-right-click-menu) - Adds a right-click menu to the DSH web UI for files and for links: a file-tree row, a file path or file mention in a chat message, or a hyperlink opens a menu that previews a file in DSH, opens it in the default application or a chosen associated one, reveals it in the file manager, copies the path, the workspace-relative path, the file name, the link address, the link text or the file contents, saves a copy elsewhere, and opens an HTTP(S) link in the sidebar browser or in the system browser, while a right-click that is not on a file or a link keeps the browser's own menu.
- [AlannerFranklin/dsh-cutout-pet](https://github.com/AlannerFranklin/dsh-cutout-pet) - Turns an uploaded picture into a floating desk pet by cutting out its background in the page, with idle animation and a live account-balance bubble.
- [alccs/dsh-visual-intelligence-engine](https://github.com/alccs/dsh-visual-intelligence-engine/tree/main/package) - Render interactive diagrams, charts and staged learning visuals alongside native prose in DeepSeek Harness.
- [AlexPeng07/dsh-custom-plugin](https://github.com/AlexPeng07/dsh-custom-plugin) - Web GUI convenience suite with appearance controls, timeline navigation, project folders, prompts, conversation export, Mermaid rendering, quote reply, and DeepSeek balance and daily token usage.
- [AlexYin-Tongji/dsh-ui-enhancer](https://github.com/AlexYin-Tongji/dsh-ui-enhancer) - Desktop-style UI enhancements for the DSH Web client: adaptive wallpapers, a desktop pet companion with an activity feed and feeding economy, @file workspace references, and the resizable file/preview right panel.
- [Aliboder/dsh-prompt-forge](https://github.com/Aliboder/dsh-prompt-forge) - Composer prompt refinement: rewrite the draft through the harness LLM, preview the result, then apply or send it, with style presets, model-aware reasoning effort, cost estimate and a settings page.
- [AliceLJY/dsh-thumb](https://github.com/AliceLJY/dsh-thumb) - A phone shell for the web GUI - the sidebar becomes an overlay drawer that closes when a session is tapped, the settings sheet goes full-screen single column, and the transcript is retuned for phone reading.
- [alingalingling/ui-status-label](https://github.com/alingalingling/ui-status-label) - Customize the "deep diving" thinking status label to anything you like.
- [Aliuyanfeng/dsh-soul](https://github.com/Aliuyanfeng/dsh-soul) - Personalization for the agent: a Web UI panel (about-you, reply style & tone, traits for headings/lists and emoji, output language, custom instructions) and `/soul` commands, with nickname/occupation/bio/style/traits/language compiled into a system prompt and re-injected into active sessions. Exposes `soulConfig` for other plugins and a `set_persona` tool the agent can call.
- [ALIve114514awa/dsh-prompt-optimizer](https://github.com/ALIve114514awa/dsh-prompt-optimizer) - Refine prompts with guided questions and editable previews using Harness AI or native Codex across Web, desktop and TUI, applying drafts only after user confirmation.
- [All3nCN/dsh-better-sidebar-N23](https://github.com/All3nCN/dsh-better-sidebar-N23) - Full workbench fork of dsh-better-sidebar: explorer, editor, terminal, Git, browser and split panes, plus a VSCode-like shell refactor with a full-width compact header and activity rail; publishes as @all3cn/dsh-better-sidebar-n23 on npm.
- [AlLHHH/dsh-perf-boost](https://github.com/AlLHHH/dsh-perf-boost) - Performance optimization and smoothness enhancement for DSH Desktop (renderer-side only; host code untouched, no on-disk config): skips offscreen message rendering, stops frames and timers while the window is hidden, lazily loads media, coalesces session-list repaints and pre-warms large session files; shows the measured main-thread saving in settings. A lightweight speed-up for lag, dropped frames, slow large-session opening and background CPU use.
- [Alone-show/dsh-quick-keys](https://github.com/Alone-show/dsh-quick-keys) - Two composer actions for the DSH Web UI: /new in the slash-command menu starts a new conversation without sending anything to the model, and Shift+Tab toggles plan mode while the conversation has focus.
- [AloneFu/dsh-calendar-plugin](https://github.com/AloneFu/dsh-calendar-plugin) - Obsidian daily notes, tasks and Day Planner time blocks in the DSH sidebar: a Monday-first month grid with per-day word-count and task dots, a compact timeline for HH:mm time blocks, the selected day's diary excerpt and task list, and write-back of checkbox state, task text and times to the Markdown files; a first-run wizard detects the vault or accepts a path.
- [AlseFum/dsh-pluginlet](https://github.com/AlseFum/dsh-pluginlet) - Turns every .pluginlet file under a repo's .dsh/pluginlets/ (directories configurable) into its own sandboxed Conversation View tab, gated by a capability grant bound to the file content hash.
- [altamkp/dsh-middle-click](https://github.com/altamkp/dsh-middle-click) - Middle-click closes a session or a sidebar tab in the DeepSeek Harness Web UI. A session closes through the row's own archive action, so the confirmation for running work and the undo notice stay exactly as the Harness renders them; a tab closes through its own close control, leaving the rule that a lone guide tab cannot be closed untouched. The gesture settles on press-and-release on the same element, so a middle drag never closes anything. Two switches on the plugin's own Settings tab turn each half off, remembered per browser.
- [Altermoe/dsh-model-logos](https://github.com/Altermoe/dsh-model-logos) - Model brand marks in the composer model selector; vendor and model marks across Settings → Models (provider rows, model rows, the add-provider dropdown); and a per provider/model icon picker that stores overrides in the DSH user settings document.
- [altmanWang/dsh_plugin_btc_dashboard](https://github.com/altmanWang/dsh_plugin_btc_dashboard) - DeepSeek Harness plugin adding an OKX perpetual-futures price dashboard: BTC/ETH candlesticks at 1m/3m/15m/1h/4h/1D, EMA overlays, line/Fibonacci/ruler drawing tools, close-line alerts through desktop notification, sound or email (email delivery additionally needs the dsh-qq-email plugin), and an in-panel backtester. Market data comes from OKX public endpoints and no API key is required.
- [AlyceStocking/hover-glossary](https://github.com/AlyceStocking/hover-glossary) - Shows local glossary definitions when hovering over terms in chat messages.
- [amazing-fish/dsh-piano-nav](https://github.com/amazing-fish/dsh-piano-nav) - Conversation navigation with full-history turns, collapsible process steps, final-result and structured question-and-answer keys, and pointer-following piano interaction.
- [Amengclass/dsh-settings-hub](https://github.com/Amengclass/dsh-settings-hub) - Unify third-party plugin settings in DeepSeek Harness. Auto-discover, group, and reorder plugin settings under one collapsible nav entry with drag-and-drop support.
- [amstlkqp114514/dsh-desktop-tweaks](https://github.com/amstlkqp114514/dsh-desktop-tweaks) - Desktop tweaks: F11 fullscreen toggle; enables Ctrl+C / Ctrl+V copy and paste in the terminal, both switchable in the General settings.
- [an-an-618/dsh-hook-followup](https://github.com/an-an-618/dsh-hook-followup) - Adds a lightweight follow-up row under each DSH answer: one anchor question plus 1-3 user-voice quick replies, so the next turn is one click away; supports always / balanced / conservative strategies and optional refusal or emotional-context skips.
