- [an-an-618/dsh-intelligent-ui](https://github.com/an-an-618/dsh-intelligent-ui) - Declarative interactive cards for DSH: the model writes a spec, the Host compiles it into a JSON program, and the client renders forms, tables, charts and lists with local state and explicit submit.
- [Analoty/dsh-auto-click](https://github.com/Analoty/dsh-auto-click) - Mouse gestures for files in the DSH Web GUI: Ctrl+click a file-tree row, a file mention in a chat message or a file link to open it with the desktop default application; right-click opens a menu with open, the operating system own Open With chooser, save a copy, copy the absolute path, copy the file contents and reveal in the file manager, while a right-click on an ordinary hyperlink offers Copy Link. A plain click is left to DSH own sidebar preview and directory rows are left to the page. Open and reveal go through DSH own sessionController, the Open With chooser is the operating system dialog (Windows Shell verb, open -a on macOS) rather than an application list kept by the plugin, and file reads stop at 8 MiB.
- [Ananpng123/dsh-disk-manager](https://github.com/Ananpng123/dsh-disk-manager) - Windows disk manager for the DeepSeek Harness right sidebar: volume overview with usage bars, a session-grouped list of the documents each conversation produced, drag files straight into the chat composer, multi-select with batch delete to Recycle Bin, an expandable folder tree with real app icons, workspace documents no session touched, on-demand folder sizes, and an explorer-style context menu. Delete goes to the recycle bin.
- [Andor-Z/dsh-turn-outline](https://github.com/Andor-Z/dsh-turn-outline) - Adds a sidebar tab that folds a session into user turns (input, tool steps, output) with one-click jump back into the conversation.
- [androidly/dsh-restart-control](https://github.com/androidly/dsh-restart-control) - Adds a restart control at the DSH Web sidebar foot: the host exits through its own graceful path and the detected supervisor (systemd, Kubernetes or Docker) boots the replacement, or the plugin starts one directly when it finds no supervisor; a frame-wide notice polls the host pid until a new process answers.
- [AngleNaris/fanframe](https://github.com/AngleNaris/fanframe) - FanFrame (繁界, “Fanxing’s realm”) lightly polishes the DeepSeek Harness Desktop UI for a more comfortable everyday experience.
- [angls4/dsh-window-drag](https://github.com/angls4/dsh-window-drag) - Restores title-bar window dragging and double-click zoom in DeepSeek Harness Desktop on macOS.
- [anitman/dsh-plugin-video-player](https://github.com/anitman/dsh-plugin-video-player) - TikTok-style video feed in a floating sidebar window: swipe local or SMB-NAS folders, or paste online video URLs (yt-dlp, bilibili/YouTube, cookies.txt login state); the window auto-fits the video aspect ratio, with fullscreen, drag-resize, Range streaming and an agent video-queue endpoint.
- [AnJiaqi0926/dsh-plugin-switchboard](https://github.com/AnJiaqi0926/dsh-plugin-switchboard) - Toggles installed plugins from a sidebar panel, groups them by origin (official, marketplace, local), flags changes that need a Host restart, and installs curated plugins from a Discover tab.
- [ankesu/dsh-live2d-pet](https://github.com/ankesu/dsh-live2d-pet) - Live2D companion for DSH: a floating Cubism model with state-driven expressions and mouse tracking; config-driven geometry (size/position from the plugin config); official Haru sample bundled (© Live2D Inc.).
- [Anna-la/dsh-simplify](https://github.com/Anna-la/dsh-simplify) - Clean-mode for the Web GUI: hover any element and right-click to remove it; removals survive reloads and are restorable from the settings page.
- [anneheartrecord/dsh-desk-pet](https://github.com/anneheartrecord/dsh-desk-pet) - macOS desk pet that lives outside the DSH page: a real always-on-top window that clears fullscreen Spaces, six states driven by local DSH, a native right-click menu with quiet mode and a session list, and a bundled skill that turns one photo into a full eighteen-pose skin on your own image tool and credentials; system Python and ctypes, no Electron and no dependencies.
- [anonamerjaxon-dev/dsh-model-grid](https://github.com/anonamerjaxon-dev/dsh-model-grid) - Responsive card grid for the DeepSeek Harness composer model picker, grouped by provider. Host-only: injects a stylesheet through webserver/index-inject without rebuilding the client bundle, and supports both the stock picker and the dsh-peakrate menu.
- [antnesswcm/dsh-shell-card-plus](https://github.com/antnesswcm/dsh-shell-card-plus) - Replaces the stock bash/pwsh terminal rows in the DSH web UI with shell command cards: long commands soft-wrap with line numbers instead of truncating, plus separate copy-command and copy-output buttons.
- [anye-99/dsh-next-step-suggest](https://github.com/anye-99/dsh-next-step-suggest) - Next-step suggestions for the DSH composer: after each completed turn, one auxiliary call on the session's current model returns 3 clickable follow-up suggestions above the input box, and clicking one fills the draft.
- [anzhaohao/DragView](https://github.com/anzhaohao/DragView) - Drag-and-drop file attachments as Codex-style cards, with in-app PDF/text/video/audio previews and system-default opening for other formats.
- [AomiRaku/dsh-accountDisplayName-edit](https://github.com/AomiRaku/dsh-accountDisplayName-edit) - Customize the username (phone number or email) and avatar shown at the bottom-left account row — for personalization, or to keep screenshots from exposing them. The editor opens from the menu you get by clicking that row.
- [apanzinc/dsh-no-preview-badge](https://github.com/apanzinc/dsh-no-preview-badge) - Removes the Preview badge on DSH.
- [AppliedYuu/dsh-WallpaperAndCost](https://github.com/AppliedYuu/dsh-WallpaperAndCost) - Merged plugin: wallpaper customization (original-resolution uploads, crop editor, per-area opacity, MP4/WebM dynamic wallpapers) + Steam Workshop wallpaper extraction (parses scene.pkg/.tex via a bundled Python script, preview/download) + DeepSeek balance & usage widget + OpenCode Go plan usage (rolling/weekly/monthly percents in a top header pill; expand for progress bars & reset countdowns). API keys are read from DSH's credential store by the host — never stored in source or the browser.
- [appthin/dsh-mcp-manager-plus](https://github.com/appthin/dsh-mcp-manager-plus) - MCP management page in DSH Settings: list, enable/disable, restart and delete MCP servers, edit them in form or JSON mode, expand a server to see the tools it registers, and paste-import MCP configs from 14 tools (Claude Code, Cursor, Codex, VS Code and more) in one go.
- [AprilCrystal/dsh-client-ui-font-scale](https://github.com/AprilCrystal/dsh-client-ui-font-scale) - Conversation font-size controls for the Web UI: a stepper in the composer row, keyboard shortcuts, and Ctrl+wheel zoom, all writing through the theme service setFontSize.
- [AprilCrystal/dsh-client-ui-gpt-helper](https://github.com/AprilCrystal/dsh-client-ui-gpt-helper) - ChatGPT-desktop-style model picker whose thinking-level slider carries a particle trail, plus a visual-only fast-mode toggle and a hold-to-lock for it.
- [Arc-huangjingtong/dsh-restart](https://github.com/Arc-huangjingtong/dsh-restart) - Adds a restart button to the macOS DSH desktop sidebar that fully quits and reopens the app; on other platforms the button hides itself.
- [ArcaneOrion/dsh-model-selector-search](https://github.com/ArcaneOrion/dsh-model-selector-search) - Session model selector with search: two-stage loose matching (normalised substring first, subsequence as fallback, so glm53 finds GLM-5.3 and ds finds DeepSeek), providers with recent successful calls pinned first, and a reasoning-effort pane that remembers each model's last explicitly chosen effort.
- [ArcaneOrion/dsh-teaching-board](https://github.com/ArcaneOrion/dsh-teaching-board) - Teaching board view: agent-generated self-contained HTML is projected into a sandboxed iframe as a board, annotated by hand (brush, four colours, eraser, undo), extended in place across several tool calls, and screenshotted back into the conversation as a real user message.
- [Archaofan/dsh-source-deck](https://github.com/Archaofan/dsh-source-deck) - In-conversation source deck for DSH Desktop (supports DSH 0.2.2-rc.1): the current chat model runs LLM search rounds, candidates render as GitHub-aware cards, and keep/exclude feedback, favorites, and the selected sources stay in the same conversation.
- [arcnapan/dsh-chat-answer-anchor](https://github.com/arcnapan/dsh-chat-answer-anchor) - Freezes the DSH chat scroll position: new replies no longer drag the viewport to the bottom — it stays where you left it. Only after you send a message does it follow to the bottom so you can see your own input, then it locks again once the answer starts streaming. Each session remembers its own position (persisted across reloads), a settings toggle turns it off, and it never intercepts your own scrolling.
- [ardesp0630/dsh-work-progress](https://github.com/ardesp0630/dsh-work-progress) - Persistent work-progress row in the band below the composer: task completion, a progress bar, and what the agent is doing right now (generating a reply or running a tool), expandable into the full task list with turns, steps, timings and context usage.
- [Arfrsa/dsh-sidebar-sections](https://github.com/Arfrsa/dsh-sidebar-sections) - Split the DSH sidebar workspace list into Active, Session tasks and Inactive sections with manual pins and an activity threshold, keeping the official search, archive filters, row menus and drag and drop, with a one-click switch back to the shipped list. The Session tasks section uses a fixed Windows workspace at D:\files\history-tasks; other platforms must create that directory manually.
- [Ariakage/dsh-claude-effort-slider](https://github.com/Ariakage/dsh-claude-effort-slider) - Replaces the composer Effort switch with a Claude Desktop-style five-level slider (Low/Medium/High/Max/Ultracode): a pill opens a popover above the composer, dragging snaps with a spring and writes once, and Ultracode maps to the model's maximum reasoning effort. Ships the Ultracode WebGL2 energy grid, a per-session Ultracode mode, and passes modifier-click through to the shipped level list.
- [ArimaKana-Akane/dsh-whale-tools](https://github.com/ArimaKana-Akane/dsh-whale-tools) - Replace the dsh-whale-widget mascot with your own image through a built-in cutout pipeline, plus a session-header restart button, a browser heartbeat, and an opt-in patch self-heal that is off by default.
- [Arrosam/dsh-better-workspace-file-manager](https://github.com/Arrosam/dsh-better-workspace-file-manager) - Adds upload, download, new folder, delete, drag-to-move, drag-to-chat and in-place text editing with auto-save to the workspace Files tab and file previews.
- [Arrosam/dsh-smart-prompt](https://github.com/Arrosam/dsh-smart-prompt) - One-click prompt refinement for the DSH composer: the ✦ button next to Send rewrites the draft into an agent-ready prompt in Standard or Light mode, with Revert and Retry; asks follow-up questions through the question card when the draft is missing information; checks that paths, identifiers, numbers, URLs, inline code and @mentions survive the rewrite and adds back any that were dropped; runs on the conversation model or a dedicated model chosen on the Plugins page.
- [Arvin-work/dsh-plugin-file-annotate](https://github.com/Arvin-work/dsh-plugin-file-annotate) - Annotatable file editor for the DSH right sidebar with a lazy file tree, inline annotations, cross-file annotation navigation, Markdown preview, and composer-draft handoff.
- [asd13006/dsh-multi-lang-ui](https://github.com/asd13006/dsh-multi-lang-ui) - Add six languages (繁體中文, 日本語, 한국어, Français, Deutsch, Español) to the DeepSeek Harness Web UI language options, with hand-polished per-language translations and automatic fallbacks (Simplified-to-Traditional conversion for zh-TW, English for the others) covering upstream updates and third-party plugins.
- [asfre/dsh-mcp-global-manager](https://github.com/asfre/dsh-mcp-global-manager) - Scans MCP configs from the tools it knows (28 built-in sources plus custom ones), syncs diffs into DSH, and lets you import, write back, or edit MCP servers from the DSH sidebar.
- [ashz555/dsh-term-glossary](https://github.com/ashz555/dsh-term-glossary) - Select a term in a DeepSeek Harness conversation and press the Search button beside it to read the explanation in place: basic meaning, purpose and usage, with the token cost of that answer. The call is auxiliary — it writes nothing to the session log.
- [Asterlyra/dsh-panel-organizer](https://github.com/Asterlyra/dsh-panel-organizer) - Hides unused panels in the left rail grouped by origin, and turns the rail More button into an expand/collapse toggle that lays out every panel.
- [Asteroid-Code-Channel/dsh-workspace-atlas](https://github.com/Asteroid-Code-Channel/dsh-workspace-atlas) - AGENTS & Skills Studio: edit AGENTS.md, create spec-compliant SKILL.md files with structured frontmatter, manage and rename skill folders, and install from Anthropic, OpenAI, Cursor, and Matt Pocock catalogs.
- [asukasec/dsh-message-preview](https://github.com/asukasec/dsh-message-preview) - Right-edge user-message navigator with an adaptive block layout that fits the available height, plus hover previews, keyboard controls, and click-to-jump navigation.
- [asun-labs/dsh-plugin-agent-switch](https://github.com/asun-labs/dsh-plugin-agent-switch) - Adds interactive agent-switch CLI terminals with tab and grid layouts, Codex delegation cards, and live or saved task output in the right sidebar. Requires agent-switch on PATH, at least one target coding CLI, and a node-pty build allowlist; implemented and tested on Windows.
- [asxiuxiu/dsh-quick-open](https://github.com/asxiuxiu/dsh-quick-open) - Quick-open palette for the Web GUI: Ctrl+P fuzzy-searches an in-memory index of the session workspace (about 51k entries, median query 5 ms) with per-segment path anchoring, dir: and file: prefixes, Tab directory drilling and :line jumps, opens the picked file in the native right sidebar, and drops a file reference into the conversation draft with Ctrl+Enter. Also adds in-file find (Ctrl+F) and a selection-to-reference action to the built-in file preview.
- [Asza-other/dsh-narrator](https://github.com/Asza-other/dsh-narrator) - Watches a turn for long silences and, once the threshold passes, injects a user message asking the model to state in prose what it is doing before carrying on, so the turn keeps going. The same text is mirrored in a lower-left overlay above the composer. The silence threshold and the minimum gap between reports are set in minutes, the prompt can be replaced wholesale, and it runs with no runtime dependencies. Requires DSH 0.2.1+ and Node.js 18+.
- [atrestandbed-bot/dsh-agents-viewer](https://github.com/atrestandbed-bot/dsh-agents-viewer) - View and edit the AGENTS.md / CLAUDE.md instruction chain in a native right-sidebar tab — global, shared, and every project directory down to the session working directory — with in-place save that creates missing files, a chat-header toggle, and an /agents command that works without the panel.
- [Audrey-cn/dsh-bot-codex-ui](https://github.com/Audrey-cn/dsh-bot-codex-ui) - Unified install for DS Bot and the Codex-style sidebar: one package that mounts both and fixes their layout conflicts (composer alignment, sidebar footer, account entry, conversation width).
- [aug-245/dsh-plugin-market-entry](https://github.com/aug-245/dsh-plugin-market-entry) - Adds a plugin market entry button to the left sidebar that opens the installed market in a main panel.
- [AumiSatori/dsh-ui-beautify](https://github.com/AumiSatori/dsh-ui-beautify) - Personalize DSH with glass surfaces, independent light and dark themes, image/video/HTML backgrounds, particles, custom greetings, overlay sidebars, task and Artifact cards, message bubbles, smooth answer streaming, and settings import/export.
- [AvenYQ/dsh-clipforge](https://github.com/AvenYQ/dsh-clipforge) - Video workbench for DeepSeek Harness that parses a link, downloads the source, transcribes it, trims or merges clips and exports GIFs. Requires ffmpeg/ffprobe and yt-dlp; whisper.cpp plus a model is optional for videos without subtitles.
- [AvenYQ/dsh-quiet](https://github.com/AvenYQ/dsh-quiet) - A grey ASCII ocean behind the whole window: whales swim continuously across the sidebar and the chat area, so a creature can drift from one column into the other. Once a conversation starts, every creature leaves through its own edge — up, down, right, or left — while the sea calms, and the sidebar keeps one frosted surface whose height follows its content, so a passing whale becomes a soft shadow behind the labels; an empty conversation brings them back in from the edges. Streaming replies reveal from blur to clear, and reduced motion falls back to a still frame and the native renderer. Neutral ink only, with day and night palettes.
- [AvenYQ/dsh-rigui](https://github.com/AvenYQ/dsh-rigui) - A personal life workbench as a sidebar panel: sleep, meals, workouts, spending, weight, schedule and to-dos share one page of cards that react to one another, and one sentence typed at the top is turned into structured records by the model configured in DSH. Records and card layout stay in local browser storage.
- [AvigatorC/dsh-conversation-width](https://github.com/AvigatorC/dsh-conversation-width) - Pins the conversation column of the dsh UI to a fixed width — a pixel value or a percentage of the column — from a row in General settings, and hides the built-in drag handles so the width stays put.
- [awaYFawa/dsh-plugins-folders](https://github.com/awaYFawa/dsh-plugins-folders) - Folders for the DSH Plugins page: group installed plugins, toggle a whole group, batch move with undo, search across groups, long-press drag to reorder, reinstall removed plugins from their record, and restart Harness with one click.
- [AWL-MC2/DSH-Plugin_awl](https://github.com/AWL-MC2/DSH-Plugin_awl) - Wallpaper-aware readability repair for the DSH Web GUI: it lifts the three low-contrast secondary text tokens with color-mix plus an adjustable text halo, adds content rules (bold, permanently underlined or recoloured links, tinted headings and body text) scoped to conversation Markdown only, offers adjustable rainbow-edge and gradient-fill heading effects, loads custom fonts from the plugin folder or the DSH home, and ships a draggable WYSIWYG preview with a WCAG contrast badge.
- [Awu12277/dsh-sleep-send](https://github.com/Awu12277/dsh-sleep-send) - Schedule-send button and panel to the right of the composer, with smart time slots, a custom date/time picker, several queued sends at once, and localStorage persistence that restores pending tasks after a page reload.
- [ayachinene285/dsh-composer-resize](https://github.com/ayachinene285/dsh-composer-resize) - Adds a drag handle above the chat composer so its height can be set by hand at any content length, with double-click to restore auto-grow capped at 168px.
- [ayahunter/dsh-trail#bundle](https://github.com/ayahunter/dsh-trail/tree/main/packages/bundle) - Replaces the Web GUI trajectory tab with a new-user-friendly round-by-round storyline, plain-language tool labels, category filters and fuzzy search.
- [ayingQAQ/dsh-web-launcher](https://github.com/ayingQAQ/dsh-web-launcher) - Windows desktop launcher bundle for DSH Web that creates a managed shortcut, then restarts DSH in the background and refreshes the existing browser page.
- [ayuanxx-max/dsh-session-accordion](https://github.com/ayuanxx-max/dsh-session-accordion) - Chat turn accordion: every interaction folds into one row with a time pill and question summary, only the newest turn stays open, and switching sessions or starting a new turn resets the open state; the execution process opens only on its own click.
- [AZ-666316/dsh-prompt-optimizer-button](https://github.com/AZ-666316/dsh-prompt-optimizer-button) - Prompt optimizer inside the DSH composer. One click rewrites the current draft with the model you already selected: it classifies the scenario (paper / agent task / daily question / English writing), returns a compact three-check diagnosis panel (is acceptance testable? missing information or capability? raising depth or widening scope?), and lets you pin a scenario manually. The draft is replaced in place, with no extra chat turn.
- [azazo1/dsh-deep-diving-back](https://github.com/azazo1/dsh-deep-diving-back) - Restores the blue shimmering Deep diving turn status row that DSH 0.1.6 and earlier showed at the end of the transcript.
- [azazo1/dsh-reduce-effects](https://github.com/azazo1/dsh-reduce-effects) - Adds a UI effects page to Settings that switches off DSH Web animations and transitions, loading and progress animations, backdrop blur, smooth scrolling, hover title scroll, shadows, gradients and JS motion one category at a time, to cut browser rendering cost.
- [Azetus/dsh-quorfloat](https://github.com/Azetus/dsh-quorfloat) - System-level desktop floating-window plugin for DeepSeek Harness (DSH): a global hotkey opens a floating window; the plugin supports workspace and session switching, approval and follow-up handling, and export of its own diagnostic logs for plugin troubleshooting.
- [azkbzxf/dsh-account-balance-pill](https://github.com/azkbzxf/dsh-account-balance-pill) - Shows the signed-in DeepSeek account's remaining balance in the DSH composer bottom bar, next to the shipped turn/step and token-usage pills, and refreshes it periodically and after each turn.
- [azkbzxf/dsh-vscode-scrollbar](https://github.com/azkbzxf/dsh-vscode-scrollbar) - Gives the DSH conversation the Visual Studio Code editor chrome: a 14px always-visible scrollbar with a flat, translucent thumb, plus a VS Code-style minimap in the right margin (half-window column, media not painted, hover-only minimapSlider, click/wheel scrolling) and auto-expanded older pages.
- [AzureSkyHuHu/dsh-chinese-mode-modern](https://github.com/AzureSkyHuHu/dsh-chinese-mode-modern) - Adds a Chinese-mode toggle beside the composer that opens a panel for switching reply, reasoning, and tool-narration language, persisted in the profile.
- [B612wang/dsh-ui-transparency](https://github.com/B612wang/dsh-ui-transparency) - Whole-window opacity for the DSH desktop window from one button at the sidebar foot: a 0-90% slider whose value is remembered between runs. It only sets the window alpha - no restyling, no extra panels, and no other feature is touched. Windows only.
- [bagpipe1289/dsh-paperflight#plugin](https://github.com/bagpipe1289/dsh-paperflight/tree/main/plugin) - PaperFlight is an experimental future-retro DSH Web workbench with session management, file previews, approvals, and optional preset-scoped browser and terminal tools.
- [Bai-2008/dsh-better-panel](https://github.com/Bai-2008/dsh-better-panel) - Replaces the model button in the DSH composer with its own two-level panel (provider list, then a searchable model list) built around a continuous draggable reasoning-effort slider: blue-to-violet fill, nebula and star particles on higher levels, and a distinct color per level. Picking a model keeps the panel open and moves the slider to that model's middle level. The implementation started from Microqian2th/dsh-codex-effort-slider 1.0.0 (credited in the README); the panel, the seat takeover, the offline test suite and the docs are new here.
- [baifagg/dsh-plugin-busy-workspace](https://github.com/baifagg/dsh-plugin-busy-workspace) - Ranks workspaces holding a session that wants attention to the top of the sidebar session list - waiting on you first, then running, then finished-but-unopened, then just read - and draws those rows with a state bar, tinted surface and coloured title, plus a one-shot settle pulse when a run stops. Every colour is taken from the active theme's own tokens, and the three windows that decide how long a finished session stays promoted are settings.
- [bainianlaoyao/easy-archive](https://github.com/bainianlaoyao/easy-archive) - Two-step inline archive on workspace sidebar rows — one click arms a red confirm, the second click archives; the archive entry is removed from the ⋮ menu.
- [baisama-cloud/dsh-composer-history](https://github.com/baisama-cloud/dsh-composer-history) - Command history for the DSH composer: press Up/Down to cycle through previously sent instructions, then restore the draft at the end of the history; works in the official desktop app and web-profile builds.
- [Baisbt/dsh-GreaterClarity-plugin](https://github.com/Baisbt/dsh-GreaterClarity-plugin) - Enhances the DSH web session view — one-click collapse of reasoning and tool chains, numbered history quick-jump, Markdown export, AI avatars.
- [baitouwuya/dsh-super-cards](https://github.com/baitouwuya/dsh-super-cards) - Native UI cards instead of Markdown: the assistant calls render_ui and the answer renders as a card in the conversation — 29 block types (metrics, tables, charts, timelines, kanban, flow charts, clickable actions), a content auto-layout mode, four placements (inline, process, dock board above the composer, turn-tail next-step card), a schema-backed settings form, and an optional automatic next-step card. Cards are rebuilt from the recorded tool arguments, so they survive replay and fork; 253 offline tests, no build step.
- [BAIYE0303/dsh-ask-first](https://github.com/BAIYE0303/dsh-ask-first) - Adds a button to the composer that restates your draft, then asks a few questions with clickable options — every question offers an "I don't know, you decide" escape — and only then rewrites the draft into a request an agent can start on. The whole flow can be finished without typing, and it never sends for you.
- [balue8246-maker/dsDragPasteALL](https://github.com/balue8246-maker/dsDragPasteALL) - Drag or paste any local file onto the DSH page; it resolves the real absolute path (Spotlight + size/mtime + head/tail fingerprint match) and inserts it into the composer, with a content-cache fallback.
- [BangBang-03/dsh-client-ui-quote](https://github.com/BangBang-03/dsh-client-ui-quote) - Turn any DSH selection into a reviewable quote — chat, file preview, terminal and diff; code quotes carry path#line. A sent quote keeps a source highlight and ordinal and jumps back, then joins a searchable review list with status, tags, multi-select insert, and Markdown/JSON export. Quotes collect above the composer as a 引用 ×N bar with per-chip edit and remove; side threads can bring their conclusion back to the main draft; a sent quote can be revised, with the old capsule marked superseded; replies can answer each quote by number.
- [BaoBao1996121/dsh-huaci-pinglun](https://github.com/BaoBao1996121/dsh-huaci-pinglun) - Selection comments and quoted conversations (dsh划词评论 / dsh-huaci-pinglun), based on the MIT-licensed BangBang-03/dsh-client-ui-quote 0.11.1 with attached textarea measurement, border-aware height and long-text scrolling.
- [BaoBao1996121/dsh-restart-ui](https://github.com/BaoBao1996121/dsh-restart-ui) - Adds a restart button to the DSH Web UI through a loopback-only endpoint that requests a relaunch using the current process executable, arguments and working directory.
- [dsh-keep-awake](https://github.com/bearice/dsh-keep-awake) - Holds the OS wake lock while any agent, subagent, or background job runs, releases it after a configurable grace period, and provides a bilingual web settings page with live status, manual hold, and display-on options.
- [Beership/dsh-download-progress](https://github.com/Beership/dsh-download-progress) - Live download progress in the DSH session header — percentage, transfer speed, ETA and phase per download, with file-size probing before a transfer starts and byte-range resume after an interruption. The progress cards render as a fixed overlay at the top-right of the chat window.
- [BEIDAOU/dsh-boot-animation-custom](https://github.com/BEIDAOU/dsh-boot-animation-custom) - Customizable startup animation for DeepSeek Harness with eight bundled clips, random rotation, tail fade and time-of-day greetings. Based on lxj5820/dsh-boot-animation, so do not install it with the upstream plugin; bundled clips and the welcome voice carry their own media terms.
- [BenHanks303/dsh-shortcut-extensions](https://github.com/BenHanks303/dsh-shortcut-extensions) - Keyboard-driven quick switching for DSH — model palette with colored provider tags, suffix tags and pins, a two-column session palette with per-question prompt preview and archived-session restore, recency-ordered session cycling, find-next-answered jump, composer focus, and configurable permission cycling, managed from a sidebar control panel.
- [BestQ-A/dsh-session-tree](https://github.com/BestQ-A/dsh-session-tree) - Hierarchical session tree for the sidebar - native subagent trees plus an optional organization-derived hierarchy, with collapsed branch counters and orphan/cycle defense.
- [better-er/dsh-classic-coding](https://github.com/better-er/dsh-classic-coding) - Slides out a Monaco editor plus a lazy-loaded file-tree panel in the DSH Web conversation, so you can edit the local workspace inline without switching to an external editor.
- [better-er/dsh-edit-diff](https://github.com/better-er/dsh-edit-diff) - Client-side re-render of edit/write tool cards with a near-linear line-level diff that drops duplicate unchanged lines and underlines the actual changed characters inside replaced lines.
- [better-er/dsh-mobile-drawer](https://github.com/better-er/dsh-mobile-drawer) - Below 1024px the sidebar collapses to a 44px floating square and expands into a 280px overlay drawer; picking a session collapses it again and the focus grab that would raise the soft keyboard after a session switch is suppressed.
- [better-er/dsh-tool-autoexpand](https://github.com/better-er/dsh-tool-autoexpand) - Auto-expands newly arrived tool-call cards in the DSH Web UI, with a sidebar toggle cycling no-op/expand/collapse and the state persisted to localStorage.
- [BeyondandSharp/dsh-plugin-scaling](https://github.com/BeyondandSharp/dsh-plugin-scaling) - Independent per-pane zoom for the DSH web shell: Ctrl+wheel over a pane, Ctrl+± / Ctrl+0 for the focused pane. Works with the official look and every skin.
- [bf185003/dsh-favicon-status](https://github.com/bf185003/dsh-favicon-status) - Browser tab status indicator for dsh web: paints the document favicon from the session list (blue spinning while work runs, amber while waiting on the user, green when finished).
- [big-head-pigeon/dsh-session-menu](https://github.com/big-head-pigeon/dsh-session-menu) - Adds mark-unread, copy session info, reveal in Finder or open with your IDE, and permanent delete (with child sessions) to the DSH sidebar session menu, plus error and unread status dots, hover-card details, one-click delete for archived rows, and a settings panel that switches each menu item on or off; the interface is available in English and Chinese.
- [bigbigman-001/dsh-quote-to-composer](https://github.com/bigbigman-001/dsh-quote-to-composer) - Select text anywhere in DeepSeek Harness, click the quote pill next to the pointer, and the selection lands in the composer as a small quote chip; hover the chip to read the full quote.
- [bigfatorangecatjohn/opening-free-0.21.0#dsh-opening](https://github.com/bigfatorangecatjohn/opening-free-0.21.0/tree/master/plugin/dsh-opening) - Full-screen boot animation for DeepSeek Harness: a wave sweeps the screen, a whale assembles from particles inside three breathing dot rings, and a four-language salute lands over a segmented progress bar; ships an 18-second cut and 3.8-4.5-second short cuts, a language picker in settings, and a four-language preview queue.
- [biggerboy/dsh-conversation-anchors](https://github.com/biggerboy/dsh-conversation-anchors) - Codex-style left-gutter tick rail for each user turn, with hover preview and click-to-scroll.
- [bilbillm/dsh-steam-startup-animation](https://github.com/bilbillm/dsh-steam-startup-animation) - Steam Big Picture-style six-second Canvas startup animation for DSH Desktop with a variable-width terminal-to-whale transition, optional stereo startup audio, volume controls, preview, Escape skip, and reduced-motion support.
- [bill9109/dsh-drag-and-drop](https://github.com/bill9109/dsh-drag-and-drop) - Cross-platform file drag-and-drop with raw path insertion, no file copying.
- [图片缩放查看器（dsh-image-zoom）](https://github.com/bingomu/dsh-image-zoom) - In-chat image viewer for DeepSeek Harness: wheel and trackpad pinch zoom at the pointer, two-finger and drag panning, gallery navigation, downloads named from the image description and timestamp, and opening workspace originals in the system default app.
- [便签墙（dsh-sticky-wall）](https://github.com/bingomu/dsh-sticky-wall) - A sticky-note wall in the DSH sidebar: nine paper colors, categories, search, archive, to-do checklists with stable item ids, and agent read/write tools.
- [bitterSmilezzz/dsh-model-selector](https://github.com/bitterSmilezzz/dsh-model-selector) - Enhanced model selector for the DSH composer with searchable provider groups, keyboard and accessibility support, and an inline reasoning-effort slider.
- [bitxeno/dsh-github-picker](https://github.com/bitxeno/dsh-github-picker) - Composer GitHub picker: click the GitHub icon at the bottom-right of the input box to search the workspace repository's issues and pull requests via the gh CLI, and insert the reference as a URL or @owner/repo#number mention.
- [Biu-Bong/dsh-menu-hidden](https://github.com/Biu-Bong/dsh-menu-hidden) - Lazy Menu for DSH — hide the duplicate DeepSeek account / official API entries in the model picker, in Settings → Models and in the subagent model list, plus sidebar and account-menu items (14 toggles); auto-fill reasoning levels (low / medium / high / max), context window and image input for new models; on DSH Desktop, add Reload / Restart to the application menu with a one-click patch to the app shell main.js (backup and one-click restore included; needs an app restart, and a DSH update reverts the patch).
- [BlackBearCC/dsh-pet-sprite](https://github.com/BlackBearCC/dsh-pet-sprite) - A pixel companion living in the chat area: hatches from an egg into one of three sprites or an LLM-drawn custom one from a text description, platform-jumps over message bubbles on its own, WASD controllable, chats in character through the models you already configured, and carries a nurture system (mood/power/health, levels, shop) driven by real token usage.
- [Blackman99/codsh#codsh-bundle](https://github.com/Blackman99/codsh/tree/main/packages/bundle) - Terminal coding agent for DeepSeek Harness with a full-screen alternate-screen UI, turn-aware session navigation, and a gated /ship workflow that takes one-line requirements through specification, TDD, and rerun acceptance checks.
- [blacktaoK/dsh-skill-zh-overlay](https://github.com/blacktaoK/dsh-skill-zh-overlay) - Chinese summaries for the dsh-skill-mcp-panel skill cards: each expanded card gets a short Chinese overview, a category tag and a few bullet points, while the English description the panel already renders is folded behind a "原文" toggle. Skills missing from the bundled map are translated on demand by the host with your current default model and cached on disk, keyed by a hash of the original text, so a new skill needs no manual entry. Skills whose own description is already Chinese are skipped.
- [BlazeNebula/dsh-todo-notes](https://github.com/BlazeNebula/dsh-todo-notes) - A three-scope TODO board for DeepSeek Harness (user, workspace and session) with nested folders that can reference one item from several places, T0–T9 priorities, drag reordering, batch actions, a floating panel that also opens as a right-sidebar tab, Markdown preview windows that can stay open with autosaving title and body editing, and agent read/write tools, with every write gated by DSH approval.
- [Blueteemo/dsh-audio-rail-macos](https://github.com/Blueteemo/dsh-audio-rail-macos) - macOS 14.2+ only: the macOS counterpart to Lingwuxin/dsh-audio-rail — the DSH conversation rail pulses with whatever the Mac is playing, a real 12-band spectrum that only changes bar length, leaving the host state colours and lengths untouched. Requires the Audio Recording permission on first run. Do not install it alongside dsh-audio-rail: both drive the same rail and register the same /audio-rail/events and /audio-rail/status routes.
- [blueziii/dsh-peakvalley](https://github.com/blueziii/dsh-peakvalley) - Composer-dock badge showing DeepSeek peak/off-peak pricing on a Beijing-time calendar whose China holiday schedule is fetched online at runtime (off-peak costs half the peak rate); auto-hides for non-DeepSeek models and cycles seven readouts on click.
- [bmhty123/dsh-comfyui-rp](https://github.com/bmhty123/dsh-comfyui-rp) - Local text-to-image automation for DeepSeek Harness: captures the conversation, sends it to a prompt session, renders with your own ComfyUI, and brings the images back to a sidebar tab, a floating frame and a gallery.
- [Bobnemimimmi/dsh-visible-action-row](https://github.com/Bobnemimimmi/dsh-visible-action-row) - Pins each message's action row — copy/like/dislike/branch controls, timestamp, and third-party additions — so it stays visible without hovering.
- [boogoo619/dsh-focus-overlay](https://github.com/boogoo619/dsh-focus-overlay) - Focus mode with a full-screen reading overlay that hides the header and composer and folds AI tool calls into summaries.
- [breakc0re/dsh-yandex-music](https://github.com/breakc0re/dsh-yandex-music) - Yandex Music inside the DSH sidebar: search, a draggable queue with repeat and shuffle, playlists with drag-and-drop editing, 700 My Wave stations plus a wave by any artist, karaoke lyrics with real timecodes and a Russian translation, volume, media keys, and playback that resumes where it stopped after a restart. Requires a Yandex account and a Plus subscription.
- [BrianHIO-x/dsh-think-expand](https://github.com/BrianHIO-x/dsh-think-expand) - Auto-expands every Think row after switching sessions, with a header switch to turn it on or off.
- [BroCC57/dsh-pending-review-glow](https://github.com/BroCC57/dsh-pending-review-glow) - Highlights sidebar session rows by state (pending approval, running, just finished) with color, animation and a matching status dot.
- [btsd321/dsh-oh-my-terminal](https://github.com/btsd321/dsh-oh-my-terminal) - Bottom terminal panel for the DSH Web GUI, providing multi-tab interactive terminals powered by node-pty (Windows ConPTY / POSIX openpty) with split panes, a side terminal list, and a dropdown new/split menu. Also compatible with DSH-better-sidebar — auto-detected at runtime, it switches to a bottom workbench tab so PTY sessions do not bind the DSH session write handle.
- [buaaflyaway/dsh-question-note](https://github.com/buaaflyaway/dsh-question-note) - Adds an optional note field under each selected option in the DSH Web UI ask_user_question popup; submitted notes travel to the model in the answer's custom field.
- [BuildWithAIHub/dsh-boot-animation-plus](https://github.com/BuildWithAIHub/dsh-boot-animation-plus) - A fork of dsh-boot-animation that plays an intro clip when DSH opens, ships four embedded clips with an in-app library, and adds batch deletion for clips you added yourself. It reuses the same plugin id as upstream, so the two cannot be installed together.
- [burningg/dsh-workspace-categories](https://github.com/burningg/dsh-workspace-categories) - Sidebar category groups for DSH workspaces: the flat project list becomes collapsible named sections, workspaces re-file by dragging a row onto a group or picking one from a dropdown, one toolbar button collapses every group at once, an appearance panel sets font size, text color and background tint for each of the three levels, and Ctrl+Tab cycles recently opened sessions.
- [dsh-code-collector](https://github.com/bycall/dsh-code-collector) - Session code collector for DeepSeek Harness: a composer-side panel gathering every code block the model produced in the current session, with by-language / by-file views, per-file version history, live search, per-entry copy, copy-all, download-all as Markdown, and click-to-jump back to the exact turn (Alt+Shift+C).
- [dsh-conversation-timeline](https://github.com/bycall/dsh-conversation-timeline) - Conversation timeline dock bar for DeepSeek Harness: a turn-level timeline above the composer with user-prompt hover tooltips and click-to-jump back to the exact chat position.
- [byf1999/dsh-plugin-workspace-split](https://github.com/byf1999/dsh-plugin-workspace-split) - Split view for the Web UI: tiles 1-3 columns x 1-3 rows of live sessions from several workspaces in one grid, with draggable dividers, sidebar-follow routing, an in-pane file preview and an in-pane changes review.
- [CAI-MH/dsh-agent-group-panel](https://github.com/CAI-MH/dsh-agent-group-panel) - Feishu multi-agent robot console: start, stop and reconnect the daemon, view status and recent activity, and jump to the task workspace.
- [caimc21069065-prog/dsh-my-music](https://github.com/caimc21069065-prog/dsh-my-music) - Full-page NetEase Cloud Music player for DSH: daily recommendations, playlist browsing by category, charts, new releases, search, playlist/album/artist pages, QR-code login, synced lyrics, queue and downloads, plus four agent tools for search, song URLs, lyrics and playlist tracks.
- [caisiyang123/dsh-tick-rail](https://github.com/caisiyang123/dsh-tick-rail) - Tick-rail conversation navigator: one tick per message you sent, a lit peak that follows the mouse with an even falloff, hover previews, click or keyboard to jump.
- [Canary-Builds/dsh-mobile-ui](https://github.com/Canary-Builds/dsh-mobile-ui) - Adds touch-friendly navigation, layout refinements, and standalone PWA styling to the existing DeepSeek Harness web interface.
- [CAOGGL/dsh-port-token](https://github.com/CAOGGL/dsh-port-token) - Puts a key badge in the bottom-right corner of the dsh Web GUI that shows the port this instance is listening on and the remote-access token printed by dsh web at startup, with a copy button per value; clicking the badge rotates the key into a cross, and clicking it again or anywhere beside it collapses the panel.
- [captainyufei/dsh-dock](https://github.com/captainyufei/dsh-dock) - UI plugin for DeepSeek Harness with a persistent customizable left Dock, cross-workspace pinned chats, a draft-first home, global search, a completion inbox and right-panel video previews.
- [CarrotFish/dsh-file-and-terminal](https://github.com/CarrotFish/dsh-file-and-terminal) - Adds host file management and an interactive Bash terminal to the DSH WebUI.
- [CaT-Hode/DSH-app](https://github.com/CaT-Hode/DSH-app) - Windows Electron companion sharing the DSH Web profile, conversations and plugins, with startup logs, update recovery and diagnostics.
- [CatLollipop/dsh-composer-draft-cache](https://github.com/CatLollipop/dsh-composer-draft-cache) - Composer enhancements: caches unsent drafts per workspace for new sessions and per Session id for existing ones, restoring them after switching away and dropping them once sent or cleared; adds an @plugin completion group that mentions an installed plugin so the agent receives that plugin's metadata as runtime context for the turn.
- [causebefore/dsh-pomodoro](https://github.com/causebefore/dsh-pomodoro) - Pomodoro focus-and-break timer for DSH Web with configurable cycles, a draggable mini panel, and in-app, sound, and browser notifications.
- [cayan0x/dsh-fold-context](https://github.com/cayan0x/dsh-fold-context) - Auto-fold context/system messages in DSH conversation — collapse think blocks, tool calls, and tool results into grouped expandable bars.
- [ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI](https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI) - Claude Code-style full-screen terminal UI: pixel-whale header, live status line, and streaming thought expansion.
- [ccfeeling/dsh-host-file-explorer](https://github.com/ccfeeling/dsh-host-file-explorer) - Left-sidebar panel that browses the filesystem of the machine the Harness runs on: directory listings with sizes and modification times, text and image previews, file downloads, file uploads into the open directory, and folder creation. The panel speaks twelve languages. Reads and writes run with the privileges of the account that runs the Harness.
- [ccll/dsh-activity-pane](https://github.com/ccll/dsh-activity-pane) - Activity overview pane for the DSH web UI: running, awaiting-response and recent sessions with child agents, progress, model and reasoning level in one sidebar pane.
- [ccq1/dsh-side-panel](https://github.com/ccq1/dsh-side-panel) - Side panel with file browser, terminal, and Git review for quick file previews.
- [cdredfox/dsh-prompt-shortcuts](https://github.com/cdredfox/dsh-prompt-shortcuts) - Pinned prompt shortcuts in a single row above the composer — one click replaces the draft with the saved text — plus a searchable panel for pinning, editing and deleting prompts, overflow collapsed into a "More" menu, storage in a Host-side JSON file, and a prompt_shortcuts agent tool that reads and writes the same file.
- [Ceelog/dsh-plugins#dsh-plugin-setting-mcp](https://github.com/Ceelog/dsh-plugins/tree/main/src/plugins/dsh-plugin-setting-mcp) - Manage MCP servers from the Web settings panel: view, add, edit, remove, enable or disable them, with hot reload on save.
- [Celebrate-W/dsh-topic-trail](https://github.com/Celebrate-W/dsh-topic-trail) - Turns each long dsh conversation into a three-level trail (topic to progress steps to milestone timeline) and shows it in a draggable overlay, where one click jumps back to the matching message in the transcript. Topics are distilled by the DeepSeek model with a zero-cost rule-based fallback, can be renamed, locked, merged by drag and drop, and support per-conversation notes plus a vector drawing board.
- [Centaurea5547196/dsh-page-zoom](https://github.com/Centaurea5547196/dsh-page-zoom) - Page zoom for the DSH Web UI: Ctrl+mouse wheel scales the entire interface in 5% steps (30%-200%), remembers the zoom level locally, and shows a magnifier with the percentage in the bottom-right corner.
- [Cerbur/clutch-dsh#clutch-dsh-office](https://github.com/Cerbur/clutch-dsh/tree/main/packages/clutch-dsh-office) - Brings active DSH sessions into a 3D 'Overcooked' kitchen: AI agents work, whale-girls wander the kitchen, and you watch from the side; status bubbles show who's cooking, waiting, or done with one-click navigation — originally built to check progress, now you just can't stop watching the whales.
- [CH4ACKO3/dsh-turn-fold](https://github.com/CH4ACKO3/dsh-turn-fold) - Codex-style turn folding for the DSH Web UI: keeps final answers visible, groups consecutive reasoning and tool activity, and shows configurable live duration, tool-call, and token metrics with multilingual summaries.
- [Chaiyahang/dsh-scratchpad](https://github.com/Chaiyahang/dsh-scratchpad) - Scratchpad panel for DeepSeek Harness: tagged notes, image/file attachments, Markdown task lists, send-to-chat, a wake-up shortcut, JSON export/import, and Agent tools.
- [chaochaokongbai/dsh-beginner-hub](https://github.com/chaochaokongbai/dsh-beginner-hub) - Beginner panel for DeepSeek Harness: routes a plain-language request into ready-to-use plans (prompt variants, matching skills, advanced moves) with a local keyword engine and fills the chosen plan into a new session in one click.
- [chargeduck/xechat-dsh](https://github.com/chargeduck/xechat-dsh) - Dockable side panel that keeps an XEChat chat session connected over a single WebSocket: markdown messages with images and emoji, server switching through #showServer and #login, an online member list, a full-screen panic overlay, and dock side, width and visibility persisted to localStorage.
- [charliesbyte/dsh-workspace-activity](https://github.com/charliesbyte/dsh-workspace-activity) - Shows a spinner on a sidebar workspace row while any of its sessions are running, and a short-lived finished mark after they stop.
- [charrywhite/dsh-sticky-notes](https://github.com/charrywhite/dsh-sticky-notes) - Draggable sticky notes with 9 skins, image notes, and agent-readable to-do lists for DeepSeek Harness.
- [CheeseFox259/dsh-better-composer](https://github.com/CheeseFox259/dsh-better-composer) - Markdown visual enhancement and session workbench for DSH Web Composer: live syntax presentation with caret-row source reveal (inactive-line markers can be hidden or faded), deterministic local completions, and list/quote continuation on line break with ordered-list renumbering; long pastes fold into persistent chips with inline context or file-mode delivery (@path across 50+ extensions) plus sidebar LLM rewrites (stop, preview diff, undo/redo); real-time context map with token gauge, composition breakdown, per-turn growth chart with anchor lines, in-place /compact, turn forking, and a Context Doctor workspace (overview, diagnostics, analysis, feedback).
- [chen731215-dev/dsh-muv-engine](https://github.com/chen731215-dev/dsh-muv-engine) - MUV engine for DeepSeek Harness: runs the regex_scripts from a character card against model output, tracks variables, renders a status bar, expands macros and LaTeX, and styles tagged code blocks such as letter, terminal, newspaper and phone. Needs dsh-muv-table installed as well, which is its companion plugin.
- [chen731215-dev/dsh-muv-table](https://github.com/chen731215-dev/dsh-muv-table) - MUV variable table editor for DeepSeek Harness: a tree view of the variables being tracked, a macro test panel for random, pick and roll, a dice shortcut bar and pick-cache management. Companion plugin for dsh-muv-engine.
- [chen8923/dsh-task-progress](https://github.com/chen8923/dsh-task-progress) - Live progress for long-running DSH tasks: a script appends JSON lines to a per-session progress file, the Web UI draws them in a floating overlay and a right-sidebar tab that also lists background jobs which reported nothing, and `dsh-progress run -- <cmd>` wraps a command that cannot report for itself.
- [ChendarCe/dsh-perf-hud](https://github.com/ChendarCe/dsh-perf-hud) - A transparent, draggable performance HUD for the DeepSeek Harness Web UI: CPU, GPU and memory usage in three compact rows that float directly over the interface without any card background, snapping to the nearest window corner so resizing or maximising keeps them in place; CPU package temperature comes from an optional elevated helper (LibreHardwareMonitor + PawnIO) and the plugin itself stays at a fraction of a percent of CPU. Windows only.
- [chendefine/dsh-composer-markdown](https://github.com/chendefine/dsh-composer-markdown) - Markdown editing aids for the DSH web composer: ordered and unordered list continuation with continuous renumbering, Tab-nested list levels, inline-code styling, and code-fence auto-closing, with the sent draft kept as literal Markdown.
- [ChengX0721/dsh-client-ui-skin-alice-greyrat](https://github.com/ChengX0721/dsh-client-ui-skin-alice-greyrat) - A pale rose-and-gold Mushoku Tensei skin for the DSH web GUI: an arcane sigil and two full-body Eris figures behind the conversation, a young Eris standee and gilt plugin glyphs in the sidebar, and its own settings card.
- [chengzhi43/dsh-file](https://github.com/chengzhi43/dsh-file) - VS Code-style file manager: browse the conversation workspace in a sidebar tree and edit files in the center column with Monaco, with theme import/export and Markdown preview.
- [chengzhicao/llm-capture](https://github.com/chengzhicao/llm-capture) - Web UI for capturing and reviewing raw LLM requests, streamed responses, token usage and tool calls.
- [chenxiachan/thoughtdag#dsh](https://github.com/chenxiachan/thoughtdag/tree/main/dsh) - Adds an editable conversation canvas to DeepSeek Harness, where graph connections select context for canvas requests, with branching, merging, and local agent session imports.
- [ChenYiming-aaa/dsh-ui-ux-pro-max](https://github.com/ChenYiming-aaa/dsh-ui-ux-pro-max) - Offline-first UI/UX design intelligence library for DeepSeek Harness: design_recommend, design_review and design_search tools with a built-in database of 84 design styles, 192 color palettes, 74 font pairings, 99 UX guidelines, 25 chart types and 22 tech stacks, Chinese-first with zero runtime dependencies.
- [ChiffonOwO/dsh-diff-review](https://github.com/ChiffonOwO/dsh-diff-review) - Change review for a turn's edited files: a pill above the composer counts this turn's files and lines and floats the file list up on hover, and a right-sidebar page shows each file's diff with syntax highlighting and a one-click revert.
- [china-wkz/dsh-window-state](https://github.com/china-wkz/dsh-window-state) - Remembers the DSH desktop window position, size, and maximized state on Windows, and restores them on the next launch.
- [ChineseRyan/dsh-plugin-workspace-archive](https://github.com/ChineseRyan/dsh-plugin-workspace-archive) - Adds an Archive workspace item to the workspace row menu: archives the sessions under that workspace through the official command, leaving the one you are currently viewing open, and refusing the batch while any session is still running. The host hides archived sessions but keeps the emptied workspace row in the sidebar; this plugin hides that row too, and restores the whole group from a footer panel backed by a ledger under $DSH_HOME/storages written behind a cross-process lock. Its count badge can be set to always, on-new-only, or never.
- [CHNAQW/dsh-app-row-brand](https://github.com/CHNAQW/dsh-app-row-brand) - Custom text and image in the Windows desktop title bar, with multi-item rotation, per-item fonts, colors, alignment and shimmer, and a Settings page. Renders nothing outside the Windows desktop shell.
- [choco9527/dsh-add-to-chat](https://github.com/choco9527/dsh-add-to-chat) - Adds lightweight selected-text references to DSH assistant replies: selections stay outside the editor as removable draft contexts, remain marked beside the source, and render as hoverable annotations after submission.
- [choco9527/dsh-product-preview](https://github.com/choco9527/dsh-product-preview) - Browse local image, video, and SVGA artifacts from conversation nodes in a Finder-style column view.
- [Choi-Peng/dsh-footer-order](https://github.com/Choi-Peng/dsh-footer-order) - DSH sidebar footer ordering: forces the sidebar.footer.action slot into a vertical stack and lets users configure the top-to-bottom order of its entries.
- [cholot-Zhou/dsh-ui-overrides](https://github.com/cholot-Zhou/dsh-ui-overrides) - File-based CSS overrides for the DeepSeek Harness Web UI — edit ~/.dsh/ui-overrides.css and reload to restyle any plugin's elements, for example making the quote bar fully opaque.
- [chongfengdy-dev/dsh-message-outline](https://github.com/chongfengdy-dev/dsh-message-outline) - Current-session message outline rail for the DSH web UI — your messages show as slim bars on the left edge; hover to expand the full outline (line number + text), click to jump instantly to that message. Pure frontend, zero external service dependencies, sessions-snapshot driven with zero polling.
- [chopperH0824/dsh-annotate-sync](https://github.com/chopperH0824/dsh-annotate-sync) - In-place HTML document preview annotation: pick elements, leave notes saved per file, send batches to the conversation, and let the agent apply changes directly.
- [ChuanTianML/dsh-chat-tidy](https://github.com/ChuanTianML/dsh-chat-tidy) - One stylesheet that retypesets the DSH conversation to measured Codex metrics: 14/22 px body, tighter headings, lists and blocks, a rounded quote bar, and Tidy Tables with a themed header, row/column rules, full-width short tables and contained scrolling for wide tables; no settings or DOM rewriting, with real before/after captures.
- [ChuanTianML/dsh-open-with](https://github.com/ChuanTianML/dsh-open-with) - Open the current registered Workspace from the Session Header or Workspace menu in a detected or configured editor, with hot re-detection and a remembered browser preference.
- [ChuanTianML/prompt-for-me](https://github.com/ChuanTianML/prompt-for-me) - Context-aware next-message suggestions in the DSH composer, with repeated Trigger cycling and explicit user review before sending.
- [chumoe/dsh-file-manager](https://github.com/chumoe/dsh-file-manager) - Replaces the sidebar workspace Files pane with a file manager that adds real composer chips for "@" file mentions, word and line selection across syntax highlighting, archives in and out, upload and download, and a jump menu to any directory on the machine.
- [chzisnull/dsh-file-finder](https://github.com/chzisnull/dsh-file-finder) - File search palette for the DSH workspace: find files by name or content, open results in DSH's native right-sidebar preview, and edit text files inline with version-conflict checks.
- [CIBUDAYI3906/dsh-prompt-trove](https://github.com/CIBUDAYI3906/dsh-prompt-trove) - Composer prompt library: reusable global and per-project prompts are stored as Markdown files and appended to the draft on click or @ mention; the global library works out of the box, while project groups appear only after "config.root" is set in the profile patch.
- [Cieux/dsh-zoom-scale](https://github.com/Cieux/dsh-zoom-scale) - Whole-interface zoom from 75% to 200% in 5% steps via a Settings row, a session-header stepper or Ctrl/⌘+wheel, with a shim that converts rects, viewport units, media queries and pointer coordinates so fixed overlays stay anchored to their triggers.
- [cinewave0/cinewave0-dsh-quick-panel](https://github.com/cinewave0/cinewave0-dsh-quick-panel) - Windows companion window for DSH Desktop: todos, reminders, live session messages, approval handling and quick input. The standalone window reuses the Electron binary at %USERPROFILE%\.dsh\electron\electron.exe (usually installed by dsh-pet) and fails to open when it is missing.
- [cjjchina3088191870-sudo/dsh-plugin-ekko-skin-manager](https://github.com/cjjchina3088191870-sudo/dsh-plugin-ekko-skin-manager) - Switch installed Rose and Miku themes and customize artwork, background opacity, and phone navigation in DSH settings.
- [cjm-m/dsh-paste-code-block](https://github.com/cjm-m/dsh-paste-code-block) - Cherry Studio-style paste: multi-line/indented/fenced text or code pasted into the DSH Web composer collapses into a bordered, collapsible, language-tagged card and is restored as a fenced code block on send.
- [Cjrrrrrrrr/dsh-plugins#dsh-sidebar-tidy](https://github.com/Cjrrrrrrrr/dsh-plugins/tree/main/packages/dsh-sidebar-tidy) - Sidebar layout fix for the DSH Web UI: caps and scrolls the plugin-action area, shrinks the inline pairing QR code and tightens the brand row, so the workspace and session list stays clickable when footer plugins grow tall.
- [clarag0d/dsh-locale-multilingual](https://github.com/clarag0d/dsh-locale-multilingual) - Adds four extra UI locales to DeepSeek Harness — Traditional Chinese (zh-TW), Japanese, Korean and French. All translation data is bundled, so it works fully offline.
- [ClockworkParrot/dsh-prompt-optimize](https://github.com/ClockworkParrot/dsh-prompt-optimize) - Adds an optimize-prompt button to the left of the composer's send button; it rewrites the draft with the session's own model route and writes the result back through the normal editor path, so a single undo restores the original.
- [Cloud017/dsh-prompt-reforge](https://github.com/Cloud017/dsh-prompt-reforge) - Adds a reforge control to the DSH composer that rewrites the current draft into a sharper prompt in place, as one undoable edit, with one-click revert and cancellation.
- [cloudatlasx/dsh-pet-plus#dsh-pet](https://github.com/cloudatlasx/dsh-pet-plus/tree/main/dsh-pet) - An enhanced fork of PC2005-cloud/dsh-pet (do not install it alongside the upstream dsh-pet): a desktop pet for DSH with schedule reminders, approval and question bubbles you can answer in place, a 20-entry history ledger, double-click corner cycling, a configurable data directory, a step/elapsed progress line, snooze and one-hour mute, a right-click menu whose order and hidden items you can edit in settings (chat first by default), and a task-queue progress bar inside the work bubble.
- [clown139880/dsh-live2d-avatar](https://github.com/clown139880/dsh-live2d-avatar) - Live2D avatar stage and desktop companion for DSH: bundled Haru sample, custom Cubism 2/3+ model loading with scale and position controls, a draggable in-page pet, an optional transparent always-on-top desktop pet window, per-conversation expression prompt control, and opt-in voice with self-hosted ASR/TTS.
- [cm860712ch-cloud/dsh-quick-input](https://github.com/cm860712ch-cloud/dsh-quick-input) - A 3×3 pad of saved phrases sitting to the left of the composer and tracking the input box's height: clicking a cell inserts its text at the caret as one undoable edit. Presets are editable in place, 9 cells per page up to 12 pages, and the UI follows the DSH language setting. Falls back to a panel above the composer when the window leaves no room.
- [CMoyuer/dsh-cad-viewer](https://github.com/CMoyuer/dsh-cad-viewer) - Adds a 3D model library in its own conversation tab, where agents store models built from CadQuery scripts or supplied as tessellated meshes; the library previews them with three-cad-viewer, organises them into folders and exports them to 10 formats including STEP, BREP, STL and DXF.
- [CNSeniorious000/dsh-generative-ui](https://github.com/CNSeniorious000/dsh-generative-ui) - Lets the AI insert streaming-generated UI into the conversation or a side panel, written in TSX — already one of the model's native languages, so there is no JSON schema to teach it. Rendered without flicker, state kept across frames with no hard remount, and a click in the card driving the next chat turn. Inline is where streaming matters most; the side Canvas is the more agentic shape. Cards get agentic capabilities of their own — run bash, curl, call the model, read and write files.
- [coii08o0o/dsh-bot-board](https://github.com/coii08o0o/dsh-bot-board) - A floating board over the chat for DS Bot's team: who is running and on which tool, who is waiting for another Bot to hand work back, who needs your approval or answer, and who failed, with a hand-off table grouped by sender, a per-Bot drawer showing the pending command or question, and a collapsed one-line pill. Read-only — it never writes to DS Bot's data. Needs the DS Bot plugin installed (built against 0.1.7 and 0.2.0); the panel stays hidden without it.
- [ComlaiGonnann/dsh-codex-rail](https://github.com/ComlaiGonnann/dsh-codex-rail) - Transforms DSH into a dual-column Codex workspace with an integrated Activity Bar, unified topbar, avatar quick access, session history navigation.
- [Como44/dsh-zh-more](https://github.com/Como44/dsh-zh-more) - Chinese-first UI enhancement: translates slash-command names and plugin-inventory cards with three display modes (Chinese only / Chinese + English / off), switching instantly without losing English.
- [constraint21/dsh-long-message-fold](https://github.com/constraint21/dsh-long-message-fold) - Folds a user message whose body renders taller than 360px in the Web chat down to 360px with a fading bottom edge and an Expand/Collapse toggle. Short messages keep their exact original look, and the expanded state is remembered per message while the page stays open.
- [converk/dsh-tweaks#git-bash-terminal-tool](https://github.com/converk/dsh-tweaks/tree/main/plugins/git-bash-terminal-tool) - Replaces the terminal tool on Windows with Git Bash.
- [converk/dsh-tweaks#prompt-history](https://github.com/converk/dsh-tweaks/tree/main/plugins/prompt-history) - Recalls prompts sent earlier in the current session with the up and down keys in an empty composer, and shows a position badge while browsing.
- [converk/dsh-tweaks#smart-list](https://github.com/converk/dsh-tweaks/tree/main/plugins/smart-list) - Continues numbered and bulleted lists in the DSH composer, so Shift+Enter after "1. item" starts a new line with the next marker.
- [cookiesheep/whale-on-desk](https://github.com/cookiesheep/whale-on-desk) - Pixel-art whale desktop companion with 29 frame-audited states — taps the glass and flashes the tab title when an approval waits, speaks a completion report when a turn ends, and adds a slash-command palette to the web client.
- [Coprexist/Copree#dsh-copree](https://github.com/Coprexist/Copree/tree/main/dsh-copree) - Embeds Copree (formerly AIsChat) into the DSH web UI as a native sidebar board. Copree is an open-source framework for AI group chat and programmable worlds: the AIs are residents, not tools — each has its own memory, state and personality, they talk to each other on their own, and you can watch or join in. Group World goes further: every group chat can bind a living world (its own web page + world AI + code + clock) that the world AI builds and edits through DSH workspace tools. This plugin ships the native sidebar board (contacts, pinned DMs and groups), real-time chat over a same-origin HTTP/WS proxy, Markdown/LaTeX rendering, group invites and image messages, immersive full-frame pages, and one-click mirroring of your worlds into DSH workspaces (folder + DSH session + world_* tools). Front end only: it proxies to a Copree backend you run yourself (default 127.0.0.1:5228, same repository).
- [copylee711/dsh-better-display](https://github.com/copylee711/dsh-better-display) - Renders assistant replies with numbered citation chips and a sources panel for web-search results, inline pictures with their source captions, and KaTeX formulas, Mermaid diagrams and highlighted code in streaming Markdown.
- [cosmoliu2002/dsh-prompt-enhancer](https://github.com/cosmoliu2002/dsh-prompt-enhancer) - Adds a ✨ button and an /enhance command to the composer that rewrite the draft into a structured prompt, flag open questions below the input, follow the current session model, and offer an undo.
- [cozylove-girl/dsh-appearance-master](https://github.com/cozylove-girl/dsh-appearance-master) - Manage skins, themes, backgrounds, and widgets in separate layers and save appearance presets for DeepSeek Harness.
- [crisvollerei/dsh-persona-shell](https://github.com/crisvollerei/dsh-persona-shell) - Re-speaks each assistant report in a chosen persona outside the working prompt, with four built-in presets, per-session switches, and the original available on demand.
- [Crosery/dsh-drop](https://github.com/Crosery/dsh-drop) - Drag or paste files into the Web composer with one image-and-file preview rail; native PNG/JPEG/WebP/GIF keep the image attachment path, while other files reuse a matching local path or stream to Host staging and become @ references only at send time. Includes browser media/PDF and bounded text previews; Office and archives show identity cards, and unsent file entries are page-local.
- [Crosery/dsh-viewer](https://github.com/Crosery/dsh-viewer) - Model-facing display_file tool that renders 36 file extensions inline in the web UI: images with a click-to-zoom lightbox, seekable video, audio, PDF, Office and OpenDocument files converted to PDF on the host via LibreOffice, and local HTML in a sandboxed frame; bytes stream from a signed HTTP route with range support, and PNG/JPEG/WebP/GIF also enter model context on image-capable routes.
- [CsBpRd/dsh-split-view](https://github.com/CsBpRd/dsh-split-view) - Splits the DSH main area into vertical columns, one live conversation per parallel task, with mouse-wheel paging past four columns, draggable dividers, a split toggle on every session-list row, a progress-only compact view and a one-click exit.
- [cshengyi023-collab/dsh-deepsea-echo](https://github.com/cshengyi023-collab/dsh-deepsea-echo) - Arknights-flavoured startup animation for the DSH Web GUI: a PRTS terminal boot, sonar scan, hologram lock-on and an operator-file splash that end in a deep-sea lockup, about 4.6 seconds, with four selectable original score presets (or sound off), skippable with a click or Esc and switchable, re-timed or replayed from Settings.
- [cshengyi023-collab/dsh-pet-companion](https://github.com/cshengyi023-collab/dsh-pet-companion) - Adds a low-key companion panel with a pomodoro timer, a todo list, drink-water and stand-up reminders, plus lesson explanations and homework tutoring through the installed desktop pet.
- [cspyz/dsh-terse-mode](https://github.com/cspyz/dsh-terse-mode) - Four adjustable terse levels plus independent switches for reasoning effort, web search and subagents, on the conversation header and in the Settings page. No output ceiling.
- [CTX666666/dsh-prompt-polish](https://github.com/CTX666666/dsh-prompt-polish) - Polish button in the composer that rewrites the draft with the model the current conversation already uses: four styles, a fidelity guard that keeps every concrete fact verbatim, one-click revert, and no API key of its own.
- [cuhaitiang0405-collab/dsh-indexbookmark](https://github.com/cuhaitiang0405-collab/dsh-indexbookmark) - Conversation question index for the DSH Web UI: every question you asked in a session, searchable, paginated, with one-click jump to the exact message.
- [cxv117793-blip/dsh-sidebar-overflow](https://github.com/cxv117793-blip/dsh-sidebar-overflow) - Puts a More row at the end of the sidebar main panel list and lets you decide, entry by entry, which panels stay visible and which move into it: a menu lists every entry with a one-click switch in either direction, a search box filters long lists, and a single Restore all clears both kinds of key. Also hides the footer action buttons, keeps the plugins entry un-collapsible, and writes each choice to the profile config so it survives a restart.
- [CXZAAA/dsh-radio](https://github.com/CXZAAA/dsh-radio) - Adds a 24px capsule to the composer status row that plays seven preset internet radio stations, with a live spectrum, now-playing metadata for Nightride FM and Radio Paradise, and remembered volume and station.
- [cyh3436332528/dsh-float-chat](https://github.com/cyh3436332528/dsh-float-chat) - Pops the DSH side chat into a frameless, always-on-top Windows desktop window that can sit over any other application, with its own settings page for appearance, pinning, remembered geometry, opacity, ephemeral threads and cache cleanup.
- [cyjyyd/dsh-ssh-tui](https://github.com/cyjyyd/dsh-ssh-tui) - Terminal UI for DeepSeek Harness on jump hosts and high-latency links: plain ANSI, incremental repaint, and a session that survives a dropped SSH connection (resume it from the log).
- [cyrus123456/dsh-johari-cognition-quadrant-dialog-composer](https://github.com/cyrus123456/dsh-johari-cognition-quadrant-dialog-composer) - Johari cognition-quadrant dialog prompt composer — adds a button above the composer that opens a 2×2 quadrant panel (known/unknown × AI-known/AI-unknown) to groom conversation context into a structured prompt written back into the draft.
- [czxgg0630/dsh-html-annotate](https://github.com/czxgg0630/dsh-html-annotate) - Preview local HTML in the right sidebar, annotate elements or regions, and send the selections with current and target styles into the current session.
- [CZY1326/dsh-restart-button](https://github.com/CZY1326/dsh-restart-button) - Adds a circular restart button to the session header, plus an optional app.asar patch that also puts a Restart row in the tray menu.
- [d0ublecl1ck/dsh-flow](https://github.com/d0ublecl1ck/dsh-flow) - Small DeepSeek Harness affordances for everyday actions — locate the current session from the sidebar header (Cmd/Ctrl+Shift+D), copy a session ID from the session row menu (Cmd/Ctrl+Shift+C), open links in the system default program including localhost, open or copy inline code from a context menu, open changed-file cards with a local IDE or reveal them in the file manager, swap Enter with Cmd/Ctrl+Enter, recall sent messages with Up/Down, and show the current workspace name inside the header open button; every one of them has its own switch in a Flow settings page.
- [d0ublecl1ck/dsh-hide-empty-workspace](https://github.com/d0ublecl1ck/dsh-hide-empty-workspace) - Auto-hide a sidebar workspace the moment its last unarchived session is archived, and add a "隐藏工作区" entry to each workspace row's own "..." menu. The restore entry sits at the bottom of the sidebar. It only toggles display and never archives, deletes or rewrites a workspace.
- [d0ublecl1ck/dsh-scratchpad-plus](https://github.com/d0ublecl1ck/dsh-scratchpad-plus) - Workspace-free chats that promote real files into their own project workspace - a maintained fork of dsh-scratchpad, kept working on current DSH.
- [d0ublecl1ck/dsh-search-enhance](https://github.com/d0ublecl1ck/dsh-search-enhance) - Search inside the New-Session workspace picker: pinyin initials and full pinyin spelling for Chinese titles, substring matching anywhere for English titles, and separator-insensitive matching.
- [Da0Mine/dsh-hide-update-badge](https://github.com/Da0Mine/dsh-hide-update-badge) - Hides the built-in DSH Desktop update badge (the download-arrow "新版本" pill beside the account button): a CSS rule for the current build plus accessible-label matching with a throttled MutationObserver, so it keeps working after a DSH upgrade. Update checking, the manual "Check for updates" menu item and the connection indicator are untouched.
- [dadazhangn/dsh-prompt-snippets](https://github.com/dadazhangn/dsh-prompt-snippets) - Save reusable prompt snippets with categories and {{placeholders}}; manage them with /snippet commands (add, list, rm, optimize) and insert into the composer from a draggable dock panel, with one-click AI refinement through the default model.
- [dadgrand/dsh-agent-teams-dashboard](https://github.com/dadgrand/dsh-agent-teams-dashboard) - Adds a Teams sidebar dashboard that lists every Agent Teams chat with its roster and task roadmap, token spend in USD and RUB, work and online time, and an archive of older teams. Requires the Agent Teams plugin.
- [daetz-coder/dsh-multi-chat](https://github.com/daetz-coder/dsh-multi-chat) - Multi-window wall for the DSH Web UI: run and monitor N conversations side-by-side in one screen, with auto-discovery, per-window controls, and an authenticated LAN gateway for phone/tablet access.
- [damlys99/dsh-morning-paper](https://github.com/damlys99/dsh-morning-paper) - A newspaper-style front page for a session you left running, as a Conversation View tab beside Chat and Trajectory: a per-turn timeline (duration, steps, tools, tokens, outcome), file churn counted per file, where the wall clock went and how much of it was the model rather than tools, which single turn ate the spend, failures nobody retried quoted with their failing command, and a context-pressure gauge measured against the routed model's own declared capacity. The briefing is folded from the durable session log and never generated by a model, so it costs no tokens, cannot hallucinate, and is byte-identical for the same log and clock; it appends nothing to the session and reaches no model, and it reports a pending approval without ever answering it. Pages are offered only when they have content, and the tab refreshes itself on turn settle and on a two-speed poll backed by a metadata-only tail probe.
- [damlys99/dsh-queue-reorder](https://github.com/damlys99/dsh-queue-reorder) - Reorder the messages queued while a DSH agent is busy, from inside the official queue dock: a drag handle and per-row ↑ / ↓ buttons on the dock's own rows, applied on the host as one concurrency-checked inbox splice. The dock is decorated rather than replaced, so previews, inline editing, steering, removal and attachments stay as shipped. A move is refused when the queue has changed since the caller last saw it, when the row is not a user prompt (a goal round prompt, for example), or when the queue belongs to a subagent session.
- [DamonKoy/dsh-web-ui#dsh-aionui-panel](https://github.com/DamonKoy/dsh-web-ui/tree/main/packages/dsh-aionui-panel) - Right-panel system for the dsh web GUI: a pixel-faithful AionUi Explorer + Preview (file tree, diff view, preview).
- [DamonKoy/dsh-web-ui#dsh-liangshen](https://github.com/DamonKoy/dsh-web-ui/tree/main/packages/dsh-liangshen) - LiangShen (梁神) agent preset for the dsh web GUI: a two-phase anchored-standard preset.
- [DamonKoy/dsh-web-ui#dsh-task-board](https://github.com/DamonKoy/dsh-web-ui/tree/main/packages/dsh-task-board) - Task board for the dsh web GUI: a sidebar entry plus a multi-column kanban view with local persistence.
- [DamonKoy/dsh-web-ui#dsh-web-ui-settings](https://github.com/DamonKoy/dsh-web-ui/tree/main/packages/dsh-web-ui-settings) - Web UI plugin group card in the dsh settings page: enable switches and config for the dsh-web-ui family.
- [Daneel0001/dsh-plugin-zh-cn](https://github.com/Daneel0001/dsh-plugin-zh-cn) - Renders installed DSH plugin titles and descriptions in Simplified Chinese: at startup it translates the plugins the local JSON cache lacks through the harness LLM service, then projects { en, zh } metadata for resolveText() to render — no DOM changes, no third-party API.
- [DanZai233/dsh-webask](https://github.com/DanZai233/dsh-webask) - Adds a button beside the composer and a global hotkey palette (default mod+shift+k) that hand one-off questions to a web chat instead of into the harness context: the draft is lifted out of the composer, copied to the clipboard and opened in the templated URL. Ships target-site presets (DeepSeek, Kimi, Qwen, 豆包, 腾讯元宝, GLM, Gemini), a local question history that can be re-opened, copied, or inserted back into the composer, and a settings tab for site, URL template, hotkey, clipboard and history.
- [datit309/dsh-live-inspector](https://github.com/datit309/dsh-live-inspector) - Real-time inspector of the files the agent reads and edits, with a file tree, in the DeepSeek Harness right sidebar: M/A/R badges, side-by-side diffs and live agent activity tracking.
- [day-day-dream/dsh-page-refresh#dsh-page-refresh](https://github.com/day-day-dream/dsh-page-refresh/tree/main/plugins/dsh-page-refresh) - Adds a refresh button and a power menu (restart / close DeepSeek Harness) to the conversation header. Client-only, zero tokens, no dependencies. The power menu is Windows-only.
- [dayutech/dsh-handy](https://github.com/dayutech/dsh-handy) - Adds a per-session notebook with autosave, a draggable key-information panel with collapsible items and queue injection, notebook and key-information inheritance for native and /fork forks, workspace-scoped session groups, session highlighting, running-workspace prioritization with a blue spinner, and close-current/left/right/all actions on every right-sidebar tab.
- [dbcaskml183-dotcom/dsh-prelude](https://github.com/dbcaskml183-dotcom/dsh-prelude) - Optimize prompts in the DeepSeek Harness composer: identify missing requirements, insert a sentence from 90 method cards, or compare and edit a full rewrite before you send.
- [Dcmwfg/dsh-deepseek-sidebar](https://github.com/Dcmwfg/dsh-deepseek-sidebar) - Opens chat.deepseek.com in the right sidebar pane from one row in the left sidebar, leaves the current conversation untouched, expands to fullscreen on demand, and shares the sidebar browser cookie jar; a bundled script makes the login survive restarts. Desktop (Electron) only; keeping the login across restarts needs the bundled PowerShell patch, which is Windows-only and must be re-applied after every DSH upgrade.
