- [Dcmwfg/dsh-screenshot#windows](https://github.com/Dcmwfg/dsh-screenshot/tree/main/packages/windows) - Region screenshot for the composer on Windows: a full-virtual-screen selector frames the region (drag the frame, eight grips, Ctrl+drag to annotate), and the finished PNG lands in the composer as a pending attachment.
- [dcrzsy/dsh-enhance-tool](https://github.com/dcrzsy/dsh-enhance-tool) - All-in-one UI enhancement layer for DeepSeek Harness web: prompt library with quick-insert and management, two-step AI polish, predicted-reply dock with click-to-send, MCP tool management, scheduled automation, smart session titles, adjustable chat width, full-width AI messages without gray background, auto-collapse for long user messages with smooth expand/collapse animations, and scene-specific entrance animations across all plugin surfaces.
- [DDSG-X/dsh-workspace-dir](https://github.com/DDSG-X/dsh-workspace-dir) - Shows the current conversation's working directory in a draggable directory panel with adjustable opacity.
- [ddy-007/dsh-client-resume-button](https://github.com/ddy-007/dsh-client-resume-button) - Adds a Continue button left of the model selector: after a turn ends aborted by the user, interrupted by a crash or errored, one click sends notices asking the model to pick that turn back up, and the button collapses while you type or attach a file.
- [deanzhang2026-max/dsh-lived-pet#dsh-pet-bridge](https://github.com/deanzhang2026-max/dsh-lived-pet/tree/main/packages/dsh-pet-bridge) - Writes the harness agent live state (thinking, working, waiting, done, error) to a state file that a bundled Electron Live2D desktop pet polls, so the pet bubble and face follow what the agent is doing.
- [DedsecLemon/dsh-obsidian](https://github.com/DedsecLemon/dsh-obsidian) - Your real Obsidian vault in the DSH right Sidebar: vault tree, full-vault search, read-view typography, wikilinks and heading anchors, properties, backlinks, highlights, in-place editing, an outline, and a right-click menu for handoff, rename and .trash.
- [deepseekbluefish/dsh-screenshot-plugin](https://github.com/deepseekbluefish/dsh-screenshot-plugin) - WeChat-style in-app screenshot button: drag a region, fine-tune with 8 resize handles, Enter to capture and Esc to cancel; auto-numbered PNGs plus a \[Shot N HH:mm] marker written into the composer. Windows-only, tested on DeepSeek Harness Desktop.
- [delaydai/dsh-jumpbar](https://github.com/delaydai/dsh-jumpbar) - User message jump bar for the DSH web GUI: a minimap-style dash strip on the right edge of the conversation, with hover preview, click-to-jump, drag-to-scrub and a viewport marker.
- [demacia1314/dsh-airdrop](https://github.com/demacia1314/dsh-airdrop) - ⭐ Drag-and-drop uploads of any file or whole folders, anywhere in the window; remote-safe — bytes go straight into the session workspace on the server, so a DSH deployed on a remote machine works exactly like localhost.
- [dengsixue-stack/dsh-file-context-menu](https://github.com/dengsixue-stack/dsh-file-context-menu) - Right-click menu for file links in chat: open with registered apps, save as, copy path or content, reveal in Finder or Explorer.
- [dengyinxi100-crypto/ai-calendar-deepseek-harness](https://github.com/dengyinxi100-crypto/ai-calendar-deepseek-harness) - Embeds a full calendar app as a sidebar panel and adds a draggable, resizable scheduled-task widget on the DSH page. Registers six agent tools (status, list, create, update, delete, summarize) that read and write the same SQLite database. The calendar's own AI is routed to the DeepSeek route already configured in DSH, and each reply shows token usage and cost, accumulated per day.
- [Dengzi555/dsh-quotalk](https://github.com/Dengzi555/dsh-quotalk) - Select response text or code to ask contextual follow-ups with streaming replies in a native sidebar.
- [dennisrongo/dsh-plugins#dsh-weather](https://github.com/dennisrongo/dsh-plugins/tree/main/plugins/dsh-weather) - Draggable weather bar in the shell overlay with forecast and geocoding from Open-Meteo; the position persists across sessions.
- [DeveloperYe/dsh-local-files](https://github.com/DeveloperYe/dsh-local-files) - Local file manager served by the DSH web server itself: whole-disk browsing, previews for images, PDF, video and audio, text, Markdown, JSON, CSV, sandboxed HTML, docx and xlsx, Range downloads, folder ZIP, drag-and-drop upload, copy/paste, conflict-checked text editing, create/rename/delete-to-trash, and xlsx cell editing saved as a copy.
- [Devin-AXIS/deepseek-design#deepseek-idesign](https://github.com/Devin-AXIS/deepseek-design/tree/main/packages/deepseek-idesign) - Visual design studio for websites, app prototypes, posters, cards, reports, and magazines, with templates, direct element editing, selection-aware AI draft handoff, and export.
- [Devin-AXIS/deepseek-design#deepseek-ivideo](https://github.com/Devin-AXIS/deepseek-design/tree/main/packages/deepseek-ivideo) - Visual video studio with an editable timeline, animation and media controls, 27 templates, whole-video and selection-aware AI draft handoff, validation, preview, and export.
- [DevinMx520/dsh-selection-translator](https://github.com/DevinMx520/dsh-selection-translator) - Adds a right-click menu to selected text in the Harness Web UI that replaces it with a Simplified Chinese translation in place, and restores the original on a second right-click.
- [devome/dsh-panel-layout](https://github.com/devome/dsh-panel-layout) - Sidebar panel menu layout: switch between a button row and a list, cap the button row at one to three rows, drag any entry to reorder it, hide the entries past the cap and list them in the shell More menu, and absorb Settings, Feedback and Sign in from the bottom-left launcher. Based on ruanimal/dsh-sidebar-panel-buttons; the two cannot be enabled at the same time.
- [devzer0o0/dsh-grok-buddy](https://github.com/devzer0o0/dsh-grok-buddy) - Puts Grok Bot's own face in the DSH sidebar session list: each row's status dot becomes a living GrokBot avatar whose six motions read as idle, working, waiting, blocked, thinking and done — color marks the project, motion marks the state.
- [dgy970516/dsh-status-turbo](https://github.com/dgy970516/dsh-status-turbo) - A status bar in the composer footer showing the current phase, how long it has been running, the current step token count and a no-progress warning, projected from session logs without polling the host, plus a detail panel that opens on click.
- [dhb861832993-star/pbr-render](https://github.com/dhb861832993-star/pbr-render) - PBR 3D model preview for game art: GLB/GLTF with textures, IBL environment lighting, orbit controls, and a material channel inspector (baseColor/normal/roughness/metallic/AO/emissive/wireframe); scalar channels shown as grayscale per PBR spec, via the pbr3d fence and pbr_render tool.
- [dhdkfkfkf/dsh-pull-to-send](https://github.com/dhdkfkfkf/dsh-pull-to-send) - Send by dragging instead of clicking. Press the composer and pull up to arm the gesture; releasing sends, dragging back into the composer cancels, and dragging outside the conversation area cancels on the spot. Mouse and pen only.
- [dhdkfkfkf/dsh-qbuddy-prompt-enhance](https://github.com/dhdkfkfkf/dsh-qbuddy-prompt-enhance) - Button beside the composer that rewrites the current draft into a more executable prompt in place. Ships four built-in presets spanning light polish to aggressive expansion, accepts user-defined presets, and switches preset, prompt model and reasoning effort directly from the button without leaving the composer. The button appears only when the draft has content, shows a running timer while enhancing, and surfaces failures with a retry.
- [DIAG5/dsh-better-input](https://github.com/DIAG5/dsh-better-input) - Input experience enhancement: voice input, AI polish, anti-overwrite, prompt optimization with diff preview, and auto locale switching following DSH UI language.
- [dialling/dsh-galaxy-view](https://github.com/dialling/dsh-galaxy-view) - Draws workspaces and conversations as a 3D star map in the stock panel slot: eight layouts (spiral, rings, armillary sphere, solar system, double helix, irregular web, cubic lattice, radial tree), eight colour themes, a memory-library map of instruction files, skills and skill calls, star-to-star fly-throughs, and a 中/EN switch. Links are drawn only where a workspace owns a conversation or one conversation descends from another.
- [diexianxiang/dsh-custom-splash](https://github.com/diexianxiang/dsh-custom-splash) - A customizable video splash screen plugin for DSH. Replace the default startup with your own local video.
- [dingchenhui0618-arch/dsh-market-sidebar](https://github.com/dingchenhui0618-arch/dsh-market-sidebar) - Adds the dshmarket plugin market to the DSH sidebar as a panel of its own, ordered above the built-in Plugins entry. Clicking the entry renders the market through the client service dshmarket publishes for hosts, so the catalog, category filters, comments and install and update flows are the ones dshmarket ships rather than a copy of them. Nothing to learn or configure: the panel is the market, with its own sidebar entry, label in Chinese or English to match the interface language, and icon. If dshmarket is not loaded the panel says so instead of failing client composition, and its locale keys, slots and style tag are released when the plugin unloads.
- [dingchenhui0618-arch/dsh-signal-pet](https://github.com/dingchenhui0618-arch/dsh-signal-pet) - A small pet in the corner of the interface that reads the real event stream instead of guessing: it drifts when idle, speeds up while the agent is thinking, draws a flowing line per running tool call, turns red and shakes with the error summary on a failure, and celebrates a turn that ran longer than ten seconds. The same widget is the at-a-glance busy and error indicator, so there is no spinner to stare at. The host half folds real harness events into a snapshot and the client half only draws it; it performs no content recognition, and it positions itself away from the composer so it never sits over what you are typing.
- [DingL0312/dsh-long-input-window](https://github.com/DingL0312/dsh-long-input-window) - Adds a full-screen dialog for writing long messages: Markdown on the left rendered live by marked on the right, with a draggable splitter that remembers its ratio. Confirming writes the text back to the composer draft, keeping command tokens and @-reference chips as a fixed prefix; Esc asks whether to discard or write back before closing.
- [dingyi580/dsh-conversation-rail](https://github.com/dingyi580/dsh-conversation-rail) - Left-edge conversation rail for the Web UI: one bar per turn, length scaled to the turn text, hover for a question and answer preview, click to jump; bars come from a host-side outline of the whole session log, so turns outside the loaded window are drawn too and history is paged in on click.
- [djs326/dsh-plugin-width-slider](https://github.com/djs326/dsh-plugin-width-slider) - All-in-one UI plugin for DSH Desktop (Windows): conversation-width slider with fullscreen preview and follow-window mode, thinking-block enhancements (forced Chinese, expand/collapse, Markdown rendering), an Open With header button, UI label localization, a resizable settings dialog, session delete, and workspace tabs.
- [Dkrillex/dsh-macos-pet](https://github.com/Dkrillex/dsh-macos-pet) - macOS desk pet forked from anneheartrecord/dsh-desk-pet, in a real always-on-top window that covers fullscreen Spaces: the pose follows whatever the local DSH is doing across six states, the right-click menu is a real AppKit one carrying quiet mode and a session list, and seven skins ship with it. Two of the seven are drawn from geometry by a script in the repo rather than generated, so making a skin needs no image model and no API credits; the bundled skill still expands one photo into all eighteen poses on your own image tool and credentials. There is no Electron and nothing to install alongside it: the window is driven through ctypes on the Python that ships with macOS.
- [dmscode/dsh-peak-valley](https://github.com/dmscode/dsh-peak-valley) - Tints the strip above the composer green or red to match DeepSeek's off-peak and peak billing windows, shows the remaining time of the current window as a progress bar, and ships six themes plus a custom-CSS hook.
- [dmscode/dsh-rightbar-tab-gestures](https://github.com/dmscode/dsh-rightbar-tab-gestures) - Closes right-sidebar tabs with a middle click or a left double click, reusing the built-in close button so the native rules (such as the last docked guide card being unclosable) still apply.
- [Dogwind221/img2img-studio#dsh-img2img-config](https://github.com/Dogwind221/img2img-studio/tree/main/plugins/dsh-img2img-config) - Adds two UI surfaces: a composer image editor (numbered marks with per-mark instructions, background removal, brush-mask erase, resize with crop/padding preview, keyboard-accessible markers; results return as attachments plus a version-1 manifest) and a Settings → vision/generation provider panel (explicit provider picker, providerId normalization, ChatGPT-web accounts synced into channels, per-channel test/try-run/copy-command, script readiness scan, save validation with .env preview, probing that only disables on explicit quota errors, Chinese/English UI) that writes providers back to the skills .env files.
- [domitor-syh/dsh-ui-skin-switcher](https://github.com/domitor-syh/dsh-ui-skin-switcher) - Replaces the model button with a Claude Desktop-style model and reasoning-effort switcher, with per-model effort memory, theme-aware styling, smooth slider feel, rounded track, hover highlight, and pixel-dot animation.
- [domparent/OLEDCare](https://github.com/domparent/OLEDCare) - OLED burn-in care for the Web GUI with pure-black surfaces, gamma-aware text dimming on an idle/focus ladder, a true-black nap screensaver with live agent status, and slow accent-hue rotation.
- [dongasai/dsh_editor](https://github.com/dongasai/dsh_editor) - A file editor in the DSH web GUI right sidebar: open any text file, edit it, and save in place, with a version gate on every save — if the file changed on disk during the edit it stops and asks instead of overwriting. The document preview gains an edit button and a two-step delete button, and a separate File editor sidebar tool brings its own file tree for create, rename or move, copy, cut and paste (including a file taken from the system clipboard), delete and drag to reorganize. Two renderer bands cover 107 plain-text, config and source extensions; the second is offered only as an open-with choice, leaving the shipped Markdown and highlighted-code views as the default. All mutations run through the plugin's own trusted host routes, behind the connection trust fence, the workspace containment check and the sandbox policy.
- [dongasai/dsh_setting_pro](https://github.com/dongasai/dsh_setting_pro) - Enlarges the DSH web settings dialog (default 1180x920, 20px steps, remembered per browser) and adds an Advanced Settings page that resizes it and hosts Host operations: restart dsh with a 3-second delay and a busy confirmation, restart once all sessions have been idle for 10s (cancellable, repeat clicks idempotent), last start time with uptime, and a dsh version/update check that compares npm dist-tags across selectable channels without upgrading. The same bundle replaces the sidebar brand area with a machine name, adds a read-only provider, model and thinking-level cell beside the composer stats pills, and a switch that registers or unregisters the three dsh_restart_* agent tools at runtime. Zero dependencies, no build step; all routes sit behind the connection trust fence.
- [DONGBIN007/dsh-sidebar-restart](https://github.com/DONGBIN007/dsh-sidebar-restart) - Adds a Restart row to the desktop app's sidebar foot beside Settings that restarts the app and reloads the page onto the newly installed plugin bundle.
- [dongfangxy/dsh-agents-md-settings](https://github.com/dongfangxy/dsh-agents-md-settings) - Adds a Settings → General row that opens ~/.dsh/AGENTS.md in the system editor, so the instruction file can be opened without hunting for it. On Windows it launches Notepad and raises the window.
- [dongfangxy/dsh-open-project](https://github.com/dongfangxy/dsh-open-project) - Adds a Windows Explorer folder context-menu entry that registers the folder as a DSH project, with a switch for the menu entry in Settings → General. Windows only, and nothing extra has to be installed.
- [dongwenxiu83-web/dsh-friendly-steps](https://github.com/dongwenxiu83-web/dsh-friendly-steps) - Folds Think and tool-call rows into a floating summary pill with red failure counts, for a cleaner writer-friendly chat view.
- [donyue7/dsh-aoci-panel](https://github.com/donyue7/dsh-aoci-panel) - AOCI cognition panel for DSH: multi-workspace AOCI overview, per-repo index and governance view, MCP binding diagnosis, and guided or batch initialization. Governance, verification and guided/batch initialization require the external aoci CLI on the host; index browsing works without it.
- [DQS-PUBLIC/dsh-price-chip](https://github.com/DQS-PUBLIC/dsh-price-chip) - Sidebar chip showing the current DeepSeek price period (peak or off-peak), the selected model's price per million tokens for cache hits, cache misses and output, the account balance and today's spend.
- [dreamfarer-space/deepseek-harness-intelligent-ui](https://github.com/dreamfarer-space/deepseek-harness-intelligent-ui) - Generative, interactive UI for DeepSeek Harness: the agent picks per reply between 26 validated native components and sandboxed streaming HTML widgets, patches a rendered UI in place by component ID, restores tabs, sorts, filters and form values after a reload, and computes slider and filter changes in the browser without another model call.
- [DreamLoomStudio/dsh-primitive-pulse](https://github.com/DreamLoomStudio/dsh-primitive-pulse) - A floating panel that lights up which HowNet semantic primitives the current answer touches, as a brain-shaped node graph, EEG-style channel traces and a composition card.
- [dreammaker2016/dsh-file-menu](https://github.com/dreammaker2016/dsh-file-menu) - Right-click menus for the DSH Web UI — copy, cut, paste and select all on text and input areas, plus copy path, reveal in the file manager and open with the default app on file references and links.
- [drliuhuan/dsh-plugin-zoom](https://github.com/drliuhuan/dsh-plugin-zoom) - Manually zoom the Harness Web UI from 75% to 200% via a Settings row, Ctrl+wheel, or a Session-header control, keeping text crisp and readable on high-DPI displays.
- [drowsylazy/dsh-thinking-effort-rheostat](https://github.com/drowsylazy/dsh-thinking-effort-rheostat) - A Claude Desktop-inspired composer slider for adjusting model reasoning effort with click, drag, or keyboard controls.
- [drscrewdriver/dsh-arrowkey-nav](https://github.com/drscrewdriver/dsh-arrowkey-nav) - Arrow-key navigation for the DSH Web GUI: Up/Down switch sessions inside the current workspace and Left/Right switch workspaces, reading session identity from the client controllers rather than the DOM, and yielding the keys back to the composer while a draft is being edited.
- [drscrewdriver/dsh-brief-sidebar](https://github.com/drscrewdriver/dsh-brief-sidebar) - A dsh-better-sidebar tab that renders a session brief — a todo board read from the `todos` projection plus a deliverables section listing the files written this turn — and hides the official todo panel above the composer while it is mounted.
- [drscrewdriver/dsh-canvas-tsx-sidebar](https://github.com/drscrewdriver/dsh-canvas-tsx-sidebar) - A dsh-better-sidebar tab that statically parses Qoder Canvas `*.canvas.tsx` files into structured pages in the DSH right sidebar (the source is never executed: no eval, no bundler, no sandbox iframe), with a file-viewer takeover for `.tsx` and a bundled Skill for authoring the format.
- [drscrewdriver/dsh-input-traffic](https://github.com/drscrewdriver/dsh-input-traffic) - Three-tier now/next/later input planning dock for the DeepSeek Harness web GUI: a red/yellow/green priority strip replaces the busy-Enter queue, plain Enter queues to later, with in-place multi-line editing, drag reorder, and session-isolated freeze/resume; the freeze button bridges a separate plugin, [dsh-session-guard](https://github.com/drscrewdriver/dsh-session-guard) (installable from dsh-market), via stopNextTurn/resume RPC + composer lock for a real per-session lock, pairing with its global peak-hour auto-pause to stop sessions during DeepSeek's high-cost windows and resume off-peak.
- [drscrewdriver/dsh-paste-dock](https://github.com/drscrewdriver/dsh-paste-dock) - Turns large text pastes into pastes/*.txt attachments with a dock above the composer and a minimal, recognisable badge in the draft.
- [drscrewdriver/dsh-sidebar-fold](https://github.com/drscrewdriver/dsh-sidebar-fold) - Folds the sidebar's add-on panel rows (schedules, agent teams, plugin market, context pages, …) under the shipped "More" row: a 28x28 chevron toggle on that row collapses them into a single header line and expands them back, the fold state persists per window, the rows kept visible are chosen with per-row switches read from the live sidebar, and each managed row's icon + label can be aligned left, centre or right. Client-only plugin: adds DOM, patches no host bundle, and uninstalls back to the shipped sidebar. Requires DSH 0.2.1-rc.3 exactly; other versions install but stay inert.
- [drscrewdriver/dsh-tidy-display](https://github.com/drscrewdriver/dsh-tidy-display) - Honest merge of two community plugins into one calmer display: the streaming reading view from dsh-better-display (fork) and the message rail from dsh-tidychat. For theme lovers it wraps reasoning prose and tool-call output in translucent message bubbles, so text no longer sits directly on busy wallpapers or skins where it is hard to read. Cross-version rc adaptation attempted across DSH 0.1.0-0.2.0 (all rc); no obvious problems so far.
- [dsh-blue/blue#blue](https://github.com/dsh-blue/blue/tree/master/packages/bundle/blue) - Plugin-tree terminal UI for DeepSeek Harness — streaming markdown transcript, tool cards, fuzzy slash commands, and live theming, with every component a hot-swappable Cordis plugin.
- [dsh-niao/dsh-niao-quick-open](https://github.com/dsh-niao/dsh-niao-quick-open) - UI enhancement suite for DeepSeek Harness: one-click workspace open (copy path, reveal in Finder, open in an auto-discovered editor), DeepSeek-web-style user-message nav rail, session to-do marks, flat-list polish, and a hard-restart button.
- [dsh-plugin-collection/dsh-group-status](https://github.com/dsh-plugin-collection/dsh-group-status) - Aggregated status badges on collapsed sidebar groups in DSH: a folded folder row shows in-progress / awaiting-approval / done / scheduled / review-pending counts, with numbers and hover detail. (sidebar badge / group status / aggregate)
- [dsh-plugin-collection/dsh-suggest-ghost](https://github.com/dsh-plugin-collection/dsh-suggest-ghost) - Input prediction for the DSH composer: your next prompt suggested as ghost text after each turn, accepted with Tab. (autocomplete / input prediction / prompt suggestion)
- [du-u-uck/DSH-Prompt-Optimization](https://github.com/du-u-uck/DSH-Prompt-Optimization) - Adds a one-click prompt optimization button to the DSH composer, with undo and an optional dedicated optimizer model.
- [duanyunlun/dsh-session-actions](https://github.com/duanyunlun/dsh-session-actions) - Sidebar conversation actions: open the row menu on right-click, rename by double-click, copy the session id, and delete a conversation together with its storage after a confirmation.
- [Duanyy0813/dsh-prompt-clarifier](https://github.com/Duanyy0813/dsh-prompt-clarifier) - Adds a prompt-refine button to the DSH composer and, when auto mode is on, refines the prompt before the agent starts by rewriting the text with recent session context through a side-channel model call, asks on the native question card when the rewrite cannot be decided, reports each stage as a command card in the chat, and keeps the original text when analysis fails.
- [dungcsnd1133/dsh-dl-brand](https://github.com/dungcsnd1133/dsh-dl-brand) - Replaces the sidebar brand mark with a custom one and registers a Vietnamese locale covering 45 namespaces and 1897 interface strings, falling back to English for anything untranslated.
- [dustcpu/dsh-geometric-splash](https://github.com/dustcpu/dsh-geometric-splash) - Page-level startup splash: a Penrose triangle light sweep over a living isometric diamond ocean, where the frame itself is the progress gauge, paced by the intro but capped by real DSH boot milestones. Any bare key skips it, ten palettes come from Monument Valley and Geometric Islands, and when the intro ends the triangle stays on as a small mark beside the composer that reflects what the agent is doing.
- [Dusty-Dustin55/dsh-file-context-menu](https://github.com/Dusty-Dustin55/dsh-file-context-menu) - Right-click files, folders, web links, and workspaces in the DeepSeek Harness chat and sidebars to reference, copy, preview, open in a chosen app or browser, reveal, or save.
- [DWJZ/dsh-restart](https://github.com/DWJZ/dsh-restart) - Adds a Restart page to the DSH Web settings panel. The page shows the PID, port, start time and uptime of the running host and restarts it through a detached helper that waits until the port stops answering before replaying the same launch invocation; the page reloads itself once the replacement host answers with a new boot id.
- [dygin/dsh-recover-context](https://github.com/dygin/dsh-recover-context) - Roll back to before any of your questions or edit the last one and resubmit, with the workspace files of the discarded turns restored.
- [e2mcc/dsh-popout-sidebar](https://github.com/e2mcc/dsh-popout-sidebar) - Pop-out sidebar: lists artifacts and a file tree with multiple preview formats, pops out into a separate browser tab you can drag to another monitor, and coexists with other sidebar plugins simultaneously.
- [EastMG/dsh-gacha-calendar](https://github.com/EastMG/dsh-gacha-calendar) - Gacha banner and event schedule lookup for anime games: sidebar button with current banners, events and countdowns for 28 gacha games, with auto-refresh, search, Windows expiry reminders and .ics export.
- [ecbob/dsh-window-size-keeper](https://github.com/ecbob/dsh-window-size-keeper) - Keeps the DSH desktop window the size you left it: records the last outer frame of the main window in physical pixels and restores it, centred on the work area, on the next start - and it acts before the window is ever shown, so the boot animation plays at the right size. The settings page offers 12 presets (1024x768 to 3840x2160), free-form custom width and height, a centre-on-restore switch and an apply-now action; a choice takes effect immediately and persists. Resizing goes through a small helper executable compiled on the user's own machine: the C# source ships inside the plugin, is built on first run with the in-box Windows csc.exe (measured ~110 ms once, cached next to the state file, ~90 ms to start), and falls back to a short-lived powershell.exe process when that is not possible. The package ships no binary, declares no npm dependencies and makes no outbound network request. Windows only.
- [EchoTint/dsh-notes-wall](https://github.com/EchoTint/dsh-notes-wall) - A sticky-notes wall pinned to the sidebar: one click opens a full-page wall, notes save as you type, and writing #tags in the text turns them into one-click filter chips. Six paper colors, pinning, search, JSON export and import.
- [ECNU/EduWork#dsh-annotation](https://github.com/ECNU/EduWork/tree/main/packages/dsh-annotation) - Select reply text, edit numbered annotations, and send several with a question using native DSH composer references, draft persistence, and submission; requires DSH 0.2.1-rc.2 or later within the 0.2.x series.
- [Edge-HH/GrokBot_in_DeepseekHerness](https://github.com/Edge-HH/GrokBot_in_DeepseekHerness) - Bring GrokBot into DeepSeek harness.
- [EdwardXiao-bit/dsh-run-button](https://github.com/EdwardXiao-bit/dsh-run-button) - Adds a Run button to shell code blocks in replies, executing the snippet through the host shell service and showing its output in a floating bottom-right dock, or in one bottom-panel tab per run.
- [el16z3c/dsh-think-ux](https://github.com/el16z3c/dsh-think-ux) - Smooth think-row experience for the DSH Web UI: think rows expand as a capped 24-line preview that glides to the bottom while reasoning streams, collapse with a short height animation on settle, and the main conversation view follows with a reader-safe smooth chase (no snaps, no yanks).
- [elangan1997-cmyk/canvas-workbench#canvas-workbench](https://github.com/elangan1997-cmyk/canvas-workbench/tree/main/canvas-workbench) - Pluggable design canvas inside DSH — standalone Excalidraw board, session image tools (edit, erase, background removal, OCR, vectorize, PSD/text rebuild), project asset library, and a Photoshop/Illustrator ⇄ canvas bridge.
- [Elefseus/dsh-pet-progress](https://github.com/Elefseus/dsh-pet-progress) - Folds the durable todo and goal events the dsh pet ignores into a percentage bubble on the pet, and falls back to a one-line activity summary for a session that never wrote a todo list. Requires the dsh web pet plugin (@linxin666/dsh-pet >= 0.4.0).
- [eloimovo/better-dsh-codex-effort-slider](https://github.com/eloimovo/better-dsh-codex-effort-slider) - Takes over the model button in the DSH composer and opens its own two-level panel (provider list, then model list with search) built around a continuous draggable reasoning-effort slider: the fill runs blue to violet, higher levels add nebula and dust particles, and every level has its own color. Drag moves one level at a time, and picking a model keeps the panel open and repositions the slider to that model's middle level.
- [Elycheng/dsh-article-annotator](https://github.com/Elycheng/dsh-article-annotator) - Article Review page in DSH's native right sidebar: turns Markdown, Word, PowerPoint and PDF articles into sentence-addressable text, lets you comment on any sentence, and hands the open review to the agent as a composer draft. Six agent tools let the model read every comment, revise the article (in place for Markdown, as a sibling <name>.revised.md for binary formats), resolve the comments, and record recurring wording preferences into a long-term writing-style memory injected into later prompts. Extraction includes a dependency-free DOCX/PPTX reader; PDF text extraction runs locally through the host's Python + pypdf (or poppler's pdftotext) and is unavailable without one of them. No network calls, no telemetry.
- [enteguo/dsh-plugin-quick-chat](https://github.com/enteguo/dsh-plugin-quick-chat) - Adds a Quick Chat entry to the Web UI sidebar that opens a floating chat window with its own model and reasoning-effort picker, optionally seeded with the current session's transcript, and keeps every side conversation in a resumable, renamable, deletable history.
- [enterhalf/dsh-session-colorful-unread-pin-jobs](https://github.com/enterhalf/dsh-session-colorful-unread-pin-jobs) - Colors each DSH sidebar session title by state: unread (blue while the model is running, green once it has finished) and pinned (yellow, read from DSH's own pinned set), composed into one left-to-right gradient. Pinning, archiving and ordering stay with DSH itself — the plugin reimplements no pinning and rewrites no official bundle — and a settings section lists every unread session.
- [eomis/packhub-workbench-assistant](https://github.com/eomis/packhub-workbench-assistant) - Adds quick installation and switching for workbench templates in DSH Desktop.
- [Ephemeral-AI-Lab/mayfly#mayfly](https://github.com/Ephemeral-AI-Lab/mayfly/tree/main/packages/mayfly) - Interactive terminal UI for DeepSeek Harness: tables, Mermaid and chart rendering, side conversations, and the same Cordis plugin model as dsh Web.
- [Ernesto-yuan/dsh-conversation-tools](https://github.com/Ernesto-yuan/dsh-conversation-tools) - A floating in-conversation find bar docked over the DSH composer (Ctrl/⌘+F or the magnifier beside the input): hits list with a match counter as you type, Enter jumps to the next hit, hovering a hit pops a scrollable full-text card with a one-click copy of the whole message, the up arrow in an empty composer recalls previously sent messages one at a time with their image and file attachments restored as drafts, hovering any image in the conversation shows a copy button that writes it to the clipboard as PNG, and every finished answer gains a regenerate button while the newest also gets continue. Client-side only, searches the messages already loaded in the conversation, and never jumps.
- [EternalNight996/dsh-theme](https://github.com/EternalNight996/dsh-theme) - Theme skin plugin for DeepSeek Harness — built-in themes / static image / dynamic 360°-follow video, one-click from the sidebar & Settings; imports persist, default skins protected.
- [EternalNight996/dsh-ui-agents-pixe](https://github.com/EternalNight996/dsh-ui-agents-pixe) - Adds a "Work Roles" tab and a pixel-art office overlay to the DeepSeek Harness Web UI: 508 built-in role cards (The Agency 255 + agency-agents-zh 253) with search, EN/ZH toggle and department filter; draggable Canvas 2D pixel characters that stand, type and pace, with idle chat that can plug into AI. One-command npm install, no dsh source changes.
- [EternalNight996/dsh-ui-three-body](https://github.com/EternalNight996/dsh-ui-three-body) - Adds a sentient floating eye to DeepSeek Harness — injects a taming kernel that makes the AI truly understand you, with 11 dynamic eye skins, ghost mode, and zero token cost when AI mode is off.
- [Ethan1999991231/dsh-sidebar-dock](https://github.com/Ethan1999991231/dsh-sidebar-dock) - Docks the workspace and session list to the left, right, top or bottom edge, optionally folding it into a 5px edge band that expands on hover, with the reveal zone and the left rail width adjustable in Settings → General; the plugin rail, brand row and settings button stay where they are.
- [EugeneVl/dsh_session_folders](https://github.com/EugeneVl/dsh_session_folders) - One-level session folders for the web sidebar - group sessions per workspace, move them by drag-and-drop or context menu, archive and restore, server-side persistence.
- [EventHorizonXT/dsh-model-navigator](https://github.com/EventHorizonXT/dsh-model-navigator) - Replace the model picker with global search, favorites, recent selections, model-family navigation, and reasoning effort selection.
- [exoticknight/dsh-better-left-sidebar](https://github.com/exoticknight/dsh-better-left-sidebar) - A clearer DeepSeek Harness sidebar with separate navigation and conversation regions and built-in widgets.
- [exoticknight/dsh-just-chat](https://github.com/exoticknight/dsh-just-chat) - One-click native conversations with independent workspaces for DeepSeek Harness.
- [Eyeing0721/dsh-cot-en2cn](https://github.com/Eyeing0721/dsh-cot-en2cn) - Shows a Chinese translation under each English thinking block in the DSH web chat, without modifying the session transcript; per-chunk caching, a configurable translation model, and a settings panel.
- [Faide-cyber/dsh-model-in-use](https://github.com/Faide-cyber/dsh-model-in-use) - Marks models in use by other running sessions in the DSH model menu, and restores the remembered reasoning effort and group collapse state for each model.
- [Fakek0f3sT/dsh-mcp-diff](https://github.com/Fakek0f3sT/dsh-mcp-diff) - Renders every file mutation in the web chat as one collapsed diff card with per-line highlighting — MCP filesystem edit_file/write_file and the built-in edit/write.
- [FallenWings27/dsh-provider-card-release](https://github.com/FallenWings27/dsh-provider-card-release) - Plays a character entrance card beside the composer when you switch model: identity name, six scored axes (intelligence, coding, speed, stability, cost, context), quota hint and a dialogue line. 23 curated cards across 15 providers; models without a curated card fall back to a data card derived from public benchmarks, or a generic card when no benchmark data exists. A web client plugin with no dependencies and no hooks.
- [fan56/dsh-tui-pi](https://github.com/fan56/dsh-tui-pi) - Full-featured TUI for DeepSeek Harness: streaming conversation, powerline status bar, real-time think/tool/todo panels, subagent dual-view, theme hot-switch — all as pure cordis plugins with zero upstream patches.
- [fangwen9527/dsh-composer-ux](https://github.com/fangwen9527/dsh-composer-ux) - Configurable send and newline keys, a composer context menu with three sources (leave it to DSH and other plugins / browser menu / plugin-styled custom menu), a quick-command panel with categorized prompt lists where each prompt can append on every send or only on the first message of a session, a create-and-move row at the bottom of each category, a prompt optimizer that rewrites the draft with a separate model call, settings-panel size memory, and automatic x-opencode-session header injection for OpenCode routes. Prompt lists live in a global ~/.dsh/quick-prompts.json that is independent of the session and the project.
- [Fantasy421/dsh-whiteboard](https://github.com/Fantasy421/dsh-whiteboard) - Shared editable Excalidraw whiteboards with per-session multi-board management, quick navigation, incremental AI edits, curves, polylines, and image exports.
- [FanXi1230/fanxi#dsh-nova](https://github.com/FanXi1230/fanxi/tree/main/plugins/dsh-nova) - A cosmic reasoning-effort slider for the DeepSeek Harness composer. Off, Low, High, and Max each have their own starfield and aurora, with smooth cross-fades and real per-session model selection.
- [FAVKTOXIC/dsh-caption-bar](https://github.com/FAVKTOXIC/dsh-caption-bar) - Moves conversation views into the Windows caption row and header utilities into a compact right rail on DSH Desktop with Codex UI, reclaiming a full row of vertical space while preserving the session title, fullscreen control, dynamic status counts and menus.
- [faxble/dsh-model-picker](https://github.com/faxble/dsh-model-picker) - Model quick picker for DeepSeek Harness: a button left of the model dropdown opens a full model-selection dialog with provider grouping, live search, provider filter, capability tags read from the user's own input config, reasoning badges, recent-model shortcuts and a reasoning-effort picker; the choice writes back to the native dropdown immediately.
- [faxble/dsh-open-with-vscode](https://github.com/faxble/dsh-open-with-vscode) - Adds a button left of the built-in Open In... in the session header that opens the current workspace directory in VS Code, fixing the desktop ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE environment leak that makes the built-in open-in-app fail with HTTP 502. Windows only: it locates Code.exe, so macOS and Linux are not supported yet.
- [Fayelin12/dsh-office](https://github.com/Fayelin12/dsh-office) - Office workspace & session dashboard: a floating 6-column sprite panel visualizing workspaces, sessions, token usage and subagents, plus Agent Mail and an office log tab. Feishu/Lark views (message feed, meeting schedules, transcripts) additionally require the `@larksuite/cli` tool installed and authenticated separately.
- [FeatherHunter/dsh-im-companion](https://github.com/FeatherHunter/dsh-im-companion) - IM Companion for dsh-im: groups Feishu/WeChat/QQ bots by Agent in DSH workspaces and enhances DSH native session states (blue/orange/red rail markers for running, needs-review and error), with left badges, one-click filter, fleet overview and drag-to-move. Clean uninstall.
- [FeatherHunter/dsh-prompt](https://github.com/FeatherHunter/dsh-prompt) - 🌹 Save every repetitive, tiresome templated prompt — one mouse click injects it into the composer and spares you the effort.
- [FeatherHunter/ilife#dsh-life-pack](https://github.com/FeatherHunter/ilife/tree/master/packages/plugin-manager) - ⚠️ Internal beta, do not download ⚠️ iLife: one DSH settings card for the iLife home-life suite — memo, calorie, chef, home, schedule and bill in a tab bar — covering every part of your home life.
- [feiyaochang123-sys/dsh-starred-turns](https://github.com/feiyaochang123-sys/dsh-starred-turns) - Right-click a dash in the conversation's right-edge turn rail to bookmark that turn: the dash grows to the rail's full width and takes the accent colour so it stays easy to find, and right-clicking again unbookmarks it. Bookmarks are scoped per conversation, keyed by the selected Session id, and persisted so they survive reloads. No layout shift - the rail keeps its fixed pitch.
- [Feizhaiqianqian/dsh-email-board](https://github.com/Feizhaiqianqian/dsh-email-board) - Read-only unread-mail board for the accounts configured in dsh-email — unread messages appear as per-account cards in the DSH right sidebar (sender, subject, time) and refresh on demand.
- [FeiZhuNiU-INFJA/dsh-stock-ticker](https://github.com/FeiZhuNiU-INFJA/dsh-stock-ticker) - A floating, semi-transparent market ticker for DeepSeek Harness showing real-time level and change percentage for the Shanghai Composite, ChiNext, STAR 50 and Hang Seng Tech indices.
- [FelixJiashengFu/dsh-recent-deliverables](https://github.com/FelixJiashengFu/dsh-recent-deliverables) - Draggable recent-deliverables panel for DSH Web that merges declared deliverables with recently modified workspace files, with filename search, type filters, inline PDF/HTML/image/text previews, and desktop open or reveal actions.
- [feng-yifan/dsh-plugins#dsh-ask-highlight](https://github.com/feng-yifan/dsh-plugins/tree/main/packages/dsh-ask-highlight) - Highlights answered ask blocks (question n/n answered) in the conversation with a soft card style; pending asks and other tool blocks keep their native look; optional expand-by-default and a settings page.
- [feng-yifan/dsh-plugins#dsh-pwa-icon](https://github.com/feng-yifan/dsh-plugins/tree/main/packages/dsh-pwa-icon) - PWA icon: sets the icon (and manifest app name) used when the Web GUI is installed or added to the desktop; ships a default icon set and accepts a custom image.
- [feng-yifan/dsh-plugins#dsh-vertical-layout](https://github.com/feng-yifan/dsh-plugins/tree/main/packages/dsh-vertical-layout) - Portrait layout for desktop windows: navigation takes the full left column, the file/terminal panel moves above the conversation, and a draggable splitter sets its height; touch devices are unaffected.
- [fengb3/dsh-session-icons](https://github.com/fengb3/dsh-session-icons) - Sidebar session-title icons: an auxiliary model call on the same route as title generation draws a 24x24 monochrome SVG metaphor shown before each session row title, with disk caching.
- [fengMax1997/dsh-line-select](https://github.com/fengMax1997/dsh-line-select) - Line-range selection: browse workspace files, preview code with line numbers, visually select a line range, and write a `@path:start-end` reference into the composer; the agent receives the exact selected lines on send.
- [fengzhaoy/dsh-web-ide](https://github.com/fengzhaoy/dsh-web-ide) - IDE tab in the dsh web client: a Monaco editor with the Dark Modern theme plus a file tree, git source control (stage/commit/push), an integrated xterm terminal and ripgrep workspace search, opened as a conversation view tab.
- [ferrisching/dsh-prompt-predict](https://github.com/ferrisching/dsh-prompt-predict) - Predicts the user's next prompt after each assistant reply and shows it as gray ghost text inside the composer; accept it whole with Tab, accept it piece by piece with Alt+→, reroll it, or dismiss it with Esc.
- [fewoot/dsh-whale-particles](https://github.com/fewoot/dsh-whale-particles) - Dot-matrix backdrop for the DSH Web UI: the dots form the DeepSeek whale or a transparent PNG you import (alpha-masked), each dot carries its own low-frequency force and follows the cursor in manual mode, while an auto screensaver mode sweeps 360-degree waves and full-screen breathing across the artwork; sliders and toggles live under Settings > Plugins.
- [FFFFFFcn/dsh-intent-translator](https://github.com/FFFFFFcn/dsh-intent-translator) - Adds a composer control that turns a plain-language draft into a structured, terminology-precise prompt, shows the professional term behind selected colloquial phrases with a one-line explanation, and offers a terms-only mode that returns professional terminology without rewriting the draft.
- [FideFeiPlane/dsh-auto-maximize](https://github.com/FideFeiPlane/dsh-auto-maximize) - Maximizes the DeepSeek Harness desktop window automatically on startup, equivalent to clicking the maximize button once. Cross-platform (Windows/macOS/Linux), best-effort, host-only.
- [fightingFirefox/dsh-web-terminal](https://github.com/fightingFirefox/dsh-web-terminal) - Claude-styled terminal panel for the DSH web GUI: agent shell commands run in a persistent real PTY with a genuine prompt, echo and exit codes, auto-open in the right sidebar and are scoped per conversation; optional live_terminal_* tools for reading, searching and screenshotting. Fork of Lancang-hzau/dsh-live-terminal (MIT); it shares its live_terminal_* tools and right-sidebar panel, so do not install it together with the upstream plugin in the same profile.
- [FinalLawer/dsh-native-code-workbench#dsh-code-workbench](https://github.com/FinalLawer/dsh-native-code-workbench/tree/master/dsh-code-workbench) - Code workbench in the right sidebar: a workspace file tree and a multi-tab Monaco editor with syntax highlighting and version-guarded saving, Tab completion from DeepSeek fill-in-the-middle, per-save checkpoints with rollback, codebase search (plus a codebase_search tool for the agent), and reference chips that insert a selection into the chat as an @path mention.
- [Findly-qwb/dsh-model-picker](https://github.com/Findly-qwb/dsh-model-picker) - Two-pane model picker that replaces the composer model menu with a searchable provider-grouped list on the left and a detail card on the right showing the selected model's context window, output cap, capability tags and reasoning-effort levels, all read from that model's own provider. The npm name dsh-model-picker belongs to another author; install through this GitHub source.
- [Firefly-TheHerta/dsh-boot-video-manager](https://github.com/Firefly-TheHerta/dsh-boot-video-manager) - A companion playlist panel for the dsh-boot-animation intro-video plugin: add clips without leaving the app, order them, and switch between sequential, random and single-clip playback. Sequential order is made deterministic by parking every clip except the one due to play in a disabled/ subdirectory of the same asset folder and rotating it back in on each boot, leaving the upstream plugin unpatched. Also supports recycling instead of deleting, pinning one clip for every launch, per-clip opt-out, and .mp4/.webm/.m4v/.mov files including non-ASCII names.
- [fishcg/dsh-input-prediction](https://github.com/fishcg/dsh-input-prediction) - Input prediction: the likely continuation of your draft appears as grey ghost text after the caret and Tab accepts it, and after each assistant turn 1-3 clickable follow-up suggestions appear above the input box and send on click. Ghost completion, suggestions and the suggestion count can be toggled in Settings, Built-in plugins.
- [fishcg/dsh-recent-tasks](https://github.com/fishcg/dsh-recent-tasks) - A collapsible Recent tasks folder at the top of the sidebar above 工作区: the most recently updated sessions, pinning that ignores recency, running-state spinners, and a per-row menu with rename, fork and archive.
- [flowers-59f/dsh-petpal](https://github.com/flowers-59f/dsh-petpal) - Download pets from petdex.dev and keep one on the desktop, following the DSH session status; the appearance is adjustable and an enterprise WeChat bot can push a notification when the agent needs confirmation or finishes a turn. Desktop mode needs macOS 12+ or Windows 10 1903+; the bundled companion is prebuilt.
- [flycucu/dsh-icon-changer](https://github.com/flycucu/dsh-icon-changer) - Replaces the DeepSeek Harness desktop app icon (exe, tray and Start Menu) with any uploaded .ico, and restores the stock icon on demand.
- [flyfly6/dsh-panes](https://github.com/flyfly6/dsh-panes) - Full-screen workspace panel for DSH: a project sidebar with per-project tabs for session, files, explorer, project search, terminal, browser, source control and a Markdown notes vault.
- [flyhigao/dsh-file-transfer](https://github.com/flyhigao/dsh-file-transfer) - Adds download buttons to the document preview toolbar and the Files tree, packs a folder download into a streamed ZIP, and adds a Files-tree upload button that streams to the Host one file at a time, capped at 1 GiB and renamed with an (n) suffix instead of overwriting.
- [flyhigao/dsh-sticky-notes](https://github.com/flyhigao/dsh-sticky-notes) - Workspace sticky notes for DSH, saved as multiple Markdown files under the current workspace.
- [foooold/dsh-message-nav](https://github.com/foooold/dsh-message-nav) - Adds a Codex-style message navigation rail for quickly locating earlier prompts in long DeepSeek Harness Web UI conversations.
- [foreee/dsh-app-restart](https://github.com/foreee/dsh-app-restart) - Adds a Restart application section to the desktop app Settings; quits gracefully and relaunches the whole application.
- [foreee/dsh-net-dashboard](https://github.com/foreee/dsh-net-dashboard) - Live network inspection dashboard in the sidebar: interfaces with IP/MAC, traffic rates, TCP/UDP session counts and latency curves, refreshed every 3 seconds. macOS only (the collectors run ifconfig, netstat, scutil and /sbin/ping).
- [ForsakenDelusion/dsh-branchnest](https://github.com/ForsakenDelusion/dsh-branchnest) - Project canvas for DSH sessions, with linked conversation cards, text-selection follow-up branches, notes, and a document-style reader.
- [fountainhead-gq/dsh-weread-gather](https://github.com/fountainhead-gq/dsh-weread-gather) - Digital Study: a private WeRead study in the DSH sidebar. The shelf folds into ten categories, and highlights and thoughts page through, copy per item, and export as front-matter Markdown (one file per book or a cross-book batch) into a folder you choose; the whole study backs up and restores as a single JSON bundle. A reading heatmap and a year-switched KPI dashboard sit on top, and search covers shelf, books, highlights and thoughts with type filters and Cmd+K. Reads WeRead through the official agent gateway using your own API key, keeps everything in local JSON and never writes back. Requires DSH >=0.2.2-rc.1.
- [FragrantLotus/dsh-plugin-chinese](https://github.com/FragrantLotus/dsh-plugin-chinese) - Fills in a Chinese description for every installed DSH plugin that ships only an English one, at startup and again after each plugin change, and never rewrites a plugin name. Plugins that already export a locale folder show the new text on refresh; the rest are patched too — the in-memory layer shows Chinese immediately, and the on-disk locale pack it writes needs one restart.
- [FragrantLotus/dsh-sidebar-entries](https://github.com/FragrantLotus/dsh-sidebar-entries) - Sidebar overhaul for the dsh web UI: long-press drag to reorder the panel icon rail, one arrow to fold it away, whole-row height caps on both the panel list and the pinned section (30% of the rail, rounded down to whole rows), a pinned-session section that mirrors the shipped row's live status (spinner, waiting-for-reply, relative time), drag-to-reorder conversation view tabs, archived Sessions sunk to the bottom of each group newest-first, forked child Sessions following their parent into the pinned section, and a generic lift that moves any plugin's sidebar-footer entry up into the rail. Each subsystem starts behind its own guard, so one that fails returns null and the rest of the plugin still mounts.
- [framefutura/dsh-mode-seat](https://github.com/framefutura/dsh-mode-seat) - Persistent agent-preset (mode) selector docked in the composer input bar: switch the mode for new tasks, and set the new-task default from any running session.
- [Free-corner/dsh-chat-tools](https://github.com/Free-corner/dsh-chat-tools) - Chat column width and alignment control, numbered question history with keyboard navigation and auto-load of older questions, and markdown outline rails for both the conversation and the file preview.
- [Friedeggrice/dsh-stealth-reader](https://github.com/Friedeggrice/dsh-stealth-reader) - Reads a few lines of a txt or epub novel inside the sidebar bottom panel, styled to pass for scratch notes; pages with Ctrl+Alt+J/K and keeps progress locally. Requires dsh-better-sidebar 0.26+ - the reader exists only as a tab in its bottom panel.
- [Frog755/dsh-prompt-vault](https://github.com/Frog755/dsh-prompt-vault) - Prompt library above the DSH composer — a 📚 button expands a searchable panel to insert saved prompts into the draft with one click (appends, never overwrites), persisted in a host-side JSON file.
- [Frog755/dsh-wallpaper](https://github.com/Frog755/dsh-wallpaper) - Persistent image or MP4 wallpaper for the DSH Web profile: a wallpaper card under Settings -> General selects a local image or MP4 (up to 300 MB), tunes surface opacity and blur, compresses video locally to H.264 when ffmpeg/ffprobe are on PATH, stores media under ~/.dsh/wallpapers, and pins the Web server to port 9191 so the saved wallpaper survives restarts.
- [FrontierMindLab/dsh-quick-compact#dsh-quick-compact](https://github.com/FrontierMindLab/dsh-quick-compact/tree/main/plugins/dsh-quick-compact) - Context ring gauge in the DSH composer toolbar: view usage, open a breakdown panel, and run /compact from the panel, with a fireworks animation.
- [FSMargoo/dsh-at-file](https://github.com/FSMargoo/dsh-at-file) - Codex-style `@file` mentions: search workspace files in the composer and attach their contents to prompts.
- [fu-silent/dsh-sidebar-home](https://github.com/fu-silent/dsh-sidebar-home) - Adds a Home panel to the sidebar for choosing a workspace; focus mode then shows only that workspace's sessions and folds the other workspaces below, and exiting focus restores the shipped sidebar.
- [FujiwaraReisen/dsh-live2dwaifu](https://github.com/FujiwaraReisen/dsh-live2dwaifu) - Shows a Live2D character in the bottom-right corner of the DSH web UI: blinking, breathing, lip-sync while talking, seven moods, and a speech bubble with its own chat, memory, corpus and stickers. Models are never bundled; every user drops in their own folder.
- [functy23/dsh-fullscreen-settings](https://github.com/functy23/dsh-fullscreen-settings) - Opens the DSH settings dialog as a full-page screen and moves the close control to a back row at the top left.
- [FunnyDiodio/dsh-workspace-picker-path](https://github.com/FunnyDiodio/dsh-workspace-picker-path) - Adds a General settings row choosing where the Add-workspace directory picker opens: a fixed custom path (typed, pasted, or picked visually) or the last directory you confirmed. Quoted and CJK paths are accepted, and a This PC level reaches other Windows drives.
- [funswe/dsh-resizable-settings](https://github.com/funswe/dsh-resizable-settings) - Makes the DSH Settings dialog resizable from its edges and corners and movable by its top strip, remembering the chosen size across opens.
- [FuqiangCraft/dsh-desktop#packages/dsh-desktop-plugin](https://github.com/FuqiangCraft/dsh-desktop/tree/main/packages/dsh-desktop-plugin) - Desktop-grade companion: browser native notifications for user questions and approval interactions, screen capture model tool, multi-agent tiling grid view, and lightweight Tauri desktop shell.
- [FurinaEsE/dsh-ui-zoom-regions](https://github.com/FurinaEsE/dsh-ui-zoom-regions) - Per-region UI zoom for DeepSeek Harness — Ctrl + wheel sets independent zoom levels for the conversation header, the left sidebar, the conversation body and the right sidebar (sidebars 25%-300%), and dragging the header bottom edge makes that header shorter or taller, growing by height only so label size stays unchanged.
- [FurinaLiF/dsh-tab-complete](https://github.com/FurinaLiF/dsh-tab-complete) - Predicts the next user message as composer ghost text using the configured DSH model; Tab fills the draft without sending, with time-aware context and cached suggestions.
- [FuriousKinght/dsh-restart-button](https://github.com/FuriousKinght/dsh-restart-button) - Adds a Windows-only button at the foot of the sidebar that restarts the DSH desktop app and reloads the page when it comes back.
- [furkancak1r/dsh-composer-plus](https://github.com/furkancak1r/dsh-composer-plus) - Composer and sidebar helpers in one plugin, each switchable in Settings: a git Changes panel (uncommitted / unstaged / staged / branch diffs), Turn rollback (chat only, or chat and files), Project memory (notes per project that later chats read, off by default), a Task board (kanban board of chats by their live state), Assistant (one lasting chat that sets reminders with dsh scheduled tasks and reads your other chats; it changes a chat only after your approval), Remote computers (the dsh of another Mac over Tailscale in the same window, off by default), One-click Continue, Auto Review "Allow for this chat", 11 extra UI languages and Automatic updates from GitHub releases.
- [FurryBear2025/dsh-session-drag-move](https://github.com/FurryBear2025/dsh-session-drag-move) - Drag a session onto another workspace in the DSH sidebar and it really moves: the session-log header cwd is rewritten and the log directory is re-homed under the target project, with a progress bar while it runs.
- [future007s/dsh-peak-indicator](https://github.com/future007s/dsh-peak-indicator) - Session header badge showing DeepSeek peak/off-peak status, per-model token prices on hover, and real session/turn costs from actual usage.
- [Fyerl/dsh-2d-model-picker](https://github.com/Fyerl/dsh-2d-model-picker) - Select a model and its reasoning effort in one drag with a two-dimensional grid.
- [fyisgod/dsh-selection-tools](https://github.com/fyisgod/dsh-selection-tools) - Select text in any Windows app to explain or translate it with the Harness agent, answered in a native always-on-top overlay.
- [fysh1010/mcp-server-fanqie#deepseek-harness](https://github.com/fysh1010/mcp-server-fanqie/tree/main/plugins/deepseek-harness) - Fanqie reader for the DeepSeek Harness sidebar: search book and media metadata, browse covers and chapter lists, read website-visible chapters, and view your bookshelf and saved highlights after QR sign-in.
- [g-yixuan/dsh-sidenote](https://github.com/g-yixuan/dsh-sidenote) - Side chats and selection annotations for DeepSeek Harness, preserving full conversation history and bringing findings back to the main thread.
- [G57651/dsh-diy-layout](https://github.com/G57651/dsh-diy-layout) - Customizes the DSH home sidebar plugin entries without harness source changes: one- or two-column panel list, icon-only mode with 500ms hover tooltips, and per-entry ordering, hiding, and a settings page for the upper panel area and the footer plugin row.
- [gaog88666-boop/dsh-legal-workbench](https://github.com/gaog88666-boop/dsh-legal-workbench) - A right-sidebar workbench for legal practice backed by a folder of Markdown notes: SOP lookup, recordings with live transcription and line-level annotations, an inline glossary, and full-text search. Optional email rules library, Pkulaw points and usage badges. Notes, recordings and annotations stay on the local machine; live transcription needs your own Volcengine key and sends audio to Volcengine.
- [gaor0605-kq/dsh-workspace-explorer](https://github.com/gaor0605-kq/dsh-workspace-explorer) - Adds a Chats/Files switch to the left-sidebar Workspace header that swaps the session list for a lazily loaded tree of the current session's workspace. Files and folders can be dragged into the composer as @path references; clicking a file opens the built-in preview; the right-click menu reveals a file in the system file manager or copies its path; entries carry VS Code-style Git status (modified, untracked, added, deleted, conflict, ignored) relative to the last commit.
- [gaoyinxin/dsh-mode-switcher](https://github.com/gaoyinxin/dsh-mode-switcher) - Adds a Plan/Build mode toggle to the composer, switched with the Tab key or a toolbar button, and remembers a separate model for each mode from the native model selector.
- [garrisonz/dsh-sidebar-width](https://github.com/garrisonz/dsh-sidebar-width) - Control the Web UI left sidebar width: lower the 264px drag minimum and optionally tune the drag maximum and the expanded default width, applied by a startup patch of the ui-layout bundle.
- [gbeta/dsh-token-speed](https://github.com/gbeta/dsh-token-speed) - Draggable ring gauge showing live model output speed (tok/s) with an expandable per-step detail panel (exact speed, TTFT, cache-hit rate, cache read/write, reasoning and input tokens, model, cumulative tokens).
- [GeekRicardo/dsh-balance](https://github.com/GeekRicardo/dsh-balance) - Composer-dock balance/usage readout for DeepSeek, Kimi Coding, OpenCode Go, Zhipu, MiniMax, OpenRouter and OpenAI Codex — provider-based, 2s polling, 5min cache.
- [GeekRicardo/dsh-convmap](https://github.com/GeekRicardo/dsh-convmap) - Conversation-map rail down the left edge of the transcript: one tick per user turn, read from the full session log on the host rather than from the rendered page, so long sessions are covered end to end. Hovering fans the neighbouring ticks open by pixel distance and floats a scrollable preview of that turn prompt and its last reply; clicking scrolls to the turn, paging older history in first when it has not been rendered yet; the tick for the visible turn stays highlighted while you scroll.
- [geguanming/dsh-office-plugin](https://github.com/geguanming/dsh-office-plugin) - Renders live multi-agent session activity as a pixel-art office in a Web UI side panel - each session is an ox/horse worker typing at its desk when busy or wandering off when idle, with click-to-view work monitors and in-place approval cards.
- [GEMLZC/dsh-font-studio](https://github.com/GEMLZC/dsh-font-studio) - Typography settings page: text, code and terminal font size and family, plus interface, conversation and code text colour, weight, italic, letter spacing and line height, with a live preview.
- [genius-alray/dsh-model-picker](https://github.com/genius-alray/dsh-model-picker) - Requires DSH 0.1.5-rc.2+. Dialog-style model picker for the DSH Web UI: fuzzy search, collapsible provider groups, favorites with a favorites-only filter, and a thinking-level dropdown, replacing the built-in model selector with official UI primitives and DSW tokens.
- [GeniusHu-tgty/dsh-prompt-better](https://github.com/GeniusHu-tgty/dsh-prompt-better) - Adds a button to the composer that rewrites the current draft into a clearer, more specific prompt through a one-shot LLM call, with one-click revert and cancel.
- [germyGao/dsh-select-assist](https://github.com/germyGao/dsh-select-assist) - Select text in any Windows app to explain, summarize, translate, or read it aloud with DeepSeek Harness — answered in a streaming always-on-top overlay with a horizontal capsule menu.
- [gezi-wen/sage-reminder](https://github.com/gezi-wen/sage-reminder) - A time module in the DSH session header: current time, time since your last message, reminders, and countdowns. Pure browser-side - data lives in localStorage and the host half registers nothing. Alerts are a border glow plus an in-app toast; no system notifications.
- [gf691271/dsh-quote-reply](https://github.com/gf691271/dsh-quote-reply) - Quote and reply the way Claude Code does it: select text, answer it in a popover, stack the pairs as blocks above the composer, send them as one message.
- [gghyoo/dsh-download-image](https://github.com/gghyoo/dsh-download-image) - Adds a Download PNG button next to the assistant message copy button in the DSH Web UI.
- [Ghidorahchaos/dsh-cherry-quote](https://github.com/Ghidorahchaos/dsh-cherry-quote) - Quote selected text from a DSH message into the composer as a blue chip, hover to read the full quote, and remove it with X or Backspace; sent quotes reach the model as a blockquote.
- [giiiiiithub/terminal](https://github.com/giiiiiithub/terminal) - Real PTY terminal panel for the DSH Web UI: host half spawns shells with node-pty (cmd.exe default on Windows), rendered in the browser with xterm.js; multi-tab sessions, dock/floating window, clipboard support.
- [GinoXiang/dsh-dailyboard](https://github.com/GinoXiang/dsh-dailyboard) - DailyBoard — a daily work task board for the DSH Web GUI with a one-month calendar of colored timeline bars and day tasks, a daily checklist, a seven-day week view with completion checkboxes and drag-to-reschedule, a draggable 24-hour day timeline, ordered per-day subtasks, weekly and every-N-days recurring tasks with per-occurrence result records, a long-term progress view opened from the calendar header, per-day completion for long-term tasks, tasks that can be marked “For others” by choosing one of the added recipients (shown as note + email), a collapsible daily email reminder that can be pushed to WeChat and can remind other people such as partners or relatives (each extra recipient has an email, a note and its own send time), and AI task extraction from natural language with smart date parsing: a single date such as “today” becomes a day task, “this week/month” starts from today unless the text says the work already happened, and pressing Enter in the input box extracts immediately.
- [GinoXiang/dsh-output-style](https://github.com/GinoXiang/dsh-output-style) - Two split composer controls for output style: a tone switch with its own preset menu, and an output-form switch with its own preset menu; each session's system prompt follows the selections, with a preset settings page.
- [gitByteFree/dsh-pomodoro-pet](https://github.com/gitByteFree/dsh-pomodoro-pet) - Pomodoro timer with four-quadrant tasks: bind focus rounds to a task, grow a desktop pet with EXP and achievements, and let the agent start rounds or propose tasks.
- [gjj-star/dsh-conversation-navigator](https://github.com/gjj-star/dsh-conversation-navigator) - Conversation navigator panel: turn-folded outline of the current session with per-step kind badges (assistant, tool, command, compaction, error), a hover bubble showing the full user question, a minimal mode that hides turn numbers for an all-badge view, keyword filter over user questions and assistant replies, click-to-jump with reading-position tracking, and load-earlier/load-all history paging. Host >= 0.1.2-rc.1; legacy hosts install @legacy/0.2.5.
- [GLFzr/dsh-file-upload](https://github.com/GLFzr/dsh-file-upload) - Codex-style drag-drop: drag any file into the DSH web GUI, it lands in ~/.dsh-dropbox, and the path is inserted into the composer as a whole blue chip.
- [GnaneshKunal/dsh-menubar#plugin](https://github.com/GnaneshKunal/dsh-menubar/tree/main/plugin) - The menu bar app itself is a separate AppKit build from the repo's app/ directory (swift build -c release on macOS); the plugin only provides the host bridge.
- [GodLike2020/dsh-mqtt-tools](https://github.com/GodLike2020/dsh-mqtt-tools) - MQTT client and embedded broker for DeepSeek Harness: one sidebar panel with saved connections, a live message log and a local broker, plus nine mqtt_* agent tools.
- [golddream-y/ratel-dsh-lumina](https://github.com/golddream-y/ratel-dsh-lumina) - Make development more fun. A subtle layer of motion for DeepSeek Harness, with customizable effects and reduced-motion support.
- [GoldenLoaf24h/dsh-prompt-suggestion](https://github.com/GoldenLoaf24h/dsh-prompt-suggestion) - Predicts your next message after each turn and shows it as gray ghost text in the composer: Tab takes the whole line, Alt+Right takes one word at a time, Enter takes it and sends, Esc dismisses, and Alt+R asks the model again without repeating the old one. Typing a few characters completes from prompts already sent in the session, Up in an empty composer recalls the last one, and a plugin-page form sets the provider, model, reasoning effort, per-key toggles and generation limits.
- [gongstudent/dsh-quote-to-agent](https://github.com/gongstudent/dsh-quote-to-agent) - Quote text selected in a DSH Web conversation, then either attach it to the current composer or dispatch it to a subagent session: the quote sits on a bar above the composer, or opens that subagent in a new pane on the right. When the target has no subagent yet, the quote is held and the composer is seeded with a spawn request, then picked up automatically once the new session appears. Client-only.
- [GooDAnDReaDY/dsh-russian-lang](https://github.com/GooDAnDReaDY/dsh-russian-lang) - Adds Russian as a native option in Settings - General - Language for DeepSeek Harness, covering both the core UI and companion plugins. Localized Markdown export, in-message translation, live typography, slash-command aliases and an Alt+L layout fix, plus a settings card, all applied through a runtime locale snapshot rather than by modifying core files.
- [goodboys155/dsh-github-radar](https://github.com/goodboys155/dsh-github-radar) - Right-sidebar GitHub panel with four rising-repository lists — created within 2 days, 7 days, 30 days, and 20k+ stars still pushed — where the first three are ranked by star velocity (stars per day) rather than total stars, so repositories that gained traction fast surface above older ones with more stars. Each row shows the repository name, its own description, language, the stars-per-day rate and up to two topics, and links to the repository. A keyword search box queries the GitHub Search API directly, debounced and IME-aware so a Chinese or Japanese composition costs one request instead of one per keystroke. Reads the public GitHub Search API anonymously: no token, no login, no account, nothing else contacted. Lists cache for 30 minutes and search results for 5; outbound requests go direct first and fall back to a listening local proxy port only if direct fails.
- [GooodWei/arcana](https://github.com/GooodWei/arcana) - A floating command deck that lists every slash command in DeepSeek Harness as runnable buttons, sorted by usage.
- [gorban/dsh-job-stop](https://github.com/gorban/dsh-job-stop) - Stop a running background job from the session-header job list, with a confirmation dialog that restates the full command.
- [gordan-code/dsh-jev-advisor](https://github.com/gordan-code/dsh-jev-advisor) - Adds human-facing TypeSafe Jev advice to DSH multiple-choice prompts, on demand by default with optional automatic requests, recommendations, probabilities, confidence, close-call warnings, copyable suggestions, one-click adoption, profile-scoped card layouts, temporary decision preferences, optional request previews with common-credential masking, and local health checks with sanitized diagnostic exports.
- [Gorkaseto/dsh-effort-picker](https://github.com/Gorkaseto/dsh-effort-picker) - Adds a sticky effort slider inside the composer model menu to select reasoning levels, with generic stops for custom models.
- [Grampusworld/dsh-prompt-optimizor](https://github.com/Grampusworld/dsh-prompt-optimizor) - Rewrites the composer draft into a tighter prompt in one click and restores the exact original in another, running on Harness's own models by default or any OpenAI-compatible endpoint, with an editable polishing prompt.
- [GraptK/dsh-composer-enhance](https://github.com/GraptK/dsh-composer-enhance) - Rewrites the composer draft, or only the selected text, with one model call that uses the recent conversation as context, with cancel, revert, fidelity and length checks, and an optional preview.
- [GraptK/dsh-quick-restart](https://github.com/GraptK/dsh-quick-restart) - Adds a Restart item to the DeepSeek Harness desktop account menu, with confirmation, progress and failure reporting.
- [gu040303-ai/dsh-prompt-enhancer](https://github.com/gu040303-ai/dsh-prompt-enhancer) - One-click plain-text prompt enhancement with optional local checks and sentence review in DeepSeek Harness.
- [GuanYang14700/dsh-hermes-panel](https://github.com/GuanYang14700/dsh-hermes-panel) - Hermes in the DSH sidebar: start and stop the gateway and dashboard, watch the dashboard beside the chat, and bridge DSH to Hermes. Windows only; requires an existing Hermes install.
- [guchang/draw2code](https://github.com/guchang/draw2code) - An Excalidraw prototyping tab for dsh-better-sidebar with structured product clarification, conflict-aware human and Agent board updates, and prototype-guided frontend generation.
- [guhanfei-ai/dsh-mindmap](https://github.com/guhanfei-ai/dsh-mindmap) - Conversational Markdown mindmap workspace: edit complete documents step by step, keep a live right-side visualization synchronized, and open, manage, and export mindmaps from the working directory.
- [GuihaiHU/dsh-temp-note](https://github.com/GuihaiHU/dsh-temp-note) - A temp note in the DSH right sidebar with session, workspace, and global scopes, browser-local autosave, one-click copy, and one-click LLM polish through the default model.
- [gulagala001/dsh-deepseek-workbench](https://github.com/gulagala001/dsh-deepseek-workbench) - Desktop only. DeepSeek web chat, temporary side conversations, the API platform and conversation migration in DSH.
- [gulagala001/dsh-intelligent-ui](https://github.com/gulagala001/dsh-intelligent-ui) - Native interactive answers, editable cards and on-demand UI skills for DSH.
- [guo-ziao/dsh-interrupt-button](https://github.com/guo-ziao/dsh-interrupt-button) - A green interrupt button beside the send box: silently stops a running agent, which then summarizes its progress and asks for your new requirements, with a customizable interrupt prompt.
- [guoliyuan97-png/dsh-game-hud](https://github.com/guoliyuan97-png/dsh-game-hud) - Game-style floating HUD: balance HP bar, context MP bar, official peak/valley pricing with countdown, auto-compaction under 5% remaining, and memory-carrying new conversation.
- [H1Kariiiiiii/dsh-icon-console](https://github.com/H1Kariiiiiii/dsh-icon-console) - Icon console for the DSH desktop app: import one icon and apply it to the desktop and Start Menu shortcuts (immediately), the application window image and the system tray image (after a restart), with automatic backup, one-click restore, a named icon library, and restart/exit controls.
- [hadesybil-hub/dsh-restart-button](https://github.com/hadesybil-hub/dsh-restart-button) - Adds a restart button next to the shutdown button in the bottom-right of the DSH Web GUI; the server comes back up and the browser reopens.
- [hahajing233-source/dsh-sticky-wall](https://github.com/hahajing233-source/dsh-sticky-wall) - Sticky notes on a wall, with five paper stocks, five inks, seven typefaces, four text sizes and your choice of pin, tape or clip.
- [HAN-050917/dsh-plugin-dsweb-harness](https://github.com/HAN-050917/dsh-plugin-dsweb-harness) - Use DeepSeek's web chat inside DSH: real chat history in the leftmost sidebar, a Chat/Work switch beside the agent mode, plus streaming answers, thinking, web search and file uploads.
- [Han-1413141/dsh-jev-thinkout](https://github.com/Han-1413141/dsh-jev-thinkout) - Shows JeV-selected task plans, progress updates and stage summaries in DSH chat, with native translation or lightweight summaries. Requires a JeV key; the Jev service is called externally and may bill. and hierarchical serial/parallel task flows.
- [Han-1413141/dsh-sticky-disclosure](https://github.com/Han-1413141/dsh-sticky-disclosure) - One-click collapse of every expanded section (Think rows, tool cards) with a live-count pill and a customizable hotkey.
- [Han-1413141/dsh-ui-hub](https://github.com/Han-1413141/dsh-ui-hub) - UI butler for every plugin UI: official/plugin categories, per-widget toggles, drag move/resize, collision avoidance and one-click auto arrange.
- [han8421/dsh-desktop-ball](https://github.com/han8421/dsh-desktop-ball) - Windows-only desktop floating ball with a bilingual WebView2 quick-chat window, including in-window question and approval cards.
- [HanaAyane/dsh-reasoning-effort](https://github.com/HanaAyane/dsh-reasoning-effort) - Adds an animated DeepSeek Chan reasoning-effort slider to DSH's native model selector, following model-declared levels with a running animation that speeds up while dragging.
- [hanbernate/dsh-save-button](https://github.com/hanbernate/dsh-save-button) - Adds a Download button to DSH Web GUI delivery cards, so files delivered with present save straight from the browser.
- [HandsYe/dsh-input-history](https://github.com/HandsYe/dsh-input-history) - Persistent prompt history for the DSH composer — Up/Down recalls sent prompts across restarts, Up with text typed filters by prefix, and cleared drafts are not recorded.
- [Hanmiao33/dsh-bubble-explain](https://github.com/Hanmiao33/dsh-bubble-explain) - Select any text in a conversation and tap the Explain button to get a streaming Markdown explanation bubble, with recursive follow-up questions.
- [hanrr92/dsh-code-quote](https://github.com/hanrr92/dsh-code-quote) - Composer code-quote collapse for the DSH Web UI: pasting a `path:line` header followed by code folds the input into one compact inline quote chip, and at send time the `agent/pre-step` hook injects the full pasted snapshot to the model as a separate context message. 0.5.x also folds sidebar file-selections that insert only a `path:lines` line (large selections), fetching the code from disk by session cwd, and preempts Ctrl+V paste interception.
- [HansHuang2683/dsh-quote-annotate](https://github.com/HansHuang2683/dsh-quote-annotate) - Select any passage in a DSH conversation to annotate it in place; the quoted text, the turn it came from and your comment are folded into the message on send, so the model can tell exactly what each remark refers to.
- [hansomgar/dsh-settings-resize](https://github.com/hansomgar/dsh-settings-resize) - Resize the Settings dialog and set its transparency from three sliders in General settings; the choice persists across restarts.
- [HanYu-me/dsh-project-kanban](https://github.com/HanYu-me/dsh-project-kanban) - A kanban board in the dsh sidebar. Every project gets its own section with four fixed columns (todo, in progress, feedback, done); add, rename, recolor, reorder and delete projects and tasks, advance a task with one click, reorder by drag and drop or with the card menu, undo a deletion, and collapse a project. Colors, radii and shadows come from the dsh theme, so it follows light and dark, and the layout reflows from four columns to two to one.
- [HaoyueQin/dsh-diff-stat](https://github.com/HaoyueQin/dsh-diff-stat) - Inline +N −M badges on edit/write tool rows and a collapsible per-turn file-change summary card with aligned diffs, review and undo — covers native edit/write, str_replace_editor and Code Dispatch sub-calls, no git required.
- [HapyRain/dsh-router-laya](https://github.com/HapyRain/dsh-router-laya) - Auto thinking-effort routing for dsh: a locally fine-tuned 7-question classifier (ModernBERT, 842MB, <1s CPU inference) picks low/high/max for no-intent messages; in-session retries escalate the tier (low to high to max) under exclusion constraints; the judge service fails safe to low if unreachable. A tier chip in the input bar shows the live tier and the last 20 judgments. All inference is local - task text never leaves the machine.
