- [Harzva/dsh-superterminal](https://github.com/Harzva/dsh-superterminal) - Native terminals for DSH 0.1.1-rc.2 on macOS, with local terminal AI sessions, SSH terminals that reconnect to the same remote task, bounded Terminal Group discussions, and handoffs with acceptance and rework.
- [hatsuyuki0103/dsh-at-any](https://github.com/hatsuyuki0103/dsh-at-any) - Codex-style `@file` mentions for the web GUI: search and reference workspace files of every format (.java, .vue, PDF, images, extension-less, hidden) with no index truncation. Replaces dsh-at-file.
- [hawkongz/dsh-chat-locator](https://github.com/hawkongz/dsh-chat-locator) - Turn-rail settings for the DSH conversation locator: tick thickness, rail on the left or right, a curved length gradient on the hovered tick, and a plain-text hover preview with adjustable line count, font size and width.
- [HaydenSmith1121/dsh-wallhaven-wallpaper](https://github.com/HaydenSmith1121/dsh-wallhaven-wallpaper) - Searches wallhaven.cc from a settings page and sets any wallpaper as the DSH web GUI background, with opacity, blur and scrim controls, a sidebar shuffle button and one-click original download; all fetching runs in the host process, so the browser never needs to reach wallhaven.
- [He2way/dsh-task-console](https://github.com/He2way/dsh-task-console) - Flip the DSH page over to a glass task board showing live jobs, subagents and session state, with an infinite canvas that unrolls a card into draggable controls, embedded web-app frames, and a conversation box that edits the canvas.
- [HEArtattaCK2332/dsh-text-explain](https://github.com/HEArtattaCK2332/dsh-text-explain) - Select a passage in a DSH conversation and read a plain-language explanation in a floating panel, which carries the surrounding message as the context. Three explanation depths (plain / general / pro) that re-explain on the spot when switched; optional web search and local file lookup (the model decides by default, and a button can force one lookup that reports only what is new); follow-up questions; and a button that hands the whole exchange to a new session in standby mode - the background is queued without waking the model or spending tokens. Settings live on the host in settings.json, and nothing is written until you press save. The repository also ships an OpenAI-compatible endpoint and a browser extension (source).
- [Heeweelee/dsh-prompt-suggestions](https://github.com/Heeweelee/dsh-prompt-suggestions) - Composer suggestions for the DSH chat input: Up / Down step back and forth through every prompt sent in the current session — read from the session event log, so a message accepted from a prediction or sent by queueing counts too — Ctrl+Up opens a searchable history picker, and grey ghost text predicts the next message, accepted with the Right arrow. Every key only edits the draft; nothing is ever sent automatically.
- [heiheiha798/dsh-plugin-response-window](https://github.com/heiheiha798/dsh-plugin-response-window) - Web UI plugin that folds each staged response's think and tool calls into per-segment bounded, scrollable slide windows with a configurable height, keeping every intermediate response visible.
- [heiyedehen/dsh-whale-whispers](https://github.com/heiyedehen/dsh-whale-whispers) - A tab in the conversation view bar that keeps the lines your agent wanted to keep, with tools that let it add, list and remove them.
- [hellmage/dsh-chat-lanes](https://github.com/hellmage/dsh-chat-lanes) - Splits the DSH chat window into two columns: your messages pinned in a clickable left rail and the model replies flowing on the right, with Enter-to-search across the conversation.
- [hellofuture2068/dsh-simple-view](https://github.com/hellofuture2068/dsh-simple-view) - Declutters the DSH chat: hides agent execution-log rows, tightens spacing and fonts, wraps messages in bubbles, and injects an editable reply-concisely system instruction.
- [helloworld010234/dsh-fluid-chat-width](https://github.com/helloworld010234/dsh-fluid-chat-width) - Fluid conversation width for the DSH web UI: the chat column follows the window minus a 64px margin, capped at 1560px and floored at 640px, while the built-in drag handles still override it.
- [HelmsmanOfDesire/dsh-countdown](https://github.com/HelmsmanOfDesire/dsh-countdown) - A workday countdown shown as an hourglass on every DSH page. The sand volume follows the elapsed fraction of the current work period; periods can be added, removed and edited, including ones that span midnight, and when the clock is outside every period the panel says so instead of counting.
- [逐段翻译 · dsh-bilingual](https://github.com/Hemuchuan/dsh-bilingual) - Adds paragraph-by-paragraph translation and a bilingual view to assistant replies in the DeepSeek Harness desktop app: each Chinese paragraph gets an English translation underneath and each English paragraph gets a Chinese one underneath, behind a session-header toggle; code blocks and formulas stay untranslated, and tables get a translated copy underneath. Translations come from the DeepSeek API (the plugin reuses DSH's saved DEEPSEEK_API_KEY, or a key you paste in its settings), so it needs network access and each uncached paragraph is a billable request.
- [HengryLiang/dsh-plugin-ai-drama](https://github.com/HengryLiang/dsh-plugin-ai-drama) - AI short-drama workspace for DeepSeek Harness, with a dedicated drama agent preset, 35 agent tools and 10 bundled skills covering outline, characters, script, storyboard and export.
- [HERO476/dsh-sidecard-ask](https://github.com/HERO476/dsh-sidecard-ask) - Select text in the DSH Web GUI and ask about it in place (floating button, or the Alt+Q shortcut): an independent subagent that inherits no parent context answers, streaming into a side card hosted by the native right sidebar, dsh-better-sidebar, or a built-in overlay — and the question can be routed to the main conversation instead.
- [HeShen-1/dsh-composer-enhance](https://github.com/HeShen-1/dsh-composer-enhance) - Composer prompt enhancement for DSH Web: a ✨ button rewrites the current draft in place after asking only about genuine ambiguity, and an empty composer shows a grey next-message ghost that Tab or a click fills into the draft without sending.
- [heshuren371/dsh-music-player](https://github.com/heshuren371/dsh-music-player) - Local music player: scans a folder for audio and video files, plays them in a Music tab with crossfade, a 10-band EQ and ReplayGain, and can write matched tags, covers and filenames back to the files with atomic replacement.
- [hg1048596-pixel/dsh-ppt-canvas](https://github.com/hg1048596-pixel/dsh-ppt-canvas) - Element-level PowerPoint canvas that edits the opened .pptx in place: click, marquee-select or pick a whole slide, describe the change, and the AI writes byte-preserving OOXML patches with undo and redo.
- [hg1048596-pixel/dsh-recall-unread](https://github.com/hg1048596-pixel/dsh-recall-unread) - Adds a strip above the composer that lists sent-but-unread (steering) text messages and recalls them before the model reads them, one by one or all at once.
- [hiJoeLee/dsh-suggest-actions](https://github.com/hiJoeLee/dsh-suggest-actions) - Up to 5 clickable next steps under every reply — one click sends that sentence as your message. No extra model call, duplicate angles dropped, the recommended one moved to the top, and hovering shows the full text. A small button on the right fills the composer (clearing it first) so you can edit before sending.
- [hiJoeLee/dsh-whale-brand](https://github.com/hiJoeLee/dsh-whale-brand) - Puts your own animated vector logo over the DSH brand mark in the sidebar and beside the start-page headline, taking over the three single slots sidebar.brand.mark, sidebar.brand.name and conversation.hero.brand.mark. The bundled patch only inserts the plugin into the loader — the official brand plugin (@deepseek-ai/dsh-client-ui-brand-official) holds those slots too, so you must disable it in the profile's cordis.patch.yml and restart. Ships a swimming whale that follows light and dark themes through currentColor and is tuned to stay legible at the sidebar's 24 px.
- [Hilbert-beinghappy/seektty](https://github.com/Hilbert-beinghappy/seektty) - Terminal workspace for DeepSeek Harness with local VS Code JSON/JSONC theme import, inherited terminal background effects, mouse text selection, structured tool and diff views, Session/Profile/plugin management, and Skill/MCP browsers.
- [Him666233/dsh-inline-diff#plugin](https://github.com/Him666233/dsh-inline-diff/tree/main/plugin) - Inline diff cards for every edit, write and str_replace_editor call: old/new line numbers, character-level highlighting, 57-language detection with a red/green-safe syntax palette, a side-by-side viewer, Read-row line ranges, and a settings page for display style and highlighting.
- [HimA6652/dsh-client-ui-model-seat](https://github.com/HimA6652/dsh-client-ui-model-seat) - Merges AprilCrystal/dsh-client-ui-gpt-helper and zszz3/dsh-model-provider-prefix — two composer model-seat plugins that cannot be installed together — into one control: a Provider / Model trigger opening a two-column card where hovering the provider column swaps the models without moving the card, a separate reasoning-effort trigger with a particle slider, and a search that spans every provider while the card keeps its size.
- [himeope/dsh-queue-first-enter](https://github.com/himeope/dsh-queue-first-enter) - Pressing Enter with an empty composer steers the first queued message into the running turn while the agent is busy.
- [hiocobueno653-pixel/dsh-ui-polish](https://github.com/hiocobueno653-pixel/dsh-ui-polish) - UI polish layer for the DSH web client: frosted-glass menu surfaces, a draggable reasoning-effort slider that replaces the level radio list, and model-menu navigation that reaches the model list in one click and closes on a single Esc.
- [hj9178/dsh-throwtomatoes-plugin](https://github.com/hj9178/dsh-throwtomatoes-plugin) - Turns the composer send button into a tomato button while a task is running and the draft is empty. One or two taps hand the model a hidden note on its next step asking it to reflect on the approach and to finish by asking the user how to continue; three taps within a second stop the run first and send the same kind of note. Thrown-tomato animations and sound can be switched off in settings, which also counts every tomato thrown.
- [hjj345/dsh-sm-context-piano](https://github.com/hjj345/dsh-sm-context-piano) - A Codex-style conversation navigator for the DeepSeek Harness Web GUI.
- [hmr-BH/dsh-round-rightclick](https://github.com/hmr-BH/dsh-round-rightclick) - Radial right-click menu on conversation turns in the DSH web GUI for forking a session from a chosen turn, interrupting generation, copying the working directory path or session ID, opening the working directory, and exporting session logs.
- [Hobartoakes/dsh-model-shelf](https://github.com/Hobartoakes/dsh-model-shelf) - Replaces the chat model picker with a collapsible provider-grouped list (640x720 max) that shows each model name, provider name, provider ID and exact model ID, plus a manually managed uncommon list and a separate favorites list. Classification is user-driven only: no usage tracking and no auto-sorting. Search covers model name, model ID, provider name, provider ID and account notes.
- [hongcha-dsh/dsh-watchlist](https://github.com/hongcha-dsh/dsh-watchlist) - Follow list above the sidebar conversation list: a status dot per conversation (unread, waiting for you, running, failed), newest reply first, running ones last, and a follow toggle beside the model selector.
- [HongMing-Huang/dsh-file-upload](https://github.com/HongMing-Huang/dsh-file-upload) - Claude-style drag-and-drop/paperclip file upload with content sniffing, document-to-Markdown via Microsoft MarkItDown (built-in JS fallback), text inlining, and a read_document tool for agents.
- [hongxin/dsh-tvision](https://github.com/hongxin/dsh-tvision) - Turbo Vision-style character-cell window manager for dsh agents: overlapping framed windows with drop shadows, a Borland menu bar, a function-key strip, mouse drag and resize, approvals as modal dialogs, and five skins including the classic Borland blue.
- [honlnk/dsh-input-assist](https://github.com/honlnk/dsh-input-assist) - Ghost-text completion for the composer: FIM suggestions stream in inline after a typing pause (SSE, first token rendered immediately), Tab accepts one word at a time and Shift+Tab takes all, in-flight requests are truly cancelled on retyping or Esc. Plus two-layer typo checking: a browser-local dictionary scan (Chinese wrong phrases, English misspellings and proper-noun capitalization, user-defined per-browser entries) with a debounced LLM context pass, underlined in red in the text with per-item navigation and click-to-fix.
- [Hope2023Dcr/dsh-gallery](https://github.com/Hope2023Dcr/dsh-gallery) - Output gallery in the DSH sidebar, built to fix DSH reporting produced files only after a task ends: it watches your on-disk output folders and shows files as they appear, so you can verify and intervene mid-task instead of after. Auto-refreshing image thumbnails with zoom and pan, documents, code, data, archives and links that open in DSH native preview, per-conversation filtering, and multi-folder support for sessions whose cwd is outside the default output folder. Zero dependencies.
- [houdinicc/dsh-raindrop](https://github.com/houdinicc/dsh-raindrop) - Read-only Raindrop.io dashboard in the sidebar with collection and tag distribution, top source domains, content types, a 12-month trend and a cleanup backlog, plus two read-only agent tools for analyzing saved bookmarks. Requires a Raindrop.io API token (create an app and paste the token).
- [houdinicc/dsh-todos](https://github.com/houdinicc/dsh-todos) - Todoist-style todo management in the sidebar with projects, labels and priorities, CJK-aware natural-language quick-add, repeat rules, a stats dashboard, import and export, seven agent tools and due-time reminders.
- [houyanchao/dsh-timeline](https://github.com/houyanchao/dsh-timeline) - DSH productivity plugin: timeline navigation, prompt library, conversation export, quick notes, and more.
- [hoyyang/dsh-improve-prompt](https://github.com/hoyyang/dsh-improve-prompt) - One-click prompt improvement for the DSH composer: press the sparkle button to rewrite the draft into an agent-ready prompt, replaced in place and undoable, with a deterministic fidelity check that keeps every path, identifier, number, URL and backticked token verbatim, plus a length ceiling that blocks inflation.
- [HPNY/dsh-portfolio](https://github.com/HPNY/dsh-portfolio) - A-share and OTC fund dashboard forked from SuCriss/dsh-leekbox (MIT): indices, market boards, limit-up sentiment thermometer, dragon-tiger list, full-market search, a watchlist with in-place position editing and price alerts, a stock detail window with K-line, money flow and F10, a signal screener, 7x24 news, AI stock analysis, and an OTC fund tab with NAV, estimated NAV and a fund detail window showing stage returns, a NAV chart and the disclosed top-10 holdings with live quotes. It keeps upstream's /api/leekbox/* routes and $DSH_HOME/.leekbox-*.json data files, so install it instead of SuCriss/dsh-leekbox rather than alongside it.
- [HsiangNianian/dsh-auto-continue](https://github.com/HsiangNianian/dsh-auto-continue) - Auto-resumes interrupted DSH Web requests: sends a queued 「继续」 after network, timeout or host-crash failures, with error classification, adaptive backoff, templated continue text and browser notifications.
- [hsthanb4/dsh-magpie-usage](https://github.com/hsthanb4/dsh-magpie-usage) - DeepSeek Harness (DSH) plugin: Embeds upstream quota and usage progress rings from your local Magpie gateway (127.0.0.1:3425) right beside the composer context meter, with session-aware active model tracking and 5-hour limit reset tooltips.
- [Htao-uu/dsh-mobile-shell](https://github.com/Htao-uu/dsh-mobile-shell) - DeepSeek App-style mobile shell for the DSH Web UI with drawer navigation, app-like composer and settings; best paired with the dsh-android app, which supplies the native Android back key (standalone use works but is not recommended).
- [hu669293657/dsh-turn-tools](https://github.com/hu669293657/dsh-turn-tools) - Adds deliverable-file buttons to the end of each agent turn (opens files with the OS default app) and a floating dot rail for jumping between conversation turns.
- [Hu9956/DSH-model-controls](https://github.com/Hu9956/DSH-model-controls) - Switch providers and models, adjust reasoning effort; each model remembers its own level.
- [huaguoxingkong/dsh-prompt-rewriter](https://github.com/huaguoxingkong/dsh-prompt-rewriter) - Adds one button beside the composer that rewrites the draft into a more complete prompt in place, with no preview step, plus undo, a stop action, and optional conversation context.
- [huahuazz/DSH_question_map](https://github.com/huahuazz/DSH_question_map) - Select text in a conversation to ask a context-free side question in a floating card, and browse every question as a network graph.
- [hualidan42-beep/dsh-plugin-task-radar](https://github.com/hualidan42-beep/dsh-plugin-task-radar) - Monitor workspace sessions, subagents, todo progress and token usage in a floating panel.
- [huang-chunc/dsh-user-message-timeline](https://github.com/huang-chunc/dsh-user-message-timeline) - User Message Timeline - floating pill rail with hover preview, click/drag jump and pagination, switchable left/right.
- [huangfuren/dsh-conversation](https://github.com/huangfuren/dsh-conversation) - Conversation outline panel for the DSH web surface: user questions as root nodes (index, time, tool count and text volume per turn) with the Markdown headings of assistant replies hanging beneath them, plus level slider, search scope, bookmarks, copy and export as Markdown, section jump, keyboard navigation, a right-edge density rail and per-session reading-position memory. Read-only UI: it never touches prompts, tool results or provider traffic, and writes nothing outside its own localStorage keys.
- [huanghai-lab/dsh-custom-instructions](https://github.com/huanghai-lab/dsh-custom-instructions) - Manage global AGENTS.md in DSH Web with Markdown preview, reusable templates, version history, import/export, failed-import rollback, and revision-conflict protection.
- [huanglianqi/dsh-math-render](https://github.com/huanglianqi/dsh-math-render) - Renders LaTeX in the chat — formulas inside your own message bubble, plus a live preview of the composer draft while you type.
- [huanglianqi/dsh-math-snippets](https://github.com/huanglianqi/dsh-math-snippets) - LaTeX snippet expansion in the composer, built from Obsidian LaTeX Suite's own defaults — 138 triggers expand as they are typed, Tab walks the tabstops, and the table of 142 is editable in one JSON file.
- [huangxp12/dsh-plugin-search](https://github.com/huangxp12/dsh-plugin-search) - Search field on the DeepSeek Harness Plugins page that filters the installed and official plugin cards as you type, matching each card title, description and package name; space-separated keywords must all match.
- [huanleyabo/dsh-selection-translate](https://github.com/huanleyabo/dsh-selection-translate) - Select text in the DSH web client and a translate button appears at the end of the selection; clicking it translates that text in place. Translation goes through the host llm service, so it reuses the model the current session already has selected and needs no separate API key; an OpenAI-compatible endpoint can be configured instead. The result opens in a popover that stays inside the viewport, with one-click copy, a row of language buttons for instant retranslation, and smart Chinese-to-English / other-to-Chinese as the default. Configuration is stored host-side and its API key is never sent to the browser.
- [huaxiren6/DSH-QrPairing](https://github.com/huaxiren6/DSH-QrPairing) - Floating phone-pairing QR button for the DSH WebUI. One click opens the dsh-remote-link pairing QR (image + 6-digit code + TTL) in an in-app overlay — works in both browser and DSH Desktop (same-origin img, no iframe).
- [Hudson-Junior-Wang/dsh-search-switcher](https://github.com/Hudson-Junior-Wang/dsh-search-switcher) - One-click search-engine switcher beside the DeepSeek Harness model picker; changes the dsh-free-search provider, handy on overseas university networks that block the DeepSeek Official search endpoint.
- [hufeide/dsh-sidebar-assistant](https://github.com/hufeide/dsh-sidebar-assistant) - A sidebar assistant plugin for the Web interface, allows users to select any text, displays the model’s reasoning process and final answer in a floating panel.
- [huhu23333/dsh-plugin-subagent-model-route](https://github.com/huhu23333/dsh-plugin-subagent-model-route) - Annotates every subagent with the model route it actually ran on: a badge line in each parent-catalog row and a readout in a child session's own header.
- [HuiHuangDDFS/dsh-codex-mini-pet](https://github.com/HuiHuangDDFS/dsh-codex-mini-pet) - Adds separate Chat and Work modes to DeepSeek Harness, embeds DeepSeek's official chat page, and provides an animated desktop companion with quick chat and task-completion notifications.
- [huihuangdi/dsh-model-provider-badge](https://github.com/huihuangdi/dsh-model-provider-badge) - Shows the provider behind a session's current model as a compact badge in the DSH Web composer tool row, left of the model selector.
- [huiliyi37/dsh-tianshu-tui](https://github.com/huiliyi37/dsh-tianshu-tui) - A terminal UI (TUI) for DeepSeek Harness.
- [HUITianYi/dsh-whale-desktop-launcher](https://github.com/HUITianYi/dsh-whale-desktop-launcher) - Windows desktop launcher for DSH with a whale-girl icon, background server startup, and a Chromium app-mode window without browser chrome.
- [hulibuld/dsh-lifecard#plugin](https://github.com/hulibuld/dsh-lifecard/tree/main/plugin) - Local-first character archive in the DSH sidebar: it interviews you about someone and writes the answers into Markdown cards, imports SillyTavern character cards, writes a novel chapter by chapter, and roleplays with a card plus its world book; anything written into a card has to be quotable from material you wrote.
- [hulibuld/dsh-reading](https://github.com/hulibuld/dsh-reading) - Reads EPUB, MOBI, AZW3, TXT and PDF books in a side panel with pagination, table-of-contents jumping, highlights, notes, bookmarks, full-text search, anchored AI questions, per-chapter distillation, Legado-compatible book-source search that adds online books chapter by chapter, and a bundled reading-coach skill. PDF support is text-based only; scanned or encrypted PDFs are rejected.
- [hulibuld/dsh-refresh-restart](https://github.com/hulibuld/dsh-refresh-restart) - Adds two buttons beside the sidebar toggle at the top-left of the DSH desktop UI, on macOS, Windows and Linux. Reload refreshes only the UI layer while a turn already generating keeps running; restart asks for one confirmation, quits the whole app and reopens it with sessions and workspaces intact. Restart is a POST that must carry a random per-boot token read from the plugin's status route. The plugin makes no network calls, reads no session content and has zero runtime dependencies.
- [hulibuld/dsh-salary-flap](https://github.com/hulibuld/dsh-salary-flap) - A split-flap salary board for the DSH Web UI. Enter monthly salary, hours per day, working days per month and a start time, and the board settles today's earnings every 2 seconds on eight flip cells (currency symbol, four integer digits, decimal point, two decimals). Shows 0.00 before the shift, caps at the daily wage after it, and handles shifts that cross midnight. Six color schemes, drag anywhere (the position is kept on this machine), and a close button that collapses the board into a pill in the session header. Pure client-side bundle: no network calls and no dependencies.
- [HumXC/dsh-nestquay](https://github.com/HumXC/dsh-nestquay) - Workspace groups for the DSH sidebar with group navigation and session status indicators.
- [huningsong/dsh-todos-diy](https://github.com/huningsong/dsh-todos-diy) - Work todo reminders in a sidebar panel with four stage tabs (not started, in progress, overdue, done) plus separate recurring and paused filters, priority derived from the end time in real time, pinning that survives overdue, and enable/disable for recurring tasks. Recurrence covers workdays (following China's statutory holidays and make-up workdays), selected weekdays, monthly dates with month-end rollover, and 5-field cron; one-off tasks default to start and end reminders (start optional, end required); meetings can disable the end reminder. Alerts appear in a page overlay with optional browser notifications, snooze and quiet hours. Twelve agent tools; weekly/monthly reports, natural-language quick add, and cross-view search; data is a single local JSON file with completed tasks archived per quarter.
- [hunter118/dsh-s7r](https://github.com/hunter118/dsh-s7r) - A retro macOS System 7-style workstation UI for DeepSeek Harness with desktop windows, Workspace and Agent management, Finder, Terminal, Monitor, context tools, and desk accessories.
- [Huuuuung/dsh-artifact-index](https://github.com/Huuuuung/dsh-artifact-index) - The missing backend for the dsh-artifacts sidebar tab — serves the /report artifact index and the artifact bytes it renders.
- [huzhengcai471-crypto/dsh-startup-secret](https://github.com/huzhengcai471-crypto/dsh-startup-secret) - Full-screen Changzhengji-themed boot animation for DSH with a six-step authorization sequence, seven built-in real-voice packs, two switchable emblems, five switchable character portraits, and an optional access-password gate.
- [HwFee/HwFee-dsh#genui](https://github.com/HwFee/HwFee-dsh/tree/master/plugins/genui) - Generative UI: the model calls render_ui with a React snippet and the answer renders as an interactive card in the conversation. The snippet runs in a sandboxed iframe with an opaque origin, so it cannot read the host page, its storage or its cookies, and the frame CSP blocks the network; a message from a card is only sent after the user confirms it, and a failing card shows its error with the source behind a toggle.
- [HwFee/HwFee-dsh#predict](https://github.com/HwFee/HwFee-dsh/tree/master/plugins/predict) - Next-prompt suggestions: after each answer it predicts your next message as grey ghost text in the composer. Tab fills it without sending, and the prediction model is selectable.
- [HwFee/HwFee-dsh#system-fonts](https://github.com/HwFee/HwFee-dsh/tree/master/plugins/system-fonts) - Separate font slots for content, code and terminal text, plus an interface font, an interface-size stepper and an inline-code size stepper, with line-height and letter-spacing controls. Typography only — it never touches colour.
- [hyperion2144/dsh-desktop-tauriapp](https://github.com/hyperion2144/dsh-desktop-tauriapp) - Tauri 2 desktop app for DeepSeek Harness (macOS + Windows; installers on the repository's Releases page) with a built-in dsh runtime: tray-resident launcher with per-profile ports and windows, a process guardian that auto-heals up to three times, local or remote dsh, task-completion notifications with a Dock badge, and a live startup console. This plugin is the app's in-page half — its desktop chrome and notification bridge only take effect when DSH runs inside the desktop app; added to a regular DSH with `dsh plugin add`, it provides the bundled skill for packaging a DSH Web GUI as a Tauri app.
- [hyrinx/dsh-plugin-open-with](https://github.com/hyrinx/dsh-plugin-open-with) - Adds a capsule split-button to the conversation header: launch VS Code, terminal, or File Explorer at the current workspace directory in one click.
- [hytime/dsh-client-ui-shortcuts](https://github.com/hytime/dsh-client-ui-shortcuts) - Profile-aware keyboard shortcuts for DSH Web: supports Question and Approval cards, Standard/Vim/Custom profiles, and capability-gated Session and Workspace navigation.
- [hyzyn/dsh-plugin-kit#kit-settings](https://github.com/hyzyn/dsh-plugin-kit/tree/main/packages/kit-settings) - Host page for kit plugin configuration in the DSH Web GUI: registers a "Plugin configuration" section in Settings — peer to the built-in General section — through the official settings.section slot and declares a settings.kit.item child slot, which dsh-plugin-kit plugins render their own config cards into. Installing it alone shows an empty page; cards appear as slot-registering kit plugins are installed.
- [hyzyn/dsh-plugin-kit#tty](https://github.com/hyzyn/dsh-plugin-kit/tree/main/packages/tty) - Terminal panel for the DSH Web GUI: a sidebar "Terminal" entry opens a multi-tab xterm.js interactive terminal over a real PTY (node-pty), with SSH remote tabs (ssh2, agent / key / password auth), a cwd following the current session, and tty_list / tty_capture / tty_send agent tools.
- [hzys7/dsh-prompt-expander](https://github.com/hzys7/dsh-prompt-expander) - Adds a sparkle button beside the model picker that rewrites the current draft into a spec sized to the draft — two or three steps for a small ask, a longer checklist only when the request has several parts — and puts the original text back when pressed again. Greetings and small talk pass through untouched without calling the model.
- [Iambatman1928/dsh-lawagent-ui](https://github.com/Iambatman1928/dsh-lawagent-ui) - Legal training workbench: three-pane academic UI that reads the "Minglü" (lawagent preset) learning profile (case dossiers, flashcards, wrong-answer book, notes) and sends instructions into a Minglü session with one click.
- [Iambatman1928/dsh-lawkb-ui](https://github.com/Iambatman1928/dsh-lawkb-ui) - Legal knowledge-base workbench: hybrid keyword/statistical/vector retrieval over local statute .doc/.docx corpora, faceted filtering, charge-name and civil-cause trees, a document editor, and four AI tools (citation check, contract review, AI analysis, AI drafting).
- [Iambatman1928/dsh-xingye](https://github.com/Iambatman1928/dsh-xingye) - Chat agent for a DeepSeek Harness session: create characters and write their personas, keep several archive threads per character, undo or rewind the locally stored transcript, and record story beats in an event book.
- [Icather/dsh-clean-desktop-shell](https://github.com/Icather/dsh-clean-desktop-shell) - Clean desktop shell for DSH: launch by double-clicking a desktop shortcut like any normal desktop app, live backend monitoring with one-click tray start/restart/stop (progress dialogs), offline auto-reconnect, single instance. Reuses your existing web profile, no restyling of the web UI. Windows + macOS.
- [ice5kysl/dsh-file-explorer-kit](https://github.com/ice5kysl/dsh-file-explorer-kit) - In-session "Files" tab (conversation.view, order 20) for dsh Web that browses the active session's workspace directory with breadcrumbs and previews files inline — sanitized Markdown (marked + DOMPurify), images, line-numbered text and PDF — served by read-only /dsh-files host routes registered on ctx.webServer; no write endpoints. Bilingual zh/en.
- [ice5kysl/dsh-workspace-kit](https://github.com/ice5kysl/dsh-workspace-kit) - Workspace-first enhanced sidebar for dsh Web that shadows the built-in workspace browser at low priority (one-click toggle back to the official one) and adds soft workspace archive/restore, per-workspace SVG icons and accent colors, drag reorder and title/path/session-content search, plus a ⌘K Spotlight palette fuzzy-searching workspaces and sessions; the host half ships read-only workspace_find/workspace_list model tools and /workspace-find /workspace-list slash commands. Bilingual zh/en.
- [iceriverlin/dsh-session-upload](https://github.com/iceriverlin/dsh-session-upload) - Composer attach button for DSH Web: multi-file picks become inline draft capsules, files are stored under the workspace uploads root with an auto-extracted UTF-8 .txt sidecar (txt/docx/xlsx/pptx/zip/odt, zero dependencies), and sent messages carry only the file name — never a raw path.
- [IDKURLOVE/dsh-reset-button](https://github.com/IDKURLOVE/dsh-reset-button) - Adds a Restart button beside the permission control in the composer toolbar that relaunches DSH in one click, so freshly installed plugins apply without closing and reopening the app.
- [idoall/dsh-quick-replies](https://github.com/idoall/dsh-quick-replies) - Manageable one-tap text chips above the session composer; idle sessions queue the message and running sessions steer at the next safe boundary without rewriting the draft.
- [idoall/dsh-update-status](https://github.com/idoall/dsh-update-status) - Read-only DeepSeek Harness Web sidebar version chip that shows the running DSH version, npm latest/next/alpha channels, compatibility labels, and a copy-only upgrade command.
- [iimaguest/dsh-contradictions-indicator](https://github.com/iimaguest/dsh-contradictions-indicator) - Shows a 0-100 conversation coherence badge and commentary by running parallel contradiction analysis on the live DSH session, with a manual trigger and interval-based auto-analysis (on by default, editable per session or globally).
- [ikun1031/dsh-effort-dial-plus](https://github.com/ikun1031/dsh-effort-dial-plus) - A merge of the dsh-effort-dial card structure and the reasoning-slider gradient/particle effects: a Codex-style reasoning-effort card in the composer with one control for model and effort, per-level accent colors, a quota line on the top level, and a blue-purple gradient with drifting particles. It takes over the composer model slot, so it cannot be enabled alongside dsh-effort-dial or reasoning-slider.
- [imkingjh999/dsh-shorts-wall](https://github.com/imkingjh999/dsh-shorts-wall) - Adds a docked-first vertical shorts wall to the DSH Web UI with YouTube Shorts and Bilibili sources, keyword rotation, four-corner resizing, and Alt+S/Alt+M shortcuts.
- [Imnotndesh/dsh-peak-pricing-warning](https://github.com/Imnotndesh/dsh-peak-pricing-warning) - Shows DeepSeek peak/off-peak pricing status, a countdown to the next rate change, and the estimated session cost as inline text beside the model selector.
- [imwhatiam/dsh-mosaic](https://github.com/imwhatiam/dsh-mosaic) - Renders a dsh-view fence in an assistant reply as cards — text, tables, charts, Mermaid diagrams and sandboxed HTML widgets — with an editable model prompt, a render_view tool and a bundled skill.
- [IndieXiaoLong/DSH-Enhance](https://github.com/IndieXiaoLong/DSH-Enhance) - Adds conversation naming, file drag references, edit-and-resend, a message timeline, and a floating settings panel to the DSH Web GUI.
- [ingeb0rga/dsh-sidebar-hover](https://github.com/ingeb0rga/dsh-sidebar-hover) - Claude Code-style sidebar for the macOS desktop app: hovering the collapsed sidebar toggle peeks the sidebar over the content, selecting a session collapses it, clicking the toggle pins it; plus back/forward session history arrows (⌘\[ / ⌘]).
- [Inoue-Cyrene/dsh-ctrl-wheel-zoom](https://github.com/Inoue-Cyrene/dsh-ctrl-wheel-zoom) - Hold Ctrl and roll the mouse wheel to zoom the whole Harness Web UI around the pointer, 50-200% in 10% steps; Ctrl +/- and Ctrl 0 from the keyboard; a bottom-right chip and a Page zoom row in Settings → General show and reset the level.
- [iqingyoung/search2chart-mcp#dsh](https://github.com/iqingyoung/search2chart-mcp/tree/main/dsh) - Native DSH plugin that renders structured data as inline charts directly inside the conversation, with zero runtime dependencies.
- [Isilsolme/dsh-splash-launcher](https://github.com/Isilsolme/dsh-splash-launcher) - Windows one-click launcher with a borderless WPF startup animation (stroke-by-stroke HARNESS lettering) that starts dsh web in the background and fades into the GUI once ready, plus a desktop_launch tool.
- [it-glen/dsh-custom-fonts](https://github.com/it-glen/dsh-custom-fonts) - Fonts and code-font settings for the UI with an English-only glyph scale (100-160%), a -4..+8 px content-size offset that actually applies, and settings kept across refreshes - a maintained fork of lsh2002/dsh-custom-fonts.
- [itchenshi/dsh-keys-setting](https://github.com/itchenshi/dsh-keys-setting) - Configures from the settings window's General page whether Enter, Shift+Enter and Ctrl/Cmd+Enter send the message or insert a line break; the engine default is kept until you change it.
- [ivanant/dsh-workspace-sidebar](https://github.com/ivanant/dsh-workspace-sidebar) - Shares one right-sidebar across every conversation in the same workspace, so you don't have to reopen it when creating or switching conversations, with a switch in Settings → General.
- [ivanlebedvideo/dsh-pushups](https://github.com/ivanlebedvideo/dsh-pushups) - Counts the prompts you send in the Web GUI and turns them into a push-up debt, shown as a chip under the composer with one-click pay-off, partial payments and a day streak.
- [iwanant/dsh-emotion-ball](https://github.com/iwanant/dsh-emotion-ball) - A 44x44 always-on-top desktop ball for DSH that reflects what the agent is actually doing (thinking, searching, web fetch, error, waiting for input) and shows an exclamation or question-mark badge when a task finishes or needs an answer while you are away. Windows only; it downloads its own Electron runtime on first use.
- [Iwctwbh/dsh-flowglass](https://github.com/Iwctwbh/dsh-flowglass) - Live flowgraph visualization for DeepSeek Harness sessions, including tool calls, parallel groups, subagent branches, and drill-down details.
- [j62268781-alt/dsh-skills-mcp-manager](https://github.com/j62268781-alt/dsh-skills-mcp-manager) - Manage skills and MCP servers from one DSH settings panel. Skills are read from disk: browse, search, preview the body, create, rename and delete, with a backup written before every removal. Global MCP servers are the real cordis.patch.yml loader rows; per-project entries mount only for sessions in that project. Each expanded card lists the tools that server actually registered, and a per-server allow/deny rule keeps only the ones you need — a blocked tool never enters the tool registry, so it costs no context on every turn. Every MCP card carries a connection status tag (connected with a tool count, retrying, gave up, activation failed) plus a one-click reconnect, so a server that fails at startup is visible instead of silently missing its tools. Imports existing configs from 26 other MCP clients.
- [JackEngineer/dsh-quick-screenshot](https://github.com/JackEngineer/dsh-quick-screenshot) - One-click screenshot button that floats over every DSH page, with a second one in the composer tool row: it captures the DeepSeek Harness window (or the whole desktop with a modifier) using the host capture tooling and puts the PNG where it is useful — straight into the composer draft as an attachment, or on the clipboard when no composer is mounted. Windows-verified, with macOS and Linux backends; no dependencies and no build step.
- [JackieJam/dsh-iridescent-model-selector](https://github.com/JackieJam/dsh-iridescent-model-selector) - Replaces the composer model button with one panel holding a provider-grouped model list with search, pinned favorite models, and a reasoning-effort slider with one stop per level the model advertises, whose aurora gradient and glow grow with the level; the last effort used with each model is restored when switching back, and the built-in selector stays registered underneath as a fallback.
- [jackma5477001/dsh-system-monitor](https://github.com/jackma5477001/dsh-system-monitor) - Displays real-time CPU and memory usage for the machine running the DSH Web server.
- [JackWoods-code/dsh-bot-toggle](https://github.com/JackWoods-code/dsh-bot-toggle) - A floating Bot / ADE switch for the DSH web UI. One click moves between ds-bot's Bot surface and the classic DSH Agent surface, while ds-bot keeps running with its data untouched.
- [jaikensai888/dsh-drawio](https://github.com/jaikensai888/dsh-drawio) - A draw.io editor as a .drawio file viewer in the DSH sidebar: diagrams are ordinary workspace files (new ones default to docs/diagrams/), edits autosave, and a file changed outside the editor is refused with a conflict bar instead of being overwritten; the editor is downloaded once from a pinned upstream release with a SHA-256 check and then runs fully offline.
- [jakehemmerle/dsh-mermaid](https://github.com/jakehemmerle/dsh-mermaid) - Renders mermaid code blocks in dsh web chat as diagrams. Each block gets a toggle in its header to switch between the diagram and the source; blocks still streaming stay as code until the message settles. Mermaid loads lazily the first time a diagram is shown. The terminal UI is unaffected.
- [jameswatt139240-crypto/dsh-ATLAS](https://github.com/jameswatt139240-crypto/dsh-ATLAS) - Beyond the built-in @: line ranges (@src/a.ts:12-40), skills, plugins and any git repo — with the token cost of each reference shown before you send.
- [jan0107/dsh-compact-window](https://github.com/jan0107/dsh-compact-window) - Small-window mode for DSH NEXT on Windows: a 425px-wide always-on-top sticky window with a drawer sidebar and animated transitions. Requires DSH NEXT; the other desktop client (dsh desktop) is not supported.
- [JanEickholt/dsh-inline-diff](https://github.com/JanEickholt/dsh-inline-diff) - Renders edit and write tool calls as always-expanded side-by-side diffs styled like git diff, with optional syntax coloring and word-level highlighting.
- [Jason25417/dsh-composer-tweaks](https://github.com/Jason25417/dsh-composer-tweaks) - Composer tweaks for the DSH input box: a Markdown source/view toggle, Markdown rendering for user messages, one mixed file-and-folder picker backed by the macOS open panel, a GenUI prompt switch inside the plus menu, an open-in-new-window action on sidebar Session rows (needs the separate `dsh-popout-window` host plugin), and a collapsible subagent tree. Requires DSH 0.2.x.
- [jasondu/dsh-ui-mobile](https://github.com/jasondu/dsh-ui-mobile) - Mobile-first DeepSeek Harness UI with an installable PWA, responsive navigation, and Web Push notifications when an agent completes successfully.
- [JasperPengC/dsh-agent-blackbox](https://github.com/JasperPengC/dsh-agent-blackbox) - Flight recorder panel for DSH sessions: live monitoring, timeline replay with a tool waterfall and event inspector, per-agent cost attribution, and a cross-session cost overview.
- [JasperPengC/dsh-mcp-skills-board](https://github.com/JasperPengC/dsh-mcp-skills-board) - MCP server and skill management for DeepSeek Harness: a sidebar panel and a plugin-page config section with search, status lights, per-row switches, expandable details, and an add-server form following Codex mcp_servers fields.
- [JAYCou/codex-history-rail](https://github.com/JAYCou/codex-history-rail) - Codex History Rail adds a Codex-style turn rail to the left gutter of a DSH conversation, with hover previews, click-to-jump, and saved favorites.
- [JayYuen666/dsh-dir-prep-organize](https://github.com/JayYuen666/dsh-dir-prep-organize) - Two buttons beside the chat input: one rewrites the draft with the current directory in view, the other inserts a role template from 85 curated entries generated from the bundled `agents/` role library, which the settings card can extend with your own `.md` role files.
- [jcx3915-pixel/dsh-subtopic](https://github.com/jcx3915-pixel/dsh-subtopic) - Select any text in a DSH conversation and tap Ctrl to get an inline sub-topic card: streaming answer, follow-up tree, and drag-to-quote reference chips.
- [jediliu005/dsh-fairy-zzz-for-jediliu](https://github.com/jediliu005/dsh-fairy-zzz-for-jediliu) - Fairy III (Zenless Zone Zero) H.D.D skin for DeepSeek Harness: boot video, an eye-core dock that can be dragged vertically so it never covers text, and time-of-day greeting voices written by a model every day (5 lines, each <= 20 characters) and synthesized locally with GPT-SoVITS, with a fallback chain of today -> latest -> bundled audio so there is always something to hear. Replies can be spoken: a trailing marker line takes priority over length heuristics, and long replies can be compressed into one sentence first. The reader hashes the utterance it just played (MD5) so each reply is voiced exactly once, and switching sessions stays silent. Personal fork of dsh-fairy-zzz with its own package, route and storage namespace, plus a rate-limited summarize endpoint, POST-only engine control and bounded TTS and greeting caches. Two diagnostic logs (client revision, decision trace) are written next to the plugin and stay on the local machine. A personal fork of AssassinVIX/fairy-zzz-plugin (upstream package dsh-fairy-zzz): install this fork or the upstream, not both - both mount the full-screen skin on the same DSH surfaces. Dynamic greetings and spoken summaries need a DeepSeek-compatible API key in ~/.dsh/fairy-tts.json (or DEEPSEEK_API_KEY); without it only the bundled greeting audio plays.
- [jediliu005/dsh-sim-folder](https://github.com/jediliu005/dsh-sim-folder) - Folder-style session panel: organize sidebar sessions into your own folders with drag-to-assign, per-session aliases and archived dimming. Display-only — it never touches DSH grouping.
- [jeffreyren1/dsh-custom-js](https://github.com/jeffreyren1/dsh-custom-js) - Load, edit, manage, and hot-reload trusted JavaScript and TypeScript userscripts in DeepSeek Harness.
- [jh0oo/dsh-desktop-restart-button](https://github.com/jh0oo/dsh-desktop-restart-button) - Adds a restart button to the Windows desktop app title bar and replaces the cold-start "loading settings" overlay with an animated boot sequence.
- [jhuanxx44/dsh-paste-path](https://github.com/jhuanxx44/dsh-paste-path) - Finder clipboard paste for macOS: Cmd+C files or folders in Finder, then Ctrl+V in the DSH composer inserts their absolute paths; ordinary text paste with Cmd+V is untouched.
- [jiahui-huang1/dsh-latex-formula-editor](https://github.com/jiahui-huang1/dsh-latex-formula-editor) - Free LaTeX formula editor in the DSH sidebar with offline MathJax/PDF.js rendering: ten symbol tiles with hover palettes, ten template groups, live preview, colour/font/size/environment controls, favourites with titles and search, and nine export formats (SVG/PNG/JPG, LaTeX/AsciiMath/Typst/HTML/MathML/OMML) with escaping and share links. Image and PDF OCR need a vision-capable model in DSH or your own OpenAI-compatible vision endpoint; everything else runs offline.
- [jiangnanquan/dsh-ux](https://github.com/jiangnanquan/dsh-ux) - Solarized light theme, compact layout, think/tool-chain collapse capsules, and balance, session cost, and usage dashboards for the DSH web UI.
- [JianGuo396/dsh-control-bar](https://github.com/JianGuo396/dsh-control-bar) - Pins an always-on-top progress bar while the agent runs a turn — the percentage is estimated from tool calls and filled to 100% when the turn ends — and badges the DeepSeek peak/off-peak pricing window so large token operations ask for confirmation only during peak hours. The bar runs as a separate Electron process and is skipped when no Electron binary is found.
- [jiangyinluwei/dsh-plugInOne](https://github.com/jiangyinluwei/dsh-plugInOne) - Gathers custom plugin settings pages and cards into draggable top tabs under a unified Plugin settings navigation row.
- [JianhangLy/dsh-prompt-boost](https://github.com/JianhangLy/dsh-prompt-boost) - Prompt enhancement for the DSH Web GUI composer: pick a style before generating (logic-only, structured, or concise), or supply or clear a custom system prompt in settings; a sparkle button or Shift+Tab rewrites the current draft through the harness model, streams the result into an inline preview (original vs enhanced) with a live thinking timer, fills it back with one click, keeps an undo bar, and offers settings for model route, temperature, length limits, context awareness, undo-bar behavior and shortcuts.
- [JianhangLy/dsh-prompt-library](https://github.com/JianhangLy/dsh-prompt-library) - Composer-side prompt library: a button next to the input opens a picker with group chips and word-split AND search over saved snippets, inserting one into the draft or appending it, filling {{variables}} before insertion, with 13 built-in templates. Snippets live in one local JSON file behind a rev lock, a per-save backup, and whole-library JSON import/export; a settings page handles editing, grouping, pinning and a 6-second delete undo.
- [jianjianzhu/dsh-harness-ui](https://github.com/jianjianzhu/dsh-harness-ui) - A full-page console panel opened from the sidebar, with eight tabs: an overview; a session list read from the sessions service (title, directory, running state, updated time, with filters); installed bundles and Loader entries from pluginInventory and pluginManager, with per-bundle enable switches; a plugin market listing public topic:dsh-plugin repositories from the GitHub search API; a model and provider editor that writes the llm-pi-ai settings namespace through remote.settings.mutate (switch a session model, add or edit providers and their model lists, and store API keys via remote.credentials, merging over the stored profile so fields the form does not expose survive an edit); MCP and skills tabs; and a usage tab that reads the host's loopback /api/dsh-usage/overview document. Services are declared through ctx.inject, so a deployment that omits one mounts without the console rather than crashing.
- [jianlong-ww/dsh-explorer-editor](https://github.com/jianlong-ww/dsh-explorer-editor) - Edits workspace text files in place inside the DSH file preview, with Monaco highlighting and project-typed completion.
- [JianYiAG/dsh-prompt-enhancer#dsh-prompt-enhancer](https://github.com/JianYiAG/dsh-prompt-enhancer/tree/main/dsh-prompt-enhancer) - Composer prompt enhancement for DeepSeek Harness: a button beside the model selector rewrites the draft through one auxiliary LLM call and writes the result back in place, with composer undo left intact; a /prompt-enhance slash command returns the rewritten prompt as a command result line without touching the draft; the enhancement call can be pinned to its own fast provider and model in the profile patch; the rewrite policy is faithful - original intent, language and information are kept, code, commands and paths are preserved verbatim, and nothing is invented or padded with placeholder text.
- [JiayuREN1127/dsh-file-drag](https://github.com/JiayuREN1127/dsh-file-drag) - Drag files and folders from the sidebar Files pane into the message composer. A dropped file becomes an inline chip inside the sentence, an uploaded attachment card, or a plain @ path, switchable in Settings.
- [jide315/dsh-reasoning-language](https://github.com/jide315/dsh-reasoning-language) - Uses a configurable system-prompt section to request a chosen reasoning language (Simplified Chinese by default), and expands thinking rows by default while preserving rows the user manually collapses.
- [jidekaixin2dian/dsh-file-actions](https://github.com/jidekaixin2dian/dsh-file-actions) - Right-click any file reference in the chat to open it, open it with an associated application, reveal it in the file manager, copy its path or contents, and save it.
- [jin1254/dsh-annotations](https://github.com/jin1254/dsh-annotations) - Select completed assistant text, add comments, and send compact annotation labels with source navigation in official DSH 0.2.1-rc.2 Web; no host patches required.
- [Jingxuan-WH/dsh-model-rank](https://github.com/Jingxuan-WH/dsh-model-rank) - Sidebar leaderboard for DeepSeek Harness (DSH): LMArena blind-test ratings, Artificial Analysis intelligence index, LiveBench 2.0 capability, OpenRouter weekly/monthly usage and market share, price, speed and context — plus a local token-usage dashboard built from your own session logs (tokens, estimated cost, per-model ranking), model detail drawers and CSV export. Daily snapshot, instant open, no API key; locally configured models are highlighted.
- [JinYihang1011/dsh-plugins#dsh-refresh-button](https://github.com/JinYihang1011/dsh-plugins/tree/main/dsh-refresh-button) - Windows only; needs PowerShell and the bundled install.ps1. Adds an interface menu to the left of the model selector with three actions that run immediately: reload the interface, restart DSH, and close DSH. The Windows build ships no built-in reload or restart entry, so this adds one.
- [JinYihang1011/dsh-plugins#dsh-ui-tweaks](https://github.com/JinYihang1011/dsh-plugins/tree/main/dsh-ui-tweaks) - One stylesheet plus two small DOM patches that line third-party sidebar entries up with the host: matching row height, padding and icon size, one consistent active background, and mutual exclusion so the cron panel and the host panels never overlap.
- [jiongboy/dsh-table-copy](https://github.com/jiongboy/dsh-table-copy) - Adds a hover toolbar to every Markdown table in DSH Web: one button copies the table as Markdown, the other copies a 2x PNG of it taken with the current theme background.
- [JiuRiWu/dsh-restart](https://github.com/JiuRiWu/dsh-restart) - Adds a Restart button to the DSH Desktop sidebar. It opens the same native confirmation dialog as the tray Exit item and, once approved, shuts the app down gracefully and relaunches it. This plugin only draws the button: it also needs the companion shell patch shipped in shell-patch/ in the same repository, which has to be applied from outside DSH while the app is closed. Installed on its own, the button stays disabled and a tooltip explains why. The companion shell patch is Windows only and rewrites the installed app.asar.
- [jiuyuechuwuhao/dsh-canvas-preview](https://github.com/jiuyuechuwuhao/dsh-canvas-preview) - Conversation Canvas tab: live-preview workspace HTML artifacts and export PNG/JPG/SVG locally (`npx dsh-canvas-preview`).
- [Jiyr0119/dsh-workspace-explorer](https://github.com/Jiyr0119/dsh-workspace-explorer) - Standalone workspace file-tree panel for the DSH web UI: a session-header icon opens an animated popup of the current workspace tree; click or drag a file to insert its reference into the composer, with search, inline preview, live settings, and zh/en i18n; single-purpose, zero-config, one-command install.
- [jjoni12/dsh-locale-ko](https://github.com/jjoni12/dsh-locale-ko) - Korean UI language pack that adds 한국어 to Settings → General → Language through the official language-pack API.
- [jjt98118-beep/dsh-moss-boot](https://github.com/jjt98118-beep/dsh-moss-boot) - Plays an intro full-frame when you open DSH, when you open a new conversation, or every time you open the one you pinned. The built-in footage is themed on a Wandering Earth-style space station and the quantum AI MOSS (550W): a red single eye ignites while the Earth’s curve slides past the viewport. Four clips ship embedded in the code — no separate video files — and the library lets you tick any number to rotate at random (never the same clip twice in a row), preview the rotation, or swap in your own videos. Sound plays without a click; the pin button sits beside Settings.
- [jjxjjjjiik-bot/dsh-chat-timeline](https://github.com/jjxjjjjiik-bot/dsh-chat-timeline) - 1:1 port of DeepSeek's official web right-side chat navigation rail (ScrollNav): hover-expandable rail, reading-position highlight, click-to-jump.
- [jkamkk/dsh-liquid-glass-input](https://github.com/jkamkk/dsh-liquid-glass-input) - Liquid Glass refraction input card for the DSH web GUI, with coupled-spring press animation. Published on npm: dsh plugin --profile web add dsh-liquid-glass-input.
- [JNNarrator/dsh-deckseek](https://github.com/JNNarrator/dsh-deckseek) - A calmer reading view for DeepSeek Harness with auto-collapsing process, a minimap-style message navigation rail, in-page search, unified failure cards, and generative MCP Apps (SEP-1865) in sandboxed iframes.
- [JoblessJoe/dsh-vitals](https://github.com/JoblessJoe/dsh-vitals) - Live btop-style CPU/memory/temperature/GPU monitor tab for the DeepSeek Harness web GUI.
- [joeseesun/qiaomu-home-dsh](https://github.com/joeseesun/qiaomu-home-dsh) - Add a Harness start page with session search, recent conversations, daily tasks, and a focus timer.
- [john-walks-slow/dsh-qol](https://github.com/john-walks-slow/dsh-qol) - Mobile-first Web UI quality-of-life pack for the DeepSeek Harness: a Chrome-style active-session tab bar, full-screen sidebar swipe open/close, overlay sidebar, collapsed-sidebar recents, keyboard-suppressing session switching, viewport/IME keyboard adaptation, touch feedback, code/table inner scrolling, hidden permission dropdown, a full-screen settings rewrite with tab memory, and a compositor-driven status pulse, no-touch-drag long-press protection — 14 features, each independently toggleable from the Settings QoL section with instant effect, persisted per browser in localStorage; pure client-side CSS/JS, mobile rules locked to viewports of 768px and below so desktop stays untouched.
- [john-walks-slow/dsh-zen](https://github.com/john-walks-slow/dsh-zen) - Zen Mode view tab and foreground time tracker for DeepSeek Harness: while the agent works, a calm conversation view shows running/done status, your latest user message, live activity hints and the final AI reply in markdown with one-line turn stats, plus screen-watching (focus) time tracking with daily/weekly Zen scores, a chat-hide toggle that hides tool calls and reasoning rows, auto enter/exit Zen on task start/complete, an Alt+Z shortcut and a Zen settings panel — pure client-side, stored in localStorage, no network.
- [johnnycls/dsh-no-setup-mode](https://github.com/johnnycls/dsh-no-setup-mode) - No-setup mode for the DSH web UI: hides advanced surfaces, auto-applies best defaults (chat mode, full access, account balance), and one-click role-play personas (maid/butler).
- [JohnshyCY/dsh-multi-window](https://github.com/JohnshyCY/dsh-multi-window) - Windows desktop only (Windows 10/11, DSH desktop 0.2.1-rc.5 or newer; not available in `dsh web`). Open any DSH conversation in its own native window, so several conversations can be shown at once.
- [JosephHy/dsh-md-plus](https://github.com/JosephHy/dsh-md-plus) - Markdown display enhancements for the DSH Web chat, applied on top of the host renderer rather than replacing it: code blocks become cards with a language label and icon-only wrap and copy buttons, long code blocks, tables and Mermaid diagrams fold behind an expand control, Mermaid fences render as SVG with a diagram and code toggle, a fullscreen viewer with wheel zoom, drag and double-click to fit, and SVG or PNG export, tables get a sticky header and CSV or Markdown copy buttons, and a settings panel adjusts the diagram palette, code font size, the default diagram view and the three fold thresholds.
- [joshua05100413-cmd/dsh-AcidCore-plugins#dsh-composer-meter](https://github.com/joshua05100413-cmd/dsh-AcidCore-plugins/tree/main/dsh-composer-meter) - Composer meter for the DeepSeek Harness Web UI: a pill under the input shows draft character count, estimated tokens and this session's estimated cost from DeepSeek's published prices (peak/off-peak, Flash/Pro or custom rates), and opens a panel with the token breakdown, a six-snapshot per-session draft vault and a draft cleaner; every write goes through the host setDraft API instead of touching the editor DOM.
- [Js2Hou/dsh-mcp-manager](https://github.com/Js2Hou/dsh-mcp-manager) - Visual MCP manager for the DSH web UI: inspect, add, remove, enable/disable MCP servers and view their live connection status from Settings → MCP.
- [jsdvjx/dsh-strata](https://github.com/jsdvjx/dsh-strata) - Session minimap in the scrollbar gutter: to-scale bands coloured by event kind, anchor dots for user messages and failures, auto-loading history at the top, a hover wall of every prompt in the session, and a three-step map scale switcher.
- [jue5hen/dsh-window-size](https://github.com/jue5hen/dsh-window-size) - Set the startup window size of DeepSeek Harness Desktop from a settings page, with 4:3, 16:9 and 16:10 presets and up to 12 custom presets.
- [juliankang4/dsh-locale-ko](https://github.com/juliankang4/dsh-locale-ko) - Adds Korean (한국어) as a native option in Settings - General - Language and translates all 2,775 Web UI strings of dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-alpha.2, experimental features included. Works in dsh web and the desktop app.
- [juliankang4/dsh-ui-scale](https://github.com/juliankang4/dsh-ui-scale) - Scales the whole DeepSeek Harness interface from 50% to 200%, menus and pop-ups included, set from Settings - General with a slider, a percent field or presets. Works in dsh web and the desktop app, which has no zoom of its own.
- [JupiterWalker/dsh-section](https://github.com/JupiterWalker/dsh-section) - Cross-project session grouping for the DSH sidebar: define named sections, assign sessions from any project into them, then collapse, rename, archive or move a whole project in one action; a toggle in the Sections panel swaps the host session browser for the section tree and restores it on demand, and each row flags live work or a completion you have not looked at yet.
- [Justin-Mai/dsh-stock-view](https://github.com/Justin-Mai/dsh-stock-view) - A collapsible, draggable popup in the top-right corner of the DSH web interface for watching A-shares, ETFs, Hong Kong and US stocks and OKX spot crypto side by side: real-time quotes, intraday and daily/weekly/monthly candlestick charts with moving averages, buy/sell target prices, group switching with drag-to-reorder, rename and delete, cross-market search, and light/dark themes. A fork of Awu12277/dsh-stock-watch that adds US quotes via Eastmoney and crypto via OKX.
- [JuYueYe/dsh-personal-model-picker](https://github.com/JuYueYe/dsh-personal-model-picker) - Switch providers from the left column and choose a model from the right; hover, focus or click a provider to update the model list.
- [jy007/dsh-calendar-board](https://github.com/jy007/dsh-calendar-board) - Month calendar panel in the DSH main column: add and edit a day's entries inline, overlay the Host's scheduled reminders for today and tomorrow coloured by run state, and merge iCalendar subscriptions with the calendars already synced into this Mac's Calendar.app; a calendar_board tool exposes the same entries to any agent session.
- [k-ying/dsh-vsceditor](https://github.com/k-ying/dsh-vsceditor) - Embeds a real VSCode editor (code-server, or your local desktop VS Code via the bundled bridge extension) as a GUI tab: agent file edits open turn-scoped red/green diffs that survive tab closes and page reloads, files lock while the agent writes, and a watchdog supervisor keeps zero orphan processes.
- [Kadima-code/dsh-subagent-fold](https://github.com/Kadima-code/dsh-subagent-fold) - Folds the built-in subagent dropdown into History / Today / Previous / Current groups by usage batch, keeping the current batch expanded; the original header is left untouched.
- [Kadima-code/dsh-subagent-toolkit](https://github.com/Kadima-code/dsh-subagent-toolkit) - Subagent Toolkit: folds the built-in subagent dropdown into History / Today / Previous / Current groups by usage batch, adds per-row and whole-group deletion of subagent sessions, and keeps the subagent model allowlist live per delegation. The original header is left untouched.
- [kahomesl/dsh-chat-diff-summary-legacy](https://github.com/kahomesl/dsh-chat-diff-summary-legacy) - A per-turn change bar above the composer showing which files the turn changed and the line deltas, for DeepSeek Harness 0.1.5-rc.1 through 0.1.7.
- [Kaiji-Z/dsh-plugin-lookatstudy](https://github.com/Kaiji-Z/dsh-plugin-lookatstudy) - A study tab with course-map, tutor and blackboard columns that turns markdown files, local folders or GitHub repos into guided courses, with lesson unlocking, mastery tracking, spaced repetition and a tutor-designed course import.
- [kaijia323/dsh-mobile-ui](https://github.com/kaijia323/dsh-mobile-ui) - Mobile web shell for DSH Web with drawer navigation, touch-sized controls, safe areas and a full-width conversation; the desktop layout stays unchanged.
- [kang7897/dsh-account-glance](https://github.com/kang7897/dsh-account-glance) - Compact card above the sidebar account avatar showing the signed-in DeepSeek account balance, the current peak or off-peak billing window with a countdown to the next switch, and the rate of both windows. The window is computed locally in Beijing time without a network request; the balance refreshes every 60 seconds and on window focus or visibility. zh/en copy, no runtime dependencies.
- [kankezhiyan/dsh-awesome-fonts](https://github.com/kankezhiyan/dsh-awesome-fonts) - Font switcher for DeepSeek Harness Web GUI: 99 UI fonts + 31 code fonts with CJK-Latin pairing.
- [KannaKuron/dsh-better-workspace](https://github.com/KannaKuron/dsh-better-workspace) - Hierarchical workspace tree for the DSH sidebar: every slash in a workspace title becomes a virtual folder (arbitrarily deep), the add-workspace directory flow gains a parent-group popup, renames re-derive the tree instantly, sessions nest on the same rule with separate visual styling, and every row carries right-click actions plus per-row appearance customization (color, text-only glow, font weight, primitives-family icon).
- [kanpengfei/dsh-prompt-sortable](https://github.com/kanpengfei/dsh-prompt-sortable) - Fork of FeatherHunter/dsh-prompt with draggable custom template ordering — drag the handle in Settings to reorder custom prompts (saved to the host); preset ordering unchanged. Do not install alongside upstream dsh-prompt: both register the same UI and /_dsh/dsh-prompt/ route.
- [KDDKBD/DeepSeek-splash-animation](https://github.com/KDDKBD/DeepSeek-splash-animation) - Plays a video while DSH opens, then dissolves over 0.3 seconds once it has played out. Ships with a default video so it works on install, and the path can be changed or cleared from the plugin settings page.
- [kelearns/dsh-navigation-bar](https://github.com/kelearns/dsh-navigation-bar) - Piano-key in-conversation navigation for the Web UI: one key per user message with hover ladder, message-preview tooltip, and active-message highlight; click to smooth-scroll.
- [kenz1117/dsh-ui-rainbowspeak](https://github.com/kenz1117/dsh-ui-rainbowspeak) - RainbowSpeak — a flowing rainbow marquee border around the chat composer card, driven by a conic-gradient ring. Live settings for speed, width, seven color schemes, direction, sides and breathing, plus session-state awareness that reacts to running, attention, error and celebration moments.
- [KevinZhangNothing/dsh-task-graph](https://github.com/KevinZhangNothing/dsh-task-graph) - Adds a Graph tab that renders one session's execution as an interactive DAG of turns, LLM steps, tools, skills, subagents, code changes and tests, with live status, retry aggregation, critical path and trajectory linkage.
- [keyboard-leo/dsh-reading-workbench](https://github.com/keyboard-leo/dsh-reading-workbench) - Reading workbench for DSH: a cover-grid bookshelf and a paginated single/double-page reader for Markdown, TXT, HTML, EPUB, MOBI and PDF, with highlight notes anchored to text offsets and three agent tools for listing books, reading highlights and pulling text excerpts.
- [kfat77/dsh-stock-ticker](https://github.com/kfat77/dsh-stock-ticker) - Adds a ticker strip above the DeepSeek Harness interface that scrolls right to left, with a customizable watchlist of CN, HK and US stocks and indices, a configurable refresh interval and alert threshold, and a click-through quote card.
- [kfirsch/dsh-hebrew-rtl](https://github.com/kfirsch/dsh-hebrew-rtl) - Hebrew RTL rendering for the web GUI: any line with Hebrew reads right-to-left (messages, GenUI, question cards, the composer and input fields), and RTL-aware Cmd+Left/Right line navigation.
- [kgwdonkey/dsh-header-controls](https://github.com/kgwdonkey/dsh-header-controls) - Restart / exit power buttons in the conversation header.
- [kialajin-l/DSH_Plus#dsh-quote-selection](https://github.com/kialajin-l/DSH_Plus/tree/main/dsh-quote-selection) - Floating toolbar on text selection in conversations: add the quote to the composer, start a new discussion from it, or copy it; quotes collapse into a card aligned to the composer and double-clicking jumps back to the quoted turn.
- [kiefeng/dsh-avatar-top](https://github.com/kiefeng/dsh-avatar-top) - Moves the sidebar account avatar out of the bottom strip with four placements, including a zero-height inline mode in the brand row, and three heights down to an avatar-only row, with adjustable spacing and a settings card on the Plugins page, while other plugins' footer widgets stay pinned at the bottom.
- [kingofpowers/dsh-chat-ctxmenu](https://github.com/kingofpowers/dsh-chat-ctxmenu) - Right-click menu for DeepSeek Harness conversation content: copy the selection or the whole message, copy message images and the preview lightbox to the clipboard as PNG (or save them), open file paths (absolute, relative to the session working directory, or ~/), reveal them in the file manager, open them with another registered application, and copy paths or links. Every candidate path is verified by the Host filesystem before an open action appears.
- [Kirkice/dsh-plugin-makima-tui](https://github.com/Kirkice/dsh-plugin-makima-tui) - A terminal workbench for dsh with streaming chat, tool execution, session management, provider management, and image input.
- [kissear/dsh-splash-screen-haavk](https://github.com/kissear/dsh-splash-screen-haavk) - Full-screen HAAVK-themed boot animation for DSH, rewritten from dsh-startup-secret 1.5.2 (which is listed separately): an intro video, a cold-blue console board and a five-step access sequence with recorded voice lines and a looping background track. Text and two accent colours are editable in DSH settings, and the intro video and BGM can be switched there; replacement media files go into lib/assets. Install it instead of dsh-startup-secret, not alongside it — the two share ~/.dsh/dsh-startup.json and the same settings section.
- [kk112222/dsh-chat-index](https://github.com/kk112222/dsh-chat-index) - Per-session question index for the DSH Web UI: a time-proportional dot rail on the right edge and an expandable chain panel showing every user question with its time, including branch/subagent sessions; clicking a node jumps to that message and highlights it.
- [kkbsgg/dsh-balance](https://github.com/kkbsgg/dsh-balance) - Shows the selected model provider's account balance next to the composer's model seat once a model and reasoning effort are selected.
- [kkhsu/dsh-conversation-icons](https://github.com/kkhsu/dsh-conversation-icons) - Assign per-session duotone SVG icons and colors from the sidebar menu or a model tool, with browser-local persistence and cross-tab updates.
- [kkisok0123/dsh-sidechat](https://github.com/kkisok0123/dsh-sidechat) - Side conversations forked from the main session, with native conversation rendering, selection annotations and handback.
- [KNGLOKIKO/dsh-plugin-approval-zh](https://github.com/KNGLOKIKO/dsh-plugin-approval-zh) - Localizes the DeepSeek Harness approval panel in the web client and translates authorization reasons from English to Chinese, with a built-in phrase dictionary first and a model-router fallback for reasons the dictionary does not cover.
- [Knight-Zjy/dsh-workspace-categories](https://github.com/Knight-Zjy/dsh-workspace-categories) - Category tree for the DSH sidebar workspace browser: workspaces and their sessions nest under user-made categories (collapsed state remembered), rows drag between categories, a new workspace can be created straight into one through the directory picker, categories rename and delete in place, sessions archive and unarchive from their own row (the built-in browser's stop-active-work confirmation included) alongside a hide / show / only-archived filter, row buttons carry the app's own hover tooltips, and a footer toggle switches the panel back to the built-in list.
- [kongbai-shike/dsh-novel-wallpaper](https://github.com/kongbai-shike/dsh-novel-wallpaper) - Renders a TXT novel as a dim text layer behind the DSH chat, with a draggable controller for table of contents, chapter paging, auto-scroll and reading settings.
- [kongbaiming5/dsh-pet-button](https://github.com/kongbaiming5/dsh-pet-button) - macOS arm64 only; requires dsh-pet installed (the bundled binary is a closed prebuilt). Desktop switch for the dsh-pet pet: one click to show or hide it, and it can start DeepSeek Harness when that is closed.
- [kongdexu/dsh-nav-pointer](https://github.com/kongdexu/dsh-nav-pointer) - Vertical message pointer rail on the left edge of the chat: one dash per user message, click to scroll to that message, hover for a preview bubble.
- [kongkongya367-glitch/dsh-bingo-ren](https://github.com/kongkongya367-glitch/dsh-bingo-ren) - Adds a draggable restart button and a plugin panel to the DeepSeek Harness desktop app: it prompts when the profile plugin list changes, records additions and removals per profile, tracks trials, reports host startup state, and offers rollback, minimal boot and recoverable cleanup.
- [konglong87/dsh-input-list](https://github.com/konglong87/dsh-input-list) - DSH prompt library and reusable snippets plugin: save, edit, delete, and append prompt templates, workflow instructions, and reusable text from a button beside the composer without sending.
- [konnga/dsh-hud](https://github.com/konnga/dsh-hud) - Read-only Git panel for the current workspace — branch, changed files, diffs and image before/after compare — plus three capsules above the composer (Git status, tool and skill activity, session running time) and per-session change attribution with review progress.
- [kouyichi/dsh-tui-app](https://github.com/kouyichi/dsh-tui-app) - Interactive terminal chat app for dsh: streaming conversation, tool cards, jobs panel, full-text search, trajectory replay, multi-session tabs, and A2A dispatch (Ink-based).
- [KouzakiUmi/dsh-code-pill](https://github.com/KouzakiUmi/dsh-code-pill) - Renders inline code in DSH chat as pills with syntax colours and optional file badges, styles chat file paths, and offers optional folded-row tinting and thinking-text ink, with a settings page for six presets, colours and fonts.
- [kouzerror/dsh-retry](https://github.com/kouzerror/dsh-retry) - Adds a native Restart DeepSeek Harness item to the Windows system tray menu and the account menu of the DSH desktop app: the plugin prepares a patched resources/app.asar at startup, a detached helper backs up the original and swaps it in after a normal exit, the patch is re-applied after each desktop update, and either item relaunches the app gracefully on the next launch. Windows desktop only; other hosts report 409 not_desktop.
- [Kseleven/dsh-pomodoro-timer](https://github.com/Kseleven/dsh-pomodoro-timer) - A pomodoro timer docked below the composer in DeepSeek Harness, with focus/short/long cycles, a resumable countdown, chime, optional system notifications and bilingual settings.
- [kuanfu0430/dsh-sidebar-branch-chat](https://github.com/kuanfu0430/dsh-sidebar-branch-chat) - Adds a Branch Chat tab to dsh-better-sidebar that opens independent archived sessions with a head/middle/tail context digest and the same tools as the main agent.
- [KukaNo996/dsh-prompt-optimization](https://github.com/KukaNo996/dsh-prompt-optimization) - Composer prompt refinement for DSH: rewrites a rough draft into a structured, unambiguous prompt with one-click undo, reference-chip protection, three model sources (composer / account / any OpenAI-compatible endpoint) and disconnect-aware cancellation.
- [kw12121212/writing-anything#dsh-writing-viewer](https://github.com/kw12121212/writing-anything/tree/main/plugins/dsh-writing-viewer) - Read-only writing-workspace panel for projects that contain a .writing-anything/ workspace: per-work overview cards, a unit tree, YAML artifact views as semantic cards or highlighted source, the source list and the decision/review queues, and in-panel unit review whose feedback is written back by wa-cli.
- [kw78/academic-agent#academic-dsh-ui](https://github.com/kw78/academic-agent/tree/main/packages/academic-dsh-ui) - DSH sidebar and panel for courses, tasks, plans, connections, course materials, automation and settings.
- [kyanray-dev/dsh-markdown-input](https://github.com/kyanray-dev/dsh-markdown-input) - Markdown input for the composer that keeps the typed Markdown as the sent text while drawing bullets and checkboxes, styling bold, italic, inline code, strikethrough, headings, quotes and fenced code blocks, continuing lists on Shift+Enter, and wrapping selections with Cmd/Ctrl+B, I and E.
- [KylinQ01/dsh-startup-animation](https://github.com/KylinQ01/dsh-startup-animation) - Startup splash for DeepSeek Harness: an animated avatar with aurora, stardust, pointer parallax and a module-assembly entrance, plus the same image as the main-UI wallpaper; both images can be swapped from a settings page that previews the animation live.
- [kyoukisu/dsh-rich-ui](https://github.com/kyoukisu/dsh-rich-ui) - ChatGPT-style rich answers inside DeepSeek Harness: entity panels, KPI cards, series charts, sortable tables, nested layers, flow diagrams and local controls.
- [kyrsieger/dsh-up-session](https://github.com/kyrsieger/dsh-up-session) - Adds cross-workspace pinned and recent session sections to the DSH sidebar, with status indicators and display settings.
- [l541402398/dsh-file-uploads](https://github.com/l541402398/dsh-file-uploads) - Upload arbitrary local files from the Web composer, show pending cards, and manage stored files in Settings.
- [Lacquervii/smooth-cursor](https://github.com/Lacquervii/smooth-cursor) - Smooth comet-mode animated caret for the DSH Web composer: a glowing comet trail replaces the native blinking caret and glides across the input as you type, with configurable accent color, thickness, and trail, IME-aware for CJK input.
- [lakabuji-arch/dsh-model-accordion](https://github.com/lakabuji-arch/dsh-model-accordion) - Provider-folded model selector for the DSH Web composer with catalog-driven reasoning effort choices.
- [lalalaleo/dsh-draft](https://github.com/lalalaleo/dsh-draft) - A Markdown-based draft-board plugin for dsh: live-preview editing and local persistence.
- [lamp23333/dsh-balance-simple](https://github.com/lamp23333/dsh-balance-simple) - A simple, information-dense DeepSeek status display for the DSH sidebar: balance, usage, and peak/off-peak countdown. Requires the DSH Web GUI with a signed-in DeepSeek account. 
- [Lancang-hzau/dsh-live-terminal](https://github.com/Lancang-hzau/dsh-live-terminal) - Visible terminal panel that streams the agent's shell commands into an in-app terminal and lets the agent capture the terminal screen as text.
- [LanFeng97/dsh-workspace-create-dialog](https://github.com/LanFeng97/dsh-workspace-create-dialog) - Codex-style Create Workspace dialog: name it and the folder is created for you, or pick one with a native chooser that remembers where you were (the native chooser is Windows-only; elsewhere use the name field).
- [langyo/dsh-mobile-upgrade](https://github.com/langyo/dsh-mobile-upgrade) - Requires DSH 0.1.5-rc.1. Mobile UI fixes for the dsh web profile: narrow-screen drawer and settings tabs, a full-width model menu, a stuck-request network chip, and a narrow-header collector that folds the lineage, subagent and background-job chips into one tappable sheet.
- [LaoganR/dsh-novel-reader](https://github.com/LaoganR/dsh-novel-reader) - Local TXT novel reader in the DSH right sidebar: bookshelf with file, directory and path import, chapter table of contents with jump, click-to-turn or vertical-scroll paging, reading progress restored across restarts, and font, line-spacing, background and night themes.
