- [laoye666-6/dsh-plugin-media-wallpaper](https://github.com/laoye666-6/dsh-plugin-media-wallpaper) - Image and video wallpapers for the DSH web workspace: auto-detected GIF/APNG/animated WebP/PNG/JPEG/MP4/WebM backgrounds with blur, brightness and dimming, tint-following UI, per-component transparency, and one-click preset wallpapers.
- [Laplace-bit/dsh-smooth-stream](https://github.com/Laplace-bit/dsh-smooth-stream) - Fluid streaming rendering and smooth scrolling for the DeepSeek Harness Web UI.
- [Larliers/dsh-skill-prompt-injector](https://github.com/Larliers/dsh-skill-prompt-injector) - Adds a right-sidebar panel that lists the skills available to the current session, searches and tags them, combines them with your own saved prompts, appends the selection to the composer in one click, and can pin skills and prompts as always-on context injected through the system prompt.
- [Larrtroffen/dsh-workspace-folders](https://github.com/Larrtroffen/dsh-workspace-folders) - Folder organization for the DeepSeek Harness sidebar: display-only Workspace folders (drag a Workspace onto a folder block to file it, nest and re-order folders, collapse with counts), durable per-Workspace nested Session folders, per-row icons chosen from 40 host icons or 48 emoji, and a Workspace-level "archive all sessions" cleanup. A fork of WSNMM3/dsh-session-keeper (MIT, itself derived from ht719/dsh-session-categories); it replaces the sidebar.workspaces region, so do not install it alongside the upstream plugins.
- [laughmaker/dsh-enhance](https://github.com/laughmaker/dsh-enhance) - DeepSeek Harness UI polish: one Markdown style across every renderer, select-text annotations with numbered pins, a sidebar that drags down to 214px and remembers its width, a first launch that fills the screen, sidebar panel and plugin icons collected into one top row of five, and a colour-cycling marker for running sessions.
- [lavapapa/dsh-composer-layout](https://github.com/lavapapa/dsh-composer-layout) - Web Composer placement for long prompts: keep it at the bottom or dock it in a resizable right column beside a long answer.
- [LCQ-1024/dsh-prompt-enhancer](https://github.com/LCQ-1024/dsh-prompt-enhancer) - Adds a prompt-enhancement button to the DSH composer that rewrites drafts into agent-ready prompts.
- [lcthe/dsh-skills-hub](https://github.com/lcthe/dsh-skills-hub) - DSH Skill Manager: browse, enable/disable, import from Codex/Claude Code/ZCode/WorkBuddy/QCoderWork, auto-discover skills with symlink support.
- [lcthe/dsh-timeline-rail](https://github.com/lcthe/dsh-timeline-rail) - Message timeline rail for DeepSeek Harness web chat: evenly spaced ticks for each user message on the right edge, click to jump, hover to preview.
- [lczment/dsh-window](https://github.com/lczment/dsh-window) - Window close behaviour for DeepSeek Harness Desktop — ask, minimise to tray, or quit, with a remembered choice editable in Settings, plus per-session and per-workspace child windows. Windows only: requires DSH Desktop 0.2.1-rc.5+ and a PowerShell installer that patches the app shell (resources/app); re-run it after every DSH upgrade, otherwise closing keeps DSH default behaviour.
- [Ledas-as/dsh-claude-video-slider](https://github.com/Ledas-as/dsh-claude-video-slider) - Unofficial Claude UI-inspired model and reasoning-effort selector using the current model's available levels, with animated purple pixel effects.
- [lee259/dsh-workbench](https://github.com/lee259/dsh-workbench) - A right-side file workspace for DeepSeek Harness Web with source previews, captured DSH diffs, tabs, and a workspace tree.
- [LeemanCheung/dsh-whale-animation](https://github.com/LeemanCheung/dsh-whale-animation) - A 60-frame theme-aware monochrome whale-dive animation with reactive water for the DSH Web turn status: embedded assets, seamless closed-loop playback, a reduced-motion PNG fallback, and lifecycle-clean removal.
- [LeeScenic/dsh-sidebar-sections](https://github.com/LeeScenic/dsh-sidebar-sections) - Collapsible section headers for the DSH sidebar: fold the functions list, the workspace list and the footer status area behind labelled header rows; sections are identified by position so cards the host or other plugins append to the footer join the status section automatically, and the account row stays pinned to the bottom (client-only DOM enhancement, no host patch).
- [leexiaode4/dsh-launch-buttons](https://github.com/leexiaode4/dsh-launch-buttons) - Multiple named launch targets in the DSH composer: left-click starts the default target, right-click opens the list.
- [left0ver/dsh-file-review](https://github.com/left0ver/dsh-file-review) - Review agent file changes in a Diff panel, add comments for follow-up edits, and undo edited or newly created files.
- [lemonorangeapple/dsh-effort-switcher](https://github.com/lemonorangeapple/dsh-effort-switcher) - A Codex-like effort switcher for Deepseek Harness.
- [lenrendre/dsh-screenshot-native](https://github.com/lenrendre/dsh-screenshot-native) - WeChat-style screenshot: freeze the desktop, drag a region or click a window, and the crop lands in the composer. A Windows-only fork of Durandalw/dsh-plugin-screenshot; do not install it alongside the upstream plugin.
- [leogottadothebest/dsh-settings-beautify](https://github.com/leogottadothebest/dsh-settings-beautify) - Normalize the DSH settings UI onto one design language: unified typography, cards, controls, focus and motion across every settings page, including pages contributed by other plugins.
- [lerwa2025-a11y/dsh-plugin-groups](https://github.com/lerwa2025-a11y/dsh-plugin-groups) - Adds a My Groups tab to the DSH Installed plugins page: custom groups with drag reordering, colored text tags, Chinese notes, group locking, real enable switches, and two preset snapshot slots.
- [Lessthinker66/dsh-open-native](https://github.com/Lessthinker66/dsh-open-native) - Click a delivered-file card to open the file with the system default application instead of DSH's built-in sidebar preview; hold Shift to keep the original preview.
- [levodoubt/dsh-longtext-input](https://github.com/levodoubt/dsh-longtext-input) - Adds a Long text button to the composer tool row: write or paste the body in an editor overlay, and on save it is written to <workspace>/.dsh-longtext/<timestamp>_<title>.md and an @ path reference to that file is appended to the composer, leaving the conversation free of the long text itself.
- [lewisyoung0914/dsh-better-todo](https://github.com/lewisyoung0914/dsh-better-todo) - Floating todo panel in the DSH Web UI — a draggable window that snaps to any screen edge, today/more grouping in the home tab, a separate all-items tab with uncompleted/completed filters and incremental loading, items that open read-only with an edit toggle in the corner, image attachments that zoom in place and other files that download, a pomodoro timer (15/30/45/60 or custom, with configurable rounds and break interval) that sends a Windows notification and waits for confirmation before the next phase, and five agent tools plus a /todo slash command for reading and editing the list.
- [LHF198/dsh-prompt-optimizer](https://github.com/LHF198/dsh-prompt-optimizer) - Improve the current chat draft before sending with a confirm-before-apply comparison.
- [lhuans/dsh-genui](https://github.com/lhuans/dsh-genui) - Renders OpenTiny GenUI charts, forms, calculators and mini apps inline in DSH replies via schemaJson fences, with user actions flowing back into the conversation.
- [Li-Mingshuang/dsh-audio-control](https://github.com/Li-Mingshuang/dsh-audio-control) - Floating audio control panel in the Web GUI: play/pause/next/previous for Windows media sessions (SMTC) and the system master volume/mute, served by a Node route half that runs a PowerShell helper.
- [LiangshouX/dsh-ide-workbench#workbench](https://github.com/LiangshouX/dsh-ide-workbench/tree/main/packages/workbench) - JetBrains-style IDE workbench in the DSH sidebar: Project file tree, Commit panel, Git history with a swimlane graph, a real PTY terminal and an embedded editor with Markdown preview and Find in Files, plus chat file links and send-to-chat references. Code intelligence is a separate plugin, dsh-ide-codeintel.
- [liangzz1998/dsh-workspace-session-drag](https://github.com/liangzz1998/dsh-workspace-session-drag) - Moves a session from one workspace to another by dragging it in the sidebar: a confirmation dialog first, subagent sessions travel with the parent, the list auto-scrolls at its edges, and an open idle session takes the move immediately through a registry refresh; moving into or out of the ungrouped group works both ways.
- [liaohongrao/dsh-session-dates](https://github.com/liaohongrao/dsh-session-dates) - Groups the sessions of every workspace in the DSH sidebar by day, with explicit date labels, a live running indicator and date-based search.
- [liaoyuqing/dsh-shutdown-idle](https://github.com/liaoyuqing/dsh-shutdown-idle) - Runs a chosen power action (shutdown / restart / hibernate / sleep / lock / logoff) once every Agent session and background job has gone idle: the plugin samples in-process sessions once per second, counts running sessions as always busy, checks running background jobs through a configurable option (considerJobs, on by default, shown with its live count in Settings), waits a confirm window and then a cancellable countdown, and shows a draggable bottom-right card with the current phase and remaining seconds; the enable toggle never survives a host restart, any newly started session or Cancel aborts the countdown, and if the agents service is unavailable the machine is treated as busy so nothing ever fires. Windows only (the built-in shutdown / rundll32 actions); every action except none degrades to none elsewhere.
- [lidongbei/dsh-session-view](https://github.com/lidongbei/dsh-session-view) - Session-first DSH sidebar: pinned, active and recent sections, workspace grouping, focus groups, search and a session trash bin.
- [liggest/dsh-alt-enter-newline](https://github.com/liggest/dsh-alt-enter-newline) - Adds Alt+Enter as a newline to the DeepSeek Harness (dsh) web composer.
- [Light-EI/dsh-chat-perf-css](https://github.com/Light-EI/dsh-chat-perf-css) - A long conversation that renders many LaTeX formulas at once can become laggy, because the browser keeps styling, laying out and painting content that is off screen. This plugin applies content-visibility: auto to the off-screen message bodies, so that work is skipped and the lag is reduced.
- [liguobao/dsh-file-viewer](https://github.com/liguobao/dsh-file-viewer) - Read-only Web client file-preview panel with image, PDF, CSV/TSV, text, source-code, Markdown, JSON and YAML renderers, large-file range loading, and a provider API for non-local content.
- [lihang-lh/dsh-moon-light](https://github.com/lihang-lh/dsh-moon-light) - A marquee mood-light ring around the DSH web UI edge that reacts to session state, turning green while the agent works, pink when done, and yellow when something needs attention, with colors, ring width, rotation speed, and flash frequency configurable from the settings panel.
- [lihang-lh/dsh-task-panel](https://github.com/lihang-lh/dsh-task-panel) - Task panel for the DSH Web sidebar: publish tasks, auto-distill related sessions, and drive plan/develop/review stages with subagents plus in-panel acceptance.
- [LihiHe/dsh-thinking-slider](https://github.com/LihiHe/dsh-thinking-slider) - Turns the composer's reasoning-effort menu into a draggable slider and takes over the stock model-and-reasoning-level chip. Dragging follows the pointer continuously; releasing snaps to the nearest level and submits one session update per drag. The track draws its own fill, tick marks and white knob, only the highest levels (xhigh, max, ultra) get a flowing brand-colour gradient, and both ends carry the lowest and highest level names. The same card expands into the official model list. Arrow keys step one level, PageUp and PageDown two, Home and End jump to the ends, and each move submits. A generation counter on the directory object discards late responses, and reduced-motion turns the animations off. All data comes from the stock modelDirectories directory; no new capabilities are added.
- [lihongxu0221/dsh-workspace-browser](https://github.com/lihongxu0221/dsh-workspace-browser) - Replaces the shipped sidebar workspace browser with Recents, a project editor that adds extra source folders, and pin, rename, fork, and archive row actions.
- [lijianbin2/dsh-subtitle-remover](https://github.com/lijianbin2/dsh-subtitle-remover) - Sidebar page that removes burned-in subtitles from a video with a local video-subtitle-remover checkout: frame preview with a start/end range, drag-drawn regions (up to six), automatic region detection reviewed by a DSH chat model, live before/after comparison, and open-file/open-folder on the result. Requires the video-subtitle-remover checkout with its models and a Python environment with torch, paddle and OpenCV.
- [lijinhao315/dsh-question-index](https://github.com/lijinhao315/dsh-question-index) - Question index: an ordered, collapsible list of your asked questions floating on the right side of the conversation; click a question to jump the session to that point. Auto-indexes each session's full history on open, persists it locally, and jumps to older questions by growing the window on demand.
- [likaileave/dsh-direct-open](https://github.com/likaileave/dsh-direct-open) - Open delivered files, changed-file rows and inline file mentions in the system default app with a left click; right-click takeover is configurable: all menus, only where DSH has none, or never. Desktop app only: the Web client has no system-open capability, so clicks stay with DSH.
- [lilcandi/dsh-continue-button](https://github.com/lilcandi/dsh-continue-button) - A continue button in the composer tool row, immediately left of the send key: one click writes an interruption-aware prompt into the draft and submits it, so an interrupted task resumes without retyping the context. The prompt names the interruption and appends the current task plus the tail of the last reply; Alt-click or right-click sends a bare 继续, and a non-empty draft is appended to rather than overwritten.
- [lilcandi/dsh-music-on](https://github.com/lilcandi/dsh-music-on) - A music dock for the DSH web GUI. A draggable capsule in the bottom-right corner opens into a player card with a queue, previous and next, a draggable progress bar, a volume knob driven by drag or mouse wheel, three loop modes and per-track cover art for resolved links (local files show a vinyl placeholder); audio files dropped onto the card play immediately. Paste a Bilibili, NetEase Cloud Music or QQ Music track or playlist link to resolve it: NetEase and QQ audio is signed by the host into an opaque HMAC token, so the browser never receives a plain CDN address and cannot forge one. A spectrum canvas along the page bottom reads a real AnalyserNode and draws in six styles (dots, bars, blocks, mirror, line, wave) with adjustable width, bar count, sensitivity, glow, height, frame rate and placement; the row is fitted to the container width, falling back to a slight overflow when the gaps would fall below half a pixel. Seven colour themes (whale blue-white, aurora, mint, sunset, rose, neon, mono). The settings section is grouped into interface, theme, playback, links and spectrum, and carries favorites and history tables.
- [lilcandi/dsh-session-live](https://github.com/lilcandi/dsh-session-live) - Live preview inside the sidebar session hover card: what the session is doing right now (running tool with elapsed time, thinking, last tool duration), an attachment thumbnail with click-to-enlarge, the latest user prompt and assistant reply with injected system scaffolding stripped out, a recent-tool icon strip, turn count plus token totals plus model name on one line, and a 12-item settings page (11 switches plus a slider) persisted to the profile.
- [liliuCourier/dsh-chat-outline](https://github.com/liliuCourier/dsh-chat-outline) - Persistent conversation outline: per-turn questions and final replies, keyword filter, one-click jump, light/full history modes, and Ctrl/Shift+click to open the same position in the Trajectory view.
- [lim1202/dsh-agent-theater](https://github.com/lim1202/dsh-agent-theater) - A conversation view beside Chat and Trajectory that turns a DSH session into a character stage, with Codex Pet sprites, comic bubbles and a completion report listing the files and attachments the turn produced.
- [Limitinfinitude/DSH-Right-Sidebar](https://github.com/Limitinfinitude/DSH-Right-Sidebar) - DSH Web right-side output dock: session-scoped tabs for reports, images, data, video, and audio, with inline previews, output quality checks, and a searchable per-turn history.
- [Lin-AxiomSight/dsh-zh-layer](https://github.com/Lin-AxiomSight/dsh-zh-layer) - A bilingual overlay layer for DSH: it overlays Chinese names and functional descriptions for plugins, commands and skills, supports three display modes and manually-triggered AI translation, and does not modify a single line of DSH source code.
- [linandadada-pixel/dsh-screenshot](https://github.com/linandadada-pixel/dsh-screenshot) - Native, instant region-screenshot button for the DeepSeek Harness composer: one click freezes the screen, a WeChat-style overlay (gray mask, green selection) appears, and the cropped PNG is inserted into the current draft. An optional eye toggle hides the whole DSH window before capturing, then restores it. No ms-screenclip, about 10 ms per drag frame, coexists with voice input. Windows 10/11 x64 only; the first capture compiles a helper with the built-in .NET Framework csc.exe.
- [linglanxin/dsh-feng-gu-lite](https://github.com/linglanxin/dsh-feng-gu-lite) - Offline Beijing-time peak/idle indicator below the conversation composer, with a 2026 calendar, uncertainty labels, and brief peak reminders; no API key or runtime network access required.
- [LingMoe404/dsh-cot-translation-reader](https://github.com/LingMoe404/dsh-cot-translation-reader) - Shows the official COT machine translation inside dsh-better-display's reading view, with a per-card original/translated toggle on better-display 0.3.12 and later.
- [lingr7/dsh-reversed-chat](https://github.com/lingr7/dsh-reversed-chat) - Moves the composer to the top of an active conversation and renders the transcript plus the turn navigator newest-first, so replies appear right under the composer.
- [linhx1999/dsh-writing-pad](https://github.com/linhx1999/dsh-writing-pad) - Session-scoped Markdown writing pad docked beside the conversation, with edit/preview modes, selection-based AI rewrites, diff review, undo, and draft recovery.
- [LinJohn8/dsh-model-group-collapse](https://github.com/LinJohn8/dsh-model-group-collapse) - Makes the provider groups in the composer model menu collapsible, keeping only the provider of the current model expanded.
- [linkbag/dsh-capyreporter](https://github.com/linkbag/dsh-capyreporter) - Always-on-top capybara task reporter for DSH Web — a transparent floating pet that narrates each trajectory step in a speech bubble, names the project it reports for, and alerts you when a task completes while you work elsewhere.
- [linshufan21/dsh-boot-compass](https://github.com/linshufan21/dsh-boot-compass) - Desktop full-screen boot splash. It covers DSH's loading card before first paint, plays a 4.5 s five-stage compass sequence, then hands off to the app; clicking anywhere skips ahead, and reopening the window from the tray replays it. The first cold start may hitch once, then runs smoothly.
- [Lionad-Morotar/dsh-jump](https://github.com/Lionad-Morotar/dsh-jump) - Press a chord, then type a letter label to click or focus any visible target.
- [liqiGeGe/dsh-plugin-execution-graph](https://github.com/liqiGeGe/dsh-plugin-execution-graph) - Adds an Execution graph conversation-view tab that renders each turn's prompts, replies, and tool calls/results as a foldable timeline with a detail panel and PNG export.
- [liqiming-whu/dsh-status-card](https://github.com/liqiming-whu/dsh-status-card) - Adds a configurable dsh-ui status card before each DSH agent reply, with bilingual templates and a live settings preview. Requires @omdsh-dev/dsh-genui from dsh-market; without it, the card markup is emitted unrendered.
- [lisihao/dsh-intelligent-UI](https://github.com/lisihao/dsh-intelligent-UI) - Optional source-preserving chat rendering with selectable themes, offline Uiverse component tools and a report-local evidence workspace. Preview for DSH 0.2.1-rc.2.
- [lisniuse/dsh-modal-enhancer](https://github.com/lisniuse/dsh-modal-enhancer) - Window-like controls for every Web UI modal: drag by title bar, eight-way resize, pin against outside clicks, maximize to the full viewport, removable backdrop blur, and per-dialog persistent state.
- [littleboylittlegirl/dsh-community-hot](https://github.com/littleboylittlegirl/dsh-community-hot) - Floating community panel for the Web UI: 24h hot topics and hot plugins TOP10 with a draggable, always-on-top button.
- [LittleLemonJam/dsh-quick-notes](https://github.com/LittleLemonJam/dsh-quick-notes) - A floating sticky-note pad for the DeepSeek Harness Web GUI with a note directory, text editing, pasted images and local persistence.
- [liu19666/dsh-model-block-picker](https://github.com/liu19666/dsh-model-block-picker) - Composer model selector grouped by provider: pick a provider card, then drill into that provider to choose a model, with cross-provider search and Reasoning Effort kept.
- [liu19666/dsh-screenshot](https://github.com/liu19666/dsh-screenshot) - Windows screenshot overlay: highlights the window, screen or taskbar under the cursor and dims the rest, then annotates (shapes, arrows, pen, text, mosaic) and drops the image into the composer without sending it.
- [liu3734/jira-tasks-dsh-plugin](https://github.com/liu3734/jira-tasks-dsh-plugin) - JIRA task panel under the DSH composer: per-workspace project key and optional JQL, listing the issues assigned to the current user with status, priority and issue-type chips, a total count, collapse and refresh, links to JIRA, plus a settings card for the base URL and token with a connection probe.
- [liudapeng0311/dsh-pwa-launcher](https://github.com/liudapeng0311/dsh-pwa-launcher) - Turns the browser PWA shortcut already on your desktop into a real launcher: it adopts that shortcut and keeps its icon, then starts dsh web silently on double-click and opens the app window; adds restart and dsh self-update (with rollback) controls to the web UI. Windows only.
- [liufeicc/dsh-model-provider-label](https://github.com/liufeicc/dsh-model-provider-label) - Shows the provider name in the current-model button and its menu, so same-named models from different providers stay distinguishable.
- [liufeicc/dsh-split-workspaces](https://github.com/liufeicc/dsh-split-workspaces) - Tiles several workspaces side by side or stacked in the Web UI, one conversation per pane, with draggable splitters and pane swapping.
- [liuhaolong16/dsh-quote-reply](https://github.com/liuhaolong16/dsh-quote-reply) - Keeps quotes out of the message body: quoting any passage from an assistant turn opens an editable quote tray above the composer, the quotes are injected into the next turn as system-prompt context (attributed with source and time), and an expandable quote row under that turn shows exactly which quotes were used. Quotes persist across page reloads and are claimed per turn; the host and client halves hand-shake on version and report a compatibility verdict.
- [liuhusk/dsh-status-card](https://github.com/liuhusk/dsh-status-card) - A permanent status card in the top-right corner of the DSH content area: one line for where this turn's time went (model, tools, waiting), plus collapsible to-dos with a progress bar, the session goal (edit/pause/resume/clear), submitted plans that open in the preview pane, the files this session changed, subagents with their token and duration figures, and this session's background jobs: running rows carry a two-step stop button, finished ones fold into a count line that stays collapsed until opened; it yields space to the content as the area narrows and folds into a round button at the limit.
- [liujia-io/dsh-image-picker](https://github.com/liujia-io/dsh-image-picker) - Adds a paperclip image-picker button to the conversation composer - pick reference images through the system file dialog and feed them straight into the official attachment pipeline (thumbnails, limit checks, upload with message).
- [liujiangnanchenjbguo/dsh-gufa-code](https://github.com/liujiangnanchenjbguo/dsh-gufa-code) - Cursor-style IDE mode for DeepSeek Harness: one click on a workspace menu turns the main panel into a VS Code-like workbench with a Monaco editor, file tree, Git panel (commit graph, push/pull, per-file revert), SSH hosts and a real PTY terminal on the left, while the official DSH conversation stays docked on the right so tool calls, file edits, approvals and session history keep working as you code. Built on a craft-first belief: write it by hand, understand every change, treat AI as an assistant and stay the first owner of your software.
- [liujuntao123/dsh-mobile-keys](https://github.com/liujuntao123/dsh-mobile-keys) - Adds a mobile hotkey mode to the DSH web composer that auto-detects touch and mobile devices and switches to Ctrl+Enter for send and plain Enter for a newline while leaving the desktop keymap untouched, with a Settings toggle, a /mobilekeys command and a window.__dshMobileKeys console API.
- [liuming-dev/dsh-plugins#annotation-rewind-scroll](https://github.com/liuming-dev/dsh-plugins/tree/main/annotation-rewind-scroll) - Annotate replies and documents for the next message, edit past user messages and rerun from that point, and use keyboard shortcuts to navigate user messages or recall previous inputs in the composer.
- [liumorrisclaw/dsh-composer-input-history](https://github.com/liumorrisclaw/dsh-composer-input-history) - Recalls messages you already sent in the DSH composer with ArrowUp; ArrowDown walks forward, restores the draft you were writing, and one more press discards it and clears the composer.
- [LiuPack/dsh-beads-panel](https://github.com/LiuPack/dsh-beads-panel) - Beads issue-graph panel in the left sidebar of the Web GUI: filter ready, in-progress, open, and all beads, read a bead's description, notes, dependencies, and comments, and create, claim, or close beads through the bd CLI. Requires the bd CLI on PATH and a workspace with a Beads database.
- [liupengprivate-blip/dsh-baidu](https://github.com/liupengprivate-blip/dsh-baidu) - A simulated search engine page in the DSH sidebar that queries the local web search API and lists the top 10 results.
- [liushimu/dsh-workspace-detached](https://github.com/liushimu/dsh-workspace-detached) - Windows x64 preview that detaches workspace/session navigation into a separate .NET Framework 4.6.2+/WebView2 Evergreen window, with grouped trees, overview/focused modes, logical drag-and-drop, activity/manual ordering and a resizable focused-view preview; no DSH Core patch and no Better Workspace runtime dependency. Derived from KannaKuron/dsh-better-workspace — do not install both.
- [liushQAQ/dsh-prompt-optimizer](https://github.com/liushQAQ/dsh-prompt-optimizer) - Smart prompt optimization & dynamic thinking effort adaptation with composer capsule, DSH/custom models, undo/re-optimize, and native settings.
- [liushuchang-2012/dsh-boot-splash](https://github.com/liushuchang-2012/dsh-boot-splash) - Boot film for the web UI: a blue whale floods black from below as the page loads, then glides left and pulls the | deepseek HARNESS wordmark out of itself before the app appears.
- [liuzwZYW/dsh-pwa-ui](https://github.com/liuzwZYW/dsh-pwa-ui) - Gives an installed dsh web app a native-looking window caption through Window Controls Overlay - the manifest route is served with display_override, the app's own sidebar brand row and session header become the drag region, and the app's own trailing controls are inset clear of the browser's caption chrome.
- [liwangzimo/dsh-calendar-diary](https://github.com/liwangzimo/dsh-calendar-diary) - A plain-notebook calendar in the DSH sidebar: a 70-day, Monday-first grid whose cells print that day's note; one note per day is stored in a .xlsx you choose, in the DesktopCal two-column layout (Excel date serial plus free text, newest date first). Clicking a day opens the editor for it, and import/export move that same format in and out, with a preview step and a conflict policy (keep, overwrite, concat or skip).
- [liweidong1722/dsh-work-list](https://github.com/liweidong1722/dsh-work-list) - Local-first DSH Web work checklist with rich-text notes, categories, folding, drag reordering, search, JSON import/export, and a restorable trash.
- [liyansong0015/dsh-sao-link-start](https://github.com/liyansong0015/dsh-sao-link-start) - Adds a configurable SAO-inspired full-screen startup animation to DeepSeek Harness.
- [liyutongzuishuai/plugin-organizer](https://github.com/liyutongzuishuai/plugin-organizer) - Files the settings entries your installed plugins add below the ones dsh ships, behind a divider with a live count, and keeps up automatically as plugins come and go.
- [lizilong1993/dsh-plain-talk](https://github.com/lizilong1993/dsh-plain-talk) - Composer toggle that shapes replies: conclusion first, inline quotes, no cross-turn references.
- [lizilong1993/dsh-unlimited-effort](https://github.com/lizilong1993/dsh-unlimited-effort) - Composer infinity switch: while the highest reasoning effort is selected, one click appends a configurable notice to the system prompt.
- [lizixuan200410-cmd/dsh-bright-text](https://github.com/lizixuan200410-cmd/dsh-bright-text) - Sets the UI text colour for light and dark themes separately, plus the body font size and the interface typeface, with system Kai/Xing/Li/Song families, four open-source calligraphy fonts and support for importing your own font file.
- [lizixuan200410-cmd/dsh-ink-whale-leap](https://github.com/lizixuan200410-cmd/dsh-ink-whale-leap) - Plays a short ink-wash whale-leap animation over the DeepSeek Harness web UI when it loads, then fades out on its own.
- [lj100886/dsh-team-canvas](https://github.com/lj100886/dsh-team-canvas) - Renders an Agent Teams swimlane canvas above the composer: lead centred with members below, tasks laid out in lanes by longest dependency path, and four SVG connector families drawn as dashed paths (task dependency as a cubic bezier from the upstream card edge to the downstream card edge, lead bus, peer bus, member-to-task tether). Cards are absolutely positioned DOM, connectors are one SVG layer sharing the same geometry constants. Layout constants and path algorithms are ported from cc-haha. Reads the live per-session agentTeam projection (roster, shared tasks, dependency edges, running state) and falls back to the session todo list when the session has no team; team messages are not part of the projection yet, so the communication panel shows an empty state. Body colours come from host theme tokens; the eight member identity colours are the deliberate exception — fixed hues mixed with the theme accent via color-mix, declared as CSS variables because the same values feed an SVG stroke attribute that rejects colour functions. Ships zh/en dictionaries with identical keys.
- [ljxdsg12/dsh-composer-polish](https://github.com/ljxdsg12/dsh-composer-polish) - Adds a Polish button to the composer tool row right of the permission control: it reads the draft you have already typed, has the current model infer your intent and rewrite the draft into a clearer prompt, then turns the button into Cancel so one click restores the exact pre-polish text.
- [lk251066/dsh-tui-pro](https://github.com/lk251066/dsh-tui-pro) - A full-screen terminal workbench for DeepSeek Harness with a durable assistant, workspace-grouped project sessions, transcript-only scrolling, and structured thinking, tool, diff, plan, and subagent views.
- [Lkrain821/dsh-pixelwhale](https://github.com/Lkrain821/dsh-pixelwhale) - Turns the "deep diving" thinking status into a pixel font, with an animated pixel blue whale spouting and wagging its tail beside it.
- [Lli-WenZe/dsh-model-console](https://github.com/Lli-WenZe/dsh-model-console) - Model console panel: switch the model for every conversation at once or one at a time, filter sessions by idle time, fold subagents under their parent session, and bulk archive or release sessions.
- [lmmzss-jk/dsh-plugin-balance](https://github.com/lmmzss-jk/dsh-plugin-balance) - Balance and live session usage for the DSH web UI: header balance button (left of the Session log), dropdown with account balance, real-time token breakdown (cache miss/hit, cache write, output), cache hit rate, and official-rate cost estimates with peak/off-peak auto-switch.
- [LN334/dsh-netease-float](https://github.com/LN334/dsh-netease-float) - Full-page NetEase Cloud Music player for DSH, reworked from stardream-1/dsh-netease-float (it reuses that package name, so the two cannot be installed side by side): now playing, full-screen lyrics, playlists, search and account sign-in.
- [lo2589/dsh-plugin-rightbar](https://github.com/lo2589/dsh-plugin-rightbar) - A shared right sidebar for the DSH web client — other plugins register tabs into the `rightbar.tab` slot instead of each pinning a `position:fixed` panel; ships a `rightbar-tab` skill for agents and an opt-in mode that moves legacy fixed panels aside.
- [loadingvx/deepseeh-harness-ultra-slash](https://github.com/loadingvx/deepseeh-harness-ultra-slash) - Separate slash-command group in the Web UI composer: /steer injects text on the agent's next step without interrupting the turn, /new starts a blank session, /skill and /docs wrap /steer with post-turn skill or docs tasks, and custom /steer aliases are configurable in Settings.
- [loadingvx/deepseek-harness-workbench-plugin](https://github.com/loadingvx/deepseek-harness-workbench-plugin) - Fills in the Web UI's missing IDE workbench: resizable chat / editor / files-and-Git columns in Conversation, multi-tab file editing with save and new-file, workspace Terminal, a file tree with name filter and open-in-external-editor, SCM (stage/commit/push/pull, branch switch, Git graph, inline diffs), a status bar, and git_* model tools.
- [lof6yo/dsh-just-settings](https://github.com/lof6yo/dsh-just-settings) - Replaces the sidebar's More menu with a single button that opens Settings.
- [lokih1028/dsh-plugin-canvas-pro](https://github.com/lokih1028/dsh-plugin-canvas-pro) - Adds a Canvas drawer and eight model tools to create, patch, version, and export HTML, Markdown, Mermaid, SVG, and React artifacts.
- [lokih1028/dsh-prompt-optimizer](https://github.com/lokih1028/dsh-prompt-optimizer) - One-click prompt enhancement and structuring button for DSH composer.
- [LoneLeave/aiplug#dsh-ai-import-ui](https://github.com/LoneLeave/aiplug/tree/main/packages/ui) - Web panel for the DSH AI history importer — a floating button and a three-column wizard to preview conversations, pick which ones to import, and file them into workspace folders.
- [LonelyHerbivore/dsh-image-question#plugin](https://github.com/LonelyHerbivore/dsh-image-question/tree/main/plugin) - Ask the user a question about a local image; the DSH Web card offers zoom, pan, box, brush and lasso annotation, NG/OK/unsure verdicts and a note, and the agent receives annotations back in source-image pixel coordinates.
- [long6177/dsh-markdown-input](https://github.com/long6177/dsh-markdown-input) - Markdown live rendering for the DSH Web UI composer: typed Markdown (bold, lists, code fences) renders as you type in a CodeMirror-based takeover composer; sent user messages render as Markdown; pasted rich text becomes clean Markdown.
- [Lostforest7/dsh-revise](https://github.com/Lostforest7/dsh-revise) - Visual frontend revision workbench: point at a real page element, describe the change, send it into the current DSH session, then review fixed-viewport before/after screenshots (1280x800 and 390x844) beside a captured source diff, accepting each revision item by item. Revision records are kept per session under .dsh-revise/ and export as a single portable HTML report.
- [lov0vo/dsh-composer-autopair](https://github.com/lov0vo/dsh-composer-autopair) - Auto-pairs brackets and quotes in the DSH chat composer, inserting the closing half with the caret in between; it also wraps selected text with the typed pair and handles brackets committed by an IME.
- [LoveIrishCoffee/dsh-effort-ultra](https://github.com/LoveIrishCoffee/dsh-effort-ultra) - A native reasoning-effort control for the composer: a blue-to-violet tier bar with a starfield and a sheen sweep. It owns the model seat and drives the official model-directories service, so the ladder comes from each model's own reasoning declaration and no other plugin is required.
- [LoveStudyXin/dsh-sticky-notes](https://github.com/LoveStudyXin/dsh-sticky-notes) - Sticky-note wall in the sidebar: six paper colors, pin and search, up to six images per note, local backups, and an agent tool that lets a session or an IM bot add a note without opening the browser.
- [lovvvve/dsh-quick-actions#composer-quick-actions](https://github.com/lovvvve/dsh-quick-actions/tree/main/packages/composer-quick-actions) - Global Quick Actions beside every session-backed composer in DSH. One press sends a preset or your own saved text, with per-action send confirmation.
- [loyalchiiina/dsh-artifact-linker](https://github.com/loyalchiiina/dsh-artifact-linker) - Turns every file artifact the agent names in chat into a clickable entry — and refuses to paint one that does not resolve. Hover resolves asynchronously, and only a file that really exists becomes a hand pointer with a dashed blue outline (30 s TTL cache, so repeated hovers cost nothing); confirmed non-artifacts stay plain text and the right-click falls through to the host menu untouched. Left click reveals the file in Explorer already selected (Shell.Application Explore plus SelectItem with SVSI_ENSUREVISIBLE, so the pane scrolls to it; explorer.exe /select is deliberately not used because it silently drops the selection when an instance is already running). Right click offers seven actions: preview in DSH's built-in sidebar, open with the default app, reveal in Explorer, save as, copy the file itself to the clipboard, copy the absolute path, or insert the path into the composer. Ctrl or Shift with left click opens with the default app, Alt with left click copies the path. Names resolve through the absolute path, the session workspace and every profile workspace, then a cached basename index built with a depth budget, shrinking from the left when a mention swallows leading words. The host half also keeps six system-prompt rules resident, so absolute paths stay in tool arguments while the body shows only file names, and every route is loopback-only.
- [loyalchiiina/dsh-chat-image-lightbox](https://github.com/loyalchiiina/dsh-chat-image-lightbox) - Full-screen lightbox for chat images: click any image to open the full-resolution original with zoom (wheel, buttons, shortcuts), drag panning, prev/next paging, filename caption and download, plus keyboard-arrow and touch-swipe navigation. One "image display control" popover next to the composer collapses every image in the conversation into a thumbnail grid whose column count (1-12) is configurable and remembered, or hides them entirely so the space closes up and restores them later; images are styled in place and no node is ever moved or reparented. The host-side display_image tool takes a local path or directory (TIFF/HEIC transcoded, originals still saved on download), a remote URL, or a keyword search over Bing, Huaban and Sogou that samples loadability before picking a source, re-saves images blocked by hotlink protection and drops dead links after a magic-byte check, returning paste-ready Markdown. It also keeps a system-prompt rule that every delivered image must appear in the reply body as ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/NEVSTOP-LAB/awesome-dsh-plugin/main/…) markdown, because collapsed tool rows are invisible to the reader.
- [loyalchiiina/dsh-font-enhancer](https://github.com/loyalchiiina/dsh-font-enhancer) - DIY the DSH UI typography and colours: 10 built-in regions plus box-select region creation, each with its own Chinese and English font, size, line-height, weight (100-900), italic switch and text colour picker; 18 Chinese and 18 English fonts ship in the pickers, every region can be overridden by one unified setting, and named theme presets can be saved, applied, renamed and deleted. Random font assignment and random colour generation work for the whole UI or per region, and 8 fonts (LXGW WenKai, Smiley Sans, ZCOOL KuaiLe, Comic Neue, Baloo 2, Fredoka, Nunito, Caveat) can be downloaded and installed with one click (only Comic Neue ships in the package) alongside your own .ttf/.otf files, with a factory-default reset that restores the shipped palette. A 字 floating ball opens the panel, which carries a collapsible quick-start guide, and position and settings persist across restarts. The ball is shown or hidden from the DSH settings floating-ball section together with the other two balls, which share one 130-skin catalogue with a per-ball sync switch.
- [loyalchiiina/dsh-lobsterai-daddy](https://github.com/loyalchiiina/dsh-lobsterai-daddy) - Turns the LobsterDaddy multi-account console into a floating ball in the DSH window: drag it anywhere and the position persists, and the panel can be set to follow the ball. Clicking it slides out a drawer that renders the console as native DOM (no iframe) from the loopback-only same-origin proxy, showing action buttons, the account LobsterAI is currently signed in as, and a per-account list with balance, check-in state and last check-in time; a resizable persisted drawer, a live online/offline dot and an account-count badge are included.
- [loyalchiiina/dsh-schedule-calendar](https://github.com/loyalchiiina/dsh-schedule-calendar) - Sidebar schedule assistant for dsh-better-sidebar (that plugin is required — the tab is registered through it): multi-city weather, Chinese almanac, lunar calendar, schedules and reminders, todos, daily and weekly reports, zodiac horoscope, check-in buffs, and per-person rest-day work schedules.
- [loyalchiiina/dsh-skill-browser](https://github.com/loyalchiiina/dsh-skill-browser) - Skills and MCP browser for DeepSeek Harness. One floating ball opens a four-tab panel: Skills, Call Ledger, MCP and Quick Start. The Skills tab browses the library you point it at (native directory chooser, defaults to ~/.dsh/skills, no path is shipped with the plugin) with two-level categories plus custom groups, tree and flat views, expand-all and collapse-all, a configurable number of cards per row (2-6), manual, keyword-based and AI grouping, and search by skill name, description or body. AI grouping sends metadata only - directory name plus the frontmatter description - never the skill body, and it never modifies any skill file; it ships a 24-hour result cache, a live progress overlay showing elapsed versus estimated seconds, and a cumulative-time badge that survives closing or switching away from the panel. Per-card, per-category and whole-library enable/disable rewrites disable-model-invocation in that SKILL.md with a timestamped backup. The Call Ledger reports call counts, success rate, trend and a ranking from tools/result events. The MCP tab reads the live loader configuration (serverName, transport, command, args, env keys), shows real Fiber states, per-server tool lists and persisted call statistics, and supports enable and disable by appending a managed block to the profile patch instead of rewriting the whole YAML, plus reconnect and export to mcpServers JSON. It also scans the machine for installed but unconfigured MCP packages and runs an AI fix pass that diagnoses failing servers and applies reconnect, enable, disable or clean actions with automatic backup. Quick Start is an eight-step guide, and 130 skins stay in sync across the three floating balls.
- [loyalchiiina/dsh-todo-float-ball](https://github.com/loyalchiiina/dsh-todo-float-ball) - Draggable floating ball that shows the current DSH session todo checklist live — done/total on its face (capsule mode also shows the running task title) plus a matching progress ring. Click to expand or fold the full list, right-click to switch capsule mode, and state colours mark in-progress (pulsing), done, pending and empty. Replaced plans are archived as history rows that can be hidden one by one, cleared and restored instead of counting towards progress, and other sessions can be pinned side by side. Data comes from the runtime sessions projection first with the official todo panel as a fallback, so the full list survives even when the official strip is collapsed. Text colour can be forced to auto, black or white, the ball snaps back to the bottom-right corner on demand, and it stays draggable right up to the top edge of the window. 130 shared skins — including two official dark and light skins that follow the client light/dark theme automatically, plus a per-ball sync switch so each ball either follows the shared skin or keeps its own — with Shadow DOM style isolation and position remembered per profile, identical on desktop and web.
- [Lphehe/dsh-composer-assist](https://github.com/Lphehe/dsh-composer-assist) - DSH input assistant for next-message prediction, draft autocomplete (text completion), and prompt polishing/rewrite. Session-local suggestions; Tab accepts into the draft without sending. Requires a configured model; calls may incur charges. Source-available; modifications require prior written permission.
- [lrplrplrp/dsh-live2d](https://github.com/lrplrplrp/dsh-live2d) - A Live2D waifu plugin for DSH.
- [lsdt45/dsh-plan-plus](https://github.com/lsdt45/dsh-plan-plus) - Plan review enhancement for DeepSeek Harness Web: reply to the plan-review card inline to have the model revise the plan, and browse, compare, edit and archive every plan version in a full-screen overlay.
- [lsdt45/dsh-workspace-plus](https://github.com/lsdt45/dsh-workspace-plus) - Hierarchical workspace tree plus browser-style session tabs for the DSH sidebar: a "/" in a workspace or session title creates a virtual folder, with sections, pinning, time buckets, drag ordering, unified 6px status dots, per-row appearance customization (colour, glow, weight, icon) and a floating full-text search over session titles and message bodies. Several settings switches restore any behaviour to the host default, and a host-side route deletes sessions permanently (DSH ships archive only). Fork of KannaKuron/dsh-better-workspace.
- [lsfcy/dsh-composer-predictions](https://github.com/lsfcy/dsh-composer-predictions) - Shows a dimmed suggestion of the user's next message in the empty composer after a settled turn; Tab accepts it into the draft and Escape or typing dismisses it.
- [lsh2002/dsh-custom-fonts](https://github.com/lsh2002/dsh-custom-fonts) - Customize the UI's Chinese and English fonts, code font, and global font-size offset on a settings page, with a live preview and one-click reset.
- [lsq-dsh-plugins/dsh-workbench-layout](https://github.com/lsq-dsh-plugins/dsh-workbench-layout) - Rearranges DSH Web into a three-column workbench with file editing, Git views, Markdown preview, and workspace terminals.
- [LTao96/dsh-plugin-overview](https://github.com/LTao96/dsh-plugin-overview) - A local overview of installed DSH plugins. It does not install packages — pair it with a plugin market. Fold third-party settings, organize notes/groups with AI, check health and toggle plugins, so nothing gets lost in clutter.
- [ltayshark0637/dsh-Shutdown-Guard](https://github.com/ltayshark0637/dsh-Shutdown-Guard) - Adds a sidebar card to DeepSeek Harness (Windows verified) that shuts the machine down once every task finishes and the card is armed, holding back while background jobs, errored sessions or manual interruptions are still in play; the 1.6.1 beta adds restart, sleep, hibernation, lock, sign-out and user-supplied scripts, and mounts on macOS and Linux too (implemented, not yet tested there).
- [LTP414/dsh-btw-marginalia](https://github.com/LTP414/dsh-btw-marginalia) - Read-only /btw side questions for DeepSeek Harness: forks the current session, injects the running turn's task, recent tool calls and partial answer so a mid-turn question has context, denies every tool call at execution time, and keeps a per-session topic history with follow-up, stop, retry and delete.
- [Luaphes/dsh-web-attention-badge](https://github.com/Luaphes/dsh-web-attention-badge) - Attention reminders: frame badge, tab-title count, and a status-colored whale favicon for sessions waiting for input or finished unopened.
- [lucagiftzek/dsh-artifacts](https://github.com/lucagiftzek/dsh-artifacts) - An Artifacts tab in the DSH sidebar that lists the files an agent produced and previews them in place with live reload, plus artifact template tools for agents.
- [lucagiftzek/dsh-workspace-rail](https://github.com/lucagiftzek/dsh-workspace-rail) - Replaces the DSH sidebar list with a workspace rail: pinned chats and folders, projects that are folders with their own AGENTS.md and files, a virtualized Recents list ordered by the last finished turn, a project_search tool, unread markers, a collapsed icon column with an unread count and a live browser tab title.
- [lucas07814-hub/dsh-pet-guardian-dragon](https://github.com/lucas07814-hub/dsh-pet-guardian-dragon) - A draggable desktop pet for the DSH web interface with interactive actions and growth states.
- [lucky01222/dsh-workbench-shell](https://github.com/lucky01222/dsh-workbench-shell) - Compact 56px app navigation rail with a separate conversation list and sidebar collapse control for the DSH web UI; also hides embedding, rerank and image/video generation models from the chat model menu and /model.
- [lucyVan/dsh-side-chat-slash](https://github.com/lucyVan/dsh-side-chat-slash) - Side chat for the DSH Web GUI with a /side slash command, forked from @flyer103/dsh-side-chat 0.7.0 and adding a host /side command, an open-request bridge, and approval pinned to never. It cannot be installed alongside the upstream @flyer103/dsh-side-chat — both register the same routes and side-chat tab type.
- [LUgABeEEE/dsh-adapt-packs](https://github.com/LUgABeEEE/dsh-adapt-packs) - Adaptation packs for third-party DSH plugin panels on phones — 44px touch floor, width reclaim, shell hit targets and floating-widget clearance — loaded by the dsh-mobile plugin from its extensions directory (dsh-mobile must be installed for them to take effect), with a settings page that installs them and a per-plugin compatibility list.
- [LUgABeEEE/dsh-global-todo](https://github.com/LUgABeEEE/dsh-global-todo) - Global cross-session todo list in the sidebar that the person and the agent both write to: ten global_todo_* tools add, batch-capture, update, attach documents and links to, append progress notes to and complete items from any session, and each item can be handed to an agent session in one of four modes with the outcome recorded on the item. The list is a single JSON file.
- [LUgABeEEE/dsh-sidebar-density](https://github.com/LUgABeEEE/dsh-sidebar-density) - Lightweight sidebar UI for DeepSeek Harness: five fold switches, three density presets and three width presets, served from one footer row plus a settings section — no dependencies and no background service.
- [LUMIAO9527/DSH-suggestions](https://github.com/LUMIAO9527/DSH-suggestions) - Context-based next-message suggestions and draft rewriting for DeepSeek Harness, with candidate paging, shortcut recording, and companion MCP tools.
- [lumine-423/dsh-session-popout](https://github.com/lumine-423/dsh-session-popout) - Drag a session out of the DSH sidebar and it opens in its own native OS window, showing the full standard conversation UI (Markdown, toolbar, settings) with only the sidebar trimmed away, so several sessions can be watched side by side instead of switching back and forth. Windows verified; macOS and Linux untested. Runs in the DSH desktop app (Electron).
- [Luminet-LT/dsh-inapp-chat](https://github.com/Luminet-LT/dsh-inapp-chat) - Adds a sidebar entry that opens DeepSeek web chat in an in-app window for quick questions while working. Fork of zerorigin-studio/dsh-deepseek-chat (already listed); it uses its own settings namespace, so both can coexist. Needs the desktop bridge (window.dsh.desktop.openWindow).
- [lumoping/dsh-todo-inbox](https://github.com/lumoping/dsh-todo-inbox) - Global cross-session todo inbox for DSH web: any session's agent records human-action todos (merge MRs, approve work orders, reviews) via inbox_add/inbox_done/inbox_list; a sidebar entry shows a count badge and an expandable list to open and complete them, persisted to ~/.dsh/todo-inbox.json.
- [luoshuizhiwei/dsh-desktop-restart](https://github.com/luoshuizhiwei/dsh-desktop-restart) - Adds a restart button to the DeepSeek Harness desktop app — it asks before restarting while work is running, and afterwards reports how long the app was down. Entries can live in the sidebar, the conversation header, the account menu or the settings page, and each one can be turned off. Extras include checking how much sign-in data is stored (a cause of startup failures) and previewing leftover temp files, counted but never deleted. Desktop app only.
- [Lushdawn/dsh-effort-dial](https://github.com/Lushdawn/dsh-effort-dial) - Codex-style reasoning-effort card for the composer: takes over the model selector and shows the current level, the model row and a segmented slider over the model's own adapter-provided levels, with a model list, a reset, and a purple aurora with twinkling particles at the maximum level.
- [LUX09947-CMYK/dsh-re](https://github.com/LUX09947-CMYK/dsh-re) - Adds a refresh button to the DeepSeek Harness desktop title bar next to the Edit menu, reloading the interface without restarting the app.
- [luxi233/dsh-settings-nav-scroll](https://github.com/luxi233/dsh-settings-nav-scroll) - Adds a visible vertical scrollbar to the DSH Web settings navigation when its sections exceed the viewport.
- [luyiheng1111/dsh-context-window-picker](https://github.com/luyiheng1111/dsh-context-window-picker) - Adds a session-scoped context window selector next to the reasoning-effort row in the DeepSeek Harness model popup: a pill showing the effective window opens a dropdown of presets below the model default, the choice applies to the next request of that session only, and a new session falls back to the profile default. It writes no config file and appends nothing to the session log.
- [LWLAymh/dsh-tool-emoji](https://github.com/LWLAymh/dsh-tool-emoji) - Replace tool-card leading icons with emoji while preserving the original cards.
- [lxj5820/dsh-boot-animation](https://github.com/lxj5820/dsh-boot-animation) - Replaces the kernel boot page with a full-window video clip that dissolves into the app; the clip pool, enter mode and fade length are configurable in Settings → Plugins.
- [ly14278/dsh-prompt-brief](https://github.com/ly14278/dsh-prompt-brief) - Adds an Optimize button to the composer that rewrites a one-line idea into a structured product brief: a positioning sentence, 3–6 key features, visual references and design tokens; it sits beside your original text in a compare dialog where you can accept it into the input, generate another version, or cancel and keep the draft untouched. No settings page and no API keys — it calls the model channel you already configured.
- [LyaxZ/dsh-fonttune](https://github.com/LyaxZ/dsh-fonttune) - A font plugin for the DeepSeek Harness Web GUI: the conversation gets its own font, size, line height, weight, tracking, word gap, paragraph gap and first-line indent, the interface font follows it, code keeps its own axis with ligature control; light and dark can each hold their own set, whole setups save as presets, and a floating panel puts every axis a dot away.
- [LyaxZ/dsh-goodies](https://github.com/LyaxZ/dsh-goodies) - Effort slider in the model menu, a live tokens-per-second and turn-total readout beside the running status, a per-answer input/output/cached report, and a completion notification when a turn finishes, for the DeepSeek Harness Web GUI.
- [LyaxZ/dsh-quick-toc](https://github.com/LyaxZ/dsh-quick-toc) - Conversation outline for DeepSeek Harness: the Markdown headings of the whole session, grouped by turn, in a side panel or a full-width curtain; hover previews can be pinned for comparison, bookmarks keep the places you come back to, and a right-click jumps to a section or copies a heading and the levels below it. Six theme colours and a translucent material, title/prompt/full-text/cross-session search, reading-position follow and a Chinese/English interface.
- [lyhue1991/dsh-soup](https://github.com/lyhue1991/dsh-soup) - Project file explorer in the details column (multi-select, drag-move, upload, download, rename, trash, system-open) with an in-session read-only preview tab for Markdown/HTML/JSON/CSV/notebook/PDF + code highlighting, a live model throughput badge, and a multiline GoalBar.
- [lyonleeeee/dsh-steer](https://github.com/lyonleeeee/dsh-steer) - Adds a live status badge to the session header and delivers guidance added mid-task without interrupting the turn.
- [lysforge/dsh-lamplit](https://github.com/lysforge/dsh-lamplit) - One shelf for WeChat Reading, GitHub book repos and your own EPUB/PDF/Markdown, with an immersive reader and AI notes.
- [lyx0717/dsh-washer](https://github.com/lyx0717/dsh-washer) - An always-on-top desktop pet (a washing machine) that renders session state as washing actions: four switchable skins, multi-session counters, and proactive done / stuck / approval alerts on macOS and Windows, plus a detail panel with recent turns and timings.
- [lyy-hast/dsh-coros-plugin](https://github.com/lyy-hast/dsh-coros-plugin) - COROS Sport panel for DeepSeek Harness: sign in to COROS inside the panel, read the account, bound devices, recovery, fitness, recent workouts, daily health and the training schedule in one dashboard, and get a plan from the model with /coros-plan. The plan page writes a fixed four-week, 16-session plan to the account only after a two-step confirmation.
- [Lzh3070/dsh-file-review-tab](https://github.com/Lzh3070/dsh-file-review-tab) - Review agent file changes as a dsh-better-sidebar sidebar tab or chat turn-tail row — line-level diffs and undo.
- [lzpway-jpg/dsh-plugin-brand-custom](https://github.com/lzpway-jpg/dsh-plugin-brand-custom) - Custom brand icon and name for the DSH Web sidebar and conversation hero, editable live from a settings card on the plugin detail page.
- [Lzz2133/dsh-manutd-fixtures](https://github.com/Lzz2133/dsh-manutd-fixtures) - Manchester United 2026/27 fixtures in the sidebar with kickoff times in your local timezone, finished matches with W/L/D results.
- [mackwan84/dsh-ui-mockup#ui-mockup](https://github.com/mackwan84/dsh-ui-mockup/tree/main/bundle/ui-mockup) - Generates UI wireframes and high-fidelity mockups in DeepSeek Harness with selectable DashScope and Volcengine image providers.
- [magian1127/deepseek-harness-zh_pro](https://github.com/magian1127/deepseek-harness-zh_pro) - UI enhancement suite for the DSH web client: Chinese locale completion, one-row stats, auto-expanded thinking, chat width, session archiving and delete, plus an optional prompt-injection switch (off by default).
- [magicOF2/dsh-autoload-history](https://github.com/magicOF2/dsh-autoload-history) - Automatically loads the full conversation history when a session opens, so older messages are ready without clicking "Load earlier".
- [magicOF2/dsh-chat-width-customizer](https://github.com/magicOF2/dsh-chat-width-customizer) - Session-header button that cycles the conversation width (748-1600px), widening the chat column, composer, and user bubbles together.
- [magicOF2/dsh-schedule](https://github.com/magicOF2/dsh-schedule) - Local long-term schedule panel: top-bar "日程" text button, today/week/history month-calendar views with stats, completion checkmarks with per-day history, schedule↔conversation links with click-to-jump, recurring tasks (daily/weekly), optional auto carry-over for unfinished one-time tasks, and dailytask_* agent tools.
- [magicOF2/dsh-turn-marks](https://github.com/magicOF2/dsh-turn-marks) - Left-edge turn-marks strip in chat: one bar per user message, click to jump to that message, hover for a preview, active bar turns white.
- [maimaiqwe/prompt-enhancer#dsh-prompt-enhance-cn](https://github.com/maimaiqwe/prompt-enhancer/tree/main/dsh-plugin) - Four-tier prompt enhancer for the composer — the system tier always emits a LangGPT-structured system prompt (# Role / Profile / Skills / Rules / Workflows / Initialization), upgrading task drafts into the system prompt of the agent that runs them, while the other tiers rewrite as a light tidy-up, a single paragraph, or a five-section brief; the iterate workbench refines an editable base under an optional requirement, with fill-back and undo. Never changes intent, never invents requirements.
- [Makk9609/dsh-find-viewer](https://github.com/Makk9609/dsh-find-viewer) - Find bar in the sidebar text preview, with whole-file match counts for plain text and code, highlighted hits, and jumps between them.
- [MannixHu/dsh-statusbar-config](https://github.com/MannixHu/dsh-statusbar-config) - Configurable statistics row below the composer via a ${variable} display template — which segments appear and how they are labelled is fully user-defined.
- [maqian1632/dsh-tray-restart](https://github.com/maqian1632/dsh-tray-restart) - Adds a single “Restart DeepSeek Harness” item to the Windows system tray menu of the DSH desktop app by patching the installed app.asar in place and re-applying that patch after each desktop update; it needs a writable DSH installation directory and adds no other tray actions.
- [MarchLiu/dsh-rich-editor](https://github.com/MarchLiu/dsh-rich-editor) - Rich Markdown notebook for the composer region: a tool-row toggle plus a CodeMirror editor card with Codex-style list editing, submitted through the conversation service.
- [MaRi23333/dsh-agent-instructions-editor](https://github.com/MaRi23333/dsh-agent-instructions-editor) - Edit global AGENTS.md and project AGENTS.md/CLAUDE.md custom instructions in DeepSeek Harness settings, with project-chain discovery, byte-budget display, and save-conflict handling.
- [MarkTina/dsh-activity-heat](https://github.com/MarkTina/dsh-activity-heat) - Scores sessions and workspaces 0-100% by time since their last activity, then shows that heat as a sidebar icon, a small heat dot before every session title, a heat line in the session hover card, and a filterable sessions/workspaces panel that also totals the turns, steps, tokens and conversation time of each workspace.
- [Martlet-Tech/dsh-open-folder-fix](https://github.com/Martlet-Tech/dsh-open-folder-fix) - Fixes the Windows workspace-open action. The shipped opener runs `explorer.exe "<file:// URI>"`, which on some hosts reports success without raising a window — the exit-code verdict cannot tell an opened window from nothing happening. This routes that one action to `explorer.exe /e,<dir>`, which raises a window reliably, and leaves every other application in the menu on the shipped path.
- [MarushiruDonato/dsh-rgb](https://github.com/MarushiruDonato/dsh-rgb) - RGB lighting effects for DeepSeek Harness: animated composer ring, sidebar rail, session indicator, and glowing brand logo.
- [matata2017/dsh-paste-collapse](https://github.com/matata2017/dsh-paste-collapse) - Codex-style paste collapse for the DeepSeek Harness composer: a paste of more than 1000 characters becomes a `[Pasted Content N chars]` placeholder, a second paste of the same size is numbered `#2`, and the host expands it back to the full text before the model reads it, so the model still receives every character. A pill on the top edge of the composer shows the preview, the sent message shows the same pill, and clicking either opens the whole paste in a side panel with a copy button. Pure in memory: nothing is written to disk and no token is saved.
- [Matcha-Eason/dsh-answer-highlighter](https://github.com/Matcha-Eason/dsh-answer-highlighter) - Automatically highlights key points, definitions, warnings, and questions in completed assistant answers using one separate model call.
- [Max-Null/dsh-chat-rail](https://github.com/Max-Null/dsh-chat-rail) - Scroll-painting message rail for DeepSeek Harness: a slim right-edge rail with one indicator per user message, scroll-spy follows the reading position, hover unfolds a canvas with full message previews, and clicking jumps to any history message; animates in sync with better-sidebar panels.
- [Max-Null/dsh-node-appearance](https://github.com/Max-Null/dsh-node-appearance) - Conversation node appearance for the DeepSeek Harness web GUI: configurable per-category and per-tool colors (left accent bar + tinted background), plus a show-thinking toggle; pure frontend rendering, takes effect instantly and persists to the DSH user settings file.
- [Max-Null/dsh-quick-toolbar](https://github.com/Max-Null/dsh-quick-toolbar) - Plugin-button aggregator with carrier + on-site-LLM extensibility: gather any plugin's scattered buttons into one entry (SSiD title bar / web floating ball), LLM registers per environment, right-click deregisters — pure data, no plugin source touched.
- [maxy1817/dsh-inline-edit](https://github.com/maxy1817/dsh-inline-edit) - Edit user messages and add or remove attachments inline, with settings to fork a conversation or regenerate its latest eligible task-starting message in place.
- [mdalboni/dsh-mobile](https://github.com/mdalboni/dsh-mobile) - Mobile viewport lock, overscroll containment, virtual keyboard resizing, and sidebar auto-retraction.
- [mdktelmdktel/dsh-agent-status](https://github.com/mdktelmdktel/dsh-agent-status) - Live step pill in the DSH session header: folds the durable session event stream into the agent's current action (tool name plus its argument summary), the step index inside the running turn, the elapsed turn time and the todo progress, polls it every 1.2 seconds, and renders blue pulse while working, green for a finished turn and red for a failed tool call; hides itself 9 seconds after a turn ends. On Windows it also opens an always-on-top desktop HUD (set DSH_AGENT_STATUS_HUD=0 to disable).
- [MDR-EX1000/dsh-desktop-kit](https://github.com/MDR-EX1000/dsh-desktop-kit) - macOS desktop shell for the DSH web UI: a plugin plus a small Tauri/WKWebView window over the loopback web surface — real macOS fullscreen, single instance, external links delegated to the system browser, and browser-style zoom.
- [meihaoyidian/life-workbench](https://github.com/meihaoyidian/life-workbench) - Personal workbench panel with an overview dashboard, custom kanban boards, a note pad and a file library, persisted to local JSON by the host.
- [meihuaanying/dsh-panel-fold](https://github.com/meihuaanying/dsh-panel-fold) - Folds the DSH sidebar's global panel launchers into the More button it already renders, so the workspace and session list below regains up to seven 36px rows. A row in the More menu toggles the fold back, and the choice is kept in localStorage.
- [meijieYUan/dsh-work-pay-note](https://github.com/meijieYUan/dsh-work-pay-note) - A draggable sticky note that counts today's and this month's earnings in real time from your work hours, with an optional photo, GIF or video background.
- [melt502/DSH-Session-Reference-UX-Patch](https://github.com/melt502/DSH-Session-Reference-UX-Patch) - Durable, reversible installer that patches DSH Desktop session-reference UX — workspace-prioritized @ candidates, title-first display, two-line menu layout, and sidebar drag-to-composer session references.
- [MEMZ-JZY/DSH-Claude-Style-Reasoning-Slider](https://github.com/MEMZ-JZY/DSH-Claude-Style-Reasoning-Slider) - Animated Claude-style reasoning-effort slider and model picker that replaces the native DSH model selector.
- [MengLs1620/dsh-deepseek-status](https://github.com/MengLs1620/dsh-deepseek-status) - Shows DeepSeek's official service status in the DSH session header, including overall and per-component status, uptime percentages, and active or recent incidents. A maintained fork of KekuKase/dsh-deepseek-status (published as @mengls1620/dsh-deepseek-status since 0.1.6); do not install it alongside the original dsh-deepseek-status — both register the same /deepseek-status route and plugin row id.
- [mengqinlol/dsh-mobile-shell](https://github.com/mengqinlol/dsh-mobile-shell) - Adapts the DSH Web GUI for mobile screens and adds a workspace file browser with previews and downloads.
- [mengyun233/dsh-codex-pet](https://github.com/mengyun233/dsh-codex-pet) - Auto-migrate Codex desktop-pet skins into DSH: an animated corner pet that mirrors agent state (thinking, tool use, awaiting approval, failed, done), with per-session frosted-glass dialogs and a full settings panel.
- [mengzhongjie/dsh-codex-sidebar](https://github.com/mengzhongjie/dsh-codex-sidebar) - Turns the DSH sidebar into a message view, where a bell button groups sessions by priority and date and each row carries its preview and workspace name.
- [mercy719/dsh-client-ui-weather](https://github.com/mercy719/dsh-client-ui-weather) - Weather dashboard for the DeepSeek Harness Web GUI with city switching, current conditions, an eight-hour trend, and a five-day forecast.
- [Meredith2328/dsh-sticky-note](https://github.com/Meredith2328/dsh-sticky-note) - Quick sticky notes on the composer toolbar: jot ideas or TODOs, auto-saved as Markdown, one click to send into the chat.
- [mervin1944/dsh-version-badge](https://github.com/mervin1944/dsh-version-badge) - Version badge above the sidebar settings button: shows the current dsh version, expands to list every core package version, and checks npm for updates with one-click deploy.
- [mervyn-teo/dsh-plugin-collapsible-steps](https://github.com/mervyn-teo/dsh-plugin-collapsible-steps) - Collapses consecutive tool-use and thinking steps between messages into a single bracketed, expandable item.
- [MeteorNOX/DeepSeek-Balance-Whale-Widget](https://github.com/MeteorNOX/DeepSeek-Balance-Whale-Widget) - A fixed-corner whale widget for the DSH web GUI — balance, today's usage and per-turn cost with peak/off-peak pricing, editable balance-alert and daily-budget bubbles, a module-based custom bubble queue with A/B weighted choices and random lines or images, 30+ vendor templates (OpenAI, OpenRouter, Kimi, SiliconFlow, Ark, Zhipu, MiniMax and more) with per-model balance and subscription quota, plus task-end sound, imported audio, custom roles and a resource manager. Local-only, no telemetry.
- [meyaomiao/dsh-files-native](https://github.com/meyaomiao/dsh-files-native) - Native-feel DSH attachments: official PNG/JPEG/WebP/GIF thumbs plus 64px cards for other files via drag, paste, or paperclip; non-images land in .dsh-uploads and sent cards sit on the user bubble.
- [mhlywxl/dsh-split-workspace](https://github.com/mhlywxl/dsh-split-workspace) - Split the DSH conversation area into unlimited native panes — side by side, stacked, 2×2 and an automatic grid — each a real session with its own composer, streaming and approvals.
- [mhxy13867806343/dsh-plugin-edit-files](https://github.com/mhxy13867806343/dsh-plugin-edit-files) - In-place text editing from the right-Sidebar document preview with ⌘S save and an external-change conflict check, plus a workspace Files tab (fuzzy search, create/rename/delete, per-file git status) and a Git changes tab whose baseline is the remote upstream — files that match the remote drop off the list.
- [mhxy13867806343/dsh-sidebar-account](https://github.com/mhxy13867806343/dsh-sidebar-account) - Sidebar balance row for DSH Desktop — account balance and a top-up button, right under the version row; refreshes every 10 minutes and whenever the window regains focus, with explicit signed-out and error states.
- [mhxy13867806343/dsh-update-vd](https://github.com/mhxy13867806343/dsh-update-vd) - DSH Desktop control center: one-click app updating (progress dialog, unattended install, resume), plus Skills / MCP / Agents / Import settings pages — import agent presets and chat history from Codex, Claude Code, TRAE and WorkBuddy, search remote sources by address, and push notes straight into the composer.
- [mhxy13867806343/my-dsh-desktop-extra](https://github.com/mhxy13867806343/my-dsh-desktop-extra) - DSH Desktop enhancement: one-click update checker, DeepSeek balance & API key viewer, and import other AI tools.
- [Mi-Chuan/box-select-copy](https://github.com/Mi-Chuan/box-select-copy) - Alt+drag a rectangular box over the DSH conversation to copy the text inside it.
- [miaomiao636/dsh-message-navigator](https://github.com/miaomiao636/dsh-message-navigator) - Codex-style message navigator for the DeepSeek Harness Web UI: a tick per user message along the conversation edge, hover or click to preview and smooth-jump to the message, with full history auto-loading.
- [MichengAI/dsh-codex-ui](https://github.com/MichengAI/dsh-codex-ui) - Rebuilds the DeepSeek Harness web sidebar, workspace tree, search, and turn navigation in a Codex-style layout.
- [Microqian2th/dsh-codex-effort-slider](https://github.com/Microqian2th/dsh-codex-effort-slider) - Drags the official reasoning-effort row into a continuous track: blue-to-violet fill, a starfield on the higher levels, and the official value text tinted to the current level. Off stays gray; no official text is rewritten.
- [Midnight-1991/dsh-term-explainer](https://github.com/Midnight-1991/dsh-term-explainer) - Adds a "Terms" action to finished assistant answers that explains the computer-science terms it detects with Chinese definitions, occurrence counts, highlighted context snippets, and clickable nested explanations from a built-in offline dictionary, with no model calls.
- [mihoojj/dsh-prompt-spark](https://github.com/mihoojj/dsh-prompt-spark) - Composer button that rewrites the current draft with four style presets, selection-only rewriting, a keyboard shortcut and instant undo; no settings page.
- [miisaka19800/dsh-restart-fab](https://github.com/miisaka19800/dsh-restart-fab) - Floating restart button for the DSH web GUI: restarts the dsh process in place with a full-screen overlay and auto-reloads the page; no launcher script required, works on Windows/macOS/Linux.
- [miiy/dsh-terminal-keybar](https://github.com/miiy/dsh-terminal-keybar) - On touch devices, supplies the keys a phone's soft keyboard lacks (Escape, Tab, Enter, Ctrl-C and the arrow keys) as a translucent on-screen column beside the active terminal.
- [milestone-17/dsh-git-branch-switcher](https://github.com/milestone-17/dsh-git-branch-switcher) - A composer chip that shows the current git branch of the session working directory and switches branches from a TRAE-style popup menu: one-click git fetch for remote branches, remote-tracking branches listed separately and checked out as local tracking branches, a create-branch flow that picks the base branch (master by default) and switches the session onto the new branch right away, a push panel that stages any checked subset of the working-tree changes, commits them and pushes with automatic upstream setup, and a merge panel with two dropdowns that infers the pull source as the default target, lists conflicting files and offers one-click abort. The chip hides itself outside git repositories.
- [milu7/dsh-directory-plugin](https://github.com/milu7/dsh-directory-plugin) - Starts the DSH desktop New Workspace folder picker in a directory you configure (Windows only) and adds a settings page for it, falling back to the stock picker when the saved folder is missing.
- [mimateinn/dsh-i18n](https://github.com/mimateinn/dsh-i18n) - Adds 20 locales (Traditional Chinese HK/TW, Japanese, Korean, French, German, Spanish, Portuguese, Italian, Russian, Ukrainian, Polish, Dutch, Turkish, Arabic, Hindi, Indonesian, Vietnamese, Thai, Swedish) to the DeepSeek Harness Web UI, with hand-polished translations, runtime fallbacks, and automatic translation of market and third-party text.
- [Minajka/dsh-todo-list](https://github.com/Minajka/dsh-todo-list) - A to-do list that lives in the right sidebar instead of the conversation: add an item, tick it off, delete it, and drag rows to reorder them. Items are kept only in the browser's localStorage, and the plugin registers no model-facing tools; a button in the conversation header opens the panel.
- [Minecraftbe/dsh-toolfold](https://github.com/Minecraftbe/dsh-toolfold) - Codex-style tool call folding, collapsing multiple tool calls into a single line.
- [MingoYung/dsh-awakening](https://github.com/MingoYung/dsh-awakening) - Startup animation picker with three bundled DeepSeek, Codex and Claude Code themes, local MP4/WebM imports and an in-app community library; optional first-screen install and restore on supported Windows DSH NEXT builds.
- [MingoYung/dsh-superagent-power](https://github.com/MingoYung/dsh-superagent-power) - Adds a shared Sup composer switch on Windows to start or gracefully stop a separate Super-agent process and release its memory. Requires a working DSH NEXT install with Node 24+, the standalone DSH CLI and an already-configured independent Super-agent profile (verified on DSH 0.2.1-rc.4/rc.5); the bundled bridge.js must also be registered in the Super-agent profile — installing the desktop package alone does not complete setup.
- [minivv/dsh-activity-bell](https://github.com/minivv/dsh-activity-bell) - Sidebar activity bell: a red badge counts sessions that finished, and clicking it swaps the sidebar list from workspace grouping to a day-grouped recent-activity list with a green dot on completions that have not been opened.
- [Missher12/dsh-missher-enhance](https://github.com/Missher12/dsh-missher-enhance) - Adds usage statistics, a highlighted footer, piano navigation, projectless sessions, and model and document helpers to DeepSeek Harness.
- [Missher12/Missher-DSH-Output-Renderer](https://github.com/Missher12/Missher-DSH-Output-Renderer) - Four assistant output layouts with full reasoning, independent tool details, and frame-synchronized or fading text.
- [MitsukiJoe/dsh-better-ux](https://github.com/MitsukiJoe/dsh-better-ux) - Web interface enhancements for DeepSeek Harness with session rename/fork shortcuts, a searchable model picker with provider filters, mobile layouts, global font scaling, context usage alerts, DeepSeek balance and tariff rates, sent-message navigation, and conversation summaries.
- [miuzel/dsh-subagent-ui](https://github.com/miuzel/dsh-subagent-ui) - Search, monitor, interrupt, and batch-archive workspace subagents, featuring real-time activity streaming and an active-agent floating panel.
- [MjxUpUp/dsh-visual](https://github.com/MjxUpUp/dsh-visual) - Interactive HTML or flowchart cards inside the conversation: the tool blocks until the user submits, then returns the clicked action, fields, comments and card state.
- [MKInori/dsh-oneclick-desktop](https://github.com/MKInori/dsh-oneclick-desktop) - Windows desktop integration, a one-click launcher and tray controller for DSH, plus a sidebar button that puts DSH and a chat.deepseek.com window side by side with one shared taskbar button. Installing it writes a desktop shortcut and registers a dshsplit: protocol, both removed by lib/desktop.ps1 -Mode remove.
