- [ml020/dsh-workbuddy](https://github.com/ml020/dsh-workbuddy) - Reskins the DSH conversation hero — brand mark and workspace picker — with a Wordless-style landing view. The native composer stays untouched, and the picker still adds new workspaces (the entry declares its own directory-flow child hole, exactly like the shipped picker).
- [mm24trg2gy-star/dsh-window-keeper](https://github.com/mm24trg2gy-star/dsh-window-keeper) - Remember the DeepSeek Harness desktop window position, size and maximized state, restoring it on next launch. The window logic lives in a standalone PowerShell script that does not depend on the client version; the plugin shell only installs it, keeps it autostarted and exposes a toggle.
- [MM998LI/dsh-dock-vitals](https://github.com/MM998LI/dsh-dock-vitals) - Live CPU, memory and GPU readout in the DeepSeek Harness composer status bar — a clickable badge with a details panel, per-metric toggles, and three refresh speeds.
- [mmyddd/dsh-ccd](https://github.com/mmyddd/dsh-ccd) - Rewrites the reasoning row: instead of the raw chain of thought it shows first-person working notes, written segment by segment while the reasoning streams in, with a toggle back to the original. Notes are stored per session so history rows keep them after a reload, the note writer runs on a configured provider or a downloadable local llama.cpp model, and the model still reads the full reasoning.
- [Mobai-read/dsh-chat-index-rail](https://github.com/Mobai-read/dsh-chat-index-rail) - Client-only right-edge chat input index rail: one bar per user message, two-stage hover preview (15 chars, expandable to 40), click-to-jump, and scroll-follow highlight; static npm bundle with no host half.
- [MochiNek0/dsh-ghost-prompt](https://github.com/MochiNek0/dsh-ghost-prompt) - Predicts your next message after each completed turn and shows it as ghost text in the composer: press → to accept, or keep typing and finish it word by word with Ctrl+→, Esc to dismiss. The prediction model follows the session by default or can be picked in the settings card, which also has an on/off switch and a speed test.
- [moeform-re/dsh-review-md-preview](https://github.com/moeform-re/dsh-review-md-preview) - Markdown preview and rendered side-by-side compare for the changes-review tab.
- [Mombrane/dsh-subagent-monitor](https://github.com/Mombrane/dsh-subagent-monitor) - Live subagent run monitor for the Web UI: a sidebar footer trigger plus a fixed top-right card panel showing each subagent of the current session in real time (running/elapsed, terminal outcomes, tree indent), with one-click jump into the child conversation and a return button, refresh-surviving and mobile-hidden by default.
- [momijijin/dsh-session-grove](https://github.com/momijijin/dsh-session-grove) - A free-form grouping layer for the DSH sidebar: nest sessions from different workspaces into draggable groups without changing workspace membership or logs.
- [Moolight/dsh-shader-workbench](https://github.com/Moolight/dsh-shader-workbench) - Node-based GLSL workbench: wire a shader graph on a canvas and preview it live, compile it to Shadertoy-compatible GLSL with multi-pass buffers (BufferA–D), hot-reload as you edit, cross-compile to HLSL / MSL / SPIR-V, and keep projects, materials, snapshots and resources (images, audio, lyrics) in a local workspace. Ships 57 built-in nodes, 8 bundled examples and 11 agent tools. No runtime dependencies.
- [Mooling0602/dsh-web-file-uploader](https://github.com/Mooling0602/dsh-web-file-uploader) - A file-upload plugin for the DeepSeek Harness.
- [MoonlitDropOfBlood/DSH-Desktop#whaleharbor-promo](https://github.com/MoonlitDropOfBlood/DSH-Desktop/tree/master/plugins/whaleharbor-promo) - Turns a plain-browser DSH session into the WhaleHarbor lite client: a standalone browser --app window with a client-style top bar and task notifications, while the full desktop installer downloads in the background (multi-source, SHA-256 verified) and an install prompt appears when ready.
- [MoonSand/dsh-desktop-restart](https://github.com/MoonSand/dsh-desktop-restart) - Adds two buttons to the sidebar footer: one reloads the interface and client plugins only, the other restarts the desktop app together with its Host. A restart is refused while a plugin install is running. Restart is Windows only and returns 409 elsewhere; reload works on all platforms.
- [MoonSand/dsh-ui-optimize](https://github.com/MoonSand/dsh-ui-optimize) - Switches that tune how DeepSeek Harness looks and behaves across the whole interface, not just a few screens; each works on its own and more arrive as the shell grows. Theme gallery of eight minimalist themes, each defining one palette for light and one for dark and applied through a single theme-token layer, plus sidebar panel folding that collapses the rail rows into one Panels launcher with search and keyboard control, and reload/restart actions at the sidebar foot. Each feature is gated by runtime detection: what the current build cannot do is disabled with a stated reason, and an unknown probe stays enabled. The sidebar Reload/Restart buttons are ported from the separately listed MoonSand/dsh-desktop-restart; uninstall that plugin before installing this one — with both, the sidebar foot shows two sets of buttons.
- [Moonshile/moonshile-dsh-plugins#dsh-workspace-sort](https://github.com/Moonshile/moonshile-dsh-plugins/tree/main/plugins/dsh-workspace-sort) - Re-sorts sidebar workspaces by last activity once per day; stable order within the day.
- [moophone/dsh-sidebar-fold](https://github.com/moophone/dsh-sidebar-fold) - Folds every panel row in the sidebar (slot-registered and plugin-injected alike) into one Plugin Hub entry, giving the workspace list its vertical space back.
- [MorGogh/widget-dock](https://github.com/MorGogh/widget-dock) - Draggable grid-aligned workbench of 28 mini-cards in the margins of the conversation (API balance, token usage, cost, context pressure, todos, goal, usage heatmap, GitHub repo, image relay), with S/M/L/XL size tiers.
- [MorixMa/dsh-download-progress](https://github.com/MorixMa/dsh-download-progress) - A download-progress indicator for the DSH session header: it reads the background-job roster and parses what downloaders already print — curl --progress-bar, wget, aria2, or a JSON progress file — into a progress ring, animated bars, transfer speed and ETA. Jobs that report nothing show as unknown progress instead of being hidden. A finished file opens with one click, or reveals in the file manager. No third-party plugin required; the package ships zero runtime dependencies.
- [MosCloud/dsh-provider-show](https://github.com/MosCloud/dsh-provider-show) - Shows which provider the composer's currently selected model belongs to, and stays silent for providers another plugin already presents.
- [mostkia/dsh-htmlui](https://github.com/mostkia/dsh-htmlui) - Renders agent-authored HTML/CSS/JS inside the conversation as sandboxed iframes, with POST and SSE back to the model and reusable templates.
- [mostkia/dsh-launcher](https://github.com/mostkia/dsh-launcher) - Adds a power button to the DSH sidebar - Shut down or Restart, each behind a confirmation - and ships a Windows tray launcher that starts DSH without a console window, supervises it so Restart can relaunch it, and offers the same start-at-logon switch.
- [Mr-Elk/dsh-composer-assistant](https://github.com/Mr-Elk/dsh-composer-assistant) - Adds next-message prediction, inline draft completion, and prompt optimization to the DSH composer, with a call budget, request stats, and glossary protection in Settings. A fork of Somnus678/dsh-input-assistant; both put their controls on the same composer, so do not install the two together.
- [Mr-giraffe1/dsh-file-drag](https://github.com/Mr-giraffe1/dsh-file-drag) - Drag a file or folder from the right Sidebar's file tree onto the composer to insert an @ reference to it; also works while answering a question card.
- [Mr-Luo0036/dsh-whale-greeting](https://github.com/Mr-Luo0036/dsh-whale-greeting) - A greeting card that speaks — 156 prebuilt voice clips with a one-click mute — plus per-turn completion reports, long-run companionship notes and a takeover approval panel in the DSH conversation area.
- [MrGnUm/dsh-continue-button](https://github.com/MrGnUm/dsh-continue-button) - Adds a one-click Continue button beside Send that resumes a stopped turn by sending the "continue" prompt; hidden while a turn is running or the session is empty.
- [mrSutivu/plugin-effort-slider](https://github.com/mrSutivu/plugin-effort-slider) - Notched reasoning-effort slider grouped with the model picker in the composer, with theme variables and English, French and Chinese labels.
- [mu915275-cmd/dsh-boot-animation](https://github.com/mu915275-cmd/dsh-boot-animation) - Plays an intro full-frame on a new conversation, or every time you open the one you pinned. Four clips ship inside the plugin itself, embedded in code with no separate video files, and an in-app library switches between them, previews the choice, and accepts your own clip. The picker has two independent sections, so the intro animation and the workspace wallpaper are picked separately; the wallpaper can be a still or a looping tail of the last N seconds, with independent transparency for the picture and the sidebar plus three blur levels. The sidebar and the workspace also get a text colour each, for when the picture is at full strength and the theme own text no longer reads.
- [MuFeng262/dsh-thinking-slider](https://github.com/MuFeng262/dsh-thinking-slider) - Replaces the composer model selector with a "model · reasoning level" trigger: a slider across every level a model reports, three switchable modes (the DSH native selector, a gradient particle rail, an energy-charge track), and a per-provider one-click fill for models that declare no reasoning levels.
- [Mulibaixi/dsh-translation-plugin](https://github.com/Mulibaixi/dsh-translation-plugin) - Translation and refinement workspace with topic memory, revision history, and main-model coordination; Normal mode uses the companion Composer extension.
- [MurasakiIzumi/dsh-ticker-jp](https://github.com/MurasakiIzumi/dsh-ticker-jp) - A floating stock ticker for DeepSeek Harness: real-time quotes for Nikkei 225 and TOPIX ETF out of the box, plus worldwide markets via Yahoo Finance — Japan (.T), US, Hong Kong (.HK), China (.SS/.SZ) and any other symbol — with a customizable watchlist, display aliases and 16 UI languages.
- [mustakimabdullah25-tech/dsh-screen-translator](https://github.com/mustakimabdullah25-tech/dsh-screen-translator) - Universal on-screen translator for DeepSeek Harness Web UI: translates text, attributes, Shadow DOM, and iframes across 45+ languages.
- [Muxiuesd/dsh-pixel](https://github.com/Muxiuesd/dsh-pixel) - Pixel art editor in a DSH conversation tab: pencil, eraser, bucket fill and eyedropper on a grid canvas that zooms in whole steps around the cursor; up to eight layers with merge, reorder, rename and full undo; a 36-slot palette whose edits move the old colour to a free slot so existing pixels never change; PNG export written straight into the session directory.
- [MYanhui/DSH-plug-in](https://github.com/MYanhui/DSH-plug-in) - Adds three settings pages to DeepSeek Harness: add, edit, enable and remove MCP servers; write agent skills by hand or install them from a GitHub repository; and keep a library of global personas with one active.
- [mzzsfy/dsh-plugin#dsh-context-manager](https://github.com/mzzsfy/dsh-plugin/tree/main/packages/dsh-context-manager) - Conversation enhancements: a history-input overlay (Alt+Up with workspace/session/global/prompt scopes), recall of unapplied interjected messages, conversation forking with fork-family version switching, and optional auto-resend after forking (off by default).
- [mzzsfy/dsh-plugin#dsh-settings-nav-icons](https://github.com/mzzsfy/dsh-plugin/tree/main/packages/dsh-settings-nav-icons) - Section icons for the settings navigation: replaces the uniform gear icons with per-section glyphs and fills the plugin-market card avatar slot with each plugin icon; a page reload after uninstall restores the official icons.
- [mzzsfy/dsh-plugin#dsh-think-expand](https://github.com/mzzsfy/dsh-plugin/tree/main/packages/dsh-think-expand) - Auto-expands streaming thinking: always keeps the latest thinking block expanded, manual actions take priority, only the last block expands when opening a session, and nothing remains after uninstall.
- [n431mn/lazy-workspace-manager](https://github.com/n431mn/lazy-workspace-manager) - Folder-scoped sidebar for DSH Web: by default only folders you load are listed with their sessions; unload a folder without deleting its registration, mark folders to load at startup, and opening a session loads its registered workspace so it does not fall into Ungrouped.
- [N9-Developer-Empowerment/DSH-Vibeify#dsh-vibeify-experience](https://github.com/N9-Developer-Empowerment/DSH-Vibeify/tree/main/plugins/dsh-vibeify-experience) - Turns completed DeepSeek Harness public-content work into a browser-local visual magazine with explicit updates and reviewed one-article sharing.
- [Nagi-ovo/dsh-visualize](https://github.com/Nagi-ovo/dsh-visualize) - In-conversation generative UI: the model renders interactive HTML cards into the chat stream, with streaming preview and sandboxed rendering.
- [nailing10086-zx/dsh-custom-ui](https://github.com/nailing10086-zx/dsh-custom-ui) - Right-side file-tree sidebar that opens workspace files, plus a DeepSeek account balance meter in the composer stats line.
- [Naitang1207/dsh-macos-quicklook](https://github.com/Naitang1207/dsh-macos-quicklook) - Adds a space-key Quick Look preview to DSH deliverable cards. Hovering a card and pressing Space opens the native macOS QLPreviewPanel for the file, which covers docx, xlsx, pptx, pdf, images and media that the sidebar preview cannot render. The panel zooms out from the card's position, returns focus to the composer when closed, and exposes hover delay, focus-return and double-click behaviour in Settings > General.
- [nanaaya98/dsh-scope-manager](https://github.com/nanaaya98/dsh-scope-manager) - Scope manager for DeepSeek Harness: binds presets, persona cards, tools, skills and MCP connections to global / workspace / session layers, with a sidebar.
- [Nancy0-0/dsh-code-nav#file-tree-actions](https://github.com/Nancy0-0/dsh-code-nav/tree/main/packages/file-tree-actions) - Right-click menu and drag support for the Files sidebar on Windows - copy absolute or workspace-relative paths, create files and folders, rename, move entries to the Recycle Bin, and drop a row into the composer to insert an @ reference.
- [NanoShiki/dsh-anki](https://github.com/NanoShiki/dsh-anki) - Native Anki workspace backed by one real headless Anki collection: browse, edit, add and review notes, suspend cards, import and export APKG, and sync with AnkiWeb, with the same collection exposed to the anki AI tool. No Anki desktop app, AnkiConnect or Qt is started. Supports Windows x64 and macOS 12 or later (Apple Silicon and Intel). The matching headless Anki core, CPython and all Python runtime dependencies are bundled, so no separate Anki or Python installation is required.
- [NanQing233/dsh-rimlight](https://github.com/NanQing233/dsh-rimlight) - Adds a comet-like light band that flows along the window edge of DeepSeek Harness — up to four in parallel, colored by each session's model, reversing direction while waiting for your reply, and returning to the composer or the workspace once the conversation ends.
- [nanshang328/dsh-power](https://github.com/nanshang328/dsh-power) - Windows-only power card injected into the DSH page: the artwork carries two hit zones (left shuts down, right restarts), the first click only turns the card into a yes/no question and the second click fires, and the restart engine stops only this instance's process tree, waits for the port to free, relaunches outside the host's Job Object and confirms the new instance answers on the plugin's own status route.
- [narkco-kang/dsh-team-workbench](https://github.com/narkco-kang/dsh-team-workbench) - Full-window agent team app for DSH: named agents with animated avatars and fallback model routes, private chats, group rooms with a lead or auto responder and @-relay, 313 agency-agents-zh roles, chat-record search, and a shared skill library.
- [NativeDog1/dsh-boot-animation](https://github.com/NativeDog1/dsh-boot-animation) - Plays an intro full-frame on a new conversation, or every time you open the one you pinned. Four clips ship inside the plugin itself — embedded in code, with no separate video files — and an in-app library switches between them, previews the choice, and accepts your own clip. The pin button sits beside Settings.
- [Natsubrei/dsh-comfyui-panel](https://github.com/Natsubrei/dsh-comfyui-panel) - The real ComfyUI editor as a sidebar tab in DeepSeek Harness, with per-session tools that read and edit the graph on screen and a panel that can start, stop and restart ComfyUI without installing anything into it.
- [Natsubrei/dsh-mermaid-panel](https://github.com/Natsubrei/dsh-mermaid-panel) - Renders the open conversation's Mermaid diagrams as a right-sidebar page, one card per diagram, each clickable to open full screen with zoom and pan; the chat's own code block is left untouched, so copying or quoting the source still works.
- [NattoCB/dsh-plugin-pin-session](https://github.com/NattoCB/dsh-plugin-pin-session) - Adds Pin/Unpin to the sidebar session-row menu and a collapsible Pinned Sessions group above the session list, with host-side persistence, hover quick-unpin, collapse memory, and live running-state dots.
- [NattoCB/dsh-plugin-sidebar-views](https://github.com/NattoCB/dsh-plugin-sidebar-views) - Adds a Workspaces / Recent-sessions switcher above the sidebar list, with a pinned-sessions group, a per-row menu (pin/unpin, copy session id), title filtering, and browser-local pin storage with a one-shot migration from dsh-plugin-pin-session.
- [NattoCB/dsh-widget-center](https://github.com/NattoCB/dsh-widget-center) - Native macOS desktop widgets for DSH: manage any number of widget instances — share quote tickers (5-provider fallback, trading-phase-aware throttling) and notes memos — each an independent NSPanel+WKWebView window spawned detached from the host, dragged anywhere, with a type-aware right-click menu. Instances are edited as cards in a three-view settings page (list / type picker / detail), and a market_quote model tool serves standardized quotes to any session. A Type Studio flow seeds a creation-mode session with an issue-template prompt for authoring new widget types.
- [NecromanAlbert/dsh-show-media](https://github.com/NecromanAlbert/dsh-show-media) - Shows a local image or short video in the current conversation card, with click-to-preview.
- [neobones/dsh-locale-es-cl](https://github.com/neobones/dsh-locale-es-cl) - Adds Chilean Spanish (es-CL) to the Web UI language list: 2,603 strings across 57 namespaces, with per-key fallback to English.
- [NeoXider/neoxider-agent-deck#dsh-agent-deck](https://github.com/NeoXider/neoxider-agent-deck/tree/main/integrations/dsh-agent-deck) - Opens the NeoXider Agent Deck desktop widget (chat, the slime pet avatar or the hinge handle) from a Harness session, and reports its mode, installed version, running state and whether it follows the same Harness.
- [Neptune810/dsh-power-button](https://github.com/Neptune810/dsh-power-button) - Adds a draggable power pill to the DeepSeek Harness Web GUI: a live status dot plus restart and stop actions, where restart is handed to a detached helper that resumes the same boot invocation once the port is released.
- [Nethur-auro/dsh-multi-root-explorer](https://github.com/Nethur-auro/dsh-multi-root-explorer) - Replaces the sidebar workspace region with a two-tab browser: several workspace roots stay registered side by side while conversations are filed under the real working directory of each session, with a read-only resource tree that previews files in the right sidebar. Hiding is per tab, display-only and fully reversible; a resource folder or file can be copied as a path or dragged into the composer as a native reference, and @ completion covers every registered root rather than the working directory alone.
- [Neumannzc/dsh-archive-manager#plugin](https://github.com/Neumannzc/dsh-archive-manager/tree/main/plugin) - Beautified archive-manager settings section listing archived sessions grouped by workspace, with a hover unarchive action and relative timestamps.
- [newbieQQ/dsh-better-workspace-group](https://github.com/newbieQQ/dsh-better-workspace-group) - Fork of z-col/dsh-workspace-groups (MIT): a grouped workspace sidebar for the DSH web client, with a science icon set and a docked default workspace; cannot be installed alongside the upstream plugin (same bundle row).
- [NewSpringWei/dsh-subagent-acp-ui](https://github.com/NewSpringWei/dsh-subagent-acp-ui) - Adds a management page for the official DeepSeek Harness ACP subagent provider, with one-click presets, a read-only connectivity check, live switches for all three entry points, and online preset updates.
- [NICEGSP/call-map](https://github.com/NICEGSP/call-map) - Adds a conversation view that maps which skills, MCP tools and subagents a session actually used, with an attribution tree, a call table and an unused inventory.
- [Nicercz007-cloud/schedule](https://github.com/Nicercz007-cloud/schedule) - Three draggable, resizable, auto-positioning floating desktop widgets for DSH Desktop — daily schedule, goals tracker, and calendar planner.
- [nickkkkkk123123/dsh-whale-girl](https://github.com/nickkkkkk123123/dsh-whale-girl) - Interactive whale-girl desktop pet for DeepSeek Harness: live balance/usage/context occupancy, slingshot toss, easter-egg bubbles, eco mode. 346KB package, zero extra processes.
- [nickzhangcomes/dsh-markup](https://github.com/nickzhangcomes/dsh-markup) - Review DeepSeek Harness responses with inline annotations, document reading, comment threads, and text revisions, then bring a reading summary back to the main conversation.
- [nico-zhuang/dsh-chat-file-actions](https://github.com/nico-zhuang/dsh-chat-file-actions) - Open and copy local files and web links from a chat turn.
- [nico-zhuang/dsh-nav-fold](https://github.com/nico-zhuang/dsh-nav-fold) - Folds and reorders global sidebar navigation; optional Workspaces, Activity, and Favorites tabs support user-bound shortcut navigation for folder and session focus, and macOS logo hiding adapts stoner-byte/dsh-hide-brand (MIT).
- [Nihao7-L/dsh-prompt-chain](https://github.com/Nihao7-L/dsh-prompt-chain) - One-click 💪 prompt enhancement (prompt): thin draft? continue 🔜, unhappy? regenerate 🔄; auto-archive history 🕐 with rollback ↩️; result syncs back to the composer with a line-by-line diff; 🗺️show the panel 🎨 customise the 🪄 prompt-button icon 📍 and button position yourself.
- [nikoart-liu/dsh-open-in-x](https://github.com/nikoart-liu/dsh-open-in-x) - Open DSH workspace directories in installed file managers, terminals, and supported editors from the Web UI.
- [ninepoin4/dsh-rich-cards](https://github.com/ninepoin4/dsh-rich-cards) - Renders the final fenced card block of an answer as a typographic card, with card and article layouts, twelve block types and credited images.
- [ninglovegithub/dsh-workspace-combiner](https://github.com/ninglovegithub/dsh-workspace-combiner) - Multi-workspace manager: bind a docs anchor plus ordered code-project repositories into one session, injecting their absolute paths, a recursive file index, a server/client endpoint index and optional coding standards, and syncing writable directories into the sandbox.
- [NingTiAn-byte/dsh-intelligent-ui](https://github.com/NingTiAn-byte/dsh-intelligent-ui) - Offline-replayable charts and interactive explanations in DSH rc.2 Web chat, with optional AMap itineraries, confirmed follow-ups, and HTML export.
- [NingTiAn-byte/dsh-linked-workspaces](https://github.com/NingTiAn-byte/dsh-linked-workspaces) - Link local project directories from a project context menu, with scoped search, source reading and explicit per-session snapshot synchronization. Supports DSH 0.2.1-alpha.4 and 0.2.1-rc.2 only.
- [NingYuanLee/dsh-image-viewer-pro](https://github.com/NingYuanLee/dsh-image-viewer-pro) - In-chat image viewer for the web UI: takes over content image clicks (Markdown images, message attachments rendered as blob: URLs, and read_image results) and opens a dialog with pointer-centered wheel zoom and an editable percentage, drag pan, 90° rotation, download with progress, copy as PNG, open with the default app, and ←/→ gallery browsing across the conversation.
- [nirvanaslash/dsh-artifact-preview](https://github.com/nirvanaslash/dsh-artifact-preview) - Adds a produced-files card row to the chat and a resizable split-screen preview panel for workspace artifacts (Markdown, source code, CSV, JSON, images, HTML), plus a localhost port preview.
- [Nishikinov/dsh-sidebar-settings-button](https://github.com/Nishikinov/dsh-sidebar-settings-button) - Adds a Settings row to the sidebar header before Plugins, which opens the shell's Settings panel.
- [Nixz0824/dsh-composer-alcove](https://github.com/Nixz0824/dsh-composer-alcove) - Collapses the composer to an iPhone-style home indicator while you scroll, then restores it on hover, click, or scroll up. Corner arcs dock the real composer into the left or right gutter.
- [NKUhan123/dsh-jupyterlab](https://github.com/NKUhan123/dsh-jupyterlab) - JupyterLab-style notebook for DSH: code and output side by side with a draggable divider, outputs that stick to the viewport while a long cell scrolls, Jupyter keyboard shortcuts (a/b/dd, m/y, Shift+Enter vs Ctrl+Enter), a scissors button to delete a cell and a chip to switch it between code and Markdown. Modified fork of @beihaizb/dsh-notebook: both register the same nb_* tools, so remove the original before installing; the Python kernel you pick needs ipykernel, jupyter_client and nbformat.
- [nnnanann/Deepseek-harness-freeze-content-on-window-resize](https://github.com/nnnanann/Deepseek-harness-freeze-content-on-window-resize) - Freezes the DSH Web GUI reading column at a fixed width and re-aligns the reading position after the window is resized or zoomed.
- [NoCandles/dsh-stock-bar](https://github.com/NoCandles/dsh-stock-bar) - Always-on quotes strip pinned to the top of the DSH window: watchlist, holdings with share counts and cost price, P&L, candlestick and intraday charts, 5-level order book, trade ledger, price alerts, and streamed AI review of the selected symbol.
- [NOirBRight/dsh-codex-sidebar](https://github.com/NOirBRight/dsh-codex-sidebar) - Codex-style details sidebar for DSH with Files, Review, Browser, and Terminal panes, transcript file links and diff stats, annotations, and Host-managed browser controls.
- [NOirBRight/dsh-llm-assistant](https://github.com/NOirBRight/dsh-llm-assistant) - Resident Web overlay seat with its own assistant session: chat, images, reminders, a duty heartbeat, and on-demand read-only task reference.
- [NOirBRight/dsh-llm-providers-ui](https://github.com/NOirBRight/dsh-llm-providers-ui) - Mounted owner of the LLM Providers Settings page: provider cards, drag-reorder card order, and picker sort.
- [nomxy/dsh-workspace-resizer](https://github.com/nomxy/dsh-workspace-resizer) - Draggable splitter between the sidebar panel list and the workspace region, so the session list can grow. Double-click restores the default layout.
- [NoneadChina/dsh-nonead-prompt-recall](https://github.com/NoneadChina/dsh-nonead-prompt-recall) - Shell-style prompt history in the DSH composer: ↑ in an empty composer recalls the previous prompt and keeps walking back, ↓ walks forward to the empty draft; history is kept per workspace, persisted across restarts and records only human input, while typing or editing a draft returns ↑/↓ to normal caret movement. Adds a Settings page to export, import and clear the history.
- [NONEMIN/dsh-sidebar-panels](https://github.com/NONEMIN/dsh-sidebar-panels) - Manage the left global panel list from Settings → General: show or hide entries, reorder them by dragging in the sidebar or in the dialog, and keep entries that have their own sidebar switch in step with it.
- [NormanFxxkingRockwell/dsh-keyboard-history](https://github.com/NormanFxxkingRockwell/dsh-keyboard-history) - Minimal ↑/↓ input history for the DSH web composer: press ↑/↓ to walk back through sent messages. Nothing else.
- [Nothree-code/folder-tree-sh](https://github.com/Nothree-code/folder-tree-sh) - Workspace file tree for the Web UI: multi-tab preview (text/DOCX/PDF/Markdown/CSV/images), inline Markdown editing, a Git changes panel, and file operations (rename/delete/copy/move/create) via context menu.
- [nothurry/stronger-dsh](https://github.com/nothurry/stronger-dsh) - Monochrome UI, native agent collaboration, token usage, local skills and conversation import with owner-scoped WeChat controls.
- [nuet9/dsh-internal-folder-browser](https://github.com/nuet9/dsh-internal-folder-browser) - Browse local file directories inside Harness with target highlighting and eight view layouts; image thumbnails and native file icons are Windows-only.
- [null-dat/dsh-whale-tail](https://github.com/null-dat/dsh-whale-tail) - Four whale-tail animations for the DeepSeek Harness thinking indicator, cycling on every thinking turn.
- [Num86/dsh-functions](https://github.com/Num86/dsh-functions) - Adds an extensible Functions menu to the DSH top bar, where plugins register one-click actions with staged confirmations and submenus. Since 0.4.0 it bundles a built-in "Restart DSH" action, so no separate restart plugin is needed.
- [Nyco1d/dsh-prompt-optimizer](https://github.com/Nyco1d/dsh-prompt-optimizer) - One-click prompt optimizer next to the composer, with a side-by-side diff panel and per-mode settings.
- [NYSM803/dsh-restart-button](https://github.com/NYSM803/dsh-restart-button) - Draggable floating restart button for the dsh Web GUI (Windows only, dsh >= 0.2.2-rc.1): confirm, then the app and its host close and come back on their own, with a cancel button, a watchdog and a clear failure message. Icon, background colour and position lock are edited in Settings.
- [objectivex666/dsh-settings-search](https://github.com/objectivex666/dsh-settings-search) - Instantly find DSH settings pages, tabs and rows by keyword, pinyin, intent or AI — and jump straight to them.
- [Odefined/dsh-mobile-webui](https://github.com/Odefined/dsh-mobile-webui) - Mobile-viewport fixes for the dsh web GUI: overlay drawer sidebar with swipe gestures, icon-only composer with a mode · model · effort status line, centered popovers, and horizontal-overflow/scrollbar centering fixes.
- [oioiphainon/dsh-widget](https://github.com/oioiphainon/dsh-widget) - A transparent always-on-top desktop fish that mirrors the current DSH session - turn, step, tool calls, todos, background jobs and team tasks - with optional login autostart, an option to stay after DSH exits, and a right-click hide.
- [omdsh-dev/dsh-annotation](https://github.com/omdsh-dev/dsh-annotation) - Select text → annotate → send with your message; replies map back to each annotation.
- [omdsh-dev/DSH-better-sidebar](https://github.com/omdsh-dev/DSH-better-sidebar) - Full sidebar workbench with file rendering and editing, terminal, Git, and subagents; third-party plugins can register new tabs.
- [omdsh-dev/dsh-genui](https://github.com/omdsh-dev/dsh-genui) - Interactive UI components rendered inline in replies: layout, charts, forms, quizzes, mermaid, 3D scenes, and an action event loop back to the model.
- [omdsh-dev/ex-setting](https://github.com/omdsh-dev/ex-setting) - Settings extensions for DSH.
- [omdsh-dev/web-components](https://github.com/omdsh-dev/web-components) - Web Components support.
- [onlyinliu/dsh-session-console](https://github.com/onlyinliu/dsh-session-console) - Session Console: a sidebar dashboard over every conversation that shows run state, token spend, cache hit rate, context pressure and a heat score, resolves a plain-language sentence such as "how is the example project going" to the matching session, and dispatches that sentence as a queued task into it. Ships five agent tools (overview, find, tail, dispatch, cancel) over the same data.
- [OnTheWay111/dsh-plugin-path-completion](https://github.com/OnTheWay111/dsh-plugin-path-completion) - Completes @path references in the Web composer: type @ (optionally ./src/ma) to list matching files and directories, pick one with the arrow keys, and the draft receives an @./relative/path reference (quoted when the path has spaces).
- [OolongBlackCoffee/dsh-wuwa-pet](https://github.com/OolongBlackCoffee/dsh-wuwa-pet) - Puts a WuwaTappo desktop pet's bubbles to work: shows the DeepSeek balance, today's spend and a per-turn cost bubble, and keeps an amber reminder bubble up while the agent waits for an answer or an approval. Talks to the pet over CDP, enabling its debug port with a one-time, revertible byte patch.
- [openAGFS/dsh-agfs](https://github.com/openAGFS/dsh-agfs) - File-browser web app for DSH: React frontend and REST API served by the host webserver, a /dsh-agfs command that opens at the current session workspace, and a browse_files model tool.
- [opencues/opencues#integrations-dsh](https://github.com/opencues/opencues/tree/master/integrations/dsh) - Word alternatives and underscore-gated fill-ins in the composer. End a line with _ and it is filled; misspellings are flagged as you type. Routes through the model dsh is already configured with, so it needs no API key.
- [openma-ai/Martty#npm](https://github.com/openma-ai/Martty/tree/main/npm) - A DSH-first Rust/ratatui agent TUI with streamed tool calls, subagents, durable sessions, and a Cordis-extensible client UI.
- [orchivo/dsh-conversation-tree](https://github.com/orchivo/dsh-conversation-tree) - A collapsible column beside the conversation that organises conversations into a workspace/topic tree, opens the real sessions in place, and exposes the tree to the assistant through 9 model tools.
- [OrderG-X/dsh-design-studio](https://github.com/OrderG-X/dsh-design-studio) - Design workbench in the right sidebar. Its tools sit in floating groups at the canvas corners and a bottom dock with text labels, and a home screen offers one prompt box, recent designs and large template cards. When the home screen's style is Auto, the plugin picks a style card that fits the subject and hands its colors and fonts to the model; your own words, a chosen card, a reference image or a default brand design system take precedence. Each design gets its own folder. When the model builds web pages, app screens, desktop app windows, WeChat mini programs, posters, slides or emails, each HTML file shows up as a screen on a canvas inside a device frame and refreshes when the file changes; while the model writes a page, the sidebar grows with it and a few cursors draw boxes, fill colors and type where it is writing, with a timer in the status bar; local dev servers are proxied with hot reload intact, and an existing HTML file or folder opens by browsing or by dragging it into the sidebar (desktop app). A design library gathers every design you made in any session, your saved inspiration (reference images, URLs, style cards) and a design market of shared templates, design systems and style cards (16 templates, 3 design systems and 20 style cards to start; search, filters, full-size screens and a live view; an offline copy ships with the plugin): start from one in a click, or share your own design to the market after a personal-data scan and an offline design check. Mini program screens get the WeChat capsule in the top-right corner, which turns white over a dark top bar, and the design check flags text and buttons hidden under it. App and mini program screens follow platform layout (status and nav bars, a tab bar of 2 to 5 items, safe areas), desktop app screens get macOS traffic lights or Windows caption buttons, and the design check flags content under the Dynamic Island, the home indicator or the window buttons. Click an element or drag a region to put its selector, source file and styles into the composer, or draw on the preview (pen, highlighter, arrow, box, circle, text) and hand the marked-up screenshot to the model; picks, markup and style changes appear in the composer as small chips while the model still gets the full text. It can extract a brand design system from your website or project (colors by role, fonts, type scale, radius, shadows, buttons, inputs and cards) and use it for every new design. The model adds a few tweak sliders to each design (spacing, radius, brand color, layout variants) that update all screens live and write back to the stylesheet in one click. A finished design can be turned into React, Vue, Next.js or plain HTML code, or for mini programs into uni-app or native WeChat code; existing uni-app (including HBuilderX projects without package.json), Taro and native mini program projects are recognized so new pages go straight in, and each built screen is diffed pixel by pixel against the design. Also has a design tokens panel, twenty style presets (each a live sample screen you can open full size), a style panel (font size, colors, radius, padding and more) that writes back to the HTML or hands the change to the model together with the CSS rule and line behind each property, versions saved before and after each turn with drag-to-compare and restore, export to images, PDF and PPTX (links between screens stay clickable), a LAN QR link for viewing on a phone, link arrows and a click-through try-it mode, a multi-size view, in-place text editing, reference-image palettes, and a design check (overflowing, clipped or overlapping text, contrast, tap target size and more). Bundles a ui-design skill and six tools: preview_page opens a preview; check_design measures every screen at real sizes in a hidden local Chrome or Edge (bars covering content, script errors, missing assets, dead links; CSS file and line for contrast and font-size issues; cropped screenshots of serious problems); look_design hands real screenshots to the model; extract_design_system builds a design system from a site or project; handoff_design writes a handoff pack (screenshots, routes, tokens, shared components); compare_design diffs the built local page against the design and marks what differs. Adds a Design mode for new sessions (Standard mode's tools plus the skill, the six tools and a design persona); each part has its own switch on the Plugins page. When DSH is opened from a phone or another computer, local-file designs are bundled into a sandboxed frame so they still show.
- [OrderG-X/dsh-pages](https://github.com/OrderG-X/dsh-pages) - Lets the model turn an answer into a web page that lives right inside the chat. The show_page tool either fills one of 8 built-in templates (report, compare, checklist, dashboard, timeline, flashcards, quiz, flow), where the model writes only the content and the plugin does layout, interaction and saving, or saves a full page the model wrote itself (calculators, games, custom layouts). Hand-written pages get the same design as base styles inside a CSS @layer, so the page's own CSS still wins. The page appears under the reply at its content height, interactive and following DSH's light/dark theme. The page's localStorage is stored in DSH per page, so what users tick or type survives reopening and new versions, and the same page open in the chat and the right sidebar stays in sync. Each edit is saved as a new version you can flip back to. After saving, the page is checked in a hidden local Chrome or Edge (script errors, horizontal overflow, dark mode not applied) and problems go back to the model. The right sidebar shows the page full-size with versions, a gallery of all pages and export to PNG, PDF or HTML. Pages run in a sandboxed, separate-origin frame with a strict CSP: no access to DSH data and no fetch, XHR or WebSocket. Works on phones with dsh-mobile.
- [OrderG-X/dsh-video-preview](https://github.com/OrderG-X/dsh-video-preview) - Plays local video and audio inside the conversation. Media files the model writes during a turn - screen recordings, transcodes, voiceovers - get a player under its final reply automatically when their paths appear in its commands, the command output or the reply; the play_video tool plays any other file. Video shows as compact cards with a hover preview, audio as a slim seek bar, streamed over HTTP Range so long files seek instead of loading into memory. Also registers a sidebar media preview renderer, since the built-in previewer treats mp4 as unviewable. The look_video tool lets the model see a video itself: a hidden local Chrome or Edge grabs frames into a time-labelled contact sheet (or big frames at given times, or a short range frame by frame), checks the audio (silence, too quiet, clipping, one channel only) and the picture (black or frozen frames), and reads MP4 / MOV headers for phone compatibility (codec, HDR, faststart and more), with ffmpeg fixes.
- [orphiczhou/dsh-region-probe](https://github.com/orphiczhou/dsh-region-probe) - A read-only Cordis Inspect provider that lets an agent see the interface it is driving: outline() reports which UI regions the running shell actually rendered, with occupancy, geometry and current or past failure state; snapshot() returns one region's rendered node tree (tag, role, aria-label, own text, geometry, computed styles) addressed by slot key or CSS selector; failures() keeps a history of regions that failed to render and whether they recovered. Regions are addressed by the anchor the shell already writes for every slot, so it needs no region list and covers regions added later or contributed by other plugins. No UI, no tools, no dependencies, no network access.
- [orphiczhou/dsh-session-tree](https://github.com/orphiczhou/dsh-session-tree) - Replaces the left sidebar session list with a tree of every session and its nested subagent descendants, with click-through to each conversation's multi-turn history and a tree_send tool for messaging any continuable session anywhere in the tree.
- [OStarsO/dsh-processing-status](https://github.com/OStarsO/dsh-processing-status) - Shows processing time and rotating Chinese tips above the DSH composer.
- [oumaekumiko0821/dsh-splash-gojo](https://github.com/oumaekumiko0821/dsh-splash-gojo) - Full-screen boot animation for the DSH web client: the boot HTML paints a cover-fit artwork on its first frame, adds a slow settle, a scanning light sweep and a progress HUD, then hands the viewport over when the app mounts. Click or press Esc / space / Enter to skip.
- [outmanwt/dsh-chat-privacy-blur](https://github.com/outmanwt/dsh-chat-privacy-blur) - Draws every question and answer in the DSH Web conversation blurred until the pointer rests on that row, and locks the mask to always-blurred while the window is unfocused; one control below the composer switches between off, hover to reveal, and always blurred, remembered per browser.
- [outmanwt/dsh-team-fishbone](https://github.com/outmanwt/dsh-team-fishbone) - Session-header fishbone panel for sub-agent assignments, live status, available token and duration metrics, shared task-board entries, and native child-session navigation.
- [overact/dsh-statusline-plus](https://github.com/overact/dsh-statusline-plus) - Displays account quotas, Git state, context usage, public API reference costs, Subagent activity, and streaming speed in DeepSeek Harness Web.
- [OverToWind/dsh-todo](https://github.com/OverToWind/dsh-todo) - To-do list in the session header: the button cycles through unfinished items and is tinted red, amber or green by due time, and the floating panel adds, edits and ticks off tasks with an optional deadline, with a collapsible done section. Items persist in localStorage and follow the DSH theme.
- [oxlyn/dsh-flyout-sidebar](https://github.com/oxlyn/dsh-flyout-sidebar) - Flyout sidebar with a file tree, uncommitted-changes list and multi-tab file/diff previews; pops out into a standalone browser tab.
- [Oxygen-Wang/dsh-cursorlike-sidebar](https://github.com/Oxygen-Wang/dsh-cursorlike-sidebar) - A Cursor-like sidebar for DeepSeek Harness with file browsing, code editing, Git diffs, terminal, side chat, and Ctrl+L references.
- [oxygenforce/dsh-agent-activity](https://github.com/oxygenforce/dsh-agent-activity) - Live done/now/next card above the composer while the agent is still working.
- [ozjxnxh/dsh-prompt-enhance-keyless](https://github.com/ozjxnxh/dsh-prompt-enhance-keyless) - Adds a sparkle button left of the model selector in the composer toolbar: one click rewrites the draft in place through the host and briefly offers an undo that restores the original text. A Prompt Enhancement settings section picks the lane — the conversation model (the provider/model the next message would use, falling back to the configured default) or a keyless Pollinations channel that needs no login and no token budget but allows one reply per 30 seconds. During that cooldown a click completes through the conversation model instead of being refused, and the first click after the window reopens returns to the keyless channel on its own. The rewrite keeps the draft's language, and a result in a different language is rejected rather than pasted over the draft.
- [OZONGO/dsh-keyboard-shortcut](https://github.com/OZONGO/dsh-keyboard-shortcut) - Adds a Keyboard Shortcuts page to the DSH Settings panel with six rebindable commands — permission-mode cycling, reasoning-effort stepping, a status-page and a task-list shortcut — plus PgUp/PgDn input navigation and an optional composer Enter remap.
- [P-PPJ/dsh-chat-search](https://github.com/P-PPJ/dsh-chat-search) - Find text inside the open conversation with Ctrl+F: matches are highlighted in the message text, previous/next step through them, clicking the count lists the matches and jumps to any of them, and the count grows while the scan runs. No collapsed block is opened and the reading position does not move.
- [P1gG0dG0d/dsh-study-ask](https://github.com/P1gG0dG0d/dsh-study-ask) - Opens a dedicated question chat in the right sidebar for text selected in a DSH conversation, so follow-up questions do not push the main conversation away and never appear in the session list.
- [p56568833/dsh-plugin-groups](https://github.com/p56568833/dsh-plugin-groups) - Category tabs (All, Appearance & UI, Usage & account and more) for the Installed list on the Plugins page, remembering the chosen tab; patch plugins named in an editable parent table are indented under the plugin they patch. Display-only, writes no files.
- [Paimonshen/dsh-coding-plugin](https://github.com/Paimonshen/dsh-coding-plugin) - Coding-learn floating panel: edits and runs Python/JavaScript/TypeScript/PowerShell with exit code and timing, grades JSON course lessons with progress export, keeps Markdown notes and snippet library, and sends code plus a question into the current conversation for the main model to answer.
- [panando/dsh-prompt-optimizer](https://github.com/panando/dsh-prompt-optimizer) - One-button prompt optimizer: rewrites the composer draft via a configurable strategy, with an editable template, few-shot examples, pinned models and reasoning effort.
- [panando/dsh-quote-sidechat](https://github.com/panando/dsh-quote-sidechat) - Select text in a reply and quote it into the composer as an atomic reference chip: delete the whole chip in one keystroke, click it to scroll back and highlight the quoted turn, and expand it into a bounded reference block at send time so the model can tell the quote apart from what the user typed. Also opens the snippet in a side chat, or copies it to the clipboard.
- [PangXitong/dsh-restart-button](https://github.com/PangXitong/dsh-restart-button) - Power button in the DSH Web UI session header that lets you close or restart the whole DSH process.
- [PaoMoXML/dsh-paste-names](https://github.com/PaoMoXML/dsh-paste-names) - Paste non-image files or folders into the chat composer as native @path references, or drag them in as absolute paths, instead of the images-only error.
- [Pasumao/dsh-plugin-workbench](https://github.com/Pasumao/dsh-plugin-workbench) - Adds a VS Code-style workspace file explorer to the dsh web GUI: file tree, editable preview with syntax highlighting, tabs, line numbers, right-click file operations, and inline image preview.
- [pc439527/dsh-model-provider](https://github.com/pc439527/dsh-model-provider) - Provider-first three-level model selector for the DSH Web model seat: pick a provider, then a model from that provider, with inline search on both levels, failed-provider retry rows, and a Model · Provider trigger.
- [pc439527/dsh-side-monitor](https://github.com/pc439527/dsh-side-monitor) - Sidebar system monitor for the DSH Web UI: live host overview (CPU/memory/network/disk), process list, and Docker container status, all read-only.
- [penghao73551/dsh-desktop-quick-actions](https://github.com/penghao73551/dsh-desktop-quick-actions) - Two round buttons at the foot of the DSH desktop sidebar on Windows: restart the app without a console window ever flashing on screen, and open the DeepSeek top-up page in your browser. The restart path launches the helper through WMI and wscript so the new process never inherits a console; it verifies the executable path before killing anything and preserves the original command line. Windows 10/11 only (the package declares os: win32).
- [penguin-oo/dsh-pathlink](https://github.com/penguin-oo/dsh-pathlink) - Ctrl+click file paths and links in chat: paths open their folder in the OS file manager (with the file selected), links open in a new browser tab.
- [pengyue-polaron/deepseek-harness-genui](https://github.com/pengyue-polaron/deepseek-harness-genui) - Code-first React and TypeScript task apps rendered inline, in Canvas, full screen, or on localhost, with interaction state available to later agent turns and approval-gated MCP and API access.
- [perdakovich/dsh-prompt-edit](https://github.com/perdakovich/dsh-prompt-edit) - In-place prompt editing in the web chat: cleanly forks session history at the edited turn, preserves image and file attachments, and auto-archives the superseded branch.
- [PerryLink/dsh-community-bench](https://github.com/PerryLink/dsh-community-bench) - Adds a Community Bench tab that reads the public task-conditioned registry and shows the same score everyone sees; it does not compute a local score.
- [PerryLink/dsh-composer-history](https://github.com/PerryLink/dsh-composer-history) - Terminal-style input history for the web composer: edge-first arrow-key recall with exact draft/caret restore, browser-local persisted history, Ctrl+R reverse search, and sliding-context awareness; 0.5.0 adds a smart input layer — cross-session snippets (/save, /load), prompt templates with variables, reuse insights, and compaction-summary highlighting.
- [ph4310822/dsh-edex-jarvis-ui#bundle](https://github.com/ph4310822/dsh-edex-jarvis-ui/tree/main/packages/bundle) - JARVIS-style sci-fi HUD shell over the DSH web GUI: electric-cyan VITAL SIGNS / RT-MONITOR / RT-LOG left bar, POWER CORE gauges and a rotating RADAR scan disc on the right with up/down traffic chart, filesystem browser with file preview, and a terminal-styled composer with a JARVIS header strip that follows the active workspace.
- [ph4310822/dsh-edex-ui#bundle](https://github.com/ph4310822/dsh-edex-ui/tree/main/packages/bundle) - eDEX-UI-style shell over the DSH web GUI: system-telemetry left bar with per-core CPU sparklines, encom-globe world-map right bar with up/down traffic chart, filesystem browser with file preview, and a terminal-styled composer that follows the active workspace.
- [Phant0Meow/dsh-meow-smooth](https://github.com/Phant0Meow/dsh-meow-smooth) - Mobile-first UX polish for the DSH Web UI: composer auto-fold, enter-as-newline on phones, compact sidebar/header/settings, zoom and overscroll lock, plus approval/question/completion notifications via in-page cards, Web Push, and webhook — zero dsh changes.
- [Phoeky/dsh-refresh-plugin](https://github.com/Phoeky/dsh-refresh-plugin) - Adds F5 / Ctrl+R page reload to the DeepSeek Harness desktop web UI, which production builds lack by default (the "Reload Page" menu item and bare-F5 accelerator are development-gated). Binds soft reload (F5, Ctrl+R) and cache-busted hard reload (F5 with a modifier, Ctrl+Shift+R, appending a _dsh_refresh timestamp) through a capture-phase client key listener.
- [Physicolor/dsh-lifeline](https://github.com/Physicolor/dsh-lifeline) - Right-side message navigation rail for DeepSeek Harness: per-message tick marks, hover-to-expand preview drawer, smooth jump to any historical message, star bookmarks, realtime slide animation with interrupt support, full session index with virtualization, dynamic panel avoidance, official --dsw-* token styling.
- [Physicolor/dsh-ui-harmonizer](https://github.com/Physicolor/dsh-ui-harmonizer) - UI harmonizer for DeepSeek Harness: normalizes unfinished or self-contradictory official UI, reconciles style conflicts between installed plugins, and unifies the visual language via official design tokens — with live-customizable chat width, markdown font size, workspace scale, UI font stack and a rounded center-column card from Settings → General; client-only dsh bundle, zero model cost, no host side, removable without residue.
- [Physicolor/dsh-widgets](https://github.com/Physicolor/dsh-widgets) - A customizable widget rail for the right side of DeepSeek Harness: 61 cards covering session stats, tokens and cache, context, background jobs, subagents, machine metrics, OpenCode Go and Command Code quota, session cost, peak pricing and the GitHub review queue. Cards install from an in-rail market into 1-4 column grids and 2x2 or 2x4 tiles, with per-instance settings, a magnification wave under the pointer and a one-click hide. Works on DSH 0.1.x and 0.2.x, web UI and desktop app.
- [picklerick422/dsh-conv-progress](https://github.com/picklerick422/dsh-conv-progress) - Live per-turn progress capsule with an empirical ETA table and a click-to-open turn history, docked under the composer.
- [picklerick422/dsh-motion](https://github.com/picklerick422/dsh-motion) - Popup and overlay motion for DeepSeek Harness: spring clip-morph entrances for nearby popups, fade-up far dialogs, coherent hover bubbles, image-zoom FLIP, and a live-preview settings page.
- [pingg02/dsh-plugins#dsh-desktop-entry](https://github.com/pingg02/dsh-plugins/tree/main/dsh-desktop-entry) - Creates a Windows desktop shortcut that starts DeepSeek Harness when needed and opens the Web UI in a Chrome or Edge app window.
- [pingg02/dsh-plugins#dsh-quote-selection](https://github.com/pingg02/dsh-plugins/tree/main/dsh-quote-selection) - Lets users quote selected conversation text with optional notes, then attaches the references to their next message.
- [pkui1mpression-design/dsh-sidebar-installed-plugins](https://github.com/pkui1mpression-design/dsh-sidebar-installed-plugins) - Sidebar panel that lists every plugin installed in the current profile and gives each one an enable/disable switch.
- [plolpl789/dsh-raw-html](https://github.com/plolpl789/dsh-raw-html) - Renders agent HTML output as real UI in the DeepSeek Harness web GUI, with visual cards, KaTeX math, Mermaid diagrams, built-in open-source fonts, and design-spec injection.
- [plyflai/dsh-ux-plus](https://github.com/plyflai/dsh-ux-plus) - A UX enhancement pack for the DSH web chat: one plugin that collects small interface conveniences, each with its own switch under Settings → UX Plus applied immediately — chat-area font size and reading width under Settings → General → Chat area, a session-row menu that copies the session ID or inherits the conversation into a new session on a chosen model, workspace and session rows ordered by recent activity with 24-hour idle auto-collapse and age labels, and settled ask_user_question cards left expanded. The switch list is generated from its own feature registry, so the pack grows as conveniences are added instead of being fixed at a count.
- [polychromate/dsh-sidebar-footer-rows](https://github.com/polychromate/dsh-sidebar-footer-rows) - Stacks the sidebar footer actions into one full-width row each, and lines their icons up on one left edge.
- [pomelo-ccc/dsh-pi-composer](https://github.com/pomelo-ccc/dsh-pi-composer) - Swaps the composer's model control for a PI-Desktop style picker: a two-pane menu with search and provider grouping, a reasoning-effort slider, ArrowUp/ArrowDown prompt-history recall, and a prompt-rewrite button with undo.
- [PPsz-qqq/dsh-restart-button](https://github.com/PPsz-qqq/dsh-restart-button) - Restart button at the right end of the session header that restarts the whole client: the Windows desktop app (Electron shell and Host, through the Host's own graceful shutdown) or the dsh web server. It can wait until running turns and background jobs finish, reopens the open session afterwards with one-click continue for interrupted sessions, flags plugin updates that only take effect after a restart, and shows Host uptime and memory on hover.
- [prettyfox/dsh-selection-quote-auto](https://github.com/prettyfox/dsh-selection-quote-auto) - Copies selected assistant text to the clipboard and shows a Comment chip that opens the inline comment box in DeepSeek Harness; the right-click quote menu and Cmd+L still work.
- [prgrmrwy/dsh-sidebar-session-provider-icon](https://github.com/prgrmrwy/dsh-sidebar-session-provider-icon) - DeepSeek Harness plugin: show a provider + model icon in the sidebar for every session, updated live from the composer model selector with a last-request fallback for history sessions; aggregator routes render the provider logo with a 10px model logo in the corner.
- [PrismScopes/dsh-aide](https://github.com/PrismScopes/dsh-aide) - VS Code-style local IDE for DeepSeek Harness: Monaco editor, explorer, search, Git, terminal, and language services. With pnpm, run `pnpm approve-builds --all` so the native build of node-pty is allowed.
- [psychiiii/dsh-three-window#personal-web](https://github.com/psychiiii/dsh-three-window/tree/publish/packages/personal-web) - Three-window workbench for dsh web: chat, construct, and a read-only review window side by side for each workspace, per-window hooks, and an anonymous multi-model review that asks before it runs, groups findings by issue, and reports in Markdown.
- [Pudge1996/dsh-composer-stretch](https://github.com/Pudge1996/dsh-composer-stretch) - Expand button for the DSH Web UI composer — appears at 3+ lines, stretches to near-fullscreen height, auto-collapses on send, and inserts a newline on Enter or Shift+Enter in expanded mode while Cmd/Ctrl+Enter sends.
- [pure-craft/dsh-actions](https://github.com/pure-craft/dsh-actions) - Deterministic project actions shared by people and agents: one actions.json, a right-sidebar run panel, and agent tools.
- [pure-craft/dsh-capability-panel](https://github.com/pure-craft/dsh-capability-panel) - Manage MCP servers, skills, and tools for your agent — true in-context state (loaded/truncated/evicted) and per-session switches.
- [pyf2818/dsh-bili-widget](https://github.com/pyf2818/dsh-bili-widget) - Floating Bilibili video mini-player for DSH — always-on-top window with discover/search/follow-UP channels, auto-playlist, mini & focus modes, persisted watch history and window minimize-to-ball.
- [python3603/dsh-weather-panel](https://github.com/python3603/dsh-weather-panel) - Sidebar weather panel: current conditions, 5-day and 24-hour forecasts, an hourly rain chart, temperature history, CMA alerts with graded reminders and county-level matching, a radar mosaic, FY-4B satellite imagery, typhoon tracks, and ECMWF / NW Pacific sea surface temperature charts.
- [pzr-Ryan/dsh-plugins#dsh-always-on-top](https://github.com/pzr-Ryan/dsh-plugins/tree/main/dsh-always-on-top) - Adds a pin button to the left of the Windows caption buttons that keeps the DSH window always on top of the desktop.
- [Qcsj777/dsh-process-panel](https://github.com/Qcsj777/dsh-process-panel) - Adds a per-answer Process drawer that streams, in Simplified Chinese, the agent thinking, tool calls and errors for that turn.
- [QDNBD/dsh-panel-pins](https://github.com/QDNBD/dsh-panel-pins) - Sidebar panel management for DeepSeek Harness: a grouped Panels launcher collects global panel rows and third-party plugin entry rows into one searchable menu — the full panel inventory (pinned and unpinned, unpinned open via temporary-pin peek), collapsible group memory, in-menu search with keyboard navigation, and hover pin/top quick-actions. A Ctrl+K command palette mirrors the inventory with quick commands; a keyboard-reachable right-click context menu covers open/pin/top/keep-in-sidebar. Supports icon-only panel rows, top-pinning with drag-to-reorder, a compact footer with granular sub-toggles, and a Settings page with height sliders, a locale-safe whitelist, pin management, and settings export/import.
- [qgd1850890306-rgb/dsh-todo-checklist](https://github.com/qgd1850890306-rgb/dsh-todo-checklist) - Todo sidecar for DeepSeek Harness: turn selected text into a todo, capture todos from a conversation, review them in a left sidebar panel, get due reminders as an in-app popup or an IM bot message, and export everything to HTML, Markdown, Excel, CSV or JSON.
- [qgx1992/dsh-ui-tools](https://github.com/qgx1992/dsh-ui-tools) - Five web UI tools in one plugin: a provider + model two-button selector with reasoning-level control, collapse/expand-all for sidebar workspace groups, a session tab listing the files the session changed (needs kernel 0.1.2-alpha.1+, silently absent on older kernels), a workspace badge next to the session title, and a settings page that toggles them.
- [qgynisc/dsh-inline-pastes](https://github.com/qgynisc/dsh-inline-pastes) - Inline image chips for the DeepSeek Harness composer — paste an image and it lands in the text as a numbered chip (image-1.png) with hover preview. Images still ship through the official attachment pipeline, names restart per message, the dock and sent-message thumbnails carry a sequence badge, and a pre-send self-check warns when the names in the text no longer match the images in that message.
- [QianLuo-Ly/dsh-weather](https://github.com/QianLuo-Ly/dsh-weather) - Top-centered weather bar for the DSH Web UI: geolocation, current conditions, 12h/7d forecasts and severe-weather notifications via Open-Meteo (free, no API key).
- [QiaoSchoco/dsh-fleeting-light](https://github.com/QiaoSchoco/dsh-fleeting-light) - Draws an ambient glow along the window edge and a moving light band around the composer, sharing one colour table and each with its own switch.
- [QiaoSchoco/dsh-lit-shot](https://github.com/QiaoSchoco/dsh-lit-shot) - Screenshot button for the DSH composer on Windows: drag a region over the frozen screen and the picture lands in the draft as a normal image attachment, with a global hotkey and an editable shortcut.
- [qiaozheng9/dsh-side-chat](https://github.com/qiaozheng9/dsh-side-chat) - Native DSH /side conversation tabs with a clean transcript and inherited context, hidden from the main session list using independent Session forks and the official chat UI, with timestamps, token usage, streaming and the command menu. Extracted from dsh-better-sidebar 0.29.0 (MIT); do not enable it alongside dsh-better-sidebar's own /side entry — the two command entries conflict, so stop one before using the other. Requires Windows DSH Desktop 0.2.1-rc.5 and dsh-delete-session for confirmed permanent cleanup.
- [qichuang321/dsh-web-terminal](https://github.com/qichuang321/dsh-web-terminal) - Windows terminal dock inside the DSH web GUI. It is the occupant of the shipped shell.bottom slot: showing it splits the screen (the columns shrink instead of being covered), hiding it returns the space and keeps the PTY session alive. Each session is a local ConPTY child spawned through node-pty and rendered by xterm.js over a loopback-fenced WebSocket (backpressure, debounced resize). Other plugins can add terminal or DOM views through a host registerView API and a window CustomEvent channel.
- [qinpeizhan77/deepseek-harness-novel-studio](https://github.com/qinpeizhan77/deepseek-harness-novel-studio) - Local-first fiction workspace that links outlines, chapters, characters, timelines, writing rules, and continuity checks to the conversation.
- [qipengxiang/dsh-chat-actions](https://github.com/qipengxiang/dsh-chat-actions) - Hide or show each button under an assistant reply, reorder them by dragging, and collect the hidden ones behind a single ⋯ button.
- [qipengxiang/dsh-enter-combination](https://github.com/qipengxiang/dsh-enter-combination) - Rebind which key sends a message and which key starts a new line in the DeepSeek Harness composer; Enter sends and Cmd/Ctrl+Enter breaks the line by default, and either chord can be re-recorded from a settings page.
- [qipengxiang/dsh-message-quote](https://github.com/qipengxiang/dsh-message-quote) - Quote any message of the current conversation into the composer: pick one from the @ menu, click the quote button on a user or assistant message, or select text and use Quote in the right-click menu. Quotes show as numbered chips in the composer and as summary bubbles above it; after sending, each quote becomes a fenced block so the model can tell quoted text from new text.
- [QIRSHENG/dsh-tray-restart](https://github.com/QIRSHENG/dsh-tray-restart) - Adds Restart App and Host, Reload Page and Force Quit items to the Windows system tray of the official DSH desktop app by patching app.asar in place, and re-applies that patch after each desktop update.
- [QIUZAIYOU/dsh-composer-rewind](https://github.com/QIUZAIYOU/dsh-composer-rewind) - Composer history that restores the whole input state: composer contents, image and file attachments, @ references and slash commands, plus the model, thinking effort, permission and agent preset used.
- [QIUZAIYOU/dsh-panel-control](https://github.com/QIUZAIYOU/dsh-panel-control) - Rearranges the panel entries at the top of the DSH sidebar: drag to reorder or sort alphabetically, hide an entry without unloading its plugin, and split entries into named groups that render as titled separators; group headers collapse on click. A layout guard caps the entry list height and makes it scroll, so a growing list no longer squeezes the session and workspace panes out of view. An optional switch lets the profile's default model propose groups, and the host whitelists the returned ids and labels before they are stored locally.
- [qiuzhiyei/dsh-companion](https://github.com/qiuzhiyei/dsh-companion) - Desktop pet companion for DSH with live task HUD, token and cost stats, balance and peak-hour alerts, reasoning-effort control, current and history conversation search, and session management.
- [qiweiii/dsh-brand-studio](https://github.com/qiweiii/dsh-brand-studio) - Custom branding for the shared DSH Web UI: a Settings → Branding panel for the name, badge, and local logo/wordmark images with size and rounding, live preview, optional page-title brand, and reset to the official look.
- [qjcnmd/dsh-reasoning-slider](https://github.com/qjcnmd/dsh-reasoning-slider) - Codex-style reasoning-effort slider embedded in the model selector.
- [qrxf/dsh-workspace-graph](https://github.com/qrxf/dsh-workspace-graph) - Draggable bubble graph of DeepSeek Harness workspaces and sessions in the Web UI: workspaces are large bubbles, sessions are small ones, and lines show real membership. The canvas supports pan, zoom and fit; filters cover workspace, status, type and archive state; search covers workspace names, paths and session titles; native actions open or create sessions, rename workspaces, and archive or restore sessions. Layouts are saved in the browser.
- [qt9494/dsh-seeyon-workbench](https://github.com/qt9494/dsh-seeyon-workbench) - Seeyon workbench that mounts OA to-dos and meetings, the skill catalog, and one-click aTrust VPN in one panel. Its vendored modules replace the author's dsh-seeyon-todo, dsh-skillhub and dsh-vpn — install this bundle or those plugins, not both. Requires a Seeyon OA account; the VPN part is Windows-only and needs the Sangfor aTrust client.
- [Quinn2006/dsh-guise](https://github.com/Quinn2006/dsh-guise) - Persona wardrobe for DeepSeek Harness: save multiple personas, apply them globally or per workspace, with a master switch, an adjustable API balance guard, and a lazy token-loving tired mode that speaks on the agent's behalf when quota runs out. Files-as-config, hot reload, zero dependencies.
- [Qulierm/dsh-macos-settings-shortcut](https://github.com/Qulierm/dsh-macos-settings-shortcut) - Restores Cmd+, to open the existing Settings dialog in DeepSeek Harness on macOS.
- [qwert702/dsh-auto-translate](https://github.com/qwert702/dsh-auto-translate) - Full-pipeline Chinese translation for the dsh web GUI: live translation of the model reasoning as it streams, in-place Chinese below each English reply, and one-line Chinese summaries for tool outputs and tool-call glosses — a standalone provider request that never enters the session transcript.
- [qwerty-k-de/dsh-attach-picker](https://github.com/qwerty-k-de/dsh-attach-picker) - Toolbar picture button in the DSH Web composer: click to pick multiple images through the native file dialog — they join the same draft-image rail as drag-and-drop and paste, with the host image-format, per-message-count and per-image-size limits enforced inline.
- [qwh15622336051/dsh-prompt-optimizer](https://github.com/qwh15622336051/dsh-prompt-optimizer) - Rewrites the composer draft into a precise prompt with a configurable model and a fully editable rewriting instruction; reads the content behind @file, @folder and @session references for context and keeps the reference chips intact; offers a one-click undo that restores the text you wrote yourself, and places the button in either composer toolbar with an adjustable colour and size.
- [qwq-wny/dsh-eta-forecast](https://github.com/qwq-wny/dsh-eta-forecast) - Adds an ETA widget to the DSH web and desktop UI that estimates when the current turn will finish from the todo list and step pace, and shows the remaining time, a progress bar and a confidence badge.
- [qwq1999/dsh-panellist](https://github.com/qwq1999/dsh-panellist) - Panellist manager: reorder the entries on the left rail, rename them, replace their icons, hide them, and fold several into a Group with a flyout menu, from a Settings tab.
- [qys0012/dsh-hotspots](https://github.com/qys0012/dsh-hotspots) - Real-time hot topics panel for the DSH web GUI: AI news and engineering news, each item with a distilled summary and the source link, shown from a sidebar entry with three views, keyword/source filters, read-state marking and hotkeys.
- [qyw233/dsh-deeplink](https://github.com/qyw233/dsh-deeplink) - Deep links: open a specific session or workspace via `?session=` / `?workspace=`.
- [qzz19970504/dsh-model-picker-control](https://github.com/qzz19970504/dsh-model-picker-control) - Model picker control for the DSH composer: a settings section beside the built-in Models page renames model display names and provider group names, adds a short coloured suffix capsule after a name, groups models from different providers into a new partition, reorders provider groups by drag or keyboard, sorts models inside a group by catalog order or name, and hides a provider or a single model without removing it from the catalog. Edits stay in a draft until you press Save, and the built-in 1017 tag and a custom capsule can be shown side by side.
- [Rabbit-bot-No-002/dsh-chat-avatar](https://github.com/Rabbit-bot-No-002/dsh-chat-avatar) - Adds an avatar to the left of each turn's final AI reply and to the right of each user message; upload your own image and set its size and circle or square frame.
- [RadicalGitter/dsh-ui-translate](https://github.com/RadicalGitter/dsh-ui-translate) - Translates visible Chinese UI and content in DeepSeek Harness Web, using a browser-local OPUS-MT model for session titles, workspace names, messages, search results, and opted-in plugin prose while keeping remote translation limited to allowlisted UI labels.
- [RAFOLIE/dsh-desktop-windowos](https://github.com/RAFOLIE/dsh-desktop-windowos) - Windows tray desktop shell: auto-installs and auto-updates the exe from GitHub Releases, creates desktop shortcuts for both the app and the web UI, and exposes a desktop_launch tool to start it from the conversation.
- [Ragnoryok1/dsh-client-locale-ru](https://github.com/Ragnoryok1/dsh-client-locale-ru) - Russian for the dsh web GUI: the whole interface plus the menus of 520 third-party plugins — 584 sections, 67,666 strings.
- [RAK7S/dsh-task-tree](https://github.com/RAK7S/dsh-task-tree) - A draggable task-tree panel inside the chat page: capture tasks by voice or text, drag to reorder, edge-snapping collapse, and a plain-text queue file you can edit or version yourself.
- [RAK7S/dsh-weather-whisper](https://github.com/RAK7S/dsh-weather-whisper) - Weather card once per session: remembers your home city so a VPN never moves it, inline city picker, theme presets, custom separators, optional TTS hook for third-party voices.
- [Rakanrkk/dsh-sidebar-scroll](https://github.com/Rakanrkk/dsh-sidebar-scroll) - Shares sidebar navigation scrolling with Projects lists; supports native workspace scroll handoff and legacy outer scrolling (DSH 0.2.2-rc.1).
- [rakibulrocky14/dsh-cli](https://github.com/rakibulrocky14/dsh-cli) - Full-screen terminal TUI for DeepSeek Harness with Web-GUI parity, live streaming, agent presets, and DeepSeek blue styling.
- [ralcer-he/dsh-progress-lens](https://github.com/ralcer-he/dsh-progress-lens) - A right-side panel that tells you in plain words what a long-running task has finished, what it is doing right now, and what it will most likely do next — read-only, never touches your session, and you can ask it a question or just chat with it while the task keeps running. Requires the dsh-better-sidebar plugin, whose right sidebar hosts the panel.
- [ramwin/dsh-workspace-tree-status](https://github.com/ramwin/dsh-workspace-tree-status) - Shows an aggregated session-status dot in front of every folder in DSH Web's sidebar workspace tree — waiting for input, just finished, or running — and bubbles the sessions of nested workspaces up to their parent folder.
- [rans725/dsh-workspace-ranswork](https://github.com/rans725/dsh-workspace-ranswork) - Sidebar workspace panel that switches between running tasks and all conversations, shown as one freely sortable card list with per-card pin, rename, copy ID, fork, stop, and archive actions.
- [Raphaelutumn/dsh-mood](https://github.com/Raphaelutumn/dsh-mood) - A tiny behavioral mood ring for AI coding agents — folds session behavior (consecutive failures, repeated calls, activity) into a four-state Mood (GOOD / CONFUSED / FRUSTRATED / OVERWHELMED) shown as a low-friction session-header status light.
- [Ratevoid/dsh-aseprite](https://github.com/Ratevoid/dsh-aseprite) - A browser pixel-art and sprite animation editor with Aseprite-compatible files, local crop/outline blueprints, and optional LLM workflow nodes.
- [RaulLazaro/dsh-preview-plugin](https://github.com/RaulLazaro/dsh-preview-plugin) - Live preview tab for DSH — embed any dev server in an iframe with transparent SPA proxying.
- [raven3005672/dsh-plugin-highlight-notes](https://github.com/raven3005672/dsh-plugin-highlight-notes) - Highlight or quote any passage of an answer, attach a note to it, then send the excerpt back to the composer as a quoted instruction.
- [Ravi666688/dsh-screen-glow](https://github.com/Ravi666688/dsh-screen-glow) - Edge glow for DeepSeek Harness: blue while it drives your mouse and keyboard, amber while it asks a question, red while it waits for approval, plus a top banner naming the current action. Desktop only: the overlay is a separate Electron process (auto-detected from the DSH desktop app or dsh-pet; override with DSH_ELECTRON_PATH) and stays dark without one.
- [rayafriandion/dsh-oc-tui](https://github.com/rayafriandion/dsh-oc-tui) - An opencode-inspired terminal UI for DeepSeek Harness that boots as a dsh profile app plugin, with a session sidebar, streaming chat, markdown tool cards, todo lists, slash-command suggestions, a telemetry footer with a context meter and inline approval prompts.
- [Rayle8/dsh-aside-note](https://github.com/Rayle8/dsh-aside-note) - Per-session note list beside the conversation: a button at the top right of the chat badges the open count and opens a resizable right-side panel where notes are added, checked off, copied, edited or appended to the chat input, with panel size and drafts kept in the browser.
- [RCYD857/857-dsh-file-attach](https://github.com/RCYD857/857-dsh-file-attach) - Drag or paste files or pictures into the DSH Web composer as one scrolling row of chips, sent as @path references.
- [ReachGa0/dsh-desktop#plugin](https://github.com/ReachGa0/dsh-desktop/tree/main/plugin) - DSH plugin (dsh-desktop-launcher) that launches the dsh-desktop Windows Electron shell from the conversation: double-click to run the DSH web in a standalone window, region screenshot with GPU-accelerated selection that auto-pastes into the chat (works with vision bridges), system tray, session manager, auto environment setup, configurable port, single-instance lock.
- [realguan/dsh-deepseek-chat-embed](https://github.com/realguan/dsh-deepseek-chat-embed) - Embeds chat.deepseek.com directly inside DeepSeek Harness desktop with persistent login sessions and dual-entry chat panels.
- [realguan/dsh-mermaid-preview](https://github.com/realguan/dsh-mermaid-preview) - Render Mermaid fenced code blocks as native diagram cards with 8 design-system themes and adaptive layout.
- [realhacker/dsh-theme-colorizer](https://github.com/RealHacker/dsh-theme-colorizer) - A Deepseek Harness plugin that gives its UI color themes.
- [REALOIYO/dsh-ember](https://github.com/REALOIYO/dsh-ember) - Composer UI for DeepSeek Harness that does two things. The reasoning-effort row in the model menu becomes a continuous slider drawn on the row itself, with the rungs read live from the model adapter so any ladder length works; each rung paints its own ember plume into a canvas behind the knob. Separately, the permission trigger renders in the theme error colour whenever full access is the active preset, including its row in the permission menu.
- [REALOIYO/dsh-settings-button](https://github.com/REALOIYO/dsh-settings-button) - Adds a gear button beside the sidebar account row that opens the Settings panel in one click. It runs the host's own settings.open command, so it reaches the shell store directly instead of going through the account menu, and the behaviour is identical on desktop and on a phone over the LAN where no keyboard shortcut is available.
- [rebornace/dsh-agent-enhancer](https://github.com/rebornace/dsh-agent-enhancer) - Agent enhancements for DeepSeek Harness Desktop: prompt history, one-click optimize with undo, next-prompt prediction, conversation and tracked-file restore, model bill, context details with compact, Auto routing, and per-provider DeepSeek balance.
- [redfox-data/redfox-skill-gallery](https://github.com/redfox-data/redfox-skill-gallery) - Adds a sidebar entry that opens a card gallery of all Red Fox Community skills from the redfox-community-dsh plugin.
