- [reg4916/dsh-bar#plugin](https://github.com/reg4916/dsh-bar/tree/master/plugin) - Host plugin for the macOS menu bar companion: live DeepSeek Harness session state, Approve/Deny for tool approvals, stop the current turn, open the exact session. The native app is a free universal build in GitHub Releases.
- [regrevia/dsh-chat-link-actions](https://github.com/regrevia/dsh-chat-link-actions) - Right-click any attachment or path in the transcript and hand it to your own action: send it to a WeChat contact, open it in Chrome, or anything else you configure — one right-click, anything you want. Ships with quote-into-composer, reveal in the OS file manager, copy path and Send to…, and lets you add your own entries (command, open with, shortcut, link, PowerShell). Menus are drawn in the app's own style, never the system one.
- [regrevia/dsh-vantage](https://github.com/regrevia/dsh-vantage) - A read-only overview tab beside the conversation: what this round asked, what was observed, what still needs checking, and a bounded history you can step through round by round.
- [renat3u/dsh-web-archive](https://github.com/renat3u/dsh-web-archive) - Collapse noisy messages (Think, Bash, etc.) in conversations.
- [renbooc/focus-alarm](https://github.com/renbooc/focus-alarm) - A draggable floating card for the DeepSeek Harness Web UI that combines a Pomodoro timer, an off-work countdown, and focus statistics. Each launch opens on a fresh focus phase; phase completion triggers a chime, card shake, and particle burst. Includes today's pomodoros, focus minutes, streak and a 7-day chart, rhythm reminders for wrapping up and eye rest, optional system notifications, tab-title timer, auto-start, +5 minutes, and a custom long-break interval.
- [researchrepo-backup/dsh-countup-pomodoro](https://github.com/researchrepo-backup/dsh-countup-pomodoro) - Count-up Pomodoro: focus counts up with no ceiling and never stops itself at 25 minutes, finishing drops straight into a short break, and a status board plus the four quadrants keep the task list in view.
- [revive123456/dsh-obsidian-notes](https://github.com/revive123456/dsh-obsidian-notes) - Obsidian-style notes panel in the DSH right sidebar: a tree over a local folder, Markdown preview with line numbers, edit and save, Obsidian callouts, tables and wikilinks, screenshot paste into the vault, one-click insert of a note path into the chat input, and batch archive or delete for sessions and workspaces.
- [rex178178/dsh-turnbar](https://github.com/rex178178/dsh-turnbar) - Video-style turn navigation for the Web UI: full-map progress bar with hover preview cards (tools/files/tokens), drag scrub, ⌘K in-conversation search, ⌘↑/⌘↓ stepping, Esc-return. Event-log powered — works beyond the loaded window. Dual-half plugin on the official composer.dock slot.
- [rezon-aki/dsh-streamfold](https://github.com/rezon-aki/dsh-streamfold) - Streaming fold for the DSH web transcript: the newest thinking window opens itself, older process rows fold into one summary, and a finished turn glides back to your prompt, which stays pinned while you read.
- [rg2304luyue/dsh-pa](https://github.com/rg2304luyue/dsh-pa) - A diamond button in the composer that rewrites your draft into a structured prompt and puts the original draft back when clicked again.
- [Ri0n72Y/dsh-workspace-scope](https://github.com/Ri0n72Y/dsh-workspace-scope) - Per-workspace Skill and MCP enablement for DeepSeek Harness: control which skills and MCP servers new sessions load, per project.
- [Rice00/dsh-job-progress](https://github.com/Rice00/dsh-job-progress) - Live progress for long-running background jobs in the DSH web GUI: a draggable floating ball in the session that owns the job, expanding into a panel with done/total, speed and ETA.
- [Rice00/dsh-loupe](https://github.com/Rice00/dsh-loupe) - Explain a selected word or sentence from a DSH conversation in a floating window: a channel of its own that leaves the session untouched, and the answer can be pinned to a session of its own.
- [Rick1125/dsh-beads-viewer](https://github.com/Rick1125/dsh-beads-viewer) - Beads issue browser in the right sidebar: filter and search the tracker with live ready and waiting counts, read issue Markdown with clickable project-file and issue links, and copy or insert the issue id. Requires the bd CLI on PATH and a project with a Beads workspace.
- [Ricketts-Guo/dsh-shortcuts](https://github.com/Ricketts-Guo/dsh-shortcuts) - Fully customizable keyboard shortcuts for the Web UI: 34 pre-registered features (sessions, views, clipboard, models, silent permission cycling, settings), one-click recording to bind your own, and a shortcut cheatsheet with built-in diagnostics.
- [ridistant8-art/dsh-cookie-meter](https://github.com/ridistant8-art/dsh-cookie-meter) - Windows-only floating percentage meter for the DSH desktop (0.2.x) cookie jar: it estimates how much of the request-header budget the dsh-auth cookies occupy, turns red past half, and clears the jar on the next launch with one click, avoiding the lockout that hits after roughly 72 launches.
- [riesbri/dshline#dshline](https://github.com/riesbri/dshline/tree/main/packages/dshline) - Terminal-native TUI frontend for DeepSeek Harness that presents sessions, models, plugins, jobs, subagents, todos, and provider configuration through Harness capabilities while preserving real terminal scrollback.
- [rinsoraa/dsh-pulse-rail](https://github.com/rinsoraa/dsh-pulse-rail) - Turns the DSH turn-navigation rail into a music visualiser: each thin bar grows and thickens with its own frequency band, from system audio captured with WASAPI loopback. Geometry only, no colour or glow, and the rail keeps its native hover preview, click-to-jump and wheel scrolling.
- [rivenshanghai/dsh-toomuch-plugins](https://github.com/rivenshanghai/dsh-toomuch-plugins) - Collapses the global panel entries above the workspace in the DSH Web sidebar into a single toggle in the sidebar footer.
- [RockChen87/colorful-notes-dsh](https://github.com/RockChen87/colorful-notes-dsh) - Colorful sticky notes for the DeepSeek Harness sidebar, with add, edit, bulk delete, and daily, weekly, monthly or one-time reminders.
- [Roilkl/dsh-daicat-pet](https://github.com/Roilkl/dsh-daicat-pet) - An animated cat companion for the DSH chat UI. It reacts to session states, shows editable click-to-read lines and per-turn token counts, and supports dragging with a squish effect, left/right flipping and Ctrl+wheel resizing.
- [romany-saad/dsh-plugins#dsh-drafts](https://github.com/romany-saad/dsh-plugins/tree/main/packages/dsh-drafts) - Get back what you typed and walked away from: a new Web conversation you leave before sending never appears in the sidebar, so its draft is out of reach. This plugin lists those drafts above the composer of the next new conversation, newest first, with their workspace and time; Open takes you back with the text and chips in place, and Discard drops one, with Undo.
- [romany-saad/dsh-plugins#dsh-quote](https://github.com/romany-saad/dsh-plugins/tree/main/packages/dsh-quote) - Show the model exactly which part you mean: select passages in a Web conversation and they go into your next message as composer chips, each with an optional note you can edit. Quoted passages stay highlighted until you send, hovering a chip shows the full quote, and the sent message shows each quote as a card.
- [rsbsnv/dsh-mobile-adapt](https://github.com/rsbsnv/dsh-mobile-adapt) - Mobile layer for DSH Web: swipe-gesture drawer sidebar with adjustable width, layered back key, bigger tap targets, Enter inserts a newline instead of sending, keyboard avoidance and a 15px font floor.
- [ruaibeite/dsh-files-to-chat](https://github.com/ruaibeite/dsh-files-to-chat) - Right-click a file or folder in the right sidebar file tree to add it to the composer as an @ reference (⌘/Ctrl and Shift multi-select insert a whole batch), open it with the default application, or reveal it in the system file manager. Open and reveal go through the built-in open-in-app host capability, so those entries appear unless the host explicitly reports the capability as unsupported.
- [ruaibeite/dsh-input-polish](https://github.com/ruaibeite/dsh-input-polish) - A polish button in the composer trailing controls that rewrites the draft in place with the model. Context is matched by topic, not by recency: anchors from the draft (paths, identifiers, quoted text, CJK bigrams) pick the earlier messages about the same task, so a session that interleaves several tasks does not leak another task goal or files into the rewrite — and the prompt explicitly lets the model ignore the extract when it does not fit. The result goes back through the same undo-able editor path a human edit uses. Drafts holding reference chips (@file, folder, session) or a claimed /command, and drafts mid-submission, disable the button instead of silently dropping references.
- [ruanimal/dsh-sidebar-panel-buttons](https://github.com/ruanimal/dsh-sidebar-panel-buttons) - Collapses the DSH Web sidebar panel menu (plugins, extensions, scheduled tasks, IM) from a vertical labelled list into a horizontal row of 36×36 icon buttons with hover tooltips; a Settings → General option switches back to the list and is remembered per browser.
- [Rudyy898/dsh-drag-path](https://github.com/Rudyy898/dsh-drag-path) - Like Codex/Claude Code: drag any file or folder (no format limit) onto the DSH web page and its real absolute path is inserted into the composer instantly — no typing paths by hand. Workspace-first smart lookup, size checks, same-name picker, batch and clipboard paste support, Windows/macOS.
- [ruide92/dsh-project-atlas](https://github.com/ruide92/dsh-project-atlas) - Project Atlas: a local-first DSH workbench that gives every project a readable name and one-sentence intent, merges duplicate project folders after confirmation, and tidies stale projects without deleting files.
- [Ruiming-cn/dsh-ask-in-sidebar](https://github.com/Ruiming-cn/dsh-ask-in-sidebar) - Ask a sidebar assistant about selected assistant text: an ephemeral right-side panel answers from a snapshot of the main conversation's LLM history with streaming Markdown, collapsible thinking and multi-turn follow-ups, without touching the main conversation.
- [Ruiming-cn/dsh-better-at](https://github.com/Ruiming-cn/dsh-better-at) - Fast `@` file/session reference caching for the DSH Web GUI: preloaded indexes, browser-local keystroke filtering and native mention serialization.
- [ruisenbai/dsh-turn-fold](https://github.com/ruisenbai/dsh-turn-fold) - Folds streaming reasoning and tool calls into collapsed step bars and collapses each finished turn into a single summary bar; step bars show native tool icons, filenames, edited line counts, and failure counts, finished turns restore duration, first-token time, token usage, generation speed, and cache hit metrics from recorded Session events, and preferences persist on the Host. Changes browser presentation only. Requires DSH 0.1.5-alpha.1.
- [ruiyukirin/dsh-prompt-optimization-master](https://github.com/ruiyukirin/dsh-prompt-optimization-master) - Adds a button to the composer that rewrites the unsent draft into a clearer, more specific prompt, with one-click restore, a settings page and an optional per-request model override.
- [runcat-tommy/dsh-chinese-poetry](https://github.com/runcat-tommy/dsh-chinese-poetry) - Token-free Chinese classical poetry tab in the DSH conversation view with search, filters, 飞花令, daily poem, favorites, festival topics and a share-card image; AI explanation reuses your own DSH session.
- [runcat-tommy/dsh-panda-calendar](https://github.com/runcat-tommy/dsh-panda-calendar) - A conversation-view tab for DeepSeek Harness Web showing solar/lunar dates, ganzhi year/month/day, Chinese zodiac, 24 solar terms, festivals, China public holidays including make-up workdays, multi-city weather, an epoch↔date timestamp converter with a time-zone picker, and bundled offline "on this day" history from free sources without an API key.
- [runcat-tommy/dsh-view-manager](https://github.com/runcat-tommy/dsh-view-manager) - View tab manager for the DeepSeek Harness Web GUI session header: enable, hide, reorder and rename the conversation view tabs (Chat / Trajectory), with locale-aware zh/en copy and npm update reminders.
- [rUrU516/dsh-quicktime-player](https://github.com/rUrU516/dsh-quicktime-player) - Sidebar video player for DSH: it registers its own right-sidebar tab type, so any video opened through the harness — the Files tab, a chat file reference, another sidebar plugin's file tree — lands in this player. Bytes stream over a Host HTTP Range route (with a tokenized loopback source so the progress bar can actually be scrubbed inside the desktop app), and the player adds wheel zoom, drag pan, frame stepping, speed and volume popovers, and a built-in guide that points at every control.
- [Ryuu-64/dsh-find-all](https://github.com/Ryuu-64/dsh-find-all) - Search titles and saved text across ordinary DeepSeek Harness (DSH) conversations in all workspaces, optionally including archived chats, with Ctrl+F / Cmd+F search inside the current conversation.
- [S-anZZz/dsh-deepseek-web-sync](https://github.com/S-anZZz/dsh-deepseek-web-sync) - Desktop only. DeepSeek Web in the Harness right Sidebar with synchronized theme, automatic launch, one-time web sign-in, and one-click question transfer.
- [s3yf1337/dsh-desktop#bundle](https://github.com/s3yf1337/dsh-desktop/tree/main/bundle) - Desktop profile for dsh: native Tauri window over the harness web surface with tray, native notifications, a file-manager panel, and one-click updates.
- [SA1992X/dsh-ctrl-enter-submit](https://github.com/SA1992X/dsh-ctrl-enter-submit) - Makes plain Enter insert a newline in the composer and Ctrl/Cmd+Enter submit, toggleable in settings.
- [Sad-Jack/deepseek-harness-russian](https://github.com/Sad-Jack/deepseek-harness-russian) - Russian UI language pack for DeepSeek Harness 0.2.1-rc.5 with 2,909 strings based on official translations, version-specific compatibility fixes, and no app archive patching.
- [Sad-Jack/dsh-resource-monitor](https://github.com/Sad-Jack/dsh-resource-monitor) - Compact macOS strip above the composer showing CPU, GPU, RAM, swap, memory pressure and local Ollama model ON/OFF status. Tested on Harness 0.2.1-rc.5.
- [sailoumili/dsh-file-opener](https://github.com/sailoumili/dsh-file-opener) - Turns file and directory paths in DSH chat into clickable buttons: click one to preview the file in the right sidebar, or open it with the system default application.
- [sailoumili/dsh-sidebar-plus](https://github.com/sailoumili/dsh-sidebar-plus) - A file-view enhancement plugin that extends the official DSH sidebar, providing source view, in-place editing, line number display, search, and font zoom.
- [Saknutella/dsh-thinking-quips](https://github.com/Saknutella/dsh-thinking-quips) - Personalises the DSH running-turn status line: rotating bilingual quips, a configurable animated loading icon (5 styles x 3 sizes) and one-click matching to the active theme colour, all in a General-settings panel, plus a # Chinese / # English sectioned quips editor. Ships with no dependencies, no build step and no config file.
- [sakuraqqq/dsh-auto-paste](https://github.com/sakuraqqq/dsh-auto-paste) - Auto-saves large pasted text as an attachment file in the DeepSeek Harness web input box; with dsh-better-sidebar installed, the paste pill opens the file in the sidebar editor and saves it back.
- [Sama05/dsh-download-progress](https://github.com/Sama05/dsh-download-progress) - Shows download, install, fetch and search progress in a floating overlay or in a compact chip above the composer, and lists background jobs that have no progress to draw.
- [samsam560/chat-share-card](https://github.com/samsam560/chat-share-card) - Turn any DeepSeek Harness answer into a polished PNG share card: click a button under the reply, the answer is re-typeset on a canvas (8 gradient presets, light/dark, three fonts, width 720/880/1080, radius, padding, font size, line height, 1x-3x export, code wrap/highlight/line numbers, custom title and footer). Fully local rendering — no upload, no third-party dependency.
- [samsam560/dog-kjdh](https://github.com/samsam560/dog-kjdh) - Splash and exit animations for DeepSeek Harness. Five hand-drawn styles (whale light-sweep, pulse, stardust, typewriter, radar rings) — or drop in your own video / GIF. A settings panel controls speed, particle count, accent colour, wording, mute and fit. The exit animation needs the bundled one-time desktop-shell patch (node tools/patch-asar.mjs --apply, reversible); without it closing only hides the window.
- [sanlisanxun/dsh-plugin-nickname](https://github.com/sanlisanxun/dsh-plugin-nickname) - Custom Chinese nicknames for dsh plugins, displayed directly in the native plugin list.
- [Sanqi-normal/dsh-model-picker](https://github.com/Sanqi-normal/dsh-model-picker) - Provider-tabbed model picker for the dsh web composer: left provider column and right model list, both independently scrollable, with cross-provider search.
- [sanqiPanax/dsh-sticker-board](https://github.com/sanqiPanax/dsh-sticker-board) - Fridge-magnet quick-task stickers docked above the composer: one-click sends a preloaded prompt into the current session, grouped long-term/short-term.
- [sanshanya/better-model-provider](https://github.com/sanshanya/better-model-provider) - Settings page for per-model capabilities: reasoning-effort levels with their wire spellings, input modalities, and token capacities, written as declarations or sparse catalog overrides.
- [SanYe-SanJiu/dsh-power-switch](https://github.com/SanYe-SanJiu/dsh-power-switch) - Windows-only process control for the harness: a sidebar button that shuts DSH down through the host's own graceful exit path, an app-window / normal-tab switch on the Plugins card that rewrites the desktop shortcut and restarts, and advanced settings for the wait before exit, the process exit code and a force-quit option.
- [Saretheya/dsh-settings-nav-order](https://github.com/Saretheya/dsh-settings-nav-order) - Reorder the settings panel nav by long-pressing and dragging a section: the dragged row turns translucent and follows the pointer, the order is saved to the plugin's own folder, and uninstalling restores the official order.
- [sbbbx/dsh-client-ui-webchat](https://github.com/sbbbx/dsh-client-ui-webchat) - Embeds chat.deepseek.com in a DSH desktop panel and moves text both ways round by round: pick any round of the web conversation and copy it, or send it as a user message into a session of a chosen workspace; and fill any answer of the current agent session back into the web input box without sending it. The embed reuses the web session, so it spends no API quota, and asks for a web login once per app start.
- [sbVAN/dsh-latex](https://github.com/sbVAN/dsh-latex) - Renders `latex` / `tex` code blocks in the chat as KaTeX formulae, and injects a system-prompt section that tells the model to write `$...$` and `$$...$$` instead of fenced code blocks.
- [scottzx/feature-blueprint](https://github.com/scottzx/feature-blueprint) - Adds mind maps and an Excalidraw canvas to the DSH sidebar, with local blueprint-file editing, Excalidraw file previews and standalone CLIs.
- [月令 Yueling](https://github.com/SDSAEWQ/yueling) - Yueling (月令) — a solar-term card for the DSH Web UI: a dot under the conversation header unfolds into today's term, its three pentads, how long until the next term, a day-pillar reading, and a year table of all 24 terms. The hexagram page casts coins and keeps a seven-day log; the jieqi skill is bundled.
- [secyborg/dsh-find-bar](https://github.com/secyborg/dsh-find-bar) - Browser-style in-page find bar (Cmd/Ctrl+F) for the desktop app, with highlight-all, a match counter, and a scope picker for the conversation or the right panel.
- [seeingrain/dsh-media-inline-preview](https://github.com/seeingrain/dsh-media-inline-preview) - All-in-one inline media previewer for mip-img/mip-video/mip-audio fences with streaming playback, ffmpeg transcoding fallback, lightbox, and trust-fenced LAN/mobile access.
- [seeingrain/dsh-upload-file](https://github.com/seeingrain/dsh-upload-file) - Upload any file to DSH, isolated per workspace and session — a composer file library with server-side thumbnails and a context menu to open, copy, re-download or delete.
- [Semidia/dsh-sampling-sliders](https://github.com/Semidia/dsh-sampling-sliders) - Model sampling sliders (temperature / maxTokens) in the composer: override every model request for all providers via the agent/request hook, with hot/persist modes.
- [Semonxue/dsh-my-newhome](https://github.com/Semonxue/dsh-my-newhome) - Replaces the DeepSeek Harness home screen with a configurable palette and background image, a custom hero and sidebar logo, default 3×2 decks of recently used workspaces and quick links (local Chrome history or a custom list), and up to four API-usage widgets (DeepSeek balance, MiniMax, Cline Pass, OpenCode Go) refreshed on a configurable interval. The decks stay on the new-session screen and every option is edited from a built-in settings panel.
- [senyayume/dsh-edit-diff](https://github.com/senyayume/dsh-edit-diff) - Redraws line-level diffs on edit, write, insert and str_replace_editor tool cards — unchanged lines render once and changed characters are underlined — covers run_code (PTC) sub-calls, adds a per-turn changed-files card, and opens or copies any of those paths from the card rows and the tool rows through a host route the plugin serves itself.
- [sereinmono/dsh-desktop-pet](https://github.com/sereinmono/dsh-desktop-pet) - A plugin that adds a desktop pet to your DeepSeek Harness, supporting the Codex pet format, you can use hatch-pet or Petdex to add your pets.
- [SereneJi/dsh-host-paths](https://github.com/SereneJi/dsh-host-paths) - Publishes the desktop shell's file-path bridge as the host-path seam the composer reads, so files and folders dropped into the composer become @path references instead of uploaded copies (images still upload through the native image channel).
- [sevenhsu7/dsh-codex-like-markdown](https://github.com/sevenhsu7/dsh-codex-like-markdown) - In-place Markdown editing for the right-sidebar document preview: a file keeps the shipped renderer's look, clicking a paragraph, heading, list, fence, or table cell puts the caret exactly where you clicked, and only the edited range is written back through ctx.fs.writeText, optionally guarded by the version it read.
- [SeverusZh/dsh-skills-mcp-group-manager](https://github.com/SeverusZh/dsh-skills-mcp-group-manager) - Group installed skills and manage MCP servers from a left-panel UI: filter the model skill catalog by enabled groups (union-deduped) and toggle MCP servers independently.
- [Sevyn-Star/dsh-receipt](https://github.com/Sevyn-Star/dsh-receipt) - Adds a corner card that estimates how much a session spent repeating a failed or empty tool call with the same arguments.
- [sfunction-guo/dsh-screenshot](https://github.com/sfunction-guo/dsh-screenshot) - Snipping tool for the composer: freeze the screen, drag a region, annotate it, and drop the result into the message box. Windows only; the selector is a bundled WinForms binary (rebuilt from the included C# source if missing) and uses the built-in .NET Framework 4.x.
- [Shall0vv/dsh-conversation-files](https://github.com/Shall0vv/dsh-conversation-files) - A left-sidebar gallery of every file the assistant wrote, edited or delivered across all conversations, grouped by file type with preview thumbnails and one-click open. Document previews and the open / open-with / Trash actions require macOS; the gallery, text previews and filters work anywhere.
- [SHAN5246992ZHUI/dsh-select-translate](https://github.com/SHAN5246992ZHUI/dsh-select-translate) - Select text in any Windows app and read the translation in an always-on-top card next to the cursor: streaming text follows the newest line, up to 15000 characters per request. Windows only.
- [Shane0315/dsh-session-filter](https://github.com/Shane0315/dsh-session-filter) - Adds a cross-workspace activity sidebar for running, waiting and unread sessions, with search, optional pinned and scheduled-delivery groups, local read tracking and confirmed archiving.
- [ShawnRen57/dsh-whale-trail](https://github.com/ShawnRen57/dsh-whale-trail) - Local-first achievement collection for DeepSeek Harness. Normal use earns 67 stamped badges across 7 series, completes 9 voyage chapters holding 36 ocean collectibles, unlocks a configurable ASCII aquarium and exports a five-badge voyage card as PNG or offline HTML. No check-ins, no model calls, no chat uploads.
- [ShawnRen57/dsh-whoop-easy-connect](https://github.com/ShawnRen57/dsh-whoop-easy-connect) - Four-step WHOOP account connection (you create your own WHOOP developer app and paste its Client ID/Secret) with automatic sync on dashboard visits, plus recovery, strain, sleep and workout charts inside DeepSeek Harness.
- [shengyvself/dsh-reading-pad](https://github.com/shengyvself/dsh-reading-pad) - Immersive read-only reading panel for DSH — the model delivers pre-formatted Markdown to a native right-sidebar tab via `reading_pad_send`, with three low-blue-light themes and 30em measure typography; also browse manuscript chapters read-only.
- [shenj13/dsh-book-library](https://github.com/shenj13/dsh-book-library) - Read books in the DSH sidebar - import EPUB/TXT/Markdown, get a five-part close reading for every chapter (paragraph summary, best sentences, what the author is doing, the AI's own opinion, what is missing), listen with TTS, ask questions while listening, and export the whole book to one HTML page. Export collects the chapters that already have a close reading rather than unread chapters.
- [SherUnlocked-4869/dsh-plugin-msg-nav](https://github.com/SherUnlocked-4869/dsh-plugin-msg-nav) - Conversation node navigation rail for the DSH Web UI: one dash per user message on the right edge, reading-position tracking, hover preview card, click-to-jump with highlight line, sliding window, and auto-hide.
- [shijiacheng520/dsh-skill-groups](https://github.com/shijiacheng520/dsh-skill-groups) - Grouped skill picker for the composer. A button beside the input box opens a panel that browses every installed skill by scene (research, office, data, writing, media, dev), with auto-classification for new skills, Chinese annotations, search and drill-down; picking one inserts the /skill-name gesture.
- [shimingming520/dsh-conversation-process-collapse](https://github.com/shimingming520/dsh-conversation-process-collapse) - Collapses each settled turn's process rows into one expandable disclosure in the DeepSeek Harness Web conversation while keeping the final answer visible.
- [shinelon/dsh-mcp-plugin](https://github.com/shinelon/dsh-mcp-plugin) - Manage DSH MCP servers from the web settings panel: list, add, edit, remove, enable/disable with live status.
- [shinelon/dsh-mermaid-plugin](https://github.com/shinelon/dsh-mermaid-plugin) - Renders ```mermaid code blocks in DSH Web chat as SVG diagrams, with a diagram/source toggle, light/dark theme following and source-preserving error fallback.
- [shinelon/dsh-workspace-group-plugin](https://github.com/shinelon/dsh-workspace-group-plugin) - Custom sidebar workspace groups for DeepSeek Harness - group directories, drag to regroup and reorder, per-group session browsing and content search, with a one-click fallback to the official workspace list.
- [shizhouyu/dsh-sidebar-notes](https://github.com/shizhouyu/dsh-sidebar-notes) - Markdown notepad in the right sidebar, side by side with the chat, with a formatting toolbar and local autosave.
- [shuuul/dsh-bioviewer](https://github.com/shuuul/dsh-bioviewer) - Registers Mol* file viewers with dsh-better-sidebar for PDB, mmCIF, MRC maps, small molecules and Chimera .cmm markers, including .gz, with a BioViewer workspace tab and bio_open / bio_add tools.
- [shyuan-hub/dsh-compact-button](https://github.com/shyuan-hub/dsh-compact-button) - Adds a Compact context button and a New session button to the context meter panel; compact submits /compact through the same command channel, and new session starts a fresh session in the same workspace.
- [shZhang1614/dsh-float-lamp](https://github.com/shZhang1614/dsh-float-lamp) - Windows floating status lamp outside the DSH window: a borderless always-on-top glowing orb on a fully transparent background — green with a breathing halo while tasks run, orange shaking for approval, blue floating for your input, dim grey idle, dark grey host offline; hovering lists each session's latest task, drag to move, and the lamp self-extinguishes within seconds when the plugin is switched off.
- [siberiah2o/dsh-plugin-terminal](https://github.com/siberiah2o/dsh-plugin-terminal) - Bottom multi-tab terminal panel (node-pty + xterm.js) pinned to the viewport bottom, always below the input box.
- [Sillykjs/dsh-agents-md](https://github.com/Sillykjs/dsh-agents-md) - Adds an Agent docs tab to the conversation view: it lists the AGENTS.md and CLAUDE.md instruction files DSH actually loads for the current session (the ~/.dsh and ~/.agents roots, plus every directory from the project root to the working directory), previews each as Markdown with per-file sizes and the 64 KiB instruction budget, edits and saves them in place after a sha256 version check, and opens the workspace folder from the path button; a saved file takes effect on the next message.
- [simmon-clap/dsh-intelligent-ui](https://github.com/simmon-clap/dsh-intelligent-ui) - Renders model answers as interactive cards inside DeepSeek Harness chat, with 24 whitelisted components and persistent turn cards.
- [SiriLee/dsh-approval-hotkeys](https://github.com/SiriLee/dsh-approval-hotkeys) - Panel hotkeys for the web UI: Enter presses the confirm action and Esc presses the cancel action on every interaction panel (approval, question, plan review).
- [sixzjd/dsh-folder-attach](https://github.com/sixzjd/dsh-folder-attach) - Drag folders into the DeepSeek Harness web composer instead of hitting 'only the desktop app supports folders'. Adds a host folder dialog, /folder, and drag/paste into @path mentions. Plain browser, no desktop app.
- [sjrslh/dsh-ui-zoom](https://github.com/sjrslh/dsh-ui-zoom) - Ctrl+wheel page zoom (60–180%, remembered) for the whole DSH UI, plus a bottom-right Settings entry that opens the host settings panel, with Ctrl+0 to reset.
- [SkyblueeeLabs/dsh-iconic-launcher](https://github.com/SkyblueeeLabs/dsh-iconic-launcher) - Customize the DeepSeek Harness desktop shortcut icon: pick a preset or upload an image, auto-remove the white backdrop, build a multi-size ICO, and write the shortcut in one click.
-
